 581  A359              ;==============================================================================
 582  A359              ; New game: start level 1.  Start-up has set the hardware (speed, layers,
 583  A359              ; clip window, palettes, memory map), which the menu leaves alone; here the
 584  A359              ; game state and the screen contents are set up from scratch, so this also
 585  A359              ; works for a second game.
 586  A359              ;==============================================================================
 587  A359              newGame:
 588  A359 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top                 ; (entered by jp from the menu)
 589  A35C 21 5C A8             ld hl,start_state               ; player, weapon slots, selection
 590  A35F 11 B8 A7             ld de,cam_x
 591  A362 01 1A 00             ld bc,start_state_end-start_state
 592  A365 ED B0                ldir
 593  A367 21 76 A8             ld hl,bullets                   ; no rounds or beam in flight
 594  A36A 11 77 A8             ld de,bullets+1
 595  A36D 01 72 05             ld bc,(NBULLETS+1)*BREC+NPART*PREC-1    ; and no particles
 596  A370 36 00                ld (hl),0
 597  A372 ED B0                ldir
 598  A374 3E 01                ld a,START_LEVEL
 599  A376 32 D2 A7             ld (level),a
 600  A379 3E 32                ld a,VTOL_HP
 601  A37B 32 D5 A7             ld (vtol_hp),a                  ; the VTOL undamaged
 602  A37E 3E 64                ld a,DEAD_PAUSE
 603  A380 32 D9 A7             ld (dead_wait),a
 604  A383 AF                   xor a
 605  A384 32 D6 A7             ld (vtol_dead),a
 606  A387 32 D7 A7             ld (dead_down),a
 607  A38A 32 D8 A7             ld (dead_vy),a
 608  A38D 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 609  A390 22 DD A7             ld (salvage_points),hl          ; no salvage yet
 610  A393 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock                 ; and no weapons in store or
 611  A396 06 12                ld b,2*(WPN_LAST+1)             ; unlocked (wpn_unlocked follows)
 612  A398 36 00        .ws:    ld (hl),0
 613  A39A 23                   inc hl
 614  A39B 10 FB                djnz .ws
 615  A39D 21 C5 A7             ld hl,wpn_slots                 ; but those fitted at the start
 616  A3A0 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 617  A3A2 7E           .wu:    ld a,(hl)
 618  A3A3 11 45 A8             ld de,wpn_unlocked
 619  A3A6 ED 32                add de,a
 620  A3A8 3E 01                ld a,1
 621  A3AA 12                   ld (de),a
 622  A3AB 23                   inc hl
 623  A3AC 23                   inc hl
 624  A3AD 10 F3                djnz .wu
 625  A3AF AF                   xor a
 626  A3B0 32 45 A8             ld (wpn_unlocked+WPN_NONE),a    ; (an empty slot isn't a weapon)
 627  A3B3                      ; fall through: the first level
 628  A3B3
 629  A3B3              ;==============================================================================
 630  A3B3              ; Start the level `level`: on newGame, and after each level completed
 631  A3B3              ; (level_complete_screen).  The VTOL goes back onto the base pad, level and
 632  A3B3              ; still; its hit points, weapons and ammo carry on.  The level's files are
 633  A3B3              ; loaded and everything in the city is set up afresh.
 634  A3B3              ;==============================================================================
 635  A3B3              next_level:
 636  A3B3 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top
 637  A3B6 21 5C A8             ld hl,start_state               ; position, heading, pitch, speed
 638  A3B9 11 B8 A7             ld de,cam_x
 639  A3BC 01 0D 00             ld bc,speed+1-cam_x
 640  A3BF ED B0                ldir
 641  A3C1 21 76 A8             ld hl,bullets                   ; no rounds or beam in flight
 642  A3C4 11 77 A8             ld de,bullets+1
 643  A3C7 01 72 05             ld bc,(NBULLETS+1)*BREC+NPART*PREC-1    ; and no particles
 644  A3CA 36 00                ld (hl),0
 645  A3CC ED B0                ldir
 646  A3CE 3E 01                ld a,1
 647  A3D0 32 DB A7             ld (wave_number),a              ; the level's first zombie wave
 648  A3D3 AF                   xor a
 649  A3D4 32 DA A7             ld (level_done),a
 650  A3D7 32 DC A7             ld (game_over),a
 651  A3DA ED 91 56 1E          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_LEVEL    ; the level's files: its record, its
 652  A3DE 3A D2 A7             ld a,(level)                    ; city's map, colours and palette,
 653  A3E1 CD 00 C0             call load_level                 ; the draw distance (level_load.asm)
 654  A3E4 30 07                jr nc,.loaded
 655  A3E6 ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; couldn't: say so, back to the menu
 656  A3EA C3 A4 C3             jp load_error_screen            ; (menu.asm)
 657  A3ED              .loaded:
 658  A3ED CD DA 28             call base_init                  ; the pad and terrace, at full strength
 659  A3F0 ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
 660  A3F4 CD A3 C3             call weapons_reset
 661  A3F7
 662  A3F7                      ; clear both buffers
 663  A3F7 ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY
 664  A3FB 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 665  A3FD 32 E9 AD             ld (horizon),a
 666  A400 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 667  A402 32 10 AE             ld (seg_base),a
 668  A405 CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 669  A408 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 670  A40A 32 10 AE             ld (seg_base),a
 671  A40D CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 672  A410 ED 91 12 09          nextreg $12,L2_BANK_A           ; Layer 2 active (displayed) bank
 673  A414 ED 91 13 0C          nextreg $13,L2_BANK_B           ; Layer 2 shadow bank (drawn into)
 674  A418 01 3B 12             ld bc,$123B
 675  A41B 3E 02                ld a,%00000010                  ; Layer 2 visible (the menu hid it)
 676  A41D ED 79                out (c),a
 677  A41F ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD
 678  A423 CD 6A E0             call ula_init                   ; ULA: FPS overlay, panel, city map
 679  A426 CD 00 25             call zombie_init                ; place the zombies on the streets
 680  A429 CD 09 36             call human_init                 ; and the humans to rescue
 681  A42C ED 91 56 1F          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_SPECIAL  ; and the level's special monsters
 682  A430 CD 00 C0             call spec_init                  ; (specials.asm)
 683  A433
 684  A433              ;==============================================================================
 685  A433              ; Main loop
 686  A433              ;==============================================================================
 687  A433              main_loop:
 688  A433 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; keys at $E000, while the logic runs
 689  A437 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan                   ; all key banks
 690  A43A ED 91 56 03          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_PLAYER   ; input/movement logic at $C000
 691  A43E CD 00 C0             call update_player
 692  A441 CD 4C A6             call setup_frame
 693  A444 CD 07 26             call zombie_update              ; walk the zombies (page at $2000)
 694  A447 ED 91 56 1F          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_SPECIAL  ; and fly the special monsters
 695  A44B CD D1 C0             call spec_update                ; (specials.asm)
 696  A44E ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS  ; weapons logic at $C000
 697  A452 CD 00 C0             call update_weapons             ; fire, move rounds, hits
 698  A455 CD A6 37             call human_update               ; map dots, rescues (page at $7000)
 699  A458 3A DC A7             ld a,(game_over)
 700  A45B B7                   or a
 701  A45C 28 07                jr z,.play
 702  A45E ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; the pad is destroyed: game over
 703  A462 C3 43 C0             jp game_over_screen             ; (menu.asm) -> main menu
 704  A465 3A DA A7     .play:  ld a,(level_done)
 705  A468 B7                   or a
 706  A469 28 07                jr z,.go
 707  A46B ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; the last wave wiped out: level
 708  A46F C3 72 C0             jp level_complete_screen        ; complete (menu.asm) -> next_level
 709  A472              .go:
 710  A472 CD A8 A4             call map_shadow
 711  A475                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 712  A475 ~                    call vtick
 713  A475                  ENDIF
 714  A475 ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY      ; Layer 2 drawing code at $E000
 715  A479 CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 716  A47C                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 717  A47C ~                    call vtick
 718  A47C                  ENDIF
 719  A47C CD 5B F4             call draw_dots
 720  A47F                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 721  A47F ~                    call vtick
 722  A47F                  ENDIF
 723  A47F CD 92 EE             call draw_city                  ; also calls vtick once per building
 724  A482 CD 17 F5             call draw_bullets               ; machine gun tracers
 725  A485 CD 2A F6             call draw_particles             ; zombie bursts
 726  A488 CD 60 F7             call draw_gunsight
 727  A48B ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD       ; ULA HUD code at $E000
 728  A48F CD E3 E1             call draw_hud
 729  A492                  IFDEF WAIT_FOR_VBLANK
 730  A492 ~                    call wait_vblank
 731  A492                  ENDIF
 732  A492 CD B2 A4             call l2_flip
 733  A495                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 734  A495 ~                    call fps_tick                   ; HUD page is still at $E000
 735  A495                  ENDIF
 736  A495 ED 91 57 0D          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES   ; the HUD sprites (humans, compass)
 737  A499 CD A0 E2             call hud_sprites                ; (hud_sprites.asm), with the new frame
 738  A49C C3 33 A4             jp main_loop
 739  A49F
 740  A49F              ;==============================================================================
 741  A49F              ; Frames per second, shown on the ULA layer above Layer 2.
 742  A49F              ;
 743  A49F              ; Interrupts stay off (the span filler borrows SP), so time is taken from the
 744  A49F              ; raster: vtick reads the current video line and counts a new 50Hz video
 745  A49F              ; frame whenever the line number has gone backwards.  It must be called at
 746  A49F              ; least once per video frame (~560K T at 28MHz); the main loop and draw_cell
 747  A49F              ; do that.  Every 50 video frames (1 second) the number of frames rendered
 748  A49F              ; becomes the FPS figure.
 749  A49F              ;==============================================================================
 750  A49F                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 751  A49F ~            vtick:
 752  A49F ~                    ld bc,$243B
 753  A49F ~                    ld a,$1E
 754  A49F ~                    out (c),a
 755  A49F ~                    inc b
 756  A49F ~                    in d,(c)                        ; line MSB
 757  A49F ~                    dec b
 758  A49F ~                    ld a,$1F
 759  A49F ~                    out (c),a
 760  A49F ~                    inc b
 761  A49F ~                    in e,(c)                        ; line LSB
 762  A49F ~                    dec b
 763  A49F ~                    ld a,$1E
 764  A49F ~                    out (c),a
 765  A49F ~                    inc b
 766  A49F ~                    in a,(c)
 767  A49F ~                    cp d
 768  A49F ~                    jr nz,vtick                     ; MSB changed mid-read: try again
 769  A49F ~                    and 1
 770  A49F ~                    ld d,a                          ; DE = video line 0..311
 771  A49F ~                    ld hl,(fps_line)
 772  A49F ~                    ld (fps_line),de
 773  A49F ~                    or a
 774  A49F ~                    sbc hl,de                       ; previous - current
 775  A49F ~                    ret c
 776  A49F ~                    ret z
 777  A49F ~                    ld hl,fps_vframes               ; line went backwards: new frame
 778  A49F ~                    inc (hl)
 779  A49F ~                    ret
 780  A49F ~
 781  A49F ~            ; Once per rendered frame, after the flip
 782  A49F ~            fps_tick:
 783  A49F ~                    call vtick
 784  A49F ~                    ld hl,fps_count
 785  A49F ~                    inc (hl)
 786  A49F ~                    ld a,(fps_vframes)
 787  A49F ~                    cp 50
 788  A49F ~                    ret c
 789  A49F ~                    sub 50
 790  A49F ~                    ld (fps_vframes),a
 791  A49F ~                    ld a,(fps_count)
 792  A49F ~                    ld (fps_value),a
 793  A49F ~                    xor a
 794  A49F ~                    ld (fps_count),a
 795  A49F ~                    jp fps_draw                     ; HUD page (paged in by main_loop)
 796  A49F                  ENDIF
 797  A49F
 798  A49F              ; A = NextReg number -> A = its value
 799  A49F              read_nextreg:
 800  A49F 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 801  A4A2 ED 79                out (c),a
 802  A4A4 04                   inc b
 803  A4A5 ED 78                in a,(c)
 804  A4A7 C9                   ret
 805  A4A8
 806  A4A8              ; Select the shadow Layer 2 buffer for drawing: remember the 8K page of its
 807  A4A8              ; first row.  Sections are then paged in on demand by seg_map / line_seg.
 808  A4A8              map_shadow:
 809  A4A8 3E 13                ld a,$13
 810  A4AA CD 9F A4             call read_nextreg
 811  A4AD 87                   add a,a                         ; 16K bank -> 8K page
 812  A4AE 32 10 AE             ld (seg_base),a
 813  A4B1 C9                   ret
 814  A4B2
 815  A4B2              ; Show the shadow buffer and make the old active buffer the new shadow
 816  A4B2              l2_flip:
 817  A4B2 3E 12                ld a,$12
 818  A4B4 CD 9F A4             call read_nextreg
 819  A4B7 5F                   ld e,a                          ; old active
 820  A4B8 3E 13                ld a,$13
 821  A4BA CD 9F A4             call read_nextreg
 822  A4BD ED 92 12             nextreg $12,a                   ; shadow -> active
 823  A4C0 7B                   ld a,e
 824  A4C1 ED 92 13             nextreg $13,a                   ; old active -> shadow
 825  A4C4 C9                   ret
 826  A4C5
 827  A4C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 828  A4C5              ; Layer 2 writes go through MMU slot 0 ($0000-$1FFF) only.  Each 8K page of
 829  A4C5              ; the buffer is a 32-row section, so row y is page seg_base + y/32 and its
 830  A4C5              ; pixels are at (y & 31)*256 + x.  Sections are paged in as drawing reaches
 831  A4C5              ; them.  Slots 4-5 keep the ULA screen.
 832  A4C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 833  A4C5
 834  A4C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 835  A4C5              ; Wait until the raster is outside the display area (line >= 192)
 836  A4C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 837  A4C5              wait_vblank:
 838  A4C5 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 839  A4C8 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 840  A4CA ED 79                out (c),a
 841  A4CC 04                   inc b
 842  A4CD ED 78                in a,(c)
 843  A4CF E6 01                and 1
 844  A4D1 C0                   ret nz                          ; line >= 256
 845  A4D2 05                   dec b
 846  A4D3 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 847  A4D5 ED 79                out (c),a
 848  A4D7 04                   inc b
 849  A4D8 ED 78                in a,(c)
 850  A4DA FE C0                cp 192
 851  A4DC 38 E7                jr c,wait_vblank
 852  A4DE C9                   ret
 853  A4DF
 854  A4DF              ; HL -> map byte for the cell the camera is in
 855  A4DF              cam_cell_ptr:
 856  A4DF 3A BD A7             ld a,(cam_z+2)
 857  A4E2 6F                   ld l,a
 858  A4E3 26 00                ld h,0
 859  A4E5                      ROWOFS
 859  A4E5 29          >        add hl,hl
 859  A4E6 29          >        add hl,hl
 859  A4E7 29          >        add hl,hl
 859  A4E8 29          >        add hl,hl
 859  A4E9 29          >        add hl,hl
 859  A4EA 29          >        add hl,hl
 860  A4EB 3A BA A7             ld a,(cam_x+2)
 861  A4EE ED 31                add hl,a
 862  A4F0 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 863  A4F3 19                   add hl,de
 864  A4F4 C9                   ret
 865  A4F5
 866  A4F5              ; A = damage to the VTOL (a building crash, a ghost): its hit points
 867  A4F5              ; (vtol_hp) fall by that, not below 0.  At 0 it is destroyed (vtol_dead): it
 868  A4F5              ; spins down out of control (vtol_wreck, player.asm), then the game is over.
 869  A4F5              vtol_damage:
 870  A4F5 B7                   or a
 871  A4F6 C8                   ret z
 872  A4F7 4F                   ld c,a
 873  A4F8 3A D5 A7             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 874  A4FB 91                   sub c
 875  A4FC 30 01                jr nc,.hp
 876  A4FE AF                   xor a
 877  A4FF 32 D5 A7     .hp:    ld (vtol_hp),a
 878  A502 C0                   ret nz
 879  A503 3E 01                ld a,1
 880  A505 32 D6 A7             ld (vtol_dead),a
 881  A508 C9                   ret
 882  A509
 883  A509              ; HL = the height the VTOL rests at: MIN_Y when its whole footprint is over
 884  A509              ; road, or just clear (COLL_H) of the highest roof under it
 885  A509              floor_y:
 886  A509 CD 74 A5             call vtol_ground
 887  A50C 21 08 00             ld hl,MIN_Y
 888  A50F C8                   ret z
 889  A510 2A 5A A8             ld hl,(vg_max)
 890  A513 11 0D 00             ld de,COLL_H+1
 891  A516 19                   add hl,de
 892  A517 C9                   ret
 893  A518
 894  A518              ; Weapon type details, by WPN_*: its full load, reload frames a shot (0:
 895  A518              ; doesn't reload; the AMMO_INF weapons) and the slots it may be fitted in
 896  A518              ; (resident, for the weapons page and the load-out screen)
 897  A518 00 00 1F     wpn_cfg:    db 0, 0, WS_ANY                             ; WPN_NONE (empty)
 898  A51B FF 00 01                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_CENTRE                   ; WPN_GUN
 899  A51E FF 00 1F                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_ANY                      ; WPN_LASER
 900  A521 FF 00 01                 db AMMO_INF, 0, WS_CENTRE                   ; WPN_CHAIN
 901  A524 0C 0F 1E                 db ROCKET_SHOTS, ROCKET_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS   ; WPN_ROCKETS
 902  A527 04 1E 1E                 db MISSILE_SHOTS, MISSILE_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS ; WPN_MISSILE
 903  A52A 00 00 00                 db 0, 0, 0                                  ; WPN_BOMB (none yet)
 904  A52D 32 07 1E                 db BOFORS_SHOTS, BOFORS_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS   ; WPN_BOFORS
 905  A530 32 07 1E                 db PROX_SHOTS, PROX_RELOAD, WS_PYLONS       ; WPN_PROX
 906  A533                      ASSERT WPN_ROCKETS == 4 && WPN_MISSILE == 5 && WPN_BOFORS == 7
 907  A533                      ASSERT WPN_PROX == 8
 908  A533                      ASSERT $ - wpn_cfg == WCFG_SIZE*(WPN_LAST+1)
 909  A533
 910  A533              ; A = weapon type -> HL = its wpn_cfg entry.  Preserves BC, DE.
 911  A533              wpn_cfg_ptr:
 912  A533 6F                   ld l,a
 913  A534 87                   add a,a
 914  A535 85                   add a,l                         ; * WCFG_SIZE
 915  A536 21 18 A5             ld hl,wpn_cfg
 916  A539 ED 31                add hl,a
 917  A53B C9                   ret
 918  A53C                      ASSERT WCFG_SIZE == 3
 919  A53C
 920  A53C              ; A = weapon type, C = slot (0 = the centre slot): carry set if the weapon
 921  A53C              ; may be fitted in that slot (WCFG_SLOTS).  Preserves DE.
 922  A53C              wpn_fits:
 923  A53C CD 33 A5             call wpn_cfg_ptr
 924  A53F 3E 02                ld a,WCFG_SLOTS
 925  A541 ED 31                add hl,a
 926  A543 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 927  A544 41                   ld b,c
 928  A545 04                   inc b
 929  A546 0F           .sh:    rrca                            ; bit C into carry
 930  A547 10 FD                djnz .sh
 931  A549 C9                   ret
 932  A54A
 933  A54A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 934  A54A              ; From the LEVEL COMPLETE screen (menu.asm, slot 6): page the load-out screen
 935  A54A              ; into slot 6 (from here, resident) and show it; it goes on to next_level
 936  A54A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 937  A54A              loadout_go:
 938  A54A ED 91 56 20          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_LOADOUT
 939  A54E C3 00 C0             jp loadout_screen
 940  A551
 941  A551              ; From the main menu (menu.asm, slot 6): the redefine keys screen
 942  A551              keydef_go:
 943  A551 ED 91 56 21          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_KEYDEF
 944  A555 C3 00 C0             jp keydef_screen
 945  A558
 946  A558              ; Back to the main menu, from another slot 6 page (the redefine keys screen)
 947  A558              menu_go:
 948  A558 ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU
 949  A55C C3 00 C0             jp main_menu
 950  A55F
 951  A55F              ; Z if the VTOL is landed: speed 0 and resting on the floor under it (the
 952  A55F              ; road, the landing pad or a roof: floor_y), and not a wreck.  Landed, its
 953  A55F              ; weapons don't fire (update_weapons) and humans can board or get off
 954  A55F              ; (human_update).
 955  A55F              vtol_landed:
 956  A55F 3A D6 A7             ld a,(vtol_dead)
 957  A562 B7                   or a
 958  A563 C0                   ret nz                          ; a wreck
 959  A564 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 960  A567 B7                   or a
 961  A568 C0                   ret nz                          ; moving
 962  A569 CD 09 A5             call floor_y
 963  A56C ED 5B BE A7          ld de,(cam_y)
 964  A570 B7                   or a
 965  A571 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; Z: resting on it
 966  A573 C9                   ret
 967  A574
 968  A574              ; The VTOL's footprint: the corners and the long-side midpoints of its
 969  A574              ; VTOL_HL x VTOL_HW (half size) rectangle, turned to the heading, round
 970  A574              ; (cam_x, cam_z).  The rectangle is smaller than a tile, so these six points
 971  A574              ; find every cell it overlaps (to within a tile corner nicking a long side).
 972  A574              ; HL = vg_max = the highest ground under them; Z if all road (0).
 973  A574              vtol_ground:
 974  A574 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)                      ; forward (sin, cos), right (cos, -sin)
 975  A577 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 976  A57A 22 4E A8             ld (vg_sin),hl
 977  A57D 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)
 978  A580 C6 40                add a,64
 979  A582 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 980  A585 22 50 A8             ld (vg_cos),hl
 981  A588 EB                   ex de,hl
 982  A589 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
 983  A58C CD 7F A7             call fmul
 984  A58F 22 54 A8             ld (vg_fz),hl                   ; nose offset z = HL cos
 985  A592 ED 5B 50 A8          ld de,(vg_cos)
 986  A596 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
 987  A599 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 988  A59C 22 56 A8             ld (vg_rx),hl                   ; side offset x = HW cos
 989  A59F ED 5B 4E A8          ld de,(vg_sin)
 990  A5A3 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
 991  A5A6 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 992  A5A9 22 52 A8             ld (vg_fx),hl                   ; nose offset x = HL sin
 993  A5AC ED 5B 4E A8          ld de,(vg_sin)
 994  A5B0 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
 995  A5B3 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 996  A5B6 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
 997  A5B9 22 58 A8             ld (vg_rz),hl                   ; side offset z = -HW sin
 998  A5BC 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 999  A5BF 22 5A A8             ld (vg_max),hl
1000  A5C2 2A 52 A8             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; nose: the front corners
1001  A5C5 ED 5B 54 A8          ld de,(vg_fz)
1002  A5C9 CD ED A5             call .side
1003  A5CC 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; the long-side midpoints
1004  A5CF 11 00 00             ld de,0
1005  A5D2 CD ED A5             call .side
1006  A5D5 2A 52 A8             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; tail: the back corners
1007  A5D8 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1008  A5DB E5                   push hl
1009  A5DC 2A 54 A8             ld hl,(vg_fz)
1010  A5DF CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1011  A5E2 EB                   ex de,hl
1012  A5E3 E1                   pop hl
1013  A5E4 CD ED A5             call .side
1014  A5E7 2A 5A A8             ld hl,(vg_max)
1015  A5EA 7C                   ld a,h
1016  A5EB B5                   or l
1017  A5EC C9                   ret
1018  A5ED              ; HL, DE = an offset along the length (x, z): the points it +- the side offset
1019  A5ED E5           .side:  push hl
1020  A5EE D5                   push de
1021  A5EF ED 4B 56 A8          ld bc,(vg_rx)
1022  A5F3 09                   add hl,bc
1023  A5F4 EB                   ex de,hl
1024  A5F5 ED 4B 58 A8          ld bc,(vg_rz)
1025  A5F9 09                   add hl,bc
1026  A5FA EB                   ex de,hl
1027  A5FB CD 10 A6             call .pt
1028  A5FE D1                   pop de
1029  A5FF E1                   pop hl
1030  A600 ED 4B 56 A8          ld bc,(vg_rx)
1031  A604 B7                   or a
1032  A605 ED 42                sbc hl,bc
1033  A607 EB                   ex de,hl
1034  A608 ED 4B 58 A8          ld bc,(vg_rz)
1035  A60C B7                   or a
1036  A60D ED 42                sbc hl,bc
1037  A60F EB                   ex de,hl
1038  A610              ; HL, DE = offset (x, z) from the centre: vg_max = max(vg_max, its ground)
1039  A610 ED 4B B9 A7  .pt:    ld bc,(cam_x+1)
1040  A614 09                   add hl,bc
1041  A615 7C                   ld a,h                          ; column
1042  A616 EB                   ex de,hl
1043  A617 ED 4B BC A7          ld bc,(cam_z+1)
1044  A61B 09                   add hl,bc
1045  A61C 6C                   ld l,h                          ; row
1046  A61D 26 00                ld h,0
1047  A61F                      ROWOFS
1047  A61F 29          >        add hl,hl
1047  A620 29          >        add hl,hl
1047  A621 29          >        add hl,hl
1047  A622 29          >        add hl,hl
1047  A623 29          >        add hl,hl
1047  A624 29          >        add hl,hl
1048  A625 ED 31                add hl,a
1049  A627 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
1050  A62A 19                   add hl,de
1051  A62B 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1052  A62C E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
1053  A62E C8                   ret z                           ; road
1054  A62F CD 3F A6             call bld_height
1055  A632 ED 5B 5A A8          ld de,(vg_max)
1056  A636 B7                   or a
1057  A637 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1058  A639 D8                   ret c                           ; lower than the highest so far
1059  A63A 19                   add hl,de
1060  A63B 22 5A A8             ld (vg_max),hl
1061  A63E C9                   ret
1062  A63F
1063  A63F              ; A = map byte -> HL = height in world units (levels 0-15, PAD_CODE = the
1064  A63F              ; base landing pad, TERRACE_CODE = the terrace round it)
1065  A63F              bld_height:
1066  A63F E6 1F                and 31
1067  A641 87                   add a,a
1068  A642 21 31 A0             ld hl,htab
1069  A645 ED 31                add hl,a
1070  A647 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1071  A648 23                   inc hl
1072  A649 66                   ld h,(hl)
1073  A64A 6F                   ld l,a
1074  A64B C9                   ret
1075  A64C
1076  A64C              ;==============================================================================
1077  A64C              ; Per-frame view setup: trig, rotated building edge vectors, horizon
1078  A64C              ;==============================================================================
1079  A64C              setup_frame:
1080  A64C 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)
1081  A64F CD C8 A6             call get_sin
1082  A652 22 EA AD             ld (sin_y),hl
1083  A655 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)
1084  A658 C6 40                add a,64
1085  A65A CD C8 A6             call get_sin
1086  A65D 22 EC AD             ld (cos_y),hl
1087  A660 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
1088  A663 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
1089  A666 22 EE AD             ld (sin_p),hl
1090  A669 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
1091  A66C C6 40                add a,64
1092  A66E CD C8 A6             call get_sin
1093  A671 22 F0 AD             ld (cos_p),hl
1094  A674
1095  A674                      ; ground plane relative to camera: dy = -camY
1096  A674 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
1097  A677 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1098  A67A E5                   push hl
1099  A67B ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
1100  A67F CD 7F A7             call fmul
1101  A682 22 F2 AD             ld (yb),hl                      ; Y contribution of ground
1102  A685 E1                   pop hl
1103  A686 ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
1104  A68A CD 7F A7             call fmul
1105  A68D 22 F4 AD             ld (zb),hl                      ; Z contribution of ground
1106  A690
1107  A690                      ; frustum cull factor c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|
1108  A690 2A EE AD             ld hl,(sin_p)
1109  A693 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
1110  A696 CD B3 A7             call zhalf
1111  A699 EB                   ex de,hl
1112  A69A 2A F0 AD             ld hl,(cos_p)
1113  A69D B7                   or a
1114  A69E ED 52                sbc hl,de
1115  A6A0 22 06 AE             ld (cull_c),hl
1116  A6A3
1117  A6A3 21 00 01             ld hl,BLD_SIZE
1118  A6A6 DD 21 F6 AD          ld ix,vecs0
1119  A6AA CD D4 A6             call make_vecs
1120  A6AD
1121  A6AD 3A BA A7             ld a,(cam_x+2)
1122  A6B0 32 0E AE             ld (cx),a
1123  A6B3 3A BD A7             ld a,(cam_z+2)
1124  A6B6 32 0F AE             ld (cz),a
1125  A6B9
1126  A6B9 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
1127  A6BC C6 18                add a,PMAX
1128  A6BE 21 00 A0             ld hl,hztab
1129  A6C1 ED 31                add hl,a
1130  A6C3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1131  A6C4 32 E9 AD             ld (horizon),a
1132  A6C7 C9                   ret
1133  A6C8
1134  A6C8              ; A = angle -> HL = sin (S7.8).  Pages the sine table into slot 0 (PAGE_SIN):
1135  A6C8              ; call it only outside the Layer 2 drawing (it is: setup_frame, the player,
1136  A6C8              ; vtol_ground), never between a section's paging and its writes.
1137  A6C8              get_sin:
1138  A6C8 ED 91 50 0E          nextreg SIN_MMU,PAGE_SIN        ; the table at $0000 (slot 0)
1139  A6CC 6F                   ld l,a
1140  A6CD 26 00                ld h,high sin_lo
1141  A6CF 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1142  A6D0 24                   inc h
1143  A6D1 66                   ld h,(hl)
1144  A6D2 6F                   ld l,a
1145  A6D3 C9                   ret
1146  A6D4
1147  A6D4              ; Rotate the building edge vectors (s,0,0) and (0,0,s) into camera space.
1148  A6D4              ; HL = s, IX -> 16 bytes: VX(X,Y,Z) VZ(X,Y,Z) corner offset (X,Z1)
1149  A6D4              make_vecs:
1150  A6D4 22 08 AE             ld (mv_s),hl
1151  A6D7 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1152  A6DB CD 7F A7             call fmul
1153  A6DE 22 0A AE             ld (mv_c),hl                    ; s*cosY
1154  A6E1 2A 08 AE             ld hl,(mv_s)
1155  A6E4 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1156  A6E8 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1157  A6EB 22 0C AE             ld (mv_n),hl                    ; s*sinY
1158  A6EE                      ; VX = ( s cosY, -s sinY sinP, s sinY cosP )
1159  A6EE 2A 0A AE             ld hl,(mv_c)
1160  A6F1 DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
1161  A6F4 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
1162  A6F7 2A 0C AE             ld hl,(mv_n)
1163  A6FA ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
1164  A6FE CD 7F A7             call fmul
1165  A701 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1166  A704 DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
1167  A707 DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h
1168  A70A 2A 0C AE             ld hl,(mv_n)
1169  A70D ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
1170  A711 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1171  A714 DD 75 04             ld (ix+4),l
1172  A717 DD 74 05             ld (ix+5),h
1173  A71A                      ; VZ = ( -s sinY, -s cosY sinP, s cosY cosP )
1174  A71A 2A 0C AE             ld hl,(mv_n)
1175  A71D CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1176  A720 DD 75 06             ld (ix+6),l
1177  A723 DD 74 07             ld (ix+7),h
1178  A726 2A 0A AE             ld hl,(mv_c)
1179  A729 ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
1180  A72D CD 7F A7             call fmul
1181  A730 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1182  A733 DD 75 08             ld (ix+8),l
1183  A736 DD 74 09             ld (ix+9),h
1184  A739 2A 0A AE             ld hl,(mv_c)
1185  A73C ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
1186  A740 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1187  A743 DD 75 0A             ld (ix+10),l
1188  A746 DD 74 0B             ld (ix+11),h
1189  A749                      ; cell centre -> footprint corner (-s/2,-s/2) in yaw space
1190  A749 2A 0C AE             ld hl,(mv_n)
1191  A74C ED 5B 0A AE          ld de,(mv_c)
1192  A750 B7                   or a
1193  A751 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1194  A753 CB 2C                sra h
1195  A755 CB 1D                rr l
1196  A757 DD 75 0C             ld (ix+12),l                    ; X: s/2 (sinY - cosY)
1197  A75A DD 74 0D             ld (ix+13),h
1198  A75D 2A 0C AE             ld hl,(mv_n)
1199  A760 ED 5B 0A AE          ld de,(mv_c)
1200  A764 19                   add hl,de
1201  A765 CB 2C                sra h
1202  A767 CB 1D                rr l
1203  A769 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
1204  A76C DD 75 0E             ld (ix+14),l                    ; Z1: -s/2 (sinY + cosY)
1205  A76F DD 74 0F             ld (ix+15),h
1206  A772 C9                   ret
1207  A773
1208  A773              ;==============================================================================
1209  A773              ; Fixed point maths
1210  A773              ;==============================================================================
1211  A773
1212  A773              ; HL = -HL
1213  A773              neg_hl:
1214  A773 AF                   xor a
1215  A774 95                   sub l
1216  A775 6F                   ld l,a
1217  A776 9F                   sbc a,a
1218  A777 94                   sub h
1219  A778 67                   ld h,a
1220  A779 C9                   ret
1221  A77A
1222  A77A              ; HL = |HL|
1223  A77A              abs_hl:
1224  A77A CB 7C                bit 7,h
1225  A77C C8                   ret z
1226  A77D 18 F4                jr neg_hl
1227  A77F
1228  A77F              ; fmul: HL = HL * DE >> 8
1229  A77F              ;   HL = signed 16-bit integer, DE = S7.8 factor in -1.0..+1.0
1230  A77F              ;   Sign/magnitude using two 8x8 MUL D,E.  Preserves IX, IY.
1231  A77F              fmul:
1232  A77F 7C                   ld a,h
1233  A780 AA                   xor d
1234  A781 F5                   push af                         ; result sign in bit 7
1235  A782 CB 7C                bit 7,h
1236  A784 28 06                jr z,.ap
1237  A786 AF                   xor a
1238  A787 95                   sub l
1239  A788 6F                   ld l,a
1240  A789 9F                   sbc a,a
1241  A78A 94                   sub h
1242  A78B 67                   ld h,a
1243  A78C CB 7A        .ap:    bit 7,d
1244  A78E 28 06                jr z,.bp
1245  A790 AF                   xor a
1246  A791 93                   sub e
1247  A792 5F                   ld e,a
1248  A793 9F                   sbc a,a
1249  A794 92                   sub d
1250  A795 57                   ld d,a
1251  A796 7A           .bp:    ld a,d
1252  A797 B7                   or a
1253  A798 20 10                jr nz,.one                      ; |factor| == 1.0
1254  A79A 7B                   ld a,e
1255  A79B 55                   ld d,l
1256  A79C ED 30                mul d,e                         ; lo * f
1257  A79E 4A                   ld c,d
1258  A79F 54                   ld d,h
1259  A7A0 5F                   ld e,a
1260  A7A1 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; hi * f
1261  A7A3 7B                   ld a,e
1262  A7A4 81                   add a,c
1263  A7A5 6F                   ld l,a
1264  A7A6 7A                   ld a,d
1265  A7A7 CE 00                adc a,0
1266  A7A9 67                   ld h,a
1267  A7AA F1           .one:   pop af
1268  A7AB F0                   ret p
1269  A7AC AF                   xor a
1270  A7AD 95                   sub l
1271  A7AE 6F                   ld l,a
1272  A7AF 9F                   sbc a,a
1273  A7B0 94                   sub h
1274  A7B1 67                   ld h,a
1275  A7B2 C9                   ret
1276  A7B3
1277  A7B3              ; HL = 0.5 * HL (signed): the top/bottom frustum slope, VIEW_CY/128
1278  A7B3              zhalf:
1279  A7B3 CB 2C                sra h
1280  A7B5 CB 1D                rr l
1281  A7B7 C9                   ret
1282  A7B8
1283  A7B8              ;==============================================================================
1284  A7B8              ; Shared data (resident: used by the main code and by both pages)
1285  A7B8              ;==============================================================================
1286  A7B8              ; --- player state ---
1287  A7B8              ; (cam_x .. wflash_t are set from start_state by newGame)
1288  A7B8 00 00 00     cam_x:      ds 3                        ; frac, integer lo, hi
1289  A7BB 00 00 00     cam_z:      ds 3
1290  A7BE 00 00        cam_y:      ds 2
1291  A7C0 00           yaw_frac:   ds 1                        ; fraction below yaw (keep together:
1292  A7C1 00           yaw:        ds 1                        ; yaw_frac/yaw is one 8.8 word)
1293  A7C2 00           pitch_frac: ds 1                        ; fraction below pitch (keep together:
1294  A7C3 00           pitch:      ds 1                        ; pitch_frac/pitch is one signed 8.8
1295  A7C4                                                      ; word; drawing uses pitch only)
1296  A7C4 00           speed:      ds 1

1298  A7C5              ; is wpn_slots + n*2.
1299  A7C5 00 00 00...  wpn_slots:  ds WPN_SLOTS*2
1300  A7CF 00           wpn_sel:    ds 1                        ; selected slot (keys 1-5), fires on SPACE
1301  A7D0 00           wflash_slot: ds 1                       ; empty slot requested: which, and
1302  A7D1 00           wflash_t:   ds 1                        ; frames of yellow/blue flash left
1303  A7D2 01           level:      db START_LEVEL              ; current level (newGame: START_LEVEL)
1304  A7D3 06           view_r:     db VIEW_R                   ; draw distance, tiles (load_level)
1305  A7D4 05           dot_r:      db VIEW_R-1                 ; street dots' radius (load_level)
1306  A7D5 32           vtol_hp:    db VTOL_HP                  ; the VTOL's hit points (newGame)
1307  A7D6 00           vtol_dead:  db 0                        ; 1: destroyed (hit points 0)
1308  A7D7 00           dead_down:  db 0                        ; 1: the wreck has hit the ground
1309  A7D8 00           dead_vy:    db 0                        ; its falling speed, units a frame
1310  A7D9 64           dead_wait:  db DEAD_PAUSE               ; frames on the ground to GAME OVER
1311  A7DA 00           level_done: db 0                        ; 1: the level's last wave is wiped out
1312  A7DB 01           wave_number: db 1                       ; current zombie wave of the level,
1313  A7DC                                                      ; 1..LV_WAVES (newGame: 1)
1314  A7DC 00           game_over:  db 0                        ; 1: the landing pad was destroyed
1315  A7DD 00 00        salvage_points: dw 0                    ; salvage points, 16-bit (newGame: 0)
1316  A7DF 00           gun_tilt:   db 0                        ; centre gun tilt, signed (weapons_reset)
1317  A7E0 00           barr_n:     db 0                        ; barricades in barr_tab (barricade_init)
1318  A7E1 00 00 00...  barr_tab:   ds NBARR*BARR_REC           ; barricades: x, z, hit points
1319  A83B 40           sight_y:    db VIEW_CY                  ; the gunsight's row (gun_aim)
1320  A83C              ; Weapons unlocked by salvage (salvage_award, menu.asm), not yet fitted: a
1321  A83C              ; count per weapon type (WPN_*), for the load-out page (newGame: none)
1322  A83C 00 00 00...  wpn_stock:  ds WPN_LAST+1
1323  A845              ; 1: the weapon type has been unlocked (fitted at the start, or by salvage):
1324  A845              ; the load-out screen lists it, even with none in store (newGame)
1325  A845 00 00 00...  wpn_unlocked: ds WPN_LAST+1
1326  A84E                      ASSERT wpn_unlocked == wpn_stock + WPN_LAST + 1
1327  A84E 00 00        vg_sin:     dw 0                        ; vtol_ground: heading sin, cos,
1328  A850 00 00        vg_cos:     dw 0
1329  A852 00 00        vg_fx:      dw 0                        ; nose offset (x, z),
1330  A854 00 00        vg_fz:      dw 0
1331  A856 00 00        vg_rx:      dw 0                        ; side offset (x, z),
1332  A858 00 00        vg_rz:      dw 0

1334  A85C

1336  A85C              start_state:

   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - city page (8K page PAGE_CITY, runs at $E000)

  42+ E720              ; A = row y (0..VIEW_H-1): page in its section, return A = y & 31.
  43+ E720              ; Preserves BC, DE, L.
  44+ E720              seg_map:
  45+ E720 C5                   push bc
  46+ E721 4F                   ld c,a
  47+ E722 07                   rlca
  48+ E723 07                   rlca
  49+ E724 07                   rlca
  50+ E725 E6 03                and 3                           ; section 0..3
  51+ E727 47                   ld b,a

  55+ E72F ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
  56+ E732 79                   ld a,c

  58+ E735 C1                   pop bc
  59+ E736 C9                   ret
  60+ E737
  61+ E737              ; Line drawer stepped H off the section (H = 32 going down, $FF going up):
  62+ E737              ; page in the neighbouring section and wrap H.  Preserves A, BC, DE, L.
  63+ E737              line_seg:
  64+ E737 F5                   push af
  65+ E738 7C                   ld a,h
  66+ E739 FE 20                cp 32
  67+ E73B 3A B2 FC             ld a,(seg_page)
  68+ E73E 20 05                jr nz,.up
  69+ E740 3C                   inc a                           ; down into the next section
  70+ E741 26 00                ld h,0
  71+ E743 18 03                jr .set
  72+ E745 3D           .up:    dec a                           ; up into the previous section
  73+ E746 26 1F                ld h,31
  74+ E748 32 B2 FC     .set:   ld (seg_page),a
  75+ E74B ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
  76+ E74E F1                   pop af
  77+ E74F C9                   ret

  79+ E750              ; HL = (HL * DE) >> 16   HL signed, DE unsigned 0.16 fraction
  80+ E750              mul_frac:
  81+ E750 7C                   ld a,h
  82+ E751 32 C9 FB             ld (mf_sign),a
  83+ E754 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
  84+ E757 44                   ld b,h                          ; ah
  85+ E758 4D                   ld c,l                          ; al

  97+ E767 3E 00                ld a,0

 106+ E773 67                   ld h,a
 107+ E774 D1                   pop de

 115+ E780 C3 73 A7             jp neg_hl

 117+ E783              ; IX = (HL << 16) / DE   with HL < DE (unsigned) -> 0.16 fraction
 118+ E783              div_frac:
 119+ E783 DD 21 00 00          ld ix,0
 120+ E787 3E 10                ld a,16
 121+ E789 DD 29        .lp:    add ix,ix
 122+ E78B 29                   add hl,hl
 123+ E78C 38 08                jr c,.sub
 124+ E78E B7                   or a
 125+ E78F ED 52                sbc hl,de
 126+ E791 30 06                jr nc,.set
 127+ E793 19                   add hl,de
 128+ E794 18 05                jr .nx
 129+ E796 B7           .sub:   or a
 130+ E797 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 131+ E799 DD 23        .set:   inc ix
 132+ E79B 3D           .nx:    dec a
 133+ E79C 20 EB                jr nz,.lp
 134+ E79E C9                   ret
 135+ E79F
 136+ E79F              ; E = A / C, A = A mod C  (8-bit unsigned)
 137+ E79F              div8:
 138+ E79F 5F                   ld e,a
 139+ E7A0 AF                   xor a
 140+ E7A1 06 08                ld b,8
 141+ E7A3 CB 23        .lp:    sla e
 142+ E7A5 17                   rla
 143+ E7A6 38 03                jr c,.s
 144+ E7A8 B9                   cp c
 145+ E7A9 38 02                jr c,.n
 146+ E7AB 91           .s:     sub c
 147+ E7AC 1C                   inc e
 148+ E7AD 10 F4        .n:     djnz .lp
 149+ E7AF C9                   ret
 150+ E7B0
 151+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 152+ E7B0              ; Perspective projection
 153+ E7B0              ;   in : HL -> X,Y,Z (camera space, inside the view frustum, Z > 0)
 154+ E7B0              ;   out: A = screen x (0..255), C = screen y (0..VIEW_H-1)
 155+ E7B0              ;   sx = 128 + 128*X/Z,  sy = 64 - 128*Y/Z   (90 x 53 degree field of view)
 156+ E7B0              ;   Z is normalised to 9 bits (256..511) with a barrel shift (count from a
 157+ E7B0              ;   log2 table) that is also applied to X and Y, which are then multiplied
 158+ E7B0              ;   by a 16-bit reciprocal from rtab.  |X| <= Z keeps Xn within 9 bits.
 159+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 160+ E7B0              project:
 161+ E7B0 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 162+ E7B1 23                   inc hl
 163+ E7B2 46                   ld b,(hl)
 164+ E7B3 23                   inc hl                          ; BC = X
 165+ E7B4 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 166+ E7B5 23                   inc hl
 167+ E7B6 56                   ld d,(hl)
 168+ E7B7 23                   inc hl                          ; DE = Y
 169+ E7B8 D5                   push de
 170+ E7B9 C5                   push bc
 171+ E7BA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 172+ E7BB 23                   inc hl
 173+ E7BC 66                   ld h,(hl)
 174+ E7BD 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 175+ E7BE CD 01 E8             call zpatch
 176+ E7C1
 177+ E7C1 E1                   pop hl                          ; X
 178+ E7C2 7C                   ld a,h
 179+ E7C3 F5                   push af
 180+ E7C4 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 181+ E7C7 4F                   ld c,a
 182+ E7C8 F1                   pop af
 183+ E7C9 17                   rla
 184+ E7CA 38 09                jr c,.xn
 185+ E7CC 3E 80                ld a,128
 186+ E7CE 81                   add a,c
 187+ E7CF 30 0A                jr nc,.xd
 188+ E7D1 3E FF                ld a,255
 189+ E7D3 18 06                jr .xd
 190+ E7D5 3E 80        .xn:    ld a,128
 191+ E7D7 91                   sub c
 192+ E7D8 30 01                jr nc,.xd
 193+ E7DA AF                   xor a
 194+ E7DB 32 C8 FB     .xd:    ld (pj_sx),a
 195+ E7DE
 196+ E7DE E1                   pop hl                          ; Y
 197+ E7DF                      ; fall through
 198+ E7DF
 199+ E7DF              ; HL = Y -> C = screen y, A = screen x of the last project.  Uses the Z
 200+ E7DF              ; patched into scale_comp by the last project, so on its own it projects a
 201+ E7DF              ; point straight above or below that one when pitch is 0 (same X and Z).
 202+ E7DF              proj_y:
 203+ E7DF 7C                   ld a,h
 204+ E7E0 F5                   push af
 205+ E7E1 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 206+ E7E4 4F                   ld c,a
 207+ E7E5 F1                   pop af
 208+ E7E6 17                   rla
 209+ E7E7 38 08                jr c,.yn
 210+ E7E9 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 211+ E7EB 91                   sub c
 212+ E7EC 30 0E                jr nc,.yd
 213+ E7EE AF                   xor a
 214+ E7EF 18 0B                jr .yd
 215+ E7F1 3E 40        .yn:    ld a,VIEW_CY
 216+ E7F3 81                   add a,c
 217+ E7F4 38 04                jr c,.yc
 218+ E7F6 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
 219+ E7F8 38 02                jr c,.yd
 220+ E7FA 3E 7F        .yc:    ld a,VIEW_H-1
 221+ E7FC 4F           .yd:    ld c,a
 222+ E7FD 3A C8 FB             ld a,(pj_sx)
 223+ E800 C9                   ret
 224+ E801
 225+ E801              ; HL = Z (camera space): normalise it and patch its shift and reciprocal
 226+ E801              ; into scale_comp, which then gives A = |component| * 128 / Z
 227+ E801              zpatch:
 228+ E801 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 229+ E803 20 03                jr nz,.z1
 230+ E805 B4                   or h
 231+ E806 20 03                jr nz,.zok
 232+ E808 21 01 00     .z1:    ld hl,1
 233+ E80B 7C           .zok:   ld a,h
 234+ E80C FE 02                cp 2
 235+ E80E 38 0B                jr c,.lt512
 236+ E810 5F                   ld e,a                          ; Z >= 512: shift right by log2(H)
 237+ E811 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 238+ E813 1A                   ld a,(de)
 239+ E814 47                   ld b,a
 240+ E815 EB                   ex de,hl
 241+ E816 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 242+ E818 EB                   ex de,hl
 243+ E819 18 14                jr .nrm
 244+ E81B 06 00        .lt512: ld b,0
 245+ E81D B7                   or a
 246+ E81E 20 0F                jr nz,.nrm                      ; 256..511 already normalised
 247+ E820 5D                   ld e,l                          ; Z < 256: shift left by 8-log2(L)
 248+ E821 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 249+ E823 1A                   ld a,(de)
 250+ E824 D6 08                sub 8
 251+ E826 4F                   ld c,a                          ; negative shift count
 252+ E827 ED 44                neg
 253+ E829 47                   ld b,a
 254+ E82A EB                   ex de,hl
 255+ E82B ED 28                bsla de,b
 256+ E82D EB                   ex de,hl
 257+ E82E 41                   ld b,c
 258+ E82F 78           .nrm:   ld a,b                          ; patch shift and reciprocal into
 259+ E830 32 50 E8             ld (scale_comp.sh+1),a                  ; scale_comp's immediates
 260+ E833 26 E4                ld h,high rtab_lo               ; L = Zn - 256
 261+ E835 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 262+ E836 32 6C E8             ld (scale_comp.rl+1),a
 263+ E839 32 82 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+1),a
 264+ E83C 24                   inc h
 265+ E83D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 266+ E83E 32 72 E8             ld (scale_comp.rh+1),a
 267+ E841 32 83 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+2),a
 268+ E844 C9                   ret
 269+ E845
 270+ E845              ; HL = signed component -> A = round(|HL| * 128 / Z)
 271+ E845              ; The shift and reciprocal for this Z are patched in by project.
 272+ E845              scale_comp:
 273+ E845 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 274+ E847 28 06                jr z,.ap
 275+ E849 AF                   xor a
 276+ E84A 95                   sub l
 277+ E84B 6F                   ld l,a
 278+ E84C 9F                   sbc a,a
 279+ E84D 94                   sub h
 280+ E84E 67                   ld h,a
 281+ E84F              .ap:
 282+ E84F 3E 00        .sh:    ld a,0
 283+ E851 B7                   or a
 284+ E852 28 10                jr z,.sd
 285+ E854 EB                   ex de,hl
 286+ E855 FA 5E E8             jp m,.l
 287+ E858 47                   ld b,a
 288+ E859 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 289+ E85B EB                   ex de,hl
 290+ E85C 18 06                jr .sd
 291+ E85E ED 44        .l:     neg
 292+ E860 47                   ld b,a
 293+ E861 ED 28                bsla de,b
 294+ E863 EB                   ex de,hl
 295+ E864 7C           .sd:    ld a,h
 296+ E865 FE 02                cp 2
 297+ E867 30 27                jr nc,.big
 298+ E869 44           .ok:    ld b,h                          ; bit 8 of Xn
 299+ E86A 55                   ld d,l
 300+ E86B 1E 00        .rl:    ld e,0
 301+ E86D ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rl
 302+ E86F 4A                   ld c,d
 303+ E870 55                   ld d,l
 304+ E871 1E 00        .rh:    ld e,0
 305+ E873 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rh
 306+ E875 79                   ld a,c
 307+ E876 83                   add a,e
 308+ E877 6F                   ld l,a
 309+ E878 7A                   ld a,d
 310+ E879 CE 00                adc a,0
 311+ E87B 67                   ld h,a                          ; HL = (Xl*R) >> 8
 312+ E87C 0E 00                ld c,0
 313+ E87E 05                   dec b
 314+ E87F 20 06                jr nz,.nh
 315+ E881 11 00 00     .r16:   ld de,0
 316+ E884 19                   add hl,de                       ; + (256*R) >> 8
 317+ E885 CB 11                rl c
 318+ E887 24           .nh:    inc h                           ; + rounding (0.5 after >>9)
 319+ E888 20 01                jr nz,.nr
 320+ E88A 0C                   inc c
 321+ E88B 79           .nr:    ld a,c
 322+ E88C 1F                   rra
 323+ E88D 7C                   ld a,h
 324+ E88E 1F                   rra                             ; A = (C:H) >> 1  = P >> 9
 325+ E88F C9                   ret
 326+ E890 21 FF 01     .big:   ld hl,511                       ; clamp (rounding at frustum edge)
 327+ E893 18 D4                jr .ok
 328+ E895
 329+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 330+ E895              ; Outcode: HL -> vertex (X,Y,Z) -> A = bits
 331+ E895              ;   1 near (Z<NEAR)  2 left (X<-Z)  4 right (X>Z)
 332+ E895              ;   8 top (Y>0.5Z)   16 bottom (Y<-0.5Z)
 333+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 334+ E895              calc_oc:
 335+ E895 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 336+ E896 23                   inc hl
 337+ E897 56                   ld d,(hl)                       ; DE = X
 338+ E898 23                   inc hl
 339+ E899 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 340+ E89A 23                   inc hl
 341+ E89B 46                   ld b,(hl)                       ; BC = Y
 342+ E89C 23                   inc hl
 343+ E89D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 344+ E89E 23                   inc hl
 345+ E89F 66                   ld h,(hl)
 346+ E8A0 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 347+ E8A1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 348+ E8A3 20 0D                jr nz,.nr
 349+ E8A5 7C                   ld a,h
 350+ E8A6 B7                   or a
 351+ E8A7 3E 00                ld a,0
 352+ E8A9 20 09                jr nz,.n1
 353+ E8AB 7D                   ld a,l
 354+ E8AC FE 10                cp NEAR
 355+ E8AE 3E 00                ld a,0
 356+ E8B0 30 02                jr nc,.n1
 357+ E8B2 3E 01        .nr:    ld a,1
 358+ E8B4 E5           .n1:    push hl
 359+ E8B5 19                   add hl,de                       ; Z+X
 360+ E8B6 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 361+ E8B8 28 02                jr z,.n2
 362+ E8BA F6 02                or 2
 363+ E8BC E1           .n2:    pop hl
 364+ E8BD E5                   push hl
 365+ E8BE B7                   or a
 366+ E8BF ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; Z-X
 367+ E8C1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 368+ E8C3 28 02                jr z,.n3
 369+ E8C5 F6 04                or 4
 370+ E8C7 E1           .n3:    pop hl
 371+ E8C8 CB 2C                sra h
 372+ E8CA CB 1D                rr l                            ; 0.5Z
 373+ E8CC E5                   push hl
 374+ E8CD B7                   or a
 375+ E8CE ED 42                sbc hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z-Y
 376+ E8D0 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 377+ E8D2 28 02                jr z,.n4
 378+ E8D4 F6 08                or 8
 379+ E8D6 E1           .n4:    pop hl
 380+ E8D7 09                   add hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z+Y
 381+ E8D8 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 382+ E8DA C8                   ret z
 383+ E8DB F6 10                or 16
 384+ E8DD C9                   ret
 385+ E8DE
 386+ E8DE              ; Signed distance of vertex IX to clip plane (clip_plane bit). >= 0 is inside.
 387+ E8DE              plane_dist:
 388+ E8DE DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 389+ E8E1 DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 390+ E8E4 3A D9 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 391+ E8E7 FE 01                cp 1
 392+ E8E9 20 05                jr nz,.p2
 393+ E8EB 11 F0 FF             ld de,-NEAR
 394+ E8EE 19                   add hl,de
 395+ E8EF C9                   ret
 396+ E8F0 DD 5E 00     .p2:    ld e,(ix+0)
 397+ E8F3 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
 398+ E8F6 FE 02                cp 2
 399+ E8F8 20 02                jr nz,.p3
 400+ E8FA 19                   add hl,de
 401+ E8FB C9                   ret
 402+ E8FC FE 04        .p3:    cp 4
 403+ E8FE 20 04                jr nz,.p4
 404+ E900 B7                   or a
 405+ E901 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 406+ E903 C9                   ret
 407+ E904 F5           .p4:    push af
 408+ E905 CD B3 A7             call zhalf
 409+ E908 F1                   pop af
 410+ E909 DD 5E 02             ld e,(ix+2)
 411+ E90C DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 412+ E90F FE 08                cp 8
 413+ E911 20 04                jr nz,.p5
 414+ E913 B7                   or a
 415+ E914 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 416+ E916 C9                   ret
 417+ E917 19           .p5:    add hl,de
 418+ E918 C9                   ret
 419+ E919
 420+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 421+ E919              ; Sutherland-Hodgman clip of clip_src (clip_n verts) against clip_plane into
 422+ E919              ; clip_dst.  Vertex = X,Y,Z,flag,corner (8 bytes).  flag=1 means the edge from
 423+ E919              ; this vertex to the next is a real building edge (gets outlined); edges
 424+ E919              ; created along a clip plane get flag=0 so the screen border isn't outlined.
 425+ E919              ; corner = vtx offset of an unclipped building corner that is on screen, so
 426+ E919              ; its cached projection can be reused; $80 + plane bit for a vertex the
 427+ E919              ; clipper put on that plane; $FF for any other vertex.
 428+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 429+ E919              clip_poly:
 430+ E919 3A D6 FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 431+ E91C 3D                   dec a
 432+ E91D 87                   add a,a
 433+ E91E 87                   add a,a
 434+ E91F 87                   add a,a
 435+ E920 2A DA FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 436+ E923 ED 31                add hl,a
 437+ E925 22 DE FB             ld (clip_s),hl
 438+ E928 E5                   push hl
 439+ E929 DD E1                pop ix
 440+ E92B CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 441+ E92E 22 E2 FB             ld (clip_ds),hl
 442+ E931 2A DA FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 443+ E934 22 E0 FB             ld (clip_e),hl
 444+ E937 FD 2A DC FB          ld iy,(clip_dst)
 445+ E93B AF                   xor a
 446+ E93C 32 D7 FB             ld (clip_on),a
 447+ E93F 3A D6 FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 448+ E942 32 D8 FB             ld (clip_cnt),a
 449+ E945 DD 2A E0 FB  .loop:  ld ix,(clip_e)
 450+ E949 CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 451+ E94C 22 E4 FB             ld (clip_de),hl
 452+ E94F CB 7C                bit 7,h
 453+ E951 20 2C                jr nz,.eout
 454+ E953 3A E3 FB             ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 455+ E956 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 456+ E958 28 0D                jr z,.ein
 457+ E95A                      ; S out, E in: intersection carries S's edge flag
 458+ E95A DD 2A DE FB          ld ix,(clip_s)
 459+ E95E DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 460+ E961 32 E6 FB             ld (clip_if),a
 461+ E964 CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 462+ E967 DD 2A E0 FB  .ein:   ld ix,(clip_e)
 463+ E96B 06 08                ld b,8                          ; whole vertex, with its corner byte
 464+ E96D DD 7E 00     .cpy:   ld a,(ix+0)
 465+ E970 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 466+ E973 DD 23                inc ix
 467+ E975 FD 23                inc iy
 468+ E977 10 F4                djnz .cpy
 469+ E979 21 D7 FB             ld hl,clip_on
 470+ E97C 34                   inc (hl)
 471+ E97D 18 0E                jr .next
 472+ E97F 3A E3 FB     .eout:  ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 473+ E982 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 474+ E984 20 07                jr nz,.next
 475+ E986                      ; S in, E out: intersection starts a clip-plane edge
 476+ E986 AF                   xor a
 477+ E987 32 E6 FB             ld (clip_if),a
 478+ E98A CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 479+ E98D 2A E0 FB     .next:  ld hl,(clip_e)
 480+ E990 22 DE FB             ld (clip_s),hl
 481+ E993 11 08 00             ld de,8
 482+ E996 19                   add hl,de
 483+ E997 22 E0 FB             ld (clip_e),hl
 484+ E99A 2A E4 FB             ld hl,(clip_de)
 485+ E99D 22 E2 FB             ld (clip_ds),hl
 486+ E9A0 21 D8 FB             ld hl,clip_cnt
 487+ E9A3 35                   dec (hl)
 488+ E9A4 20 9F                jr nz,.loop
 489+ E9A6 3A D7 FB             ld a,(clip_on)
 490+ E9A9 32 D6 FB             ld (clip_n),a
 491+ E9AC C9                   ret
 492+ E9AD
 493+ E9AD              ; Output S + t*(E-S) at IY, t = dS/(dS-dE)
 494+ E9AD              clip_isect:
 495+ E9AD                  IFDEF LEVEL_FAST_PATH
 496+ E9AD                      ; Level flight: a vertical building edge (S and E share X and Z)
 497+ E9AD                      ; meets the top or bottom plane at (X, +-0.5Z, Z) - no divide or
 498+ E9AD                      ; interpolation.  At pitch 0 vertical edges can't cross the near or
 499+ E9AD                      ; side planes, so this covers every plane they do cross.
 500+ E9AD 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
 501+ E9B0 B7                   or a
 502+ E9B1 20 49                jr nz,.gen
 503+ E9B3 3A D9 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 504+ E9B6 FE 08                cp 8
 505+ E9B8 38 42                jr c,.gen                       ; not top or bottom
 506+ E9BA 2A DE FB             ld hl,(clip_s)
 507+ E9BD ED 5B E0 FB          ld de,(clip_e)
 508+ E9C1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 509+ E9C2 BE                   cp (hl)
 510+ E9C3 20 37                jr nz,.gen
 511+ E9C5 23                   inc hl
 512+ E9C6 13                   inc de
 513+ E9C7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 514+ E9C8 BE                   cp (hl)
 515+ E9C9 20 31                jr nz,.gen                      ; X differs
 516+ E9CB 23                   inc hl
 517+ E9CC 23                   inc hl
 518+ E9CD 23                   inc hl
 519+ E9CE 13                   inc de
 520+ E9CF 13                   inc de
 521+ E9D0 13                   inc de
 522+ E9D1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 523+ E9D2 BE                   cp (hl)
 524+ E9D3 20 27                jr nz,.gen
 525+ E9D5 23                   inc hl
 526+ E9D6 13                   inc de
 527+ E9D7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 528+ E9D8 BE                   cp (hl)
 529+ E9D9 20 21                jr nz,.gen                      ; Z differs
 530+ E9DB DD 2A DE FB          ld ix,(clip_s)
 531+ E9DF DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; X and Z from S (= E)
 532+ E9E2 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 533+ E9E5 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 534+ E9E8 FD 77 01             ld (iy+1),a
 535+ E9EB DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 536+ E9EE DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 537+ E9F1 FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 538+ E9F4 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 539+ E9F7 3A D9 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 540+ E9FA 18 6D                jr .ty                          ; Y = +-0.5Z
 541+ E9FC              .gen:
 542+ E9FC                  ENDIF
 543+ E9FC 2A E2 FB             ld hl,(clip_ds)
 544+ E9FF CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 545+ EA02 22 E7 FB             ld (ci_a),hl
 546+ EA05 2A E4 FB             ld hl,(clip_de)
 547+ EA08 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 548+ EA0B ED 5B E7 FB          ld de,(ci_a)
 549+ EA0F 19                   add hl,de
 550+ EA10 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = |dS|+|dE|, HL = |dS|
 551+ EA11 E5                   push hl
 552+ EA12 B7                   or a
 553+ EA13 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 554+ EA15 E1                   pop hl
 555+ EA16 38 06                jr c,.dv
 556+ EA18 DD 21 FF FF          ld ix,$FFFF
 557+ EA1C 18 03                jr .t
 558+ EA1E CD 83 E7     .dv:    call div_frac
 559+ EA21 DD 22 E9 FB  .t:     ld (ci_t),ix
 560+ EA25 AF                   xor a
 561+ EA26 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 562+ EA29 FD 75 00             ld (iy+0),l
 563+ EA2C FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 564+ EA2F 3E 02                ld a,2
 565+ EA31 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 566+ EA34 FD 75 02             ld (iy+2),l
 567+ EA37 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 568+ EA3A 3E 04                ld a,4
 569+ EA3C CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 570+ EA3F FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 571+ EA42 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 572+ EA45                      ; snap the point exactly onto the plane so rounding can't leave a
 573+ EA45                      ; gap between the polygon and the screen edge
 574+ EA45 FD 6E 04             ld l,(iy+4)
 575+ EA48 FD 66 05             ld h,(iy+5)                     ; Z
 576+ EA4B 3A D9 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 577+ EA4E FE 01                cp 1
 578+ EA50 20 0A                jr nz,.s2
 579+ EA52 FD 36 04 10          ld (iy+4),NEAR                  ; near:   Z = NEAR
 580+ EA56 FD 36 05 00          ld (iy+5),0
 581+ EA5A 18 27                jr .nz
 582+ EA5C FE 02        .s2:    cp 2
 583+ EA5E 20 05                jr nz,.s4
 584+ EA60 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl                     ; left:   X = -Z
 585+ EA63 18 18                jr .sx
 586+ EA65 FE 04        .s4:    cp 4
 587+ EA67 28 14                jr z,.sx                        ; right:  X = Z
 588+ EA69 F5           .ty:    push af
 589+ EA6A CD B3 A7             call zhalf
 590+ EA6D F1                   pop af
 591+ EA6E FE 08                cp 8
 592+ EA70 28 03                jr z,.sy                        ; top:    Y = 0.5Z
 593+ EA72 CD 73 A7             call neg_hl                     ; bottom: Y = -0.5Z
 594+ EA75 FD 75 02     .sy:    ld (iy+2),l
 595+ EA78 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 596+ EA7B 18 06                jr .nz
 597+ EA7D FD 75 00     .sx:    ld (iy+0),l
 598+ EA80 FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 599+ EA83 3A E6 FB     .nz:    ld a,(clip_if)
 600+ EA86 FD 77 06             ld (iy+6),a
 601+ EA89 3A D9 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)               ; new vertex: no cached projection,
 602+ EA8C F6 80                or $80                          ; but remember the plane it is on
 603+ EA8E FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a
 604+ EA91 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 605+ EA94 FD 09                add iy,bc
 606+ EA96 21 D7 FB             ld hl,clip_on
 607+ EA99 34                   inc (hl)
 608+ EA9A C9                   ret
 609+ EA9B
 610+ EA9B              ; A = coordinate offset -> HL = S.c + (E.c - S.c) * t
 611+ EA9B              lerp_c:
 612+ EA9B 2A E0 FB             ld hl,(clip_e)
 613+ EA9E ED 31                add hl,a
 614+ EAA0 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 615+ EAA1 23                   inc hl
 616+ EAA2 56                   ld d,(hl)
 617+ EAA3 2A DE FB             ld hl,(clip_s)
 618+ EAA6 ED 31                add hl,a
 619+ EAA8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 620+ EAA9 23                   inc hl
 621+ EAAA 66                   ld h,(hl)
 622+ EAAB 6F                   ld l,a
 623+ EAAC E5                   push hl
 624+ EAAD EB                   ex de,hl
 625+ EAAE B7                   or a
 626+ EAAF ED 52                sbc hl,de
 627+ EAB1 ED 5B E9 FB          ld de,(ci_t)
 628+ EAB5 CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
 629+ EAB8 D1                   pop de
 630+ EAB9 19                   add hl,de
 631+ EABA C9                   ret
 632+ EABB
 633+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 634+ EABB              ; Draw one face of the current building
 635+ EABB              ;   HL -> 4 vertex offsets into vtx[] (index*16), df_mask = edges to outline
 636+ EABB              ;   (bit k = edge from corner k to corner k+1)
 637+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 638+ EABB              draw_face:
 639+ EABB 22 CA FB             ld (df_ptr),hl
 640+ EABE 01 00 FF             ld bc,$FF00                     ; B = AND of outcodes, C = OR
 641+ EAC1 3A 85 FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 642+ EAC4 57                   ld d,a
 643+ EAC5                      REPT 4
 644+ EAC5 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 645+ EAC6 23          >        inc hl
 646+ EAC7 C6 06       >        add a,6

 651+ EAE5 47          >        ld b,a

 654+ EAE8 4F          >        ld c,a

 645+ EAEA 23          >        inc hl

 648+ EAEE 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 649+ EAEF 5F          >        ld e,a
 650+ EAF0 A0          >        and b
 651+ EAF1 47          >        ld b,a
 652+ EAF2 7B          >        ld a,e
 653+ EAF3 B1          >        or c
 654+ EAF4 4F          >        ld c,a

 668+ EB19 1C          >        inc e

 669+ EB1F 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 669+ EB20 12          >        ld (de),a

 669+ EB24 12          >        ld (de),a

 669+ EB29 E6 02       >        and 2

 669+ EB2D 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB2E                      FACEVTX 4
 670+ EB2E 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 670+ EB2F 23          >        inc hl
 670+ EB30 C6 07       >        add a,7
 670+ EB32 4F          >        ld c,a
 670+ EB33 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 670+ EB34 12          >        ld (de),a
 670+ EB35 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB36 0C          >        inc c
 670+ EB37 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 670+ EB38 12          >        ld (de),a
 670+ EB39 1C          >        inc e

 670+ EB3D E6 04       >        and 4

 670+ EB40 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB41 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB42                      FACEVTX 8
 671+ EB42 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 671+ EB43 23          >        inc hl
 671+ EB44 C6 07       >        add a,7
 671+ EB46 4F          >        ld c,a
 671+ EB47 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 671+ EB48 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB49 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB4A 0C          >        inc c
 671+ EB4B 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 671+ EB4C 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB4D 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB4E 3A CD FB    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 671+ EB51 E6 08       >        and 8
 671+ EB53 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB54 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB55 1C          >        inc e
 672+ EB56 3E 04                ld a,4
 673+ EB58 32 AB FC             ld (p2n),a
 674+ EB5B C3 48 EC             jp .render
 675+ EB5E
 676+ EB5E 32 CC FB     .clip:  ld (df_or),a
 677+ EB61 3A CD FB             ld a,(df_mask)
 678+ EB64 32 CE FB             ld (df_m),a
 679+ EB67 2A CA FB             ld hl,(df_ptr)
 680+ EB6A FD 21 EB FB          ld iy,clipA
 681+ EB6E 06 04                ld b,4
 682+ EB70 7E           .cp:    ld a,(hl)

 686+ EB77 3A 85 FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 687+ EB7A DD 67                ld ixh,a
 688+ EB7C 0E 06                ld c,6
 689+ EB7E DD 7E 00     .cpb:   ld a,(ix+0)
 690+ EB81 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 691+ EB84 DD 23                inc ix
 692+ EB86 FD 23                inc iy
 693+ EB88 0D                   dec c
 694+ EB89 20 F3                jr nz,.cpb
 695+ EB8B 3A CE FB             ld a,(df_m)
 696+ EB8E 5F                   ld e,a
 697+ EB8F E6 01                and 1
 698+ EB91 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a                     ; edge flag from mask
 699+ EB94 7B                   ld a,e
 700+ EB95 0F                   rrca
 701+ EB96 32 CE FB             ld (df_m),a
 702+ EB99 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; the corner's outcode (IX at +6)
 703+ EB9C B7                   or a
 704+ EB9D 3E FF                ld a,$FF
 705+ EB9F 20 03                jr nz,.cn
 706+ EBA1 3A CF FB             ld a,(df_cur)                   ; on screen: projection is cached
 707+ EBA4 FD 77 01     .cn:    ld (iy+1),a
 708+ EBA7 FD 23                inc iy
 709+ EBA9 FD 23                inc iy
 710+ EBAB 10 C3                djnz .cp
 711+ EBAD 3E 04                ld a,4
 712+ EBAF 32 D6 FB             ld (clip_n),a
 713+ EBB2 21 EB FB             ld hl,clipA
 714+ EBB5 22 DA FB             ld (clip_src),hl
 715+ EBB8 21 4B FC             ld hl,clipB
 716+ EBBB 22 DC FB             ld (clip_dst),hl
 717+ EBBE 3E 01                ld a,1
 718+ EBC0 32 D9 FB     .pl:    ld (clip_plane),a
 719+ EBC3 21 CC FB             ld hl,df_or
 720+ EBC6 A6                   and (hl)
 721+ EBC7 28 17                jr z,.skip
 722+ EBC9 CD 19 E9             call clip_poly
