 724+ EBCF FE 03                cp 3
 725+ EBD1 D8                   ret c
 726+ EBD2 2A DA FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 727+ EBD5 ED 5B DC FB          ld de,(clip_dst)
 728+ EBD9 ED 53 DA FB          ld (clip_src),de
 729+ EBDD 22 DC FB             ld (clip_dst),hl
 730+ EBE0 3A D9 FB     .skip:  ld a,(clip_plane)
 731+ EBE3 87                   add a,a
 732+ EBE4 FE 20                cp 32
 733+ EBE6 20 D8                jr nz,.pl
 734+ EBE8
 735+ EBE8 DD 2A DA FB          ld ix,(clip_src)
 736+ EBEC FD 21 80 E0          ld iy,p2buf
 737+ EBF0 3A D6 FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 738+ EBF3 32 AB FC             ld (p2n),a
 739+ EBF6 47                   ld b,a
 740+ EBF7 C5           .pj:    push bc
 741+ EBF8 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 742+ EBFB FE 80                cp $80
 743+ EBFD 30 0C                jr nc,.pf
 744+ EBFF C6 07                add a,7                         ; unclipped on-screen corner:
 745+ EC01 6F                   ld l,a                          ; reuse its cached sx, sy
 746+ EC02 3A 85 FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 747+ EC05 67                   ld h,a
 748+ EC06 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 749+ EC07 2C                   inc l
 750+ EC08 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 751+ EC09 18 24                jr .ps
 752+ EC0B DD E5        .pf:    push ix
 753+ EC0D E1                   pop hl
 754+ EC0E CD B0 E7             call project
 755+ EC11 5F                   ld e,a
 756+ EC12 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 757+ EC15 3C                   inc a
 758+ EC16 28 16                jr z,.p0                        ; $FF: an ordinary vertex
 759+ EC18 3D                   dec a
 760+ EC19                      ; On a clip plane: put it exactly on that screen edge (projection
 761+ EC19                      ; rounding could leave it a pixel inside, opening a 1-pixel gap).
 762+ EC19 1F                   rra                             ; near: nothing
 763+ EC1A 1F                   rra
 764+ EC1B 30 02                jr nc,.e1
 765+ EC1D 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
 766+ EC1F 1F           .e1:    rra
 767+ EC20 30 02                jr nc,.e2
 768+ EC22 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
 769+ EC24 1F           .e2:    rra
 770+ EC25 30 02                jr nc,.e3
 771+ EC27 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
 772+ EC29 1F           .e3:    rra
 773+ EC2A 30 02                jr nc,.p0
 774+ EC2C 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
 775+ EC2E 7B           .p0:    ld a,e
 776+ EC2F FD 77 00     .ps:    ld (iy+0),a
 777+ EC32 FD 71 01             ld (iy+1),c
 778+ EC35 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 779+ EC38 FD 77 02             ld (iy+2),a
 780+ EC3B 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 781+ EC3E DD 09                add ix,bc
 782+ EC40 01 04 00             ld bc,4
 783+ EC43 FD 09                add iy,bc
 784+ EC45 C1                   pop bc
 785+ EC46 10 AF                djnz .pj
 786+ EC48
 787+ EC48              .render:
 788+ EC48                      ; close the polygon: copy vertex 0 after the last
 789+ EC48 3A AB FC             ld a,(p2n)
 790+ EC4B 87                   add a,a
 791+ EC4C 87                   add a,a
 792+ EC4D 21 80 E0             ld hl,p2buf
 793+ EC50 ED 31                add hl,a
 794+ EC52 3A 80 E0             ld a,(p2buf)
 795+ EC55 77                   ld (hl),a
 796+ EC56 23                   inc hl
 797+ EC57 3A 81 E0             ld a,(p2buf+1)
 798+ EC5A 77                   ld (hl),a
 799+ EC5B                  IFDEF DRAW_LINES
 800+ EC5B ~                    call fill_poly
 801+ EC5B ~                    ld a,EDGE_COL
 802+ EC5B ~                    ld (line_col),a
 803+ EC5B ~                    jp outline_poly
 804+ EC5B                  ELSE
 805+ EC5B C3 5E EC             jp fill_poly                    ; solid faces only
 806+ EC5E                  ENDIF
 807+ EC5E
 808+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 809+ EC5E              ; Convex polygon fill from p2buf (8-bit screen coords).
 810+ EC5E              ;   Each edge is walked with an exact integer DDA into one of two x tables:
 811+ EC5E              ;   downward edges -> TA, upward edges -> TB.  For a convex polygon every row
 812+ EC5E              ;   then has one entry in each, and the span between them is filled.
 813+ EC5E              ;   Winding doesn't matter.
 814+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 815+ EC5E              fill_poly:
 816+ EC5E 3A AB FC             ld a,(p2n)
 817+ EC61 47                   ld b,a
 818+ EC62 21 81 E0             ld hl,p2buf+1
 819+ EC65 0E FF                ld c,255
 820+ EC67 16 00                ld d,0
 821+ EC69 7E           .mm:    ld a,(hl)
 822+ EC6A B9                   cp c
 823+ EC6B 30 01                jr nc,.m1
 824+ EC6D 4F                   ld c,a
 825+ EC6E BA           .m1:    cp d
 826+ EC6F 38 01                jr c,.m2
 827+ EC71 57                   ld d,a
 828+ EC72 23           .m2:    inc hl
 829+ EC73 23                   inc hl
 830+ EC74 23                   inc hl
 831+ EC75 23                   inc hl
 832+ EC76 10 F1                djnz .mm
 833+ EC78 79                   ld a,c
 834+ EC79 BA                   cp d
 835+ EC7A C8                   ret z                           ; zero height - outline only
 836+ EC7B 32 AC FC             ld (fy0),a
 837+ EC7E 7A                   ld a,d
 838+ EC7F 32 AD FC             ld (fy1),a
 839+ EC82
 840+ EC82 DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
 841+ EC86 3A AB FC             ld a,(p2n)
 842+ EC89 32 AE FC             ld (fe_cnt),a
 843+ EC8C DD 56 00     .ed:    ld d,(ix+0)
 844+ EC8F DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)
 845+ EC92 DD 46 04             ld b,(ix+4)
 846+ EC95 DD 4E 05             ld c,(ix+5)
 847+ EC98 7B                   ld a,e
 848+ EC99 B9                   cp c
 849+ EC9A 28 15                jr z,.nx                        ; horizontal edge
 850+ EC9C 38 0A                jr c,.down
 851+ EC9E 7A                   ld a,d                          ; going up: swap ends, use TB
 852+ EC9F 50                   ld d,b
 853+ ECA0 47                   ld b,a
 854+ ECA1 7B                   ld a,e
 855+ ECA2 59                   ld e,c
 856+ ECA3 4F                   ld c,a
 857+ ECA4 3E E3                ld a,high TB
 858+ ECA6 18 02                jr .walk
 859+ ECA8 3E E2        .down:  ld a,high TA
 860+ ECAA DD E5        .walk:  push ix
 861+ ECAC CD C7 EC             call walk_edge
 862+ ECAF DD E1                pop ix
 863+ ECB1 01 04 00     .nx:    ld bc,4
 864+ ECB4 DD 09                add ix,bc
 865+ ECB6 21 AE FC             ld hl,fe_cnt
 866+ ECB9 35                   dec (hl)
 867+ ECBA 20 D0                jr nz,.ed
 868+ ECBC
 869+ ECBC 3A 6D FB             ld a,(face_col)                 ; shaded colour of this face
 870+ ECBF 32 B4 FC             ld (l2_cls_byte),a              ; DMA source byte for long spans
 871+ ECC2 57                   ld d,a
 872+ ECC3 5F                   ld e,a                          ; PUSH pair for short spans
 873+ ECC4 C3 4C ED             jp fill_spans
 874+ ECC7
 875+ ECC7              ; Walk an edge (D=xa, E=ya) -> (B=xb, C=yb), yb > ya, into table page A.
 876+ ECC7              ; Bresenham style: x += q each row, fractional r/dy accumulated in a biased
 877+ ECC7              ; byte (carry == wrap), so the end point is hit exactly.
 878+ ECC7              walk_edge:
 879+ ECC7 67                   ld h,a
 880+ ECC8 6B                   ld l,e
 881+ ECC9 79                   ld a,c
 882+ ECCA 93                   sub e
 883+ ECCB 4F                   ld c,a                          ; C = dy (1..191)
 884+ ECCC 78                   ld a,b
 885+ ECCD 92                   sub d                           ; dx
 886+ ECCE 38 37                jr c,.neg
 887+ ECD0 B9                   cp c
 888+ ECD1 30 07                jr nc,.pdv
 889+ ECD3 DD 6F                ld ixl,a                        ; steep: q = 0, r = dx
 890+ ECD5 CD 40 ED             call .setup
 891+ ECD8 18 20                jr .p0
 892+ ECDA CD 9F E7     .pdv:   call div8                       ; E = q, A = r
 893+ ECDD DD 63                ld ixh,e
 894+ ECDF DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 895+ ECE1 CD 40 ED             call .setup
 896+ ECE4 DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 897+ ECE6 B7                   or a
 898+ ECE7 28 11                jr z,.p0
 899+ ECE9 72           .pl:    ld (hl),d
 900+ ECEA 2C                   inc l
 901+ ECEB 7A                   ld a,d
 902+ ECEC DD 84                add a,ixh
 903+ ECEE 57                   ld d,a
 904+ ECEF 7B                   ld a,e
 905+ ECF0 DD 85                add a,ixl
 906+ ECF2 30 02                jr nc,.pl1
 907+ ECF4 81                   add a,c
 908+ ECF5 14                   inc d
 909+ ECF6 5F           .pl1:   ld e,a
 910+ ECF7 10 F0                djnz .pl
 911+ ECF9 C9                   ret
 912+ ECFA 72           .p0:    ld (hl),d
 913+ ECFB 2C                   inc l
 914+ ECFC 7B                   ld a,e
 915+ ECFD DD 85                add a,ixl
 916+ ECFF 30 02                jr nc,.p01
 917+ ED01 81                   add a,c
 918+ ED02 14                   inc d
 919+ ED03 5F           .p01:   ld e,a
 920+ ED04 10 F4                djnz .p0
 921+ ED06 C9                   ret
 922+ ED07
 923+ ED07 ED 44        .neg:   neg
 924+ ED09 B9                   cp c
 925+ ED0A 30 07                jr nc,.ndv
 926+ ED0C DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 927+ ED0E CD 40 ED             call .setup
 928+ ED11 18 20                jr .n0
 929+ ED13 CD 9F E7     .ndv:   call div8
 930+ ED16 DD 63                ld ixh,e
 931+ ED18 DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 932+ ED1A CD 40 ED             call .setup
 933+ ED1D DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 934+ ED1F B7                   or a
 935+ ED20 28 11                jr z,.n0
 936+ ED22 72           .nl:    ld (hl),d
 937+ ED23 2C                   inc l
 938+ ED24 7A                   ld a,d
 939+ ED25 DD 94                sub ixh
 940+ ED27 57                   ld d,a
 941+ ED28 7B                   ld a,e
 942+ ED29 DD 85                add a,ixl
 943+ ED2B 30 02                jr nc,.nl1
 944+ ED2D 81                   add a,c
 945+ ED2E 15                   dec d
 946+ ED2F 5F           .nl1:   ld e,a
 947+ ED30 10 F0                djnz .nl
 948+ ED32 C9                   ret
 949+ ED33 72           .n0:    ld (hl),d
 950+ ED34 2C                   inc l
 951+ ED35 7B                   ld a,e
 952+ ED36 DD 85                add a,ixl
 953+ ED38 30 02                jr nc,.n01
 954+ ED3A 81                   add a,c
 955+ ED3B 15                   dec d
 956+ ED3C 5F           .n01:   ld e,a
 957+ ED3D 10 F4                djnz .n0
 958+ ED3F C9                   ret
 959+ ED40
 960+ ED40              ; B = dy+1 rows, E = biased accumulator (dy/2 - dy), C = -dy
 961+ ED40 41           .setup: ld b,c
 962+ ED41 04                   inc b
 963+ ED42 79                   ld a,c
 964+ ED43 CB 3F                srl a
 965+ ED45 91                   sub c
 966+ ED46 5F                   ld e,a
 967+ ED47 79                   ld a,c
 968+ ED48 ED 44                neg
 969+ ED4A 4F                   ld c,a
 970+ ED4B C9                   ret
 971+ ED4C
 972+ ED4C              ;==============================================================================
 973+ ED4C              ; Span filler.  Rows fy0..fy1, x from TA/TB.  DE = colour pair.
 974+ ED4C              ; Short spans use the stack pointer as a 2-byte write pointer into the frame
 975+ ED4C              ; buffer (interrupts are disabled), jumping into an unrolled block of PUSH DE.
 976+ ED4C              ; Spans of DMA_SPAN_MIN pixels or more are written by the zxnDMA, which only
 977+ ED4C              ; needs its length and destination reloaded (see clear_screen).
 978+ ED4C              ;==============================================================================
 979+ ED4C              fill_spans:
 980+ ED4C 3A AC FC             ld a,(fy0)
 981+ ED4F CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; first row's section into slot 0
 982+ ED52 ED 73 B0 FC          ld (saved_sp),sp
 983+ ED56 3A AC FC             ld a,(fy0)
 984+ ED59 47                   ld b,a
 985+ ED5A 3A AD FC             ld a,(fy1)
 986+ ED5D DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 987+ ED5F
 988+ ED5F              fs_loop:
 989+ ED5F 26 E2                ld h,high TA
 990+ ED61 68                   ld l,b
 991+ ED62 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 992+ ED63 24                   inc h
 993+ ED64 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 994+ ED65 B9                   cp c
 995+ ED66 38 03                jr c,.ord
 996+ ED68 69                   ld l,c
 997+ ED69 4F                   ld c,a
 998+ ED6A 7D                   ld a,l
 999+ ED6B 6F           .ord:   ld l,a                          ; A = left x, C = right x
1000+ ED6C 78                   ld a,b
1001+ ED6D E6 1F                and 31
1002+ ED6F 67                   ld h,a                          ; row within the paged section
1003+ ED70 79                   ld a,c
1004+ ED71 95                   sub l                           ; width-1
1005+ ED72 FE 13                cp DMA_SPAN_MIN-1
1006+ ED74 30 13                jr nc,.dma
1007+ ED76 CB 3F        .push:  srl a
1008+ ED78 38 03                jr c,.even
1009+ ED7A 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; odd width: single pixel first
1010+ ED7B 18 01                jr .go
1011+ ED7D 3C           .even:  inc a
1012+ ED7E 69           .go:    ld l,c
1013+ ED7F 23                   inc hl                          ; one past right edge (may be $C000)
1014+ ED80 F9                   ld sp,hl
1015+ ED81 6F                   ld l,a
1016+ ED82 3E 80                ld a,low push_end
1017+ ED84 95                   sub l
1018+ ED85 6F                   ld l,a
1019+ ED86 26 E1                ld h,high push_end
1020+ ED88 E9                   jp (hl)
1021+ ED89
1022+ ED89                      ; Long span: zxnDMA from the fixed colour byte (port A) to HL.
1023+ ED89                      ; Everything but length and destination was set up by clear_screen.
1024+ ED89 3C           .dma:   inc a
1025+ ED8A 20 03                jr nz,.dmal
1026+ ED8C 3D                   dec a                           ; 256 wide: length needs the high
1027+ ED8D 18 E7                jr .push                        ; byte, so PUSH it (A = width-1)
1028+ ED8F              .dmal:
1029+ ED8F 4F                   ld c,a
1030+ ED90 3E 25                ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_LO
1031+ ED92 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1032+ ED94 79                   ld a,c
1033+ ED95 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; length = width
1034+ ED97 3E AD                ld a,DMA_WR4_CONT_MODE
1035+ ED99 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1036+ ED9B 7D                   ld a,l
1037+ ED9C D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; port B = y*256 + x
1038+ ED9E 7C                   ld a,h
1039+ ED9F D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1040+ EDA1 3E CF                ld a,DMA_LOAD
1041+ EDA3 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1042+ EDA5 3E 87                ld a,DMA_ENABLE
1043+ EDA7 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; CPU waits until the span is written
1044+ EDA9 C3 80 E1             jp push_end
1045+ EDAC
1046+ EDAC              ;==============================================================================
1047+ EDAC              ; Outline the polygon in p2buf, skipping edges flagged 0 (clip edges)
1048+ EDAC              ;==============================================================================
1049+ EDAC              outline_poly:
1050+ EDAC DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
1051+ EDB0 3A AB FC             ld a,(p2n)
1052+ EDB3 47                   ld b,a
1053+ EDB4 C5           .lp:    push bc
1054+ EDB5 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
1055+ EDB8 B7                   or a
1056+ EDB9 28 0F                jr z,.sk
1057+ EDBB DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
1058+ EDBE DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
1059+ EDC1 DD 5E 04             ld e,(ix+4)
1060+ EDC4 DD 56 05             ld d,(ix+5)
1061+ EDC7 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
1062+ EDCA 01 04 00     .sk:    ld bc,4
1063+ EDCD DD 09                add ix,bc
1064+ EDCF C1                   pop bc
1065+ EDD0 10 E2                djnz .lp
1066+ EDD2 C9                   ret
1067+ EDD3
1068+ EDD3              ;==============================================================================
1069+ EDD3              ; Bresenham line, on-screen 8-bit coordinates.
1070+ EDD3              ;   L = x0, H = y0, E = x1, D = y1, colour in line_col.  Preserves IX, IY.
1071+ EDD3              ;   Pages Layer 2 sections into slot 0 as the line crosses them.
1072+ EDD3              ;==============================================================================
1073+ EDD3              draw_line:
1074+ EDD3 3A AF FC             ld a,(line_col)
1075+ EDD6 4F                   ld c,a
1076+ EDD7 7B                   ld a,e
1077+ EDD8 95                   sub l
1078+ EDD9 30 06                jr nc,.xp
1079+ EDDB ED 44                neg
1080+ EDDD 06 2D                ld b,OP_DEC_L
1081+ EDDF 18 02                jr .xd
1082+ EDE1 06 2C        .xp:    ld b,OP_INC_L
1083+ EDE3 5F           .xd:    ld e,a                          ; |dx|
1084+ EDE4 78                   ld a,b
1085+ EDE5 32 0D EE             ld (.smx1),a
1086+ EDE8 32 2A EE             ld (.smx2),a
1087+ EDEB 7A                   ld a,d
1088+ EDEC 94                   sub h
1089+ EDED 30 06                jr nc,.yp
1090+ EDEF ED 44                neg
1091+ EDF1 06 25                ld b,OP_DEC_H
1092+ EDF3 18 02                jr .yd
1093+ EDF5 06 24        .yp:    ld b,OP_INC_H
1094+ EDF7 57           .yd:    ld d,a                          ; |dy|
1095+ EDF8 78                   ld a,b
1096+ EDF9 32 11 EE             ld (.smy1),a
1097+ EDFC 32 21 EE             ld (.smy2),a
1098+ EDFF 7C                   ld a,h
1099+ EE00 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; page in the start row's section
1100+ EE03 67                   ld h,a                          ; y within the section
1101+ EE04 7B                   ld a,e
1102+ EE05 BA                   cp d
1103+ EE06 38 13                jr c,.ymaj
1104+ EE08 43                   ld b,e
1105+ EE09 04                   inc b
1106+ EE0A CB 3F                srl a
1107+ EE0C 71           .xl:    ld (hl),c
1108+ EE0D 2C           .smx1:  inc l
1109+ EE0E 92                   sub d
1110+ EE0F 30 07                jr nc,.xs
1111+ EE11 24           .smy1:  inc h
1112+ EE12 CB 6C                bit 5,h                         ; left the 32-row section?
1113+ EE14 C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1114+ EE17 83                   add a,e
1115+ EE18 10 F2        .xs:    djnz .xl
1116+ EE1A C9                   ret
1117+ EE1B 42           .ymaj:  ld b,d
1118+ EE1C 04                   inc b
1119+ EE1D 7A                   ld a,d
1120+ EE1E CB 3F                srl a
1121+ EE20 71           .yl:    ld (hl),c
1122+ EE21 24           .smy2:  inc h
1123+ EE22 CB 6C                bit 5,h
1124+ EE24 C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1125+ EE27 93                   sub e
1126+ EE28 30 02                jr nc,.ys
1127+ EE2A 2C           .smx2:  inc l
1128+ EE2B 82                   add a,d
1129+ EE2C 10 F2        .ys:    djnz .yl
1130+ EE2E C9                   ret
1131+ EE2F
1132+ EE2F              ;==============================================================================
1133+ EE2F              ; Clear the viewport (rows 0..VIEW_H-1) of the back buffer with the zxnDMA,
1134+ EE2F              ; one 8K section (32 rows) at a time through slot 0: sky above the horizon
1135+ EE2F              ; row, ground below.  The fills reuse the l2_fill command block from
1136+ EE2F              ; layer2_cls.asm, patching its destination (port B) and length; the source
1137+ EE2F              ; (port A) is the fixed byte l2_cls_byte.  Zero-length fills are skipped.
1138+ EE2F              ;==============================================================================
1139+ EE2F              clear_screen:
1140+ EE2F 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; C = first row of the section
1141+ EE31 79           .sec:   ld a,c
1142+ EE32 CD 20 E7             call seg_map
1143+ EE35 3A E9 AD             ld a,(horizon)                  ; sky rows here = clamp(hz - C, 0, 32)
1144+ EE38                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1145+ EE38 3C                   inc a                           ; the horizon row too: at the horizon
1146+ EE39                                                      ; it is all distant wall, and it hides
1147+ EE39                                                      ; a 1-row rounding gap under the
1148+ EE39                                                      ; wall's top edge when pitched
1149+ EE39                  ENDIF
1150+ EE39 91                   sub c
1151+ EE3A 30 01                jr nc,.s1
1152+ EE3C AF                   xor a
1153+ EE3D FE 21        .s1:    cp 33
1154+ EE3F 38 02                jr c,.s2
1155+ EE41 3E 20                ld a,32
1156+ EE43 32 B3 FC     .s2:    ld (cs_sky),a
1157+ EE46 B7                   or a
1158+ EE47 28 0D                jr z,.gnd
1159+ EE49 C5                   push bc
1160+ EE4A 47                   ld b,a                          ; sky: section start, rows*256 bytes
1161+ EE4B 0E 00                ld c,0
1162+ EE4D 21 00 00             ld hl,0
1163+ EE50                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1164+ EE50 3E 01                ld a,WALL_FAMILY*4+SHADE_FACE   ; "sky" is the perimeter wall
1165+ EE52                  ELSE
1166+ EE52 ~                    ld a,SKY_COL
1167+ EE52                  ENDIF
1168+ EE52 CD 7E EE             call dma_fill
1169+ EE55 C1                   pop bc
1170+ EE56 3A B3 FC     .gnd:   ld a,(cs_sky)
1171+ EE59 FE 20                cp 32
1172+ EE5B 28 11                jr z,.nx
1173+ EE5D C5                   push bc
1174+ EE5E 67                   ld h,a                          ; ground: after the sky rows
1175+ EE5F 2E 00                ld l,0
1176+ EE61 ED 44                neg
1177+ EE63 C6 20                add a,32
1178+ EE65 47                   ld b,a
1179+ EE66 0E 00                ld c,0
1180+ EE68 3E 07                ld a,GROUND_COL
1181+ EE6A CD 7E EE             call dma_fill
1182+ EE6D C1                   pop bc
1183+ EE6E 79           .nx:    ld a,c
1184+ EE6F C6 20                add a,32
1185+ EE71 4F                   ld c,a
1186+ EE72 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
1187+ EE74 20 BB                jr nz,.sec
1188+ EE76                      ; Leave the DMA ready for fill_spans: length high byte 0, so each span
1189+ EE76                      ; only reloads length low, destination, LOAD and ENABLE.  fill_poly
1190+ EE76                      ; puts each building's colour in the source byte l2_cls_byte.
1191+ EE76 3E 45        .spans: ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_HI
1192+ EE78 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1193+ EE7A AF                   xor a
1194+ EE7B D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1195+ EE7D C9                   ret
1196+ EE7E
1197+ EE7E              ; DMA fill: HL = destination, BC = length (non-zero), A = colour
1198+ EE7E              dma_fill:
1199+ EE7E 32 B4 FC             ld (l2_cls_byte),a
1200+ EE81 22 C1 FC             ld (l2_fill_bstrt),hl
1201+ EE84 ED 43 BC FC          ld (l2_fill_length),bc
1202+ EE88 21 B5 FC             ld hl,l2_fill
1203+ EE8B 06 12                ld b,l2_fill_cmd_len
1204+ EE8D 0E 6B                ld c,IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT
1205+ EE8F ED B3                otir                            ; CPU waits while the DMA runs
1206+ EE91 C9                   ret
1207+ EE92
1208+ EE92              ;==============================================================================
1209+ EE92              ; City traversal - back to front without sorting.
1210+ EE92              ;   For grid-aligned boxes in separate cells, a box can only hide boxes whose
1211+ EE92              ;   cell offsets from the camera cell are larger (or equal) on both axes in
1212+ EE92              ;   the same quadrant.  So: rows from the far edges inwards, and within each
1213+ EE92              ;   row columns from the far edges inwards, the camera row/column last.
1214+ EE92              ;==============================================================================
1215+ EE92              draw_city:
1216+ EE92                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1217+ EE92 CD 9B F7             call occ_begin
1218+ EE95 3A 87 FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1219+ EE98 B7                   or a
1220+ EE99 28 0E                jr z,draw_cells                 ; pitched: the painter's order as is
1221+ EE9B CD C4 F7             call occ_cells                  ; front to back: keep what isn't hidden
1222+ EE9E 3A 88 FB             ld a,(occ_full)
1223+ EEA1 B7                   or a
1224+ EEA2 CA 04 FB             jp z,occ_draw_list              ; back to front: draw it
1225+ EEA5 AF                   xor a                           ; too many to keep: draw this frame
1226+ EEA6 32 87 FB             ld (occ_on),a                   ; the plain way
1227+ EEA9                  ENDIF
1228+ EEA9              draw_cells:
1229+ EEA9 3A 0F AE             ld a,(cz)
1230+ EEAC 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
1231+ EEAF 96                   sub (hl)
1232+ EEB0 32 68 FB             ld (cell_j),a
1233+ EEB3 3A 68 FB     .b:     ld a,(cell_j)
1234+ EEB6 21 0F AE             ld hl,cz
1235+ EEB9 BE                   cp (hl)
1236+ EEBA 28 09                jr z,.f
1237+ EEBC CD E1 EE             call draw_row
1238+ EEBF 21 68 FB             ld hl,cell_j
1239+ EEC2 34                   inc (hl)
1240+ EEC3 18 EE                jr .b
1241+ EEC5 3A 0F AE     .f:     ld a,(cz)
1242+ EEC8 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
1243+ EECB 86                   add a,(hl)
1244+ EECC 32 68 FB             ld (cell_j),a
1245+ EECF 3A 68 FB     .f2:    ld a,(cell_j)
1246+ EED2 21 0F AE             ld hl,cz
1247+ EED5 BE                   cp (hl)
1248+ EED6 28 09                jr z,draw_row                   ; camera row last
1249+ EED8 CD E1 EE             call draw_row
1250+ EEDB 21 68 FB             ld hl,cell_j
1251+ EEDE 35                   dec (hl)
1252+ EEDF 18 EE                jr .f2
1253+ EEE1
1254+ EEE1              draw_row:
1255+ EEE1 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1256+ EEE4 FE 40                cp GRID
1257+ EEE6 D0                   ret nc
1258+ EEE7 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
1259+ EEEA 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
1260+ EEED 96                   sub (hl)
1261+ EEEE 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
1262+ EEF1 CD 55 EF             call row_start
1263+ EEF4 3A 67 FB     .l:     ld a,(cell_i)
1264+ EEF7 21 0E AE             ld hl,cx
1265+ EEFA BE                   cp (hl)
1266+ EEFB 28 1F                jr z,.r
1267+ EEFD CD 82 EF             call try_cell
1268+ EF00 21 67 FB             ld hl,cell_i
1269+ EF03 34                   inc (hl)
1270+ EF04 2A 80 FB             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell +x: (X,Z1) += (cosY,sinY)
1271+ EF07 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1272+ EF0B 19                   add hl,de
1273+ EF0C 22 80 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1274+ EF0F 2A 82 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1275+ EF12 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1276+ EF16 19                   add hl,de
1277+ EF17 22 82 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1278+ EF1A 18 D8                jr .l
1279+ EF1C 3A 0E AE     .r:     ld a,(cx)
1280+ EF1F 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
1281+ EF22 86                   add a,(hl)
1282+ EF23 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
1283+ EF26 CD 55 EF             call row_start
1284+ EF29 3A 67 FB     .r2:    ld a,(cell_i)
1285+ EF2C 21 0E AE             ld hl,cx
1286+ EF2F BE                   cp (hl)
1287+ EF30 28 50                jr z,try_cell                   ; camera column last
1288+ EF32 CD 82 EF             call try_cell
1289+ EF35 21 67 FB             ld hl,cell_i
1290+ EF38 35                   dec (hl)
1291+ EF39 2A 80 FB             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell -x
1292+ EF3C ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1293+ EF40 B7                   or a
1294+ EF41 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1295+ EF43 22 80 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1296+ EF46 2A 82 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1297+ EF49 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1298+ EF4D B7                   or a
1299+ EF4E ED 52                sbc hl,de
1300+ EF50 22 82 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1301+ EF53 18 D4                jr .r2
1302+ EF55
1303+ EF55              ; Yaw-rotated centre of cell (cell_i, cell_j) -> row_x, row_z1.  Moving one
1304+ EF55              ; cell (256 units) in x then changes (X, Z1) by exactly the raw (cosY, sinY).
1305+ EF55              row_start:
1306+ EF55 3A 67 FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1307+ EF58 67                   ld h,a
1308+ EF59 2E 80                ld l,128
1309+ EF5B ED 5B B9 A7          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1310+ EF5F B7                   or a
1311+ EF60 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1312+ EF62 22 78 FB             ld (t_dx),hl
1313+ EF65 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1314+ EF68 67                   ld h,a
1315+ EF69 2E 80                ld l,128
1316+ EF6B ED 5B BC A7          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1317+ EF6F B7                   or a
1318+ EF70 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1319+ EF72 22 7A FB             ld (t_dz),hl
1320+ EF75 CD 03 F4             call rot_yaw
1321+ EF78 22 80 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1322+ EF7B 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1323+ EF7E 22 82 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1324+ EF81 C9                   ret
1325+ EF82
1326+ EF82              try_cell:
1327+ EF82 3A 67 FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1328+ EF85 FE 40                cp GRID
1329+ EF87 D0                   ret nc
1330+ EF88                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
1331+ EF88 ~                    call vtick                      ; keep the FPS clock sampling
1332+ EF88                  ENDIF
1333+ EF88                      ; fall through
1334+ EF88
1335+ EF88              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1336+ EF88              ; Transform and draw the building in (cell_i, cell_j)
1337+ EF88              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1338+ EF88              draw_cell:
1339+ EF88 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1340+ EF8B 6F                   ld l,a
1341+ EF8C 26 00                ld h,0
1342+ EF8E                      ROWOFS
1342+ EF8E 29          >        add hl,hl
1342+ EF8F 29          >        add hl,hl
1342+ EF90 29          >        add hl,hl
1342+ EF91 29          >        add hl,hl
1342+ EF92 29          >        add hl,hl
1342+ EF93 29          >        add hl,hl
1343+ EF94 3A 67 FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1344+ EF97 ED 31                add hl,a
1345+ EF99 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
1346+ EF9C 19                   add hl,de
1347+ EF9D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1348+ EF9E 4F                   ld c,a
1349+ EF9F E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
1350+ EFA1 20 1A                jr nz,.nst
1351+ EFA3                      ; street: the zombies and humans standing there (bits 7, 6)
1352+ EFA3 79                   ld a,c
1353+ EFA4 E6 C0                and CELL_ZOMBIE|CELL_HUMAN
1354+ EFA6 C8                   ret z                           ; nobody
1355+ EFA7                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1356+ EFA7 3A 87 FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1357+ EFAA B7                   or a
1358+ EFAB 28 08                jr z,.sdraw
1359+ EFAD E5                   push hl
1360+ EFAE CD A3 F8             call occ_zombie                 ; level flight: list them
1361+ EFB1 E1                   pop hl
1362+ EFB2 C3 90 F8             jp occ_human
1363+ EFB5              .sdraw:
1364+ EFB5                  ENDIF
1365+ EFB5 E5                   push hl
1366+ EFB6 CD CA 2C             call zombie_cell
1367+ EFB9 E1                   pop hl
1368+ EFBA C3 09 37             jp human_cell
1369+ EFBD FE 12        .nst:   cp VOID_CODE
1370+ EFBF C8                   ret z                           ; out of bounds: never drawn
1371+ EFC0 79           .bld:   ld a,c                          ; humans on the roof, monsters
1372+ EFC1 E6 C0                and CELL_ZOMBIE|CELL_HUMAN      ; (ghosts) above it?
1373+ EFC3 28 21                jr z,draw_bld                   ; nobody
1374+ EFC5                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1375+ EFC5 3A 87 FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1376+ EFC8 B7                   or a
1377+ EFC9 28 0E                jr z,.hdraw
1378+ EFCB C5                   push bc
1379+ EFCC E5                   push hl
1380+ EFCD CD 90 F8             call occ_human                  ; listed before the building, so
1381+ EFD0 E1                   pop hl                          ; drawn after it
1382+ EFD1 E5                   push hl
1383+ EFD2 CD A3 F8             call occ_zombie
1384+ EFD5 E1                   pop hl
1385+ EFD6 C1                   pop bc
1386+ EFD7 18 0D                jr draw_bld
1387+ EFD9              .hdraw:
1388+ EFD9                  ENDIF
1389+ EFD9 E5                   push hl
1390+ EFDA CD E6 EF             call draw_bld                   ; the building, then the ghosts
1391+ EFDD E1                   pop hl                          ; above it and the humans on its
1392+ EFDE E5                   push hl                         ; roof
1393+ EFDF CD CA 2C             call zombie_cell
1394+ EFE2 E1                   pop hl
1395+ EFE3 C3 09 37             jp human_cell
1396+ EFE6
1397+ EFE6              ; The building in the cell at HL (C = its map byte)
1398+ EFE6              draw_bld:
1399+ EFE6 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map         ; same index in city_col (city bank)
1400+ EFE9 19                   add hl,de
1401+ EFEA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1402+ EFEB E6 FC                and %11111100
1403+ EFED 32 6C FB             ld (bld_col),a                  ; colour family (shade bits clear)
1404+ EFF0 79                   ld a,c
1405+ EFF1 CD 3F A6             call bld_height
1406+ EFF4 22 69 FB             ld (bld_h),hl
1407+ EFF7                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1408+ EFF7                      ; Perimeter wall (outer row/column): everything above the horizon is
1409+ EFF7                      ; already wall-coloured, so draw it only up to camera height - its
1410+ EFF7                      ; top edge then lies exactly on the horizon line, at any pitch.
1411+ EFF7 AF                   xor a
1412+ EFF8 32 6B FB             ld (bld_wall),a
1413+ EFFB 3A 67 FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1414+ EFFE 3C                   inc a
1415+ EFFF E6 3E                and GRID-2                      ; zero for column 0 or GRID-1
1416+ F001 28 08                jr z,.wall
1417+ F003 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1418+ F006 3C                   inc a
1419+ F007 E6 3E                and GRID-2
1420+ F009 20 0B                jr nz,.nwall
1421+ F00B 2A BE A7     .wall:  ld hl,(cam_y)
1422+ F00E 22 69 FB             ld (bld_h),hl
1423+ F011 3E 01                ld a,1
1424+ F013 32 6B FB             ld (bld_wall),a
1425+ F016              .nwall:
1426+ F016                  ENDIF
1427+ F016                      ; world corners: the building fills its cell
1428+ F016 3A 67 FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1429+ F019 67                   ld h,a
1430+ F01A 2E 00                ld l,0
1431+ F01C 22 70 FB             ld (bld_x0),hl
1432+ F01F 24                   inc h
1433+ F020 22 72 FB             ld (bld_x1),hl
1434+ F023 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1435+ F026 67                   ld h,a
1436+ F027 22 74 FB             ld (bld_z0),hl
1437+ F02A 24                   inc h
1438+ F02B 22 76 FB             ld (bld_z1),hl
1439+ F02E
1440+ F02E 21 F6 AD             ld hl,vecs0
1441+ F031 22 6E FB             ld (bld_vecs),hl
1442+ F034 21 00 01             ld hl,256                       ; cull radius: 181 (half diagonal) * sqrt2
1443+ F037 22 96 FB             ld (bld_r),hl
1444+ F03A
1445+ F03A                      ; yaw-space centre of this cell, kept up to date by draw_row
1446+ F03A 2A 82 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1447+ F03D 22 7C FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1448+ F040 2A 80 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
1449+ F043 22 7E FB             ld (t_x),hl
1450+ F046                      ; Horizontal frustum cull, valid at any pitch: a visible point has
1451+ F046                      ; |X|*c <= Z1 with c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|. Reject the bounding circle.
1452+ F046 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
1453+ F049 ED 5B 06 AE          ld de,(cull_c)
1454+ F04D CD 7F A7             call fmul
1455+ F050 ED 5B 7C FB          ld de,(t_z1)
1456+ F054 B7                   or a
1457+ F055 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1458+ F057 ED 5B 96 FB          ld de,(bld_r)
1459+ F05B B7                   or a
1460+ F05C ED 52                sbc hl,de
1461+ F05E F0                   ret p
1462+ F05F                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1463+ F05F 3A 87 FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1464+ F062 B7                   or a                            ; (carry clear)
1465+ F063 C4 18 FA             call nz,occ_pretest
1466+ F066 D8                   ret c                           ; surely hidden: skip the transform
1467+ F067                  ENDIF
1468+ F067                      ; centre -> corner (x0,z0), still in yaw space
1469+ F067 DD 2A 6E FB          ld ix,(bld_vecs)
1470+ F06B DD 6E 0C             ld l,(ix+12)
1471+ F06E DD 66 0D             ld h,(ix+13)
1472+ F071 ED 5B 7E FB          ld de,(t_x)
1473+ F075 19                   add hl,de
1474+ F076 22 00 E0             ld (vtx+0),hl
1475+ F079 DD 6E 0E             ld l,(ix+14)
1476+ F07C DD 66 0F             ld h,(ix+15)
1477+ F07F ED 5B 7C FB          ld de,(t_z1)
1478+ F083 19                   add hl,de
1479+ F084 22 7C FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1480+ F087 CD 37 F4             call rot_pitch
1481+ F08A 22 02 E0             ld (vtx+2),hl
1482+ F08D ED 53 04 E0          ld (vtx+4),de
1483+ F091 2A 6E FB             ld hl,(bld_vecs)
1484+ F094 11 55 FB             ld de,cur_vx
1485+ F097 01 0C 00             ld bc,12
1486+ F09A ED B0                ldir                            ; cur_vx, cur_vz
1487+ F09C
1488+ F09C                      ; vertical edge vector (0, h cosP, h sinP)

1507+ F19D                      ; Back face culling is exact in world space for an axis aligned box.
1508+ F19D                      ; C = visible faces: bit0 -X, bit1 +X, bit2 -Z, bit3 +Z, bit4 top

1551+ F1FA 28 0F                jr z,.w2

1553+ F1FF 3C                   inc a
1554+ F200 47                   ld b,a
1555+ F201 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)

1559+ F20B CB 51        .w2:    bit 2,c
1560+ F20D 28 0F                jr z,.w3

1562+ F212 47                   ld b,a
1563+ F213 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1564+ F216 3D                   dec a
1565+ F217 CD DD F3             call nb_cover

1567+ F21C CB 91                res 2,c
1568+ F21E CB 59        .w3:    bit 3,c
1569+ F220 28 0F                jr z,.w4

1571+ F225 47                   ld b,a
1572+ F226 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)

1577+ F231 79           .w4:    ld a,c
1578+ F232 32 D0 FB             ld (bld_vis),a
1579+ F235 B7                   or a
1580+ F236 C8                   ret z                           ; nothing of it can be seen

1585+ F240 32 86 FB             ld (bld_used0),a
1586+ F243
1587+ F243                      ; outcodes, trivial reject, project the on-screen corners.  Each
1588+ F243                      ; bottom corner v (0-3) is done with the top corner above it (v+4).
1589+ F243 3E FF                ld a,$FF
1590+ F245 32 98 FB             ld (and_oc),a
1591+ F248 DD 21 00 E0          ld ix,vtx
1592+ F24C 06 04                ld b,4
1593+ F24E C5           .ocl:   push bc
1594+ F24F 3E FF                ld a,$FF

1596+ F254 3A 84 FB             ld a,(bld_used)
1597+ F257 0F                   rrca
1598+ F258 32 84 FB             ld (bld_used),a
1599+ F25B 30 0A                jr nc,.top                      ; hidden corner: not needed
1600+ F25D CD BE F3             call vtx_oc
1601+ F260 32 99 FB             ld (bot_oc),a
1602+ F263 B7                   or a
1603+ F264 CC D0 F3             call z,vtx_proj
1604+ F267 11 40 00     .top:   ld de,64
1605+ F26A DD 19                add ix,de

1607+ F26F CB 5F                bit 3,a                         ; this corner's top (bit v+4)
1608+ F271 28 23                jr z,.nt
1609+ F273 CD BE F3             call vtx_oc
1610+ F276 B7                   or a
1611+ F277 20 1D                jr nz,.nt
1612+ F279                  IFDEF LEVEL_FAST_PATH

1614+ F27C 21 99 FB             ld hl,bot_oc
1615+ F27F B6                   or (hl)
1616+ F280 20 11                jr nz,.full                     ; pitched, or bottom not projected
1617+ F282                      ; Level flight: the top corner has the bottom corner's X and Z, so
1618+ F282                      ; it shares its screen x and its 1/Z (still patched into
1619+ F282                      ; scale_comp); only the screen y is new.
1620+ F282 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
1621+ F285 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)

1624+ F28E DD 71 08             ld (ix+8),c                     ; sy
1625+ F291 18 03                jr .nt
1626+ F293              .full:
1627+ F293                  ENDIF

1630+ F299 DD 19                add ix,de

1632+ F29C 10 B0                djnz .ocl

1634+ F2A1 B7                   or a

1636+ F2A3
1637+ F2A3                      ; Shading: the roof is lighter; of the visible sides, the one the
1638+ F2A3                      ; camera sees most squarely (furthest from its plane) is the base
1639+ F2A3                      ; colour and the other is darker.
1640+ F2A3 3A 6C FB             ld a,(bld_col)

1653+ F2C4                      ; both an X and a Z side: compare camera distances to the planes
1654+ F2C4 2A 70 FB             ld hl,(bld_x0)

1658+ F2CE 3A D0 FB             ld a,(bld_vis)
1659+ F2D1 1F                   rra
1660+ F2D2 38 0A                jr c,.dx
1661+ F2D4 2A B9 A7             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
1662+ F2D7 ED 5B 72 FB          ld de,(bld_x1)
1663+ F2DB B7                   or a
1664+ F2DC ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; camX - x1

1666+ F2DF 2A 74 FB             ld hl,(bld_z0)

1675+ F2F7 B7                   or a
1676+ F2F8 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; camZ - z1
1677+ F2FA D1           .dz:    pop de                          ; DE = X distance, HL = Z distance
1678+ F2FB B7                   or a
1679+ F2FC ED 52                sbc hl,de
1680+ F2FE 3A 6C FB             ld a,(bld_col)                  ; SHADE_DARK = 0
1681+ F301 38 05                jr c,.zdark                     ; Z side is further round: darker
1682+ F303 32 D3 FB             ld (col_x),a

1684+ F308 32 D4 FB     .zdark: ld (col_z),a
1685+ F30B              .shd:
1686+ F30B                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1687+ F30B 3A 6B FB             ld a,(bld_wall)
1688+ F30E B7                   or a
1689+ F30F 28 0B                jr z,.nws
1690+ F311 3A 6C FB             ld a,(bld_col)                  ; wall: every side in the base colour,
1691+ F314 F6 01                or SHADE_FACE                   ; to match the wall-coloured sky
1692+ F316 32 D3 FB             ld (col_x),a
1693+ F319 32 D4 FB             ld (col_z),a
1694+ F31C              .nws:
1695+ F31C                  ENDIF

1697+ F31F 4F                   ld c,a
1698+ F320                      ; An edge shared with a face drawn later is left to that face:
1699+ F320                      ; X faces skip their top edge if the roof shows and the vertical
1700+ F320                      ; edge shared with a visible Z face; Z faces skip the top edge.

1702+ F322 CB 61                bit 4,c
1703+ F324 28 02                jr z,.m1
1704+ F326 CB 90                res 2,b

1718+ F340 C2 B2 F8             jp nz,occ_record                ; level flight: draw it later
1719+ F343                  ENDIF
1720+ F343
1721+ F343              ; Draw the visible faces of the building whose corners are at vtx_hi:00,
1722+ F343              ; from bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z and col_top
1723+ F343              draw_faces:

1725+ F346 CB 47                bit 0,a

1769+ F3BE
1770+ F3BE              ; IX -> vertex: outcode into (ix+6), ANDed into and_oc.  A = outcode.
1771+ F3BE              vtx_oc:
1772+ F3BE DD E5                push ix
1773+ F3C0 E1                   pop hl
1774+ F3C1 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc

1776+ F3C7 21 98 FB             ld hl,and_oc
1777+ F3CA A6                   and (hl)
1778+ F3CB 77                   ld (hl),a
1779+ F3CC DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
1780+ F3CF C9                   ret
1781+ F3D0
1782+ F3D0              ; IX -> vertex inside the frustum: project it into (ix+7) sx, (ix+8) sy
1783+ F3D0              vtx_proj:
1784+ F3D0 DD E5                push ix
1785+ F3D2 E1                   pop hl
1786+ F3D3 CD B0 E7             call project

1788+ F3D9 DD 71 08             ld (ix+8),c
1789+ F3DC C9                   ret
1790+ F3DD
1791+ F3DD              ; B = i, A = j of a neighbouring cell (may be off the map) -> carry set if
1792+ F3DD              ; it holds a building at least as tall as this one (bld_h).  Keeps C.
1793+ F3DD              nb_cover:
1794+ F3DD FE 40                cp GRID
1795+ F3DF D0                   ret nc                          ; off the map (carry clear)
1796+ F3E0 6F                   ld l,a
1797+ F3E1 78                   ld a,b
1798+ F3E2 FE 40                cp GRID

1801+ F3E7                      ROWOFS
1801+ F3E7 29          >        add hl,hl
1801+ F3E8 29          >        add hl,hl
1801+ F3E9 29          >        add hl,hl
1801+ F3EA 29          >        add hl,hl
1801+ F3EB 29          >        add hl,hl
1801+ F3EC 29          >        add hl,hl

1806+ F3F4 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE

1810+ F3FE B7                   or a
1811+ F3FF ED 52                sbc hl,de
1812+ F401 3F                   ccf                             ; carry = neighbour >= this building
1813+ F402 C9                   ret
1814+ F403
1815+ F403              ; Yaw rotation of (t_dx, t_dz):  Z1 = dx sinY + dz cosY -> t_z1
1816+ F403              ;                               X  = dx cosY - dz sinY -> HL
1817+ F403              rot_yaw:
1818+ F403 2A 78 FB             ld hl,(t_dx)
1819+ F406 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1820+ F40A CD 7F A7             call fmul
1821+ F40D E5                   push hl
1822+ F40E 2A 7A FB             ld hl,(t_dz)
1823+ F411 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1824+ F415 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1825+ F418 D1                   pop de
1826+ F419 19                   add hl,de
1827+ F41A 22 7C FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1828+ F41D 2A 7A FB             ld hl,(t_dz)
1829+ F420 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1830+ F424 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1831+ F427 E5                   push hl
1832+ F428 2A 78 FB             ld hl,(t_dx)
1833+ F42B ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1834+ F42F CD 7F A7             call fmul
1835+ F432 D1                   pop de
1836+ F433 B7                   or a
1837+ F434 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1838+ F436 C9                   ret
1839+ F437
1840+ F437              ; Pitch rotation of a ground point at t_z1:
1841+ F437              ;   HL = Y = yb - Z1 sinP     DE = Z = zb + Z1 cosP
1842+ F437              rot_pitch:
1843+ F437 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1844+ F43A ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
1845+ F43E CD 7F A7             call fmul
1846+ F441 EB                   ex de,hl
1847+ F442 2A F2 AD             ld hl,(yb)
1848+ F445 B7                   or a
1849+ F446 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1850+ F448 E5                   push hl
1851+ F449 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1852+ F44C ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
1853+ F450 CD 7F A7             call fmul
1854+ F453 ED 5B F4 AD          ld de,(zb)
1855+ F457 19                   add hl,de
1856+ F458 EB                   ex de,hl
1857+ F459 E1                   pop hl
1858+ F45A C9                   ret
1859+ F45B
1860+ F45B              ;==============================================================================
1861+ F45B              ; Street intersection markers - one dot per grid corner near the camera
1862+ F45B              ;==============================================================================
1863+ F45B              draw_dots:
1864+ F45B 3A 0F AE             ld a,(cz)
1865+ F45E 21 D4 A7             ld hl,dot_r
1866+ F461 96                   sub (hl)
1867+ F462 32 B6 FB             ld (dot_j),a
1868+ F465 3A D4 A7             ld a,(dot_r)
1869+ F468 87                   add a,a
1870+ F469 C6 02                add a,2
1871+ F46B 32 B7 FB             ld (dot_rows),a
1872+ F46E 3A B6 FB     .row:   ld a,(dot_j)
1873+ F471 FE 41                cp GRID+1
1874+ F473 30 5E                jr nc,.nrow
1875+ F475                      ; first corner of the row by full rotation, then step along x:
1876+ F475                      ; +256 world units in x adds exactly (cosY, sinY) to (X, Z1)
1877+ F475 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
1878+ F478 21 D4 A7             ld hl,dot_r
1879+ F47B 96                   sub (hl)
1880+ F47C 32 B5 FB             ld (dot_i),a
1881+ F47F 67                   ld h,a
1882+ F480 2E 00                ld l,0
1883+ F482 ED 5B B9 A7          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1884+ F486 B7                   or a
1885+ F487 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1886+ F489 22 78 FB             ld (t_dx),hl
1887+ F48C 3A B6 FB             ld a,(dot_j)
1888+ F48F 67                   ld h,a
1889+ F490 2E 00                ld l,0
1890+ F492 ED 5B BC A7          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1891+ F496 B7                   or a
1892+ F497 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1893+ F499 22 7A FB             ld (t_dz),hl
1894+ F49C CD 03 F4             call rot_yaw
1895+ F49F 22 7E FB             ld (t_x),hl
1896+ F4A2 3A D4 A7             ld a,(dot_r)
1897+ F4A5 87                   add a,a
1898+ F4A6 C6 02                add a,2
1899+ F4A8 32 B8 FB             ld (dot_cols),a
1900+ F4AB 3A B5 FB     .col:   ld a,(dot_i)
1901+ F4AE FE 41                cp GRID+1
1902+ F4B0 DC DE F4             call c,plot_dot
1903+ F4B3 2A 7E FB             ld hl,(t_x)
1904+ F4B6 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
1905+ F4BA 19                   add hl,de
1906+ F4BB 22 7E FB             ld (t_x),hl
1907+ F4BE 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1908+ F4C1 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
1909+ F4C5 19                   add hl,de
1910+ F4C6 22 7C FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1911+ F4C9 21 B5 FB             ld hl,dot_i
1912+ F4CC 34                   inc (hl)
1913+ F4CD 21 B8 FB             ld hl,dot_cols
1914+ F4D0 35                   dec (hl)
1915+ F4D1 20 D8                jr nz,.col
1916+ F4D3 21 B6 FB     .nrow:  ld hl,dot_j
1917+ F4D6 34                   inc (hl)
1918+ F4D7 21 B7 FB             ld hl,dot_rows
1919+ F4DA 35                   dec (hl)
1920+ F4DB 20 91                jr nz,.row
1921+ F4DD C9                   ret
1922+ F4DE
1923+ F4DE              ; Plot the ground point (t_x, t_z1)
1924+ F4DE              plot_dot:
1925+ F4DE 2A 7E FB             ld hl,(t_x)
1926+ F4E1 22 B9 FB             ld (tmpv+0),hl
1927+ F4E4 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
1928+ F4E7 ED 5B 06 AE          ld de,(cull_c)
1929+ F4EB CD 7F A7             call fmul
1930+ F4EE ED 5B 7C FB          ld de,(t_z1)
1931+ F4F2 B7                   or a
1932+ F4F3 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1933+ F4F5 F0                   ret p                           ; outside horizontal frustum / behind
1934+ F4F6 CD 37 F4             call rot_pitch
1935+ F4F9 22 BB FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
1936+ F4FC ED 53 BD FB          ld (tmpv+4),de
1937+ F500 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1938+ F503 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
1939+ F506 B7                   or a
1940+ F507 C0                   ret nz
1941+ F508 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1942+ F50B CD B0 E7             call project
1943+ F50E 6F                   ld l,a
1944+ F50F 79                   ld a,c
1945+ F510 CD 20 E7             call seg_map
1946+ F513 67                   ld h,a
1947+ F514 36 17                ld (hl),DOT_COL
1948+ F516 C9                   ret
1949+ F517
1950+ F517              ;==============================================================================
1951+ F517              ; Rounds and the laser beam (`bullets`, laser_rec; moved by weapons.asm): a
1952+ F517              ; line along the path each covered this frame, from where it started to
1953+ F517              ; where it is.  A fresh round's path starts at the muzzle, below and behind
1954+ F517              ; the view, so its start is moved along the line to where it enters the
1955+ F517              ; view and pinned to that screen edge: the shot rises from the bottom row.
1956+ F517              ; Drawn over the city; a line whose end is outside the view is skipped.
1957+ F517              ;==============================================================================
1958+ F517              draw_bullets:
1959+ F517 DD 21 76 A8          ld ix,bullets                   ; the rounds, then the laser beam
1960+ F51B 06 19                ld b,NBULLETS+1
1961+ F51D C5           .lp:    push bc
1962+ F51E DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
1963+ F521 B7                   or a
1964+ F522 28 53                jr z,.nx
1965+ F524 DD 7E 1A             ld a,(ix+B_KIND)
1966+ F527 FE 01                cp BK_ROCKET
1967+ F529 38 09                jr c,.rnd                       ; a round (or a Bofors shell)
1968+ F52B FE 03                cp BK_MISSILE+1
1969+ F52D 30 05                jr nc,.rnd
1970+ F52F CD 80 F5             call draw_rocket                ; a rocket or a missile
1971+ F532 18 43                jr .nx
1972+ F534 DD 7E 00     .rnd:   ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
1973+ F537 FE 03                cp 3                            ; 3: laser beam
1974+ F539 3E 1F                ld a,BULLET_COL
1975+ F53B 20 02                jr nz,.col
1976+ F53D 3E 23                ld a,LASER_COL
1977+ F53F 32 AF FC     .col:   ld (line_col),a
1978+ F542 DD E5                push ix                         ; end: where it is now
1979+ F544 E1                   pop hl
1980+ F545 23                   inc hl                          ; -> B_X, B_Y, B_Z
1981+ F546 CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
1982+ F549 20 2C                jr nz,.nx                       ; not in view
1983+ F54B 32 A9 FB             ld (bl_x),a
1984+ F54E 79                   ld a,c
1985+ F54F 32 AA FB             ld (bl_y),a
1986+ F552 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1987+ F555 11 AB FB             ld de,bh_cam
1988+ F558 01 06 00             ld bc,6
1989+ F55B ED B0                ldir
1990+ F55D DD E5                push ix                         ; start of this frame's path
1991+ F55F E1                   pop hl
1992+ F560 11 0F 00             ld de,B_PX
1993+ F563 19                   add hl,de
1994+ F564 CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
1995+ F567 C4 BF F6             call nz,bul_clip                ; outside: where the line enters
1996+ F56A 5F                   ld e,a
1997+ F56B 51                   ld d,c
1998+ F56C 3A A9 FB             ld a,(bl_x)
1999+ F56F 6F                   ld l,a
2000+ F570 3A AA FB             ld a,(bl_y)
2001+ F573 67                   ld h,a
2002+ F574 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2003+ F577 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC
2004+ F57A DD 19                add ix,de
2005+ F57C C1                   pop bc
2006+ F57D 10 9E                djnz .lp
2007+ F57F C9                   ret
2008+ F580
2009+ F580              ; Rocket IX: a white streak ROCKET_LEN (1 m) long from its head back along
2010+ F580              ; its path, then a yellow trail to ROCKET_TRAIL (1.5 m) behind the head.
2011+ F580              ; Drawn only when every point drawn is in view (it's tiny: no clipping).
2012+ F580              draw_rocket:
2013+ F580 DD E5                push ix
2014+ F582 E1                   pop hl
2015+ F583 23                   inc hl                          ; head: B_X, B_Y, B_Z
2016+ F584 CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
2017+ F587 C0                   ret nz                          ; not in view
2018+ F588 32 A9 FB             ld (bl_x),a
2019+ F58B 79                   ld a,c
2020+ F58C 32 AA FB             ld (bl_y),a
2021+ F58F 3E 0D                ld a,ROCKET_LEN                 ; the tail of the streak
2022+ F591 CD DE F5             call rk_back
2023+ F594 CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
2024+ F597 20 2D                jr nz,.head                     ; out of view: the head pixel only
2025+ F599 32 A1 FB             ld (rk_sx),a
2026+ F59C 79                   ld a,c
2027+ F59D 32 A2 FB             ld (rk_sy),a
2028+ F5A0 3E 13                ld a,ROCKET_TRAIL               ; the end of the trail
2029+ F5A2 CD DE F5             call rk_back
2030+ F5A5 CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
2031+ F5A8 20 12                jr nz,.white
2032+ F5AA 5F                   ld e,a                          ; yellow trail first, so the white
2033+ F5AB 51                   ld d,c                          ; streak stays on top
2034+ F5AC 3A A1 FB             ld a,(rk_sx)
2035+ F5AF 6F                   ld l,a
2036+ F5B0 3A A2 FB             ld a,(rk_sy)
2037+ F5B3 67                   ld h,a
2038+ F5B4 3E 1F                ld a,TRAIL_COL
2039+ F5B6 32 AF FC             ld (line_col),a
2040+ F5B9 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2041+ F5BC 3A A1 FB     .white: ld a,(rk_sx)
2042+ F5BF 5F                   ld e,a
2043+ F5C0 3A A2 FB             ld a,(rk_sy)
2044+ F5C3 57                   ld d,a
2045+ F5C4 18 08                jr .line
2046+ F5C6 3A A9 FB     .head:  ld a,(bl_x)
2047+ F5C9 5F                   ld e,a
2048+ F5CA 3A AA FB             ld a,(bl_y)
2049+ F5CD 57                   ld d,a
2050+ F5CE 3A A9 FB     .line:  ld a,(bl_x)
2051+ F5D1 6F                   ld l,a
2052+ F5D2 3A AA FB             ld a,(bl_y)
2053+ F5D5 67                   ld h,a
2054+ F5D6 3E 0B                ld a,ROCKET_COL
2055+ F5D8 32 AF FC             ld (line_col),a
2056+ F5DB C3 D3 ED             jp draw_line
2057+ F5DE
2058+ F5DE              ; A = distance d (units) -> HL -> rk_pt = rocket IX's head - d * (sin, slope,
2059+ F5DE              ; cos): d back along its path
2060+ F5DE              rk_back:
2061+ F5DE 32 A0 FB             ld (rk_d),a
2062+ F5E1 DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)
2063+ F5E4 DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+B_SIN+1)
2064+ F5E7 01 01 00             ld bc,B_X
2065+ F5EA CD 12 F6             call .ax
2066+ F5ED 22 9A FB             ld (rk_pt),hl
2067+ F5F0 DD 5E 0B             ld e,(ix+B_SLOPE)
2068+ F5F3 DD 56 0C             ld d,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
2069+ F5F6 01 03 00             ld bc,B_Y
2070+ F5F9 CD 12 F6             call .ax
2071+ F5FC 22 9C FB             ld (rk_pt+2),hl
2072+ F5FF DD 5E 09             ld e,(ix+B_COS)
2073+ F602 DD 56 0A             ld d,(ix+B_COS+1)
2074+ F605 01 05 00             ld bc,B_Z
2075+ F608 CD 12 F6             call .ax
2076+ F60B 22 9E FB             ld (rk_pt+4),hl
2077+ F60E 21 9A FB             ld hl,rk_pt
2078+ F611 C9                   ret
2079+ F612              ; DE = S7.8 component, BC = offset of the coordinate -> HL = coord - d * DE
2080+ F612 C5           .ax:    push bc
2081+ F613 3A A0 FB             ld a,(rk_d)
2082+ F616 6F                   ld l,a
2083+ F617 26 00                ld h,0
2084+ F619 CD 7F A7             call fmul
2085+ F61C EB                   ex de,hl
2086+ F61D DD E5                push ix
2087+ F61F E1                   pop hl
2088+ F620 C1                   pop bc
2089+ F621 09                   add hl,bc
2090+ F622 7E                   ld a,(hl)
2091+ F623 23                   inc hl
2092+ F624 66                   ld h,(hl)
2093+ F625 6F                   ld l,a
2094+ F626 B7                   or a
2095+ F627 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2096+ F629 C9                   ret
2097+ F62A
2098+ F62A              ;==============================================================================
2099+ F62A              ; Zombie burst particles: one pixel each, coloured by age (PART_COL0 + 4 per
2100+ F62A              ; frame of age, bright to dark green), drawn over the city
2101+ F62A              ;==============================================================================
2102+ F62A              draw_particles:
2103+ F62A DD 21 19 AB          ld ix,particles
2104+ F62E 06 3C                ld b,NPART
2105+ F630 C5           .lp:    push bc
2106+ F631 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
2107+ F634 B7                   or a
2108+ F635 28 19                jr z,.nx
2109+ F637 DD E5                push ix
2110+ F639 E1                   pop hl
2111+ F63A 23                   inc hl                          ; -> x, y, z
2112+ F63B CD 59 F6             call bul_cam
2113+ F63E 20 10                jr nz,.nx                       ; not in view
2114+ F640 6F                   ld l,a                          ; x
2115+ F641 79                   ld a,c
2116+ F642 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; row's section -> A = y & 31
2117+ F645 67                   ld h,a
2118+ F646 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; colour: its age-1 colour (+10)
2119+ F649 3D                   dec a                           ; + (age-1)*4
2120+ F64A 87                   add a,a
2121+ F64B 87                   add a,a
2122+ F64C DD 86 0A             add a,(ix+10)
2123+ F64F 77                   ld (hl),a
2124+ F650 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
2125+ F653 DD 19                add ix,de
2126+ F655 C1                   pop bc
2127+ F656 10 D8                djnz .lp
2128+ F658 C9                   ret
2129+ F659
2130+ F659              ; HL -> world x, y, z: camera space into tmpv; A = outcode, Z set if in view
2131+ F659              ; (then A = screen x, C = screen y)
2132+ F659              bul_cam:
2133+ F659 11 A3 FB             ld de,bp_x
2134+ F65C 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2135+ F65F ED B0                ldir
2136+ F661 2A A3 FB             ld hl,(bp_x)
2137+ F664 ED 5B B9 A7          ld de,(cam_x+1)
2138+ F668 B7                   or a
2139+ F669 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2140+ F66B 22 78 FB             ld (t_dx),hl
2141+ F66E 2A A7 FB             ld hl,(bp_z)
2142+ F671 ED 5B BC A7          ld de,(cam_z+1)
2143+ F675 B7                   or a
2144+ F676 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2145+ F678 22 7A FB             ld (t_dz),hl
2146+ F67B CD 03 F4             call rot_yaw
2147+ F67E 22 B9 FB             ld (tmpv+0),hl
2148+ F681 CD 37 F4             call rot_pitch                  ; at ground level
2149+ F684 22 BB FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2150+ F687 ED 53 BD FB          ld (tmpv+4),de
2151+ F68B 2A A5 FB             ld hl,(bp_y)                    ; + height * (cosP, sinP)
2152+ F68E ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
2153+ F692 CD 7F A7             call fmul
2154+ F695 ED 5B BB FB          ld de,(tmpv+2)
2155+ F699 19                   add hl,de
2156+ F69A 22 BB FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2157+ F69D 2A A5 FB             ld hl,(bp_y)
2158+ F6A0 ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
2159+ F6A4 CD 7F A7             call fmul
2160+ F6A7 ED 5B BD FB          ld de,(tmpv+4)
2161+ F6AB 19                   add hl,de
2162+ F6AC 22 BD FB             ld (tmpv+4),hl
2163+ F6AF 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2164+ F6B2 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
2165+ F6B5 B7                   or a
2166+ F6B6 C0                   ret nz
2167+ F6B7 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2168+ F6BA CD B0 E7             call project
2169+ F6BD BF                   cp a                            ; Z set
2170+ F6BE C9                   ret
2171+ F6BF
2172+ F6BF              ; tmpv = the start, outside the planes in A; bh_cam = the end, in view.
2173+ F6BF              ; -> A = screen x, C = screen y where the line enters the view, pinned to
2174+ F6BF              ; the edge it crosses.
2175+ F6BF              bul_clip:
2176+ F6BF DD E5                push ix
2177+ F6C1 32 B1 FB             ld (bc_oc),a
2178+ F6C4 21 00 00             ld hl,0
2179+ F6C7 22 B2 FB             ld (bc_t),hl
2180+ F6CA 0E 01                ld c,1                          ; each plane the start is outside
2181+ F6CC 3A B1 FB     .pl:    ld a,(bc_oc)
2182+ F6CF A1                   and c
2183+ F6D0 28 34                jr z,.np
2184+ F6D2 79                   ld a,c
2185+ F6D3 32 D9 FB             ld (clip_plane),a
2186+ F6D6 C5                   push bc
2187+ F6D7 DD 21 B9 FB          ld ix,tmpv
2188+ F6DB CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; start: < 0
2189+ F6DE E5                   push hl
2190+ F6DF DD 21 AB FB          ld ix,bh_cam
2191+ F6E3 CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; end: >= 0
2192+ F6E6 D1                   pop de
2193+ F6E7 B7                   or a
2194+ F6E8 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; fe - fs
2195+ F6EA EB                   ex de,hl
2196+ F6EB CD 73 A7             call neg_hl                     ; -fs
2197+ F6EE CD 83 E7             call div_frac                   ; t = -fs / (fe - fs)
2198+ F6F1 DD E5                push ix
2199+ F6F3 E1                   pop hl
2200+ F6F4 ED 5B B2 FB          ld de,(bc_t)                    ; the latest crossing counts
2201+ F6F8 B7                   or a
2202+ F6F9 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2203+ F6FB C1                   pop bc
2204+ F6FC 38 08                jr c,.np
2205+ F6FE 19                   add hl,de
2206+ F6FF 22 B2 FB             ld (bc_t),hl
2207+ F702 79                   ld a,c
2208+ F703 32 B4 FB             ld (bc_pl),a
2209+ F706 CB 21        .np:    sla c
2210+ F708 79                   ld a,c
2211+ F709 FE 20                cp 32
2212+ F70B 20 BF                jr nz,.pl
2213+ F70D DD 21 B9 FB          ld ix,tmpv                      ; start += t * (end - start)
2214+ F711 06 03                ld b,3
2215+ F713 C5           .lc:    push bc
2216+ F714 DD 6E F2             ld l,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv)           ; end - start
2217+ F717 DD 66 F3             ld h,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv+1)
2218+ F71A DD 5E 00             ld e,(ix+0)
2219+ F71D DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
2220+ F720 B7                   or a
2221+ F721 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2222+ F723 D5                   push de
2223+ F724 ED 5B B2 FB          ld de,(bc_t)
2224+ F728 CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
2225+ F72B D1                   pop de
2226+ F72C 19                   add hl,de
2227+ F72D DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
2228+ F730 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
2229+ F733 DD 23                inc ix
2230+ F735 DD 23                inc ix
2231+ F737 C1                   pop bc
2232+ F738 10 D9                djnz .lc
2233+ F73A DD E1                pop ix
2234+ F73C 21 B9 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2235+ F73F CD B0 E7             call project
2236+ F742 5F                   ld e,a
2237+ F743 3A B4 FB             ld a,(bc_pl)                    ; pin to the edge it crossed
2238+ F746 FE 10                cp 16
2239+ F748 20 02                jr nz,.t
2240+ F74A 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
2241+ F74C FE 08        .t:     cp 8
2242+ F74E 20 02                jr nz,.l
2243+ F750 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
2244+ F752 FE 02        .l:     cp 2
2245+ F754 20 02                jr nz,.r
2246+ F756 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
2247+ F758 FE 04        .r:     cp 4
2248+ F75A 20 02                jr nz,.d
2249+ F75C 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
2250+ F75E 7B           .d:     ld a,e
2251+ F75F C9                   ret
2252+ F760
2253+ F760              ;==============================================================================
2254+ F760              ; Gunsight in the centre of the Layer 2 viewport
2255+ F760              ;==============================================================================
2256+ F760              draw_gunsight:
