2265+ F771 3A 3B A8             ld a,(sight_y)
2266+ F774 67                   ld h,a
2267+ F775 57                   ld d,a
2268+ F776 2E 83                ld l,131
2269+ F778 1E 86                ld e,134
2270+ F77A CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2271+ F77D 3A 3B A8             ld a,(sight_y)
2272+ F780 D6 06                sub 6
2273+ F782 67                   ld h,a
2274+ F783 C6 03                add a,3
2275+ F785 57                   ld d,a
2276+ F786 2E 80                ld l,128
2277+ F788 5D                   ld e,l
2278+ F789 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2279+ F78C 3A 3B A8             ld a,(sight_y)
2280+ F78F C6 03                add a,3
2281+ F791 67                   ld h,a
2282+ F792 C6 03                add a,3
2283+ F794 57                   ld d,a
2284+ F795 2E 80                ld l,128
2285+ F797 5D                   ld e,l
2286+ F798 C3 D3 ED             jp draw_line
2287+ F79B
2288+ F79B                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
2289+ F79B              ;==============================================================================
2290+ F79B              ; Occlusion culling (OCCLUSION_CULL, level flight only)
2291+ F79B              ;
2292+ F79B              ; In level flight every screen column is a vertical slice of the world through
2293+ F79B              ; the camera.  The cells are walked in exactly the reverse of the painter's
2294+ F79B              ; order, i.e. front to back.  Each building is transformed as usual, then
2295+ F79B              ; tested against OCC_YBUF, which holds per column the row from which the
2296+ F79B              ; screen is known to be covered down to the ground:
2297+ F79B              ;   * hidden - in every column it spans, its highest point is no higher than
2298+ F79B              ;     that row - it is dropped;
2299+ F79B              ;   * otherwise it lowers the row across its columns to its lowest top
2300+ F79B              ;     corner, and its corners and face settings are kept in a record.
2301+ F79B              ; The kept buildings and the zombie cells are then drawn back to front.
2302+ F79B              ; This is safe because buildings that share a column meet the walk front to
2303+ F79B              ; back (the grid order guarantees it), the camera is above the ground (what
2304+ F79B              ; is behind sits higher on screen), the cover row is the lowest top corner
2305+ F79B              ; (an underestimate) and bounds are clamped inwards to the screen.  A
2306+ F79B              ; building with a corner behind the camera is never dropped and never covers.
2307+ F79B              ;==============================================================================
2308+ F79B              OCC_YBUF        equ $4000       ; per column: covered from this row down
2309+ F79B              OCC_LIST        equ $4100       ; kept buildings and zombie cells, 2 bytes each
2310+ F79B              OCC_REC0        equ $42         ; first record (high byte); 256 bytes each,
2311+ F79B              OCC_RECN        equ $60         ; up to $5F: 30 records ($6000 on: city_col)
2312+ F79B              ; Record: copy of vtx (bytes 0-8 of each 16), then
2313+ F79B              OR_VIS          equ 128         ; bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z, col_top
2314+ F79B              ; A list entry is a record's high byte (< $60), a cell with humans as its
2315+ F79B              ; city_col address ($6000-$6FFF) or a cell with zombies as its city_map
2316+ F79B              ; address ($7000-$7FFF).
2317+ F79B
2318+ F79B              ; Start of the city: occlusion on only in level flight
2319+ F79B              occ_begin:
2320+ F79B AF                   xor a
2321+ F79C 32 87 FB             ld (occ_on),a
2322+ F79F 32 88 FB             ld (occ_full),a
2323+ F7A2 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
2324+ F7A5 B7                   or a
2325+ F7A6 C0                   ret nz
2326+ F7A7 3C                   inc a
2327+ F7A8 32 87 FB             ld (occ_on),a
2328+ F7AB 21 00 41             ld hl,OCC_LIST
2329+ F7AE 22 89 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2330+ F7B1 3E 42                ld a,OCC_REC0
2331+ F7B3 32 8B FB             ld (occ_rec),a
2332+ F7B6 21 00 40             ld hl,OCC_YBUF                  ; nothing covered yet
2333+ F7B9 36 80                ld (hl),VIEW_H
2334+ F7BB 11 01 40             ld de,OCC_YBUF+1
2335+ F7BE 01 FF 00             ld bc,255
2336+ F7C1 ED B0                ldir
2337+ F7C3 C9                   ret
2338+ F7C4
2339+ F7C4              ; The cells of draw_cells in exactly the reverse order.  Each half row
2340+ F7C4              ; starts from the same cell as draw_row and steps the same way, so every
2341+ F7C4              ; cell gets bit-identical yaw-space coordinates.
2342+ F7C4              occ_cells:
2343+ F7C4 3A 0F AE             ld a,(cz)
2344+ F7C7 32 68 FB             ld (cell_j),a
2345+ F7CA CD F2 F7             call occ_row                    ; camera row first
2346+ F7CD 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2347+ F7D0 47                   ld b,a
2348+ F7D1 C5           .f:     push bc
2349+ F7D2 21 68 FB             ld hl,cell_j
2350+ F7D5 34                   inc (hl)
2351+ F7D6 CD F2 F7             call occ_row                    ; then cz+1 .. cz+R
2352+ F7D9 C1                   pop bc
2353+ F7DA 10 F5                djnz .f
2354+ F7DC 3A 0F AE             ld a,(cz)
2355+ F7DF 32 68 FB             ld (cell_j),a
2356+ F7E2 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2357+ F7E5 47                   ld b,a
2358+ F7E6 C5           .n:     push bc
2359+ F7E7 21 68 FB             ld hl,cell_j
2360+ F7EA 35                   dec (hl)
2361+ F7EB CD F2 F7             call occ_row                    ; then cz-1 .. cz-R
2362+ F7EE C1                   pop bc
2363+ F7EF 10 F5                djnz .n
2364+ F7F1 C9                   ret
2365+ F7F2
2366+ F7F2              occ_row:
2367+ F7F2 3A 68 FB             ld a,(cell_j)
2368+ F7F5 FE 40                cp GRID
2369+ F7F7 D0                   ret nc
2370+ F7F8                      ; right half, as draw_row: start at cx+R, step down to cx
2371+ F7F8 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
2372+ F7FB 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
2373+ F7FE 86                   add a,(hl)
2374+ F7FF 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
2375+ F802 CD 55 EF             call row_start
2376+ F805 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2377+ F808 47                   ld b,a
2378+ F809 CD 75 F8     .d:     call occ_stepm
2379+ F80C 10 FB                djnz .d
2380+ F80E 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
2381+ F811 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
2382+ F814 CD 82 EF             call try_cell                   ; camera column first
2383+ F817 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2384+ F81A 47                   ld b,a
2385+ F81B C5           .r:     push bc
2386+ F81C 21 67 FB             ld hl,cell_i
2387+ F81F 34                   inc (hl)
2388+ F820 CD 5E F8             call occ_stepp
2389+ F823 CD 82 EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx+1 .. cx+R
2390+ F826 C1                   pop bc
2391+ F827 10 F2                djnz .r
2392+ F829                      ; left half, as draw_row: start at cx-R, step up to cx-1
2393+ F829 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
2394+ F82C 21 D3 A7             ld hl,view_r
2395+ F82F 96                   sub (hl)
2396+ F830 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
2397+ F833 CD 55 EF             call row_start
2398+ F836 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2399+ F839 3D                   dec a
2400+ F83A 47                   ld b,a
2401+ F83B CD 5E F8     .u:     call occ_stepp
2402+ F83E 10 FB                djnz .u
2403+ F840 3A 0E AE             ld a,(cx)
2404+ F843 3D                   dec a
2405+ F844 32 67 FB             ld (cell_i),a
2406+ F847 CD 82 EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx-1 .. cx-R
2407+ F84A 3A D3 A7             ld a,(view_r)
2408+ F84D 3D                   dec a
2409+ F84E 47                   ld b,a
2410+ F84F C5           .l:     push bc
2411+ F850 21 67 FB             ld hl,cell_i
2412+ F853 35                   dec (hl)
2413+ F854 CD 75 F8             call occ_stepm
2414+ F857 CD 82 EF             call try_cell
2415+ F85A C1                   pop bc
2416+ F85B 10 F2                djnz .l
2417+ F85D C9                   ret
2418+ F85E
2419+ F85E              ; One cell +x / -x in yaw space: (X, Z1) +/-= (cosY, sinY).  Keeps B.
2420+ F85E              occ_stepp:
2421+ F85E 2A 80 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
2422+ F861 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
2423+ F865 19                   add hl,de
2424+ F866 22 80 FB             ld (row_x),hl
2425+ F869 2A 82 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
2426+ F86C ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
2427+ F870 19                   add hl,de
2428+ F871 22 82 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
2429+ F874 C9                   ret
2430+ F875              occ_stepm:
2431+ F875 2A 80 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
2432+ F878 ED 5B EC AD          ld de,(cos_y)
2433+ F87C B7                   or a
2434+ F87D ED 52                sbc hl,de
2435+ F87F 22 80 FB             ld (row_x),hl
2436+ F882 2A 82 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
2437+ F885 ED 5B EA AD          ld de,(sin_y)
2438+ F889 B7                   or a
2439+ F88A ED 52                sbc hl,de
2440+ F88C 22 82 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
2441+ F88F C9                   ret
2442+ F890
2443+ F890              ; HL -> a city_map byte: list the cell (as its city_col address) if humans
2444+ F890              ; stand there (bit 6)
2445+ F890              occ_human:
2446+ F890 CB 76                bit CELL_HUMAN_B,(hl)
2447+ F892 C8                   ret z
2448+ F893 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map
2449+ F896 19                   add hl,de
2450+ F897 EB                   ex de,hl
2451+ F898 2A 89 FB             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2452+ F89B 73                   ld (hl),e
2453+ F89C 2C                   inc l
2454+ F89D 72                   ld (hl),d
2455+ F89E 2C                   inc l
2456+ F89F 22 89 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2457+ F8A2 C9                   ret
2458+ F8A3
2459+ F8A3              ; HL -> a city_map byte: list the cell if zombies stand there, or special
2460+ F8A3              ; monsters fly over it (bit 7)
2461+ F8A3              occ_zombie:
2462+ F8A3 CB 7E                bit CELL_ZOMBIE_B,(hl)
2463+ F8A5 C8                   ret z
2464+ F8A6 EB                   ex de,hl
2465+ F8A7 2A 89 FB             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2466+ F8AA 73                   ld (hl),e
2467+ F8AB 2C                   inc l
2468+ F8AC 72                   ld (hl),d
2469+ F8AD 2C                   inc l
2470+ F8AE 22 89 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2471+ F8B1 C9                   ret
2472+ F8B2
2473+ F8B2              ; draw_cell tail in level flight: drop the building if it is hidden,
2474+ F8B2              ; otherwise make it a cover and keep it (corners in vtx, face settings)
2475+ F8B2              occ_record:
2476+ F8B2 3A 88 FB             ld a,(occ_full)
2477+ F8B5 B7                   or a
2478+ F8B6 C0                   ret nz
2479+ F8B7 3A 8B FB             ld a,(occ_rec)
2480+ F8BA FE 60                cp OCC_RECN
2481+ F8BC 38 06                jr c,.room
2482+ F8BE 3E 01                ld a,1                          ; out of records: give up this frame
2483+ F8C0 32 88 FB             ld (occ_full),a
2484+ F8C3 C9                   ret
2485+ F8C4 32 8C FB     .room:  ld (occ_cur),a
2486+ F8C7                      ; screen bounds of the corners of the drawn faces
2487+ F8C7 AF                   xor a
2488+ F8C8 32 8F FB             ld (o_xmax),a
2489+ F8CB 32 91 FB             ld (o_tcov),a
2490+ F8CE 3D                   dec a
2491+ F8CF 32 8E FB             ld (o_xmin),a
2492+ F8D2 32 90 FB             ld (o_ymin),a
2493+ F8D5 3A 86 FB             ld a,(bld_used0)
2494+ F8D8 32 8D FB             ld (o_used),a
2495+ F8DB DD 21 00 E0          ld ix,vtx
2496+ F8DF 06 08                ld b,8
2497+ F8E1 C5           .cl:    push bc
2498+ F8E2 3A 8D FB             ld a,(o_used)
2499+ F8E5 0F                   rrca
2500+ F8E6 32 8D FB             ld (o_used),a
2501+ F8E9 30 40                jr nc,.nx                       ; corner not on a drawn face
2502+ F8EB DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2503+ F8EE CB 47                bit 0,a
2504+ F8F0 20 47                jr nz,.keep                     ; behind the near plane: no bounds
2505+ F8F2 B7                   or a
2506+ F8F3 C4 D0 F3             call nz,vtx_proj                ; off screen: clamped projection
2507+ F8F6 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; sx
2508+ F8F9 21 8E FB             ld hl,o_xmin
2509+ F8FC BE                   cp (hl)
2510+ F8FD 30 01                jr nc,.x1
2511+ F8FF 77                   ld (hl),a
2512+ F900 21 8F FB     .x1:    ld hl,o_xmax
2513+ F903 BE                   cp (hl)
2514+ F904 38 01                jr c,.x2
2515+ F906 77                   ld (hl),a
2516+ F907 DD 7E 08     .x2:    ld a,(ix+8)                     ; sy
2517+ F90A 21 90 FB             ld hl,o_ymin
2518+ F90D BE                   cp (hl)
2519+ F90E 30 01                jr nc,.y1
2520+ F910 77                   ld (hl),a
2521+ F911 C1           .y1:    pop bc
2522+ F912 C5                   push bc
2523+ F913 78                   ld a,b
2524+ F914 FE 05                cp 5
2525+ F916 30 13                jr nc,.nx                       ; corners 0-3 are the bottom ones
2526+ F918 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2527+ F91B E6 10                and 16
2528+ F91D 3E 80                ld a,VIEW_H                     ; top corner below the screen:
2529+ F91F 20 03                jr nz,.tc                       ; covers nothing
2530+ F921 DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)
2531+ F924 21 91 FB     .tc:    ld hl,o_tcov
2532+ F927 BE                   cp (hl)
2533+ F928 38 01                jr c,.nx
2534+ F92A 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; lowest top corner
2535+ F92B 11 10 00     .nx:    ld de,16
2536+ F92E DD 19                add ix,de
2537+ F930 C1                   pop bc
2538+ F931 10 AE                djnz .cl
2539+ F933 CD CD FA             call occ_test
2540+ F936 D8                   ret c                           ; hidden: dropped
2541+ F937 18 01                jr .store
2542+ F939 C1           .keep:  pop bc                          ; no bounds: keep, but no cover
2543+ F93A 3A 8C FB     .store: ld a,(occ_cur)
2544+ F93D 57                   ld d,a
2545+ F93E 1E 00                ld e,0
2546+ F940 21 00 E0             ld hl,vtx
2547+ F943                      ; corners: only bytes 0-8 of each 16 are used (X,Y,Z,oc,sx,sy);
2548+ F943                      ; vtx and the record are both page aligned, so L = E throughout
2549+ F943                      REPT 8
2550+ F943 ED A0       >        ldi
2551+ F945 ED A0       >        ldi
2552+ F947 ED A0       >        ldi
2553+ F949 ED A0       >        ldi
2554+ F94B ED A0       >        ldi
2555+ F94D ED A0       >        ldi

2575+ FA13 21 8B FB             ld hl,occ_rec
2576+ FA16 34                   inc (hl)
2577+ FA17 C9                   ret
2578+ FA18
2579+ FA18              ; Before the transform: rough screen bounds of the building from its cell
2580+ FA18              ; centre (t_x, t_z1; at pitch 0 camera Z = Z1), a circle of radius OCC_R
2581+ FA18              ; around it and its height, each widened by a pixel for rounding.
2582+ FA18              ; Carry set if those bounds are already hidden.
2583+ FA18              OCC_R           equ 188             ; half the cell diagonal (181) + rounding
2584+ FA18              occ_pretest:
2585+ FA18 3A 88 FB             ld a,(occ_full)
2586+ FA1B B7                   or a
2587+ FA1C C0                   ret nz                          ; not keeping any more: no point
2588+ FA1D 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
2589+ FA20 11 34 FF             ld de,-OCC_R-NEAR
2590+ FA23 19                   add hl,de
2591+ FA24 CB 7C                bit 7,h
2592+ FA26 C2 AE FA             jp nz,.no                       ; may reach the near plane
2593+ FA29 2A 7C FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
2594+ FA2C 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2595+ FA2F 19                   add hl,de
2596+ FA30 22 92 FB             ld (o_zmin),hl
2597+ FA33 11 78 01             ld de,2*OCC_R
2598+ FA36 19                   add hl,de
2599+ FA37 22 94 FB             ld (o_zmax),hl
2600+ FA3A                      ; left edge: X-R, over the far Z if positive, the near Z if negative
2601+ FA3A 2A 7E FB             ld hl,(t_x)
2602+ FA3D 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2603+ FA40 19                   add hl,de
2604+ FA41 ED 5B 94 FB          ld de,(o_zmax)
2605+ FA45 CB 7C                bit 7,h
2606+ FA47 28 04                jr z,.l1
2607+ FA49 ED 5B 92 FB          ld de,(o_zmin)
2608+ FA4D CD B0 FA     .l1:    call occ_sx
2609+ FA50 B7                   or a
2610+ FA51 28 01                jr z,.l2
2611+ FA53 3D                   dec a
2612+ FA54 32 8E FB     .l2:    ld (o_xmin),a
2613+ FA57                      ; right edge: X+R, over the near Z if positive, the far Z if negative
2614+ FA57 2A 7E FB             ld hl,(t_x)
2615+ FA5A 11 BC 00             ld de,OCC_R
2616+ FA5D 19                   add hl,de
2617+ FA5E ED 5B 92 FB          ld de,(o_zmin)
2618+ FA62 CB 7C                bit 7,h
2619+ FA64 28 04                jr z,.r1
2620+ FA66 ED 5B 94 FB          ld de,(o_zmax)
2621+ FA6A CD B0 FA     .r1:    call occ_sx
2622+ FA6D 3C                   inc a
2623+ FA6E 20 01                jr nz,.r2
2624+ FA70 3D                   dec a
2625+ FA71 32 8F FB     .r2:    ld (o_xmax),a
2626+ FA74                      ; top: height above the eye over the near Z, or below it over the far Z
2627+ FA74 2A 69 FB             ld hl,(bld_h)
2628+ FA77 ED 5B BE A7          ld de,(cam_y)
2629+ FA7B B7                   or a
2630+ FA7C ED 52                sbc hl,de
2631+ FA7E 38 14                jr c,.below
2632+ FA80 E5                   push hl                         ; (zpatch uses DE)
2633+ FA81 2A 92 FB             ld hl,(o_zmin)
2634+ FA84 CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2635+ FA87 E1                   pop hl
2636+ FA88 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (h - camY) / Zmin
2637+ FA8B 4F                   ld c,a
2638+ FA8C 3E 3F                ld a,VIEW_CY-1
2639+ FA8E 91                   sub c
2640+ FA8F 30 17                jr nc,.ty
2641+ FA91 AF                   xor a
2642+ FA92 18 14                jr .ty
2643+ FA94 CD 73 A7     .below: call neg_hl                     ; camY - h
2644+ FA97 E5                   push hl
2645+ FA98 2A 94 FB             ld hl,(o_zmax)
2646+ FA9B CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2647+ FA9E E1                   pop hl
2648+ FA9F CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (camY - h) / Zmax
2649+ FAA2 C6 3F                add a,VIEW_CY-1
2650+ FAA4 30 02                jr nc,.ty
2651+ FAA6 3E FF                ld a,255
2652+ FAA8 32 90 FB     .ty:    ld (o_ymin),a
2653+ FAAB C3 E4 FA             jp occ_hidden
2654+ FAAE B7           .no:    or a
2655+ FAAF C9                   ret
2656+ FAB0
2657+ FAB0              ; HL = X, DE = Z -> A = screen x, clamped to 0..255
2658+ FAB0              occ_sx:

2660+ FAB1 F5                   push af
2661+ FAB2 E5                   push hl
2662+ FAB3 EB                   ex de,hl
2663+ FAB4 CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2664+ FAB7 E1                   pop hl
2665+ FAB8 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp

2667+ FABC F1                   pop af

2670+ FAC0 3E 80                ld a,128
2671+ FAC2 81                   add a,c
2672+ FAC3 D0                   ret nc
2673+ FAC4 3E FF                ld a,255
2674+ FAC6 C9                   ret

2677+ FACA D0                   ret nc
2678+ FACB AF                   xor a
2679+ FACC C9                   ret
2680+ FACD

2682+ FACD              ; in every column; otherwise lower the cover to o_tcov over those columns.

2690+ FAD9 6B                   ld l,e

2699+ FAE4
2700+ FAE4              ; Carry set if o_xmin..o_xmax are all covered from o_ymin or higher.
2701+ FAE4              ; Leaves E = o_xmin, B = column count, H = high OCC_YBUF.

 123+ E0A3 10 FB                djnz .dl
 124+ E0A5
 125+ E0A5 3E 0C                ld a,GLYPH_S                    ; gauge labels: S (speed) and
 126+ E0A7 11 E1 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_LBL+SPD_GCOL      ; A (altitude | ground)
 127+ E0AA CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 128+ E0AD 3E 0F                ld a,GLYPH_A
 129+ E0AF 11 E3 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_LBL+ALT_GCOL
 130+ E0B2 CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 131+ E0B5 21 21 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + 17*32 + SPD_GCOL  ; the gauges' cells: transparent,
 132+ E0B8 06 06                ld b,GAUGE_H/8                  ; showing the Layer 2 bars
 133+ E0BA 36 00        .ga:    ld (hl),0
 134+ E0BC 3E 02                ld a,ALT_GCOL-SPD_GCOL
 135+ E0BE ED 31                add hl,a
 136+ E0C0 36 00                ld (hl),0
 137+ E0C2 3E 1E                ld a,32-(ALT_GCOL-SPD_GCOL)
 138+ E0C4 ED 31                add hl,a
 139+ E0C6 10 F2                djnz .ga
 140+ E0C8 21 A4 E2             ld hl,lbl_int                   ; INTEGRITY (the percentage: drawn
 141+ E0CB 11 AB 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_INT+INT_COL       ; by draw_integrity)
 142+ E0CE 06 09                ld b,9
 143+ E0D0 CD D4 E1             call print_glyphs
 144+ E0D3 3E FF                ld a,$FF
 145+ E0D5 32 9F E2             ld (int_shown),a                ; (drawn next frame)
 146+ E0D8 CD 35 3A             call map_init
 147+ E0DB                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 148+ E0DB ~                    jp fps_draw                     ; initial " 0 FPS"
 149+ E0DB                  ELSE
 150+ E0DB C9                   ret
 151+ E0DC                  ENDIF
 152+ E0DC
 153+ E0DC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 154+ E0DC              ; Weapon slots (placeholders): an 8x16 box for each, 2 character cells on
 155+ E0DC              ; rows WPN_ROW, WPN_ROW+1, at columns WPN_COL0, +2, ... in the order
 156+ E0DC              ; wpn_order (as numbered on keys 1-5: 2 4 1 5 3).  A box is green if its
 157+ E0DC              ; slot holds a weapon, red if it is empty; the selected slot has a white
 158+ E0DC              ; outline.  An empty slot that was asked for flashes amber / red (WFLASH
 159+ E0DC              ; frames each, 3 times, ending red).  Under an occupied slot, on row
 160+ E0DC              ; WPN_ROW+2, the letter of its weapon (wpn_glyph): M, L, C or R.
 161+ E0DC              ; Redrawn every frame.
 162+ E0DC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 163+ E0DC              WPN_ATTR_ON     equ C_OK<<3 | 7         ; paper green, ink white (outline)
 164+ E0DC              WPN_ATTR_OFF    equ C_WARN<<3 | 7       ; paper red
 165+ E0DC              WPN_ATTR_FLASH  equ C_AMBER<<3 | 7      ; paper amber
 166+ E0DC              WPN_BMP         equ ULA_BMP + (WPN_ROW/8)*$800 + (WPN_ROW&7)*32 + WPN_COL0
 167+ E0DC                      ASSERT WPN_ROW/8 == (WPN_ROW+2)/8       ; the 3 rows in one third
 168+ E0DC                      ASSERT WPN_COL0 + 2*(WPN_SLOTS-1) < MAP_COL
 169+ E0DC
 170+ E0DC              draw_wpn:
 171+ E0DC 0E FF                ld c,$FF                        ; the slot flashing amber this frame
 172+ E0DE 3A D1 A7             ld a,(wflash_t)
 173+ E0E1 B7                   or a
 174+ E0E2 28 10                jr z,.nf
 175+ E0E4 3D                   dec a                           ; phase (t-1)/WFLASH: odd = amber
 176+ E0E5 06 FF                ld b,-1
 177+ E0E7 04           .dv:    inc b
 178+ E0E8 D6 05                sub WFLASH
 179+ E0EA 30 FB                jr nc,.dv
 180+ E0EC CB 40                bit 0,b
 181+ E0EE 28 04                jr z,.nf
 182+ E0F0 3A D0 A7             ld a,(wflash_slot)
 183+ E0F3 4F                   ld c,a
 184+ E0F4 79           .nf:    ld a,c
 185+ E0F5 32 AD E2             ld (wd_flash),a
 186+ E0F8 21 D1 A7             ld hl,wflash_t                  ; count the flash down
 187+ E0FB 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 188+ E0FC B7                   or a
 189+ E0FD 28 01                jr z,.go
 190+ E0FF 35                   dec (hl)
 191+ E100 06 00        .go:    ld b,0                          ; box position, left to right
 192+ E102 C5           .lp:    push bc
 193+ E103 78                   ld a,b
 194+ E104 87                   add a,a
 195+ E105 32 AE E2             ld (wd_col),a                   ; its column offset
 196+ E108 21 B0 E2             ld hl,wpn_order
 197+ E10B 78                   ld a,b
 198+ E10C ED 31                add hl,a
 199+ E10E 4E                   ld c,(hl)                       ; C = its slot (0 = the centre gun)
 200+ E10F 79                   ld a,c
 201+ E110 87                   add a,a
 202+ E111 21 C5 A7             ld hl,wpn_slots+WPN_TYPE
 203+ E114 ED 31                add hl,a
 204+ E116 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 205+ E117 32 AF E2             ld (wd_type),a
 206+ E11A 1E 27                ld e,WPN_ATTR_ON                ; fitted: green
 207+ E11C B7                   or a
 208+ E11D 20 0A                jr nz,.pc
 209+ E11F 1E 1F                ld e,WPN_ATTR_OFF               ; empty: red, or amber while flashing
 210+ E121 3A AD E2             ld a,(wd_flash)
 211+ E124 B9                   cp c
 212+ E125 20 02                jr nz,.pc
 213+ E127 1E 37                ld e,WPN_ATTR_FLASH
 214+ E129 3A AE E2     .pc:    ld a,(wd_col)                   ; the box's two cells
 215+ E12C 21 2B 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + WPN_ROW*32 + WPN_COL0
 216+ E12F ED 31                add hl,a
 217+ E131 73                   ld (hl),e
 218+ E132 3E 20                ld a,32
 219+ E134 ED 31                add hl,a
 220+ E136 73                   ld (hl),e
 221+ E137 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)                  ; outline if selected, else clear
 222+ E13A B9                   cp c
 223+ E13B 11 81 FF             ld de,$FF81                     ; D: top / bottom line, E: sides
 224+ E13E 28 03                jr z,.bx
 225+ E140 11 00 00             ld de,0
 226+ E143 3A AE E2     .bx:    ld a,(wd_col)
 227+ E146 21 2B 90             ld hl,WPN_BMP
 228+ E149 ED 31                add hl,a
 229+ E14B E5                   push hl
 230+ E14C 72                   ld (hl),d                       ; upper cell: top line, then sides
 231+ E14D 06 07                ld b,7
 232+ E14F 24           .b1:    inc h
 233+ E150 73                   ld (hl),e
 234+ E151 10 FC                djnz .b1
 235+ E153 E1                   pop hl
 236+ E154 3E 20                ld a,32
 237+ E156 ED 31                add hl,a
 238+ E158 E5                   push hl
 239+ E159 06 07                ld b,7                          ; lower cell: sides, then bottom line
 240+ E15B 73           .b2:    ld (hl),e
 241+ E15C 24                   inc h
 242+ E15D 10 FC                djnz .b2
 243+ E15F 72                   ld (hl),d
 244+ E160 E1                   pop hl
 245+ E161 3E 20                ld a,32                         ; the cell below: its weapon's letter
 246+ E163 ED 31                add hl,a
 247+ E165 EB                   ex de,hl
 248+ E166 3A AF E2             ld a,(wd_type)
 249+ E169 21 B5 E2             ld hl,wpn_glyph
 250+ E16C ED 31                add hl,a
 251+ E16E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 252+ E16F CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 253+ E172 C1                   pop bc
 254+ E173 04                   inc b
 255+ E174 78                   ld a,b
 256+ E175 FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 257+ E177 20 89                jr nz,.lp
 258+ E179 C9                   ret
 259+ E17A
 260+ E17A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 261+ E17A              ; Integrity: the VTOL's hit points as a percentage of VTOL_HP, "nnn%" on row
 262+ E17A              ; INT_ROW+1 under the INTEGRITY label.  VTOL_HP is 50, so the percentage is
 263+ E17A              ; just vtol_hp * 2 (a shift left).  Green, amber below INT_WARN, red below
 264+ E17A              ; INT_DANGER.  Redrawn only when it changes (int_shown).
 265+ E17A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 266+ E17A              draw_integrity:
 267+ E17A 3A D5 A7             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 268+ E17D 87                   add a,a                         ; percent = hp * 2
 269+ E17E 21 9F E2             ld hl,int_shown
 270+ E181 BE                   cp (hl)
 271+ E182 C8                   ret z                           ; unchanged
 272+ E183 77                   ld (hl),a
 273+ E184 0E 0C                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK          ; its colour
 274+ E186 FE 32                cp INT_WARN
 275+ E188 30 08                jr nc,.col
 276+ E18A 0E 0E                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_AMBER
 277+ E18C FE 19                cp INT_DANGER
 278+ E18E 30 02                jr nc,.col
 279+ E190 0E 0B                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_WARN
 280+ E192 21 CC 9A     .col:   ld hl,ULA_ATTR + (INT_ROW+1)*32 + INT_PCT_COL
 281+ E195 06 04                ld b,4
 282+ E197 71           .at:    ld (hl),c
 283+ E198 23                   inc hl
 284+ E199 10 FC                djnz .at
 285+ E19B 21 A0 E2             ld hl,int_txt                   ; the digits, no leading zeros
 286+ E19E 06 64                ld b,100
 287+ E1A0 CD C9 E1             call .dig
 288+ E1A3 06 0A                ld b,10
 289+ E1A5 CD C9 E1             call .dig
 290+ E1A8 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; units
 291+ E1A9 3A A0 E2             ld a,(int_txt)
 292+ E1AC B7                   or a
 293+ E1AD 20 10                jr nz,.pr
 294+ E1AF 3E 0D                ld a,GLYPH_SPACE
 295+ E1B1 32 A0 E2             ld (int_txt),a
 296+ E1B4 3A A1 E2             ld a,(int_txt+1)
 297+ E1B7 B7                   or a
 298+ E1B8 20 05                jr nz,.pr
 299+ E1BA 3E 0D                ld a,GLYPH_SPACE
 300+ E1BC 32 A1 E2             ld (int_txt+1),a
 301+ E1BF 21 A0 E2     .pr:    ld hl,int_txt
 302+ E1C2 11 CC 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_INT+32+INT_PCT_COL
 303+ E1C5 06 04                ld b,4
 304+ E1C7 18 0B                jr print_glyphs
 305+ E1C9 36 00        .dig:   ld (hl),0                       ; A = A mod B, (HL) = A / B
 306+ E1CB B8           .ds:    cp b
 307+ E1CC 38 04                jr c,.dd
 308+ E1CE 90                   sub b
 309+ E1CF 34                   inc (hl)
 310+ E1D0 18 F9                jr .ds
 311+ E1D2 23           .dd:    inc hl
 312+ E1D3 C9                   ret
 313+ E1D4                      ASSERT VTOL_HP == 50            ; (percent = hp * 2)
 314+ E1D4                      ASSERT INT_PCT_COL+3 < MAP_COL && INT_COL+8 < MAP_COL
 315+ E1D4
 316+ E1D4              ; HL -> B glyph codes, DE = first ULA cell: print them along the row
 317+ E1D4              print_glyphs:
 318+ E1D4 C5           .l:     push bc
 319+ E1D5 D5                   push de
 320+ E1D6 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 321+ E1D7 23                   inc hl
 322+ E1D8 E5                   push hl
 323+ E1D9 CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 324+ E1DC E1                   pop hl
 325+ E1DD D1                   pop de
 326+ E1DE 1C                   inc e
 327+ E1DF C1                   pop bc
 328+ E1E0 10 F2                djnz .l
 329+ E1E2 C9                   ret
 330+ E1E3
 331+ E1E3              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 332+ E1E3              ; Layer 2 double buffering with the hardware shadow register.
 333+ E1E3              ;   NextReg $12 = active bank (on screen), NextReg $13 = shadow bank.
 334+ E1E3              ; The game always draws into the shadow bank; l2_flip swaps the two during
 335+ E1E3              ; vertical blank, so the registers themselves record which buffer is which.
 336+ E1E3              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 337+ E1E3
 338+ E1E3              ;==============================================================================
 339+ E1E3              ; HUD: the weapon slots, and the speed, altitude and ground height gauges
 340+ E1E3              ; (the humans and the compass are hardware sprites: hud_sprites.asm).  The
 341+ E1E3              ; gauges are vertical bars on Layer 2 (in the back buffer, every frame)
 342+ E1E3              ; behind transparent ULA cells: the
 343+ E1E3              ; altitude and ground bars share one 8-pixel cell column, which ULA
 344+ E1E3              ; attributes couldn't colour (green, amber and black in one cell).
 345+ E1E3              ;==============================================================================
 346+ E1E3              draw_hud:
 347+ E1E3 CD DC E0             call draw_wpn
 348+ E1E6 CD 7A E1             call draw_integrity
 349+ E1E9                      ; (the humans' states: hardware sprites, hum_hud in human.asm)
 350+ E1E9
 351+ E1E9 CD EF E1             call draw_gauges
 352+ E1EC C3 AE 3A             jp map_player
 353+ E1EF
 354+ E1EF              ; The gauges, GAUGE_H (48) pixels tall:
 355+ E1EF              ;   S  speed, 8 wide: (speed * 55) / 32 pixels, so SPEED_MAX 28 fills it;
 356+ E1EF              ;      green, red when reversing
 357+ E1EF              ;   A  left 4 pixels: altitude, green; right 4: the height of the city tile
 358+ E1EF              ;      under the craft (0 on a road), amber.  Both 1 pixel per 4 units, so
 359+ E1EF              ;      the ceiling (MAX_Y 1.5 height units, 192) fills the bar; the ground
 360+ E1EF              ;      under the craft is always lower, so the gap between them is the
 361+ E1EF              ;      clearance.
 362+ E1EF              draw_gauges:
 363+ E1EF 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 364+ E1F2 0E 0F                ld c,HUD_COL
 365+ E1F4 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 366+ E1F6 28 04                jr z,.sp
 367+ E1F8 ED 44                neg
 368+ E1FA 0E 13                ld c,HUD_WARN
 369+ E1FC 57           .sp:    ld d,a
 370+ E1FD 1E 37                ld e,55
 371+ E1FF ED 30                mul d,e
 372+ E201 06 05                ld b,5
 373+ E203 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 374+ E205 79                   ld a,c
 375+ E206 32 BF E2             ld (g_spdc),a
 376+ E209 7B                   ld a,e
 377+ E20A CD 99 E2             call g_clamp
 378+ E20D 32 BE E2             ld (g_spd),a
 379+ E210 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
 380+ E213 CD 8B E2             call g_px
 381+ E216 32 C0 E2             ld (g_alt),a
 382+ E219 CD DF A4             call cam_cell_ptr
 383+ E21C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 384+ E21D CD 3F A6             call bld_height                 ; HL = height of this tile
 385+ E220 CD 8B E2             call g_px
 386+ E223 32 C1 E2             ld (g_gnd),a
 387+ E226 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; row from the top
 388+ E228 79           .row:   ld a,c
 389+ E229 C6 88                add a,GAUGE_TOP
 390+ E22B 47                   ld b,a
 391+ E22C 07                   rlca
 392+ E22D 07                   rlca
 393+ E22E 07                   rlca
 394+ E22F E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
 395+ E231 5F                   ld e,a
 396+ E232 3A 10 AE             ld a,(seg_base)                 ; (the back buffer)
 397+ E235 83                   add a,e
 398+ E236 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 399+ E239 78                   ld a,b
 400+ E23A E6 1F                and 31
 401+ E23C 67                   ld h,a
 402+ E23D 2E 08                ld l,SPD_GCOL*8
 403+ E23F 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 404+ E241 91                   sub c
 405+ E242 47                   ld b,a                          ; this row's level: GAUGE_H..1
 406+ E243 3A BE E2             ld a,(g_spd)                    ; speed: filled up to its level?
 407+ E246 B8                   cp b
 408+ E247 3A BF E2             ld a,(g_spdc)
 409+ E24A 30 02                jr nc,.s
 410+ E24C 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG                     ; empty: black
 411+ E24E              .s:     REPT 8
 412+ E24E 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E24F 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E250 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E251 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E252 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E253 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E254 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E255 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E256 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E257 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E258 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E259 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E25A 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E25B 2C          >        inc l
 412+ E25C 77          >        ld (hl),a
 413+ E25D 2C          >        inc l
 414+ E25E                      ENDR
 415+ E25E 2E 18                ld l,ALT_GCOL*8
 416+ E260 3A C0 E2             ld a,(g_alt)
 417+ E263 B8                   cp b
 418+ E264 3E 0F                ld a,HUD_COL
 419+ E266 30 02                jr nc,.a
 420+ E268 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG
 421+ E26A              .a:     REPT 4
 422+ E26A 77          >        ld (hl),a
 423+ E26B 2C          >        inc l
 422+ E26C 77          >        ld (hl),a
 423+ E26D 2C          >        inc l
 422+ E26E 77          >        ld (hl),a
 423+ E26F 2C          >        inc l
 422+ E270 77          >        ld (hl),a
 423+ E271 2C          >        inc l
 424+ E272                      ENDR
 425+ E272 3A C1 E2             ld a,(g_gnd)
 426+ E275 B8                   cp b
 427+ E276 3E 5F                ld a,GAUGE_AMBER
 428+ E278 30 02                jr nc,.g
 429+ E27A 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG
 430+ E27C              .g:     REPT 4
 431+ E27C 77          >        ld (hl),a
 432+ E27D 2C          >        inc l
 431+ E27E 77          >        ld (hl),a
 432+ E27F 2C          >        inc l
 431+ E280 77          >        ld (hl),a
 432+ E281 2C          >        inc l
 431+ E282 77          >        ld (hl),a
 432+ E283 2C          >        inc l
 433+ E284                      ENDR
 434+ E284 0C                   inc c
 435+ E285 79                   ld a,c
 436+ E286 FE 30                cp GAUGE_H
 437+ E288 20 9E                jr nz,.row
 438+ E28A C9                   ret
 439+ E28B
 440+ E28B              ; HL = height (world units) -> A = HL / 4, at most GAUGE_H
 441+ E28B              g_px:
 442+ E28B CB 3C                srl h
 443+ E28D CB 1D                rr l
 444+ E28F CB 3C                srl h
 445+ E291 CB 1D                rr l
 446+ E293 7C                   ld a,h
 447+ E294 B7                   or a
 448+ E295 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 449+ E297 C0                   ret nz
 450+ E298 7D                   ld a,l
 451+ E299              g_clamp:
 452+ E299 FE 31                cp GAUGE_H+1
 453+ E29B D8                   ret c
 454+ E29C 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 455+ E29E C9                   ret
 456+ E29F
 457+ E29F              ;==============================================================================
 458+ E29F              ; HUD page data
 459+ E29F              ;==============================================================================
 460+ E29F FF           int_shown:  db $FF                      ; draw_integrity: the percentage shown
 461+ E2A0 00 00 00 1D  int_txt:    db 0, 0, 0, GLYPH_PCT       ;   its glyphs: digits, %
 462+ E2A4 1A 14 11 1B  lbl_int:    db GLYPH_I, GLYPH_N, GLYPH_T, GLYPH_E, GLYPH_G, GLYPH_R, GLYPH_I
 462+ E2A8 12 13 1A
 463+ E2AB 11 1C                    db GLYPH_T, GLYPH_Y         ; INTEGRITY
 464+ E2AD FF           wd_flash:   db $FF                      ; draw_wpn: the slot flashing amber,
 465+ E2AE 00           wd_col:     db 0                        ;   the box's column offset,
 466+ E2AF 00           wd_type:    db 0                        ;   and its slot's weapon
 467+ E2B0              ; the boxes' slots, left to right (keys 2 4 1 5 3)
 468+ E2B0 01 03 00 04  wpn_order:  db 1, 3, 0, 4, 2
 468+ E2B4 02
 469+ E2B5                      ASSERT $ - wpn_order == WPN_SLOTS
 470+ E2B5              ; weapon type (WPN_*) -> its letter under the box
 471+ E2B5 0D           wpn_glyph:  db GLYPH_SPACE              ; WPN_NONE
 472+ E2B6 17                       db GLYPH_M                  ; WPN_GUN: machine gun
 473+ E2B7 10                       db GLYPH_L                  ; WPN_LASER
 474+ E2B8 18                       db GLYPH_C                  ; WPN_CHAIN: chain gun
 475+ E2B9 13                       db GLYPH_R                  ; WPN_ROCKETS: rocket pod
 476+ E2BA 0C                       db GLYPH_S                  ; WPN_MISSILE: missile pod
 477+ E2BB 0D                       db GLYPH_SPACE              ; WPN_BOMB (none yet)
 478+ E2BC 19                       db GLYPH_B                  ; WPN_BOFORS: Bofors cannon
 479+ E2BD 0B                       db GLYPH_P                  ; WPN_PROX: proximity cannon
 480+ E2BE                      ASSERT $ - wpn_glyph == WPN_LAST + 1
 481+ E2BE 00           g_spd:      db 0                        ; draw_gauges: bar heights (pixels)
 482+ E2BF 00           g_spdc:     db 0                        ;   and the speed bar's colour
 483+ E2C0 00           g_alt:      db 0
 484+ E2C1 00           g_gnd:      db 0
 485+ E2C2
 486+ E2C2              ; 8x8 glyphs: digits 0-9, then F, P, S, space
 487+ E2C2              GLYPH_F     equ 10
 488+ E2C2              GLYPH_P     equ 11
 489+ E2C2              GLYPH_S     equ 12
 490+ E2C2              GLYPH_SPACE equ 13
 491+ E2C2              GLYPH_D     equ 14
 492+ E2C2              GLYPH_A     equ 15
 493+ E2C2              GLYPH_L     equ 16
 494+ E2C2              GLYPH_T     equ 17
 495+ E2C2              GLYPH_G     equ 18
 496+ E2C2              GLYPH_R     equ 19
 497+ E2C2              GLYPH_N     equ 20
 498+ E2C2              GLYPH_MAN   equ 21
 499+ E2C2              GLYPH_SEAT  equ 22
 500+ E2C2              GLYPH_M     equ 23
 501+ E2C2              GLYPH_C     equ 24
 502+ E2C2              GLYPH_B     equ 25
 503+ E2C2              GLYPH_I     equ 26
 504+ E2C2              GLYPH_E     equ 27
 505+ E2C2              GLYPH_Y     equ 28
 506+ E2C2              GLYPH_PCT   equ 29
 507+ E2C2              ; ULA bitmap cell addresses (pixel line 0) of the gauge rows
 508+ E2C2              ULA_ROW_LBL equ ULA_BMP + (GAUGE_LROW/8)*$800 + (GAUGE_LROW&7)*32
 509+ E2C2              ULA_ROW_INT equ ULA_BMP + (INT_ROW/8)*$800 + (INT_ROW&7)*32
 510+ E2C2                      ASSERT INT_ROW/8 == (INT_ROW+1)/8       ; (+32: the next row)
 511+ E2C2              font:
 512+ E2C2 00 3C 46 4A          db $00,$3C,$46,$4A,$52,$62,$3C,$00     ; 0
 512+ E2C6 52 62 3C 00
 513+ E2CA 00 18 28 08          db $00,$18,$28,$08,$08,$08,$3E,$00     ; 1
 513+ E2CE 08 08 3E 00
 514+ E2D2 00 3C 42 02          db $00,$3C,$42,$02,$3C,$40,$7E,$00     ; 2
 514+ E2D6 3C 40 7E 00
 515+ E2DA 00 3C 42 0C          db $00,$3C,$42,$0C,$02,$42,$3C,$00     ; 3
 515+ E2DE 02 42 3C 00
 516+ E2E2 00 08 18 28          db $00,$08,$18,$28,$48,$7E,$08,$00     ; 4
 516+ E2E6 48 7E 08 00
 517+ E2EA 00 7E 40 7C          db $00,$7E,$40,$7C,$02,$42,$3C,$00     ; 5
 517+ E2EE 02 42 3C 00
 518+ E2F2 00 3C 40 7C          db $00,$3C,$40,$7C,$42,$42,$3C,$00     ; 6
 518+ E2F6 42 42 3C 00
 519+ E2FA 00 7E 02 04          db $00,$7E,$02,$04,$08,$10,$10,$00     ; 7
 519+ E2FE 08 10 10 00
 520+ E302 00 3C 42 3C          db $00,$3C,$42,$3C,$42,$42,$3C,$00     ; 8
 520+ E306 42 42 3C 00
 521+ E30A 00 3C 42 42          db $00,$3C,$42,$42,$3E,$02,$3C,$00     ; 9
 521+ E30E 3E 02 3C 00

# file opened: keys.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - keyboard (8K page PAGE_KEYS, runs at $E000 in slot 7: main_loop
   3+ E000              ; and the menu page it in, and the game logic pages in slot 6 call it)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Redefinable keys.  A key is a 16-bit code: high byte = key bank, low byte
   6+ E000              ; = bit mask within that bank.
   7+ E000              ;   banks 0-7: the ZX Spectrum keyboard half-rows (port $xxFE, address line
   8+ E000              ;              A8+bank low): read 0 = pressed
   9+ E000              ;   bank 8:    NextReg $B0, Extended Keys 0: read 1 = pressed
  10+ E000              ;   bank 9:    NextReg $B1, Extended Keys 1: read 1 = pressed
  11+ E000              ; kbd_scan, once per frame at the top of the main loop, reads all ten banks
  12+ E000              ; into key_now, inverting the ZX rows so that every bank holds 1 = pressed,
  13+ E000              ; and keeps the previous scan in key_prev.  The game asks about actions
  14+ E000              ; (K_FIRE, K_LEFT, ...), not keys: key_defs holds the key code of each
  15+ E000              ; action, so keys can be redefined (key_define) at any time.
  16+ E000              ;   key_held    A = action -> NZ if its key is down
  17+ E000              ;   key_pressed A = action -> NZ if its key went down since the last scan
  18+ E000              ;   key_find    -> NZ and HL = code of a key that is down (for a redefine
  19+ E000              ;               screen); Z if none
  20+ E000              ;   key_code_pressed  HL = key code -> NZ if that key went down
  21+ E000              ; The Next normally also enters the extended keys into the 8x5 matrix (e.g.
  22+ E000              ; cursor LEFT as CAPS SHIFT + 5).  NextReg $68 bit 4 stops that: set it with
  23+ E000              ; kbd_double_on (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE), clear it with kbd_double_off
  24+ E000              ; (FALSE, the default).
  25+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  26+ E000
  27+ E000              ; --- key codes: bank << 8 | mask ---------------------------------------------
  28+ E000              KB_SHIFT    equ $0001                   ; bank 0: $FEFE
  29+ E000              KB_Z        equ $0002
  30+ E000              KB_X        equ $0004
  31+ E000              KB_C        equ $0008
  32+ E000              KB_V        equ $0010
  33+ E000              KB_A        equ $0101                   ; bank 1: $FDFE
  34+ E000              KB_S        equ $0102
  35+ E000              KB_D        equ $0104
  36+ E000              KB_F        equ $0108
  37+ E000              KB_G        equ $0110
  38+ E000              KB_Q        equ $0201                   ; bank 2: $FBFE
  39+ E000              KB_W        equ $0202
  40+ E000              KB_E        equ $0204
  41+ E000              KB_R        equ $0208
  42+ E000              KB_T        equ $0210
  43+ E000              KB_1        equ $0301                   ; bank 3: $F7FE
  44+ E000              KB_2        equ $0302
  45+ E000              KB_3        equ $0304
  46+ E000              KB_4        equ $0308
  47+ E000              KB_5        equ $0310
  48+ E000              KB_0        equ $0401                   ; bank 4: $EFFE
  49+ E000              KB_9        equ $0402
  50+ E000              KB_8        equ $0404
  51+ E000              KB_7        equ $0408
  52+ E000              KB_6        equ $0410
  53+ E000              KB_P        equ $0501                   ; bank 5: $DFFE
  54+ E000              KB_O        equ $0502
  55+ E000              KB_I        equ $0504
  56+ E000              KB_U        equ $0508
  57+ E000              KB_Y        equ $0510
  58+ E000              KB_ENTER    equ $0601                   ; bank 6: $BFFE
  59+ E000              KB_L        equ $0602
  60+ E000              KB_K        equ $0604
  61+ E000              KB_J        equ $0608
  62+ E000              KB_H        equ $0610
  63+ E000              KB_SPACE    equ $0701                   ; bank 7: $7FFE
  64+ E000              KB_SYM      equ $0702
  65+ E000              KB_M        equ $0704
  66+ E000              KB_N        equ $0708
  67+ E000              KB_B        equ $0710
  68+ E000              KB_RIGHT    equ $0801                   ; bank 8: NextReg $B0
  69+ E000              KB_LEFT     equ $0802
  70+ E000              KB_DOWN     equ $0804
  71+ E000              KB_UP       equ $0808
  72+ E000              KB_PERIOD   equ $0810
  73+ E000              KB_COMMA    equ $0820
  74+ E000              KB_QUOTE    equ $0840
  75+ E000              KB_SEMI     equ $0880
  76+ E000              KB_EXTEND   equ $0901                   ; bank 9: NextReg $B1
  77+ E000              KB_CAPSLOCK equ $0902
  78+ E000              KB_GRAPH    equ $0904
  79+ E000              KB_TRUEVID  equ $0908
  80+ E000              KB_INVVID   equ $0910
  81+ E000              KB_BREAK    equ $0920
  82+ E000              KB_EDIT     equ $0940
  83+ E000              KB_DELETE   equ $0980
  84+ E000              KEY_BANKS   equ 10
  85+ E000              FALSE       equ 0
  86+ E000              TRUE        equ 1
  87+ E000
  88+ E000              ; --- actions: index into key_defs --------------------------------------------
  89+ E000              K_SPEED_UP  equ 0
  90+ E000              K_SPEED_DN  equ 1
  91+ E000              K_AIRBRAKE  equ 2                       ; air brake: rapid stop
  92+ E000              K_LEFT      equ 3
  93+ E000              K_RIGHT     equ 4
  94+ E000              K_CLIMB     equ 5
  95+ E000              K_DESCEND   equ 6
  96+ E000              K_NOSE_UP   equ 7
  97+ E000              K_NOSE_DN   equ 8
  98+ E000              K_FIRE      equ 9
  99+ E000              K_GUN_UP    equ 10                      ; tilt the centre gun up,
 100+ E000              K_SLOT1     equ 11                      ; select weapon slots 1-5
 101+ E000              K_GUN_DN    equ 16                      ; down,
 102+ E000              K_GUN_CTR   equ 17                      ; and back to the centre
 103+ E000              K_ACTIONS   equ 18
 104+ E000
 105+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 106+ E000              ; Read all ten key banks: key_prev = last scan, key_now = this one, every bit
 107+ E000              ; 1 = pressed
 108+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 109+ E000              kbd_scan:
 110+ E000 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 111+ E003 11 CE E0             ld de,key_prev
 112+ E006 01 0A 00             ld bc,KEY_BANKS
 113+ E009 ED B0                ldir
 114+ E00B 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 115+ E00E 01 FE FE             ld bc,$FEFE                     ; banks 0-7: half-rows $FE..$7F
 116+ E011 ED 78        .row:   in a,(c)
 117+ E013 2F                   cpl                             ; 0 = pressed -> 1 = pressed
 118+ E014 E6 1F                and %00011111
 119+ E016 77                   ld (hl),a
 120+ E017 23                   inc hl
 121+ E018 CB 00                rlc b                           ; next half-row
 122+ E01A 38 F5                jr c,.row                       ; (stops after $7F)
 123+ E01C 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B                     ; banks 8, 9: NextRegs $B0, $B1
 124+ E01F 3E B0                ld a,$B0
 125+ E021 ED 79                out (c),a
 126+ E023 04                   inc b
 127+ E024 ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; already 1 = pressed
 128+ E026 77                   ld (hl),a
 129+ E027 23                   inc hl
 130+ E028 05                   dec b
 131+ E029 3E B1                ld a,$B1
 132+ E02B ED 79                out (c),a
 133+ E02D 04                   inc b
 134+ E02E ED 78                in a,(c)
 135+ E030 77                   ld (hl),a
 136+ E031 C9                   ret
 137+ E032
 138+ E032              ; Stop the extended keys also appearing in the 8x5 matrix:
 139+ E032              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 1
 140+ E032              kbd_double_on:
 141+ E032 3E 01                ld a,TRUE
 142+ E034 18 01                jr kbd_double
 143+ E036
 144+ E036              ; Let the extended keys also appear in the 8x5 matrix (the default):
 145+ E036              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = FALSE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 0
 146+ E036              kbd_double_off:
 147+ E036              kdb_double_off:                         ; (alias: the name as first asked for)
 148+ E036 AF                   xor a                           ; FALSE
 149+ E037
 150+ E037              ; A = TRUE / FALSE: store it, and set NextReg $68 bit 4 to match, keeping
 151+ E037              ; the register's other bits
 152+ E037              kbd_double:
 153+ E037 32 C3 E0             ld (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING),a
 154+ E03A 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 155+ E03D 3E 68                ld a,$68
 156+ E03F ED 79                out (c),a
 157+ E041 04                   inc b
 158+ E042 ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; current NextReg $68
 159+ E044 CB A7                res 4,a
 160+ E046 21 C3 E0             ld hl,KBD_STOP_DOUBLING
 161+ E049 CB 46                bit 0,(hl)
 162+ E04B 28 02                jr z,.w
 163+ E04D CB E7                set 4,a
 164+ E04F ED 79        .w:     out (c),a                       ; ($253B: still selects $68)
 165+ E051 C9                   ret
 166+ E052
 167+ E052              ; A = action -> NZ if its key is down.  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 168+ E052              key_held:
 169+ E052 E5                   push hl
 170+ E053 CD 86 E0             call key_def
 171+ E056 A6                   and (hl)
 172+ E057 E1                   pop hl
 173+ E058 C9                   ret
 174+ E059
 175+ E059              ; A = action -> NZ if its key is down now but wasn't at the last scan.
 176+ E059              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 177+ E059              key_pressed:
 178+ E059 E5                   push hl
 179+ E05A C5                   push bc
 180+ E05B CD 86 E0             call key_def
 181+ E05E 4F                   ld c,a
 182+ E05F A6                   and (hl)
 183+ E060 28 09                jr z,.x                         ; not down
 184+ E062 79                   ld a,c
 185+ E063 D5                   push de
 186+ E064 11 0A 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 187+ E067 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 188+ E068 A6                   and (hl)
 189+ E069 D1                   pop de
 190+ E06A A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 191+ E06B C1           .x:     pop bc
 192+ E06C E1                   pop hl
 193+ E06D C9                   ret
 194+ E06E
 195+ E06E              ; HL = a key code (KB_...) -> NZ if that key went down since the last scan
 196+ E06E              ; (for fixed keys, such as the menu's).  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 197+ E06E              key_code_pressed:
 198+ E06E E5                   push hl
 199+ E06F C5                   push bc
 200+ E070 4D                   ld c,l                          ; mask
 201+ E071 7C                   ld a,h                          ; bank
 202+ E072 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 203+ E075 ED 31                add hl,a
 204+ E077 79                   ld a,c
 205+ E078 A6                   and (hl)
 206+ E079 28 08                jr z,.x                         ; not down
 207+ E07B D5                   push de
 208+ E07C 11 0A 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 209+ E07F 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 210+ E080 A6                   and (hl)
 211+ E081 D1                   pop de
 212+ E082 A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 213+ E083 C1           .x:     pop bc
 214+ E084 E1                   pop hl
 215+ E085 C9                   ret
 216+ E086
 217+ E086              ; A = action -> HL -> key_now[its bank], A = its mask
 218+ E086              key_def:
 219+ E086 87                   add a,a
 220+ E087 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 221+ E08A ED 31                add hl,a
 222+ E08C 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; mask
 223+ E08D 23                   inc hl
 224+ E08E F5                   push af
 225+ E08F 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; bank
 226+ E090 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 227+ E093 ED 31                add hl,a
 228+ E095 F1                   pop af
 229+ E096 C9                   ret
 230+ E097
 231+ E097              ; A = action, HL = key code: redefine the action's key
 232+ E097              key_define:
 233+ E097 EB                   ex de,hl
 234+ E098 87                   add a,a
 235+ E099 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 236+ E09C ED 31                add hl,a
 237+ E09E 73                   ld (hl),e
 238+ E09F 23                   inc hl
 239+ E0A0 72                   ld (hl),d
 240+ E0A1 EB                   ex de,hl
 241+ E0A2 C9                   ret
 242+ E0A3
 243+ E0A3              ; -> NZ and HL = code of a key that is down (lowest bank, lowest bit); Z if
 244+ E0A3              ; none.  For a redefine screen.
 245+ E0A3              key_find:
 246+ E0A3 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 247+ E0A6 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; bank
 248+ E0A8 7E           .b:     ld a,(hl)
 249+ E0A9 B7                   or a
 250+ E0AA 20 09                jr nz,.got
 251+ E0AC 23                   inc hl
 252+ E0AD 0C                   inc c
 253+ E0AE 79                   ld a,c
 254+ E0AF FE 0A                cp KEY_BANKS
 255+ E0B1 20 F5                jr nz,.b
 256+ E0B3 AF                   xor a                           ; Z: nothing down
 257+ E0B4 C9                   ret
 258+ E0B5 06 01        .got:   ld b,1                          ; lowest bit set
 259+ E0B7 1F           .m:     rra
 260+ E0B8 38 04                jr c,.f
 261+ E0BA CB 20                sla b
 262+ E0BC 18 F9                jr .m
 263+ E0BE 61           .f:     ld h,c
 264+ E0BF 68                   ld l,b
 265+ E0C0 F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ
 266+ E0C2 C9                   ret
 267+ E0C3
 268+ E0C3              ;==============================================================================
 269+ E0C3              ; Keyboard data
 270+ E0C3              ;==============================================================================
 271+ E0C3              KBD_STOP_DOUBLING:
 272+ E0C3 00                       db FALSE                    ; TRUE: NextReg $68 bit 4 set, extended
 273+ E0C4                                                      ; keys kept out of the 8x5 matrix
 274+ E0C4 00 00 00...  key_now:    ds KEY_BANKS                ; this scan, 1 = pressed
 275+ E0CE 00 00 00...  key_prev:   ds KEY_BANKS                ; the scan before (must follow key_now)
 276+ E0D8              ; The key of each action, by K_*: the defaults (key_defaults), and the
 277+ E0D8              ; ones in use (key_defs: redefinable on the redefine keys screen,
 278+ E0D8              ; keydef.asm, which can put the defaults back)
 279+ E0D8                  MACRO KEY_DEFAULTS
 280+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 281+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 282+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 283+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 284+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 285+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 286+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 287+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 288+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 289+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 290+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_R                     ; K_GUN_UP
 291+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 292+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_F                     ; K_GUN_DN
 293+ E0D8 ~                        dw KB_V                     ; K_GUN_CTR
 294+ E0D8                  ENDM
 295+ E0D8              key_defs:   KEY_DEFAULTS
 295+ E0D8 01 02       >            dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 295+ E0DA 01 01       >            dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 295+ E0DC 02 00       >            dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 295+ E0DE 02 05       >            dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 295+ E0E0 01 05       >            dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 295+ E0E2 02 02       >            dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 295+ E0E4 02 01       >            dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 295+ E0E6 04 02       >            dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 295+ E0E8 04 01       >            dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 295+ E0EA 01 07       >            dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 295+ E0EC 08 02       >            dw KB_R                     ; K_GUN_UP
 295+ E0EE 01 03 02 03 >            dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 295+ E0F2 04 03 08 03 >
 295+ E0F6 10 03       >
 295+ E0F8 08 01       >            dw KB_F                     ; K_GUN_DN
 295+ E0FA 10 00       >            dw KB_V                     ; K_GUN_CTR
 296+ E0FC                          ASSERT $ - key_defs == K_ACTIONS*2
 297+ E0FC              key_defaults: KEY_DEFAULTS
 297+ E0FC 01 02       >            dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 297+ E0FE 01 01       >            dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 297+ E100 02 00       >            dw KB_Z                     ; K_AIRBRAKE
 297+ E102 02 05       >            dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
