1406  E120                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_SPRITES, CODE_ADDR
1407  E000                      ORG CODE_ADDR
1408  E000                      INCLUDE "sprites.asm"
# file opened: sprites.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - hardware sprites, Layer 3 (8K page PAGE_SPRITES, runs at $E000
   3+ E000              ; in slot 7: page it in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES before calling)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; The Next has 128 hardware sprites, each 16x16 pixels from a pattern in the
   6+ E000              ; sprite pattern memory (16K, separate from the Z80's: 64 8-bit patterns of
   7+ E000              ; 256 bytes, one byte per pixel, row by row).  A pattern byte is an index
   8+ E000              ; into the sprite palette; SPR_TRANS ($E3, NextReg $4B) is transparent.  The
   9+ E000              ; sprite palette starts as the identity, so until spr_palette changes it a
  10+ E000              ; pattern byte is simply its RRRGGGBB colour.
  11+ E000              ;
  12+ E000              ; Sprites are drawn above Layer 2 and the ULA (priority S U L) and, with
  13+ E000              ; NextReg $15 bit 1 set, over the border as well, so the sprite screen is
  14+ E000              ; 320 x 256 pixels:
  15+ E000              ;
  16+ E000              ;     x 0..319, y 0..255;  (SPR_SCREEN_X, SPR_SCREEN_Y) = (32, 32) is the top
  17+ E000              ;     left of the 256x192 Layer 2 / ULA screen, (288, 224) its bottom right
  18+ E000              ;
  19+ E000              ; A sprite's position is its top left pixel.  Coordinates are 9 bits and
  20+ E000              ; wrap round at 512, so they may be negative (two's complement): a sprite at
  21+ E000              ; x = -8 shows its right half at the left edge.  With overlapping sprites,
  22+ E000              ; the higher numbered one is drawn on top.
  23+ E000              ;
  24+ E000              ; Use: sprites_init once (start-up), load patterns with spr_patterns or
  25+ E000              ; from a sprite bank (sprite_data.asm: page the bank into slot 7 and call
  26+ E000              ; its spr_load_bank, from outside slot 7), and a palette with spr_palette
  27+ E000              ; if wanted, then set up sprites in the shadow
  28+ E000              ; table with spr_set / spr_pos / spr_pattern / spr_flags / spr_scale /
  29+ E000              ; spr_hide / spr_show / spr_hide_all, and copy the table to the hardware
  30+ E000              ; once a frame with spr_update.  The table holds the first SPR_COUNT sprites;
  31+ E000              ; the rest stay hidden.
  32+ E000              ;
  33+ E000              ; Attributes, SPR_ATTR (5) bytes a sprite, kept in the table as they are
  34+ E000              ; sent (the 5-byte form):
  35+ E000              ;   +0  x bits 0-7
  36+ E000              ;   +1  y bits 0-7
  37+ E000              ;   +2  bits 7-4 palette offset (added to the pattern byte's top nibble),
  38+ E000              ;       bit 3 x mirror, 2 y mirror, 1 rotate 90 degrees clockwise, 0 x bit 8
  39+ E000              ;   +3  bit 7 visible, bit 6 = 1 (byte +4 follows), bits 5-0 pattern
  40+ E000              ;   +4  bits 4-3 x scale, bits 2-1 y scale (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8), 0 y bit 8
  41+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  42+ E000
  43+ E000              SPR_COUNT       equ 32          ; sprites in the shadow table (max 128)
  44+ E000              SPR_HW          equ 128         ; hardware sprites
  45+ E000              SPR_ATTR        equ 5           ; attribute bytes per sprite
  46+ E000              SPR_PATTERNS    equ 64          ; 8-bit 16x16 patterns in pattern memory
  47+ E000              SPR_SIZE        equ 16          ; pixels, each way (at scale x1)
  48+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_X    equ 32          ; sprite coordinates of the 256x192
  49+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_Y    equ 32          ; screen's top left pixel
  50+ E000              SPR_TRANS       equ $E3         ; transparent pattern byte (NextReg $4B)
  51+ E000
  52+ E000              SPRF_PAL        equ $10         ; flags (attribute +2): palette offset x 16,
  53+ E000              SPRF_XMIRROR    equ $08         ; mirror left-right,
  54+ E000              SPRF_YMIRROR    equ $04         ; mirror top-bottom,
  55+ E000              SPRF_ROT        equ $02         ; rotate 90 degrees clockwise
  56+ E000
  57+ E000              SPR_SLOT_PORT   equ $303B       ; write: select sprite / pattern slot
  58+ E000              SPR_ATTR_PORT   equ $57         ; write: attributes, from the selected sprite
  59+ E000              SPR_PAT_PORT    equ $5B         ; write: pattern bytes, from the selected slot
  60+ E000
  61+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  62+ E000              ; Start-up: sprites on, shown over the border too (the full 320 x 256),
  63+ E000              ; priority S U L; the transparent index; every hardware sprite hidden and the shadow table cleared (all hidden,
  64+ E000              ; pattern 0, at 0,0).
  65+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  66+ E000              sprites_init:
  67+ E000 ED 91 15 0B          nextreg $15,%00001011           ; priority S U L, sprites over the
  68+ E004                                                      ; border (bit 1), visible (bit 0)
  69+ E004 ED 91 4B E3          nextreg $4B,SPR_TRANS
  70+ E008 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT             ; from sprite 0, the 4-byte form
  71+ E00B AF                   xor a                           ; (+3 bit 6 clear): all hidden
  72+ E00C ED 79                out (c),a
  73+ E00E 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
  74+ E010 11 00 02             ld de,SPR_HW*4
  75+ E013 ED 79        .hw:    out (c),a
  76+ E015 1B                   dec de
  77+ E016 7A                   ld a,d
  78+ E017 B3                   or e
  79+ E018 3E 00                ld a,0
  80+ E01A 20 F7                jr nz,.hw
  81+ E01C 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
  82+ E01F 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
  83+ E021 77           .sh:    ld (hl),a                       ; x, y, flags 0
  84+ E022 23                   inc hl
  85+ E023 77                   ld (hl),a
  86+ E024 23                   inc hl
  87+ E025 77                   ld (hl),a
  88+ E026 23                   inc hl
  89+ E027 36 40                ld (hl),$40                     ; hidden, 5-byte form, pattern 0
  90+ E029 23                   inc hl
  91+ E02A 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; scale x1
  92+ E02B 23                   inc hl
  93+ E02C 10 F3                djnz .sh
  94+ E02E                      ; fall through: send it
  95+ E02E
  96+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  97+ E02E              ; Copy the shadow table to sprites 0..SPR_COUNT-1.  Once a frame, after the
  98+ E02E              ; changes (best just after the frame flip, so a move doesn't show half done).
  99+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 100+ E02E              spr_update:
 101+ E02E 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 102+ E031 AF                   xor a
 103+ E032 ED 79                out (c),a                       ; from sprite 0
 104+ E034 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
 105+ E037 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
 106+ E039 16 20                ld d,SPR_COUNT
 107+ E03B 06 05        .lp:    ld b,SPR_ATTR
 108+ E03D ED B3                otir
 109+ E03F 15                   dec d
 110+ E040 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 111+ E042 C9                   ret
 112+ E043
 113+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 114+ E043              ; Load B 8-bit patterns (256 bytes each) from HL into pattern memory, from
 115+ E043              ; pattern A (0..63).  HL ends past them.
 116+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 117+ E043              spr_patterns:
 118+ E043 50                   ld d,b
 119+ E044 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 120+ E047 ED 79                out (c),a
 121+ E049 0E 5B                ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
 122+ E04B 06 00        .lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes
 123+ E04D ED B3                otir
 124+ E04F 15                   dec d
 125+ E050 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 126+ E052 C9                   ret
 127+ E053
 128+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 129+ E053              ; Load B colours (8-bit RRRGGGBB) from HL into the sprite palette, from
 130+ E053              ; index A.  (It starts as the identity: only needed for other colours.)
 131+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 132+ E053              spr_palette:
 133+ E053 ED 91 43 20          nextreg $43,%00100000           ; edit the first sprite palette
 134+ E057 ED 92 40             nextreg $40,a
 135+ E05A 7E           .lp:    ld a,(hl)
 136+ E05B ED 92 41             nextreg $41,a
 137+ E05E 23                   inc hl
 138+ E05F 10 F9                djnz .lp
 139+ E061 C9                   ret
 140+ E062
 141+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 142+ E062              ; Sprite A (0..SPR_COUNT-1) in the shadow table.  BC = x, DE = y (sprite
 143+ E062              ; screen, signed 9 bits), H = pattern (0..63), L = flags (SPRF_*).  Shows it.
 144+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 145+ E062              spr_set:
 146+ E062 22 04 E1             ld (spr_pf),hl
 147+ E065 F5                   push af
 148+ E066 CD 84 E0             call spr_pos
 149+ E069 F1                   pop af
 150+ E06A CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 151+ E06D 23                   inc hl
 152+ E06E 23                   inc hl                          ; +2: the flags, keeping x bit 8
 153+ E06F 3A 04 E1             ld a,(spr_pf)
 154+ E072 E6 FE                and $FE
 155+ E074 5F                   ld e,a
 156+ E075 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 157+ E076 E6 01                and 1
 158+ E078 B3                   or e
 159+ E079 77                   ld (hl),a
 160+ E07A 23                   inc hl                          ; +3: visible, 5-byte form, pattern
 161+ E07B 3A 05 E1             ld a,(spr_pf+1)
 162+ E07E E6 3F                and $3F
 163+ E080 F6 C0                or $C0
 164+ E082 77                   ld (hl),a
 165+ E083 C9                   ret
 166+ E084
 167+ E084              ; Sprite A: move to BC = x, DE = y (signed 9 bits)
 168+ E084              spr_pos:
 169+ E084 D5                   push de
 170+ E085 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 171+ E088 D1                   pop de
 172+ E089 71                   ld (hl),c                       ; +0 x bits 0-7
 173+ E08A 23                   inc hl
 174+ E08B 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; +1 y bits 0-7
 175+ E08C 23                   inc hl
 176+ E08D 78                   ld a,b                          ; +2 bit 0: x bit 8
 177+ E08E E6 01                and 1
 178+ E090 47                   ld b,a
 179+ E091 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 180+ E092 E6 FE                and $FE
 181+ E094 B0                   or b
 182+ E095 77                   ld (hl),a
 183+ E096 23                   inc hl
 184+ E097 23                   inc hl
 185+ E098 7A                   ld a,d                          ; +4 bit 0: y bit 8
 186+ E099 E6 01                and 1
 187+ E09B 57                   ld d,a
 188+ E09C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 189+ E09D E6 FE                and $FE
 190+ E09F B2                   or d
 191+ E0A0 77                   ld (hl),a
 192+ E0A1 C9                   ret
 193+ E0A2
 194+ E0A2              ; Sprite A: pattern E (0..63), keeping whether it is shown
 195+ E0A2              spr_pattern:
 196+ E0A2 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 197+ E0A5 7D                   ld a,l
 198+ E0A6 C6 03                add a,3
 199+ E0A8 6F                   ld l,a
 200+ E0A9 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 201+ E0AA E6 C0                and $C0
 202+ E0AC B3                   or e
 203+ E0AD 77                   ld (hl),a
 204+ E0AE C9                   ret
 205+ E0AF
 206+ E0AF              ; Sprite A: flags E (SPRF_*: palette offset, mirrors, rotation)
 207+ E0AF              spr_flags:
 208+ E0AF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 209+ E0B2 23                   inc hl
 210+ E0B3 23                   inc hl
 211+ E0B4 7B                   ld a,e
 212+ E0B5 E6 FE                and $FE
 213+ E0B7 5F                   ld e,a
 214+ E0B8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 215+ E0B9 E6 01                and 1
 216+ E0BB B3                   or e
 217+ E0BC 77                   ld (hl),a
 218+ E0BD C9                   ret
 219+ E0BE
 220+ E0BE              ; Sprite A: scale E across, D down (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8)
 221+ E0BE              spr_scale:
 222+ E0BE D5                   push de
 223+ E0BF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 224+ E0C2 D1                   pop de
 225+ E0C3 7D                   ld a,l
 226+ E0C4 C6 04                add a,4
 227+ E0C6 6F                   ld l,a
 228+ E0C7 7B                   ld a,e
 229+ E0C8 E6 03                and 3
 230+ E0CA 07                   rlca
 231+ E0CB 07                   rlca
 232+ E0CC 5F                   ld e,a
 233+ E0CD 7A                   ld a,d
 234+ E0CE E6 03                and 3
 235+ E0D0 B3                   or e
 236+ E0D1 07                   rlca                            ; x scale bits 4-3, y scale 2-1
 237+ E0D2 5F                   ld e,a
 238+ E0D3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 239+ E0D4 E6 01                and 1                           ; (keep y bit 8)
 240+ E0D6 B3                   or e
 241+ E0D7 77                   ld (hl),a
 242+ E0D8 C9                   ret
 243+ E0D9
 244+ E0D9              ; Sprite A: hide / show
 245+ E0D9              spr_hide:
 246+ E0D9 CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 247+ E0DC CB BE                res 7,(hl)
 248+ E0DE C9                   ret
 249+ E0DF              spr_show:
 250+ E0DF CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 251+ E0E2 CB FE                set 7,(hl)
 252+ E0E4 C9                   ret
 253+ E0E5
 254+ E0E5              ; Hide every sprite in the table
 255+ E0E5              spr_hide_all:
 256+ E0E5 21 03 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow+3
 257+ E0E8 11 05 00             ld de,SPR_ATTR
 258+ E0EB 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
 259+ E0ED CB BE        .lp:    res 7,(hl)
 260+ E0EF 19                   add hl,de
 261+ E0F0 10 FB                djnz .lp
 262+ E0F2 C9                   ret
 263+ E0F3
 264+ E0F3              ; A = sprite -> HL -> its attributes in the table, +3 for spr_addr3.
 265+ E0F3              ; (The table doesn't cross a 256-byte boundary, so +n is just L + n.)
 266+ E0F3              spr_addr3:
 267+ E0F3 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 268+ E0F6 23                   inc hl
 269+ E0F7 23                   inc hl
 270+ E0F8 23                   inc hl
 271+ E0F9 C9                   ret
 272+ E0FA              spr_addr:
 273+ E0FA 57                   ld d,a
 274+ E0FB 1E 05                ld e,SPR_ATTR
 275+ E0FD ED 30                mul d,e
 276+ E0FF 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
 277+ E102 19                   add hl,de
 278+ E103 C9                   ret
 279+ E104
 280+ E104              ;==============================================================================
 281+ E104              ; Sprite data
 282+ E104              ;==============================================================================
 283+ E104              ; The patterns themselves are in the sprite banks (sprite_data.asm).
 284+ E104              ; Bank 0 has the human status sprites, loaded as patterns 0-3 by
 285+ E104              ; sprites_init, after the HUD's passenger markers:
 286+ E104              ;   HSPR_FIELD   0  waiting in the field, not yet rescued: an empty square
 287+ E104              ;   HSPR_ABOARD  1  aboard the VTOL: a person sitting in a chair
 288+ E104              ;   HSPR_SAVED   2  rescued: a green person standing
 289+ E104              ;   HSPR_DEAD    3  dead: a white person with a red cross over them
 290+ E104
 291+ E104 00 00        spr_pf:     dw 0                        ; spr_set: flags, pattern
 292+ E106 00 00 00...          ALIGN 256
 293+ E200 00 00 00...  spr_shadow: ds SPR_COUNT*SPR_ATTR       ; attributes, as sent (5-byte form)
 294+ E2A0                      ASSERT SPR_COUNT*SPR_ATTR <= 256
 295+ E2A0
# file closed: sprites.asm
1409  E2A0                      INCLUDE "hud_sprites.asm"       ; the human column, the compass bar
# file opened: hud_sprites.asm
   1+ E2A0              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E2A0              ; Z-POLICE - HUD sprites (in the sprites page, PAGE_SPRITES, at $E000 in slot
   3+ E2A0              ; 7): the human status column and the compass bar, as hardware sprites on
   4+ E2A0              ; the 320 x 256 sprite screen.  hud_sprites sets both and sends the sprite
   5+ E2A0              ; table to the hardware; main_loop calls it once a frame, after the flip.
   6+ E2A0              ;
   7+ E2A0              ; Sprites used: 0..HUMAN_MAX-1 the humans, CSPR_SPR0.. the compass bar's
   8+ E2A0              ; marks, CSPR_PTR_SPR its pointer.
   9+ E2A0              ;==============================================================================
  10+ E2A0
  11+ E2A0              hud_sprites:
  12+ E2A0 CD 14 E3             call hum_hud
  13+ E2A3 CD A9 E2             call compass_spr
  14+ E2A6 C3 2E E0             jp spr_update
  15+ E2A9
  16+ E2A9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  17+ E2A9              ; The compass bar, across the top of the screen in the top border (sprite
  18+ E2A9              ; row COMP_SPR_Y): a tape of marks every 16 yaw units (22.5 degrees), at 2
  19+ E2A9              ; pixels a yaw unit, centred on the heading at sprite x COMP_SPR_CX, across
  20+ E2A9              ; the full 320-pixel width of the sprite screen: 225 degrees (+-COMP_HALF,
  21+ E2A9              ; 80 yaw units).  Marks at the very edges are half off the screen (their x
  22+ E2A9              ; wraps, as sprite x does).  The letters N,
  23+ E2A9              ; E, S, W (red) at 0, 64, 128, 192; a yellow dot at the 45 degree points;
  24+ E2A9              ; a small white dot between.  A white pointer under the bar's middle marks
  25+ E2A9              ; the heading.  Turning right (yaw up) moves the marks left.
  26+ E2A9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  27+ E2A9              CSPR_SPR0       equ HUMAN_MAX       ; first compass mark sprite (20)
  28+ E2A9              COMP_HALF       equ 80              ; yaw units shown either side: 160 px
  29+ E2A9              CSPR_MARKS      equ 2*COMP_HALF/16+1    ; most marks in view at once (11)
  30+ E2A9              CSPR_PTR_SPR    equ CSPR_SPR0+CSPR_MARKS   ; the pointer's sprite
  31+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_Y      equ 8               ; the bar's sprite row
  32+ E2A9              COMP_PTR_Y      equ COMP_SPR_Y+SPR_SIZE    ; the pointer, under it
  33+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_CX     equ 160             ; the heading's x: the screen's middle
  34+ E2A9              COMP_SPR_X0     equ COMP_SPR_CX-2*COMP_HALF-SPR_SIZE/2  ; x of a mark =
  35+ E2A9                                                  ; this + 2 * (COMP_HALF + mark - yaw)
  36+ E2A9                      ASSERT CSPR_PTR_SPR < SPR_COUNT
  37+ E2A9                      ASSERT CSPR_E == CSPR_N+1 && CSPR_S == CSPR_N+2 && CSPR_W == CSPR_N+3
  38+ E2A9
  39+ E2A9              compass_spr:
  40+ E2A9 3E 14                ld a,CSPR_SPR0
  41+ E2AB 32 52 E3             ld (cs_spr),a
  42+ E2AE 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; mark k, at yaw k * 16
  43+ E2B0 C5           .lp:    push bc
  44+ E2B1 79                   ld a,c
  45+ E2B2 87                   add a,a
  46+ E2B3 87                   add a,a
  47+ E2B4 87                   add a,a
  48+ E2B5 87                   add a,a
  49+ E2B6 21 C1 A7             ld hl,yaw
  50+ E2B9 96                   sub (hl)                        ; mark - yaw, signed
  51+ E2BA C6 50                add a,COMP_HALF
  52+ E2BC FE A1                cp 2*COMP_HALF+1
  53+ E2BE 30 30                jr nc,.nx                       ; off the bar: not shown
  54+ E2C0 6F                   ld l,a                          ; x = COMP_SPR_X0 + 2 * that
  55+ E2C1 26 00                ld h,0
  56+ E2C3 29                   add hl,hl
  57+ E2C4 11 F8 FF             ld de,COMP_SPR_X0
  58+ E2C7 19                   add hl,de
  59+ E2C8 E5                   push hl
  60+ E2C9 79                   ld a,c                          ; which mark: a letter every 64,
  61+ E2CA E6 03                and 3                           ; a dot every 32, else a tick
  62+ E2CC 79                   ld a,c
  63+ E2CD 20 08                jr nz,.nl
  64+ E2CF 0F                   rrca
  65+ E2D0 0F                   rrca
  66+ E2D1 E6 03                and 3
  67+ E2D3 C6 04                add a,CSPR_N
  68+ E2D5 18 08                jr .pat
  69+ E2D7 E6 01        .nl:    and 1
  70+ E2D9 3E 08                ld a,CSPR_DOT
  71+ E2DB 28 02                jr z,.pat
  72+ E2DD 3E 09                ld a,CSPR_TICK
  73+ E2DF 67           .pat:   ld h,a
  74+ E2E0 2E 00                ld l,0
  75+ E2E2 C1                   pop bc                          ; BC = x
  76+ E2E3 11 08 00             ld de,COMP_SPR_Y
  77+ E2E6 3A 52 E3             ld a,(cs_spr)
  78+ E2E9 CD 62 E0             call spr_set
  79+ E2EC 21 52 E3             ld hl,cs_spr
  80+ E2EF 34                   inc (hl)
  81+ E2F0 C1           .nx:    pop bc
  82+ E2F1 0C                   inc c
  83+ E2F2 79                   ld a,c
  84+ E2F3 FE 10                cp 16
  85+ E2F5 20 B9                jr nz,.lp
  86+ E2F7 3A 52 E3             ld a,(cs_spr)                   ; hide the mark sprites not used
  87+ E2FA FE 1F        .hd:    cp CSPR_SPR0+CSPR_MARKS
  88+ E2FC 30 08                jr nc,.ptr
  89+ E2FE F5                   push af
  90+ E2FF CD D9 E0             call spr_hide
  91+ E302 F1                   pop af
  92+ E303 3C                   inc a
  93+ E304 18 F4                jr .hd
  94+ E306 3E 1F        .ptr:   ld a,CSPR_PTR_SPR               ; the pointer
  95+ E308 01 98 00             ld bc,COMP_SPR_CX-SPR_SIZE/2
  96+ E30B 11 18 00             ld de,COMP_PTR_Y
  97+ E30E 21 00 0A             ld hl,CSPR_PTR<<8
  98+ E311 C3 62 E0             jp spr_set
  99+ E314
 100+ E314              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 101+ E314              ; The human status HUD: a column of hardware sprites, sprite k for human k,
 102+ E314              ; HUD_SPR_X pixels in from the left edge of the 320 x 256 sprite screen,
 103+ E314              ; human 0 at the bottom (HUD_SPR_Y) and each next one HUD_SPR_STEP higher.
 104+ E314              ; Each shows its human's state with the human status patterns
 105+ E314              ; (sprite_data.asm): waiting in the city (HSPR_FIELD), aboard the VTOL
 106+ E314              ; (HSPR_ABOARD), rescued (HSPR_SAVED) or dead (HSPR_DEAD).  Unused humans'
 107+ E314              ; sprites are hidden.
 108+ E314              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 109+ E314              HUD_SPR_X       equ 8
 110+ E314              HUD_SPR_Y       equ 256-8-SPR_SIZE  ; the bottom one: 8 pixels above the edge
 111+ E314              HUD_SPR_STEP    equ SPR_SIZE
 112+ E314              HUD_SPR_MAX     equ (HUD_SPR_Y+HUD_SPR_STEP)/HUD_SPR_STEP   ; that fit (15)
 113+ E314                      ASSERT HSPR_FIELD == HS_WAIT-1 && HSPR_ABOARD == HS_ABOARD-1
 114+ E314                      ASSERT HSPR_SAVED == HS_SAVED-1 && HSPR_DEAD == HS_DEAD-1
 115+ E314                      ASSERT HUMAN_MAX <= SPR_COUNT
 116+ E314              hum_hud:
 117+ E314 DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 118+ E318 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; human = sprite number
 119+ E31A C5           .lp:    push bc
 120+ E31B DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 121+ E31E B7                   or a
 122+ E31F 28 20                jr z,.hide                      ; unused
 123+ E321 79                   ld a,c
 124+ E322 FE 0F                cp HUD_SPR_MAX
 125+ E324 30 1B                jr nc,.hide                     ; no room on the screen
 126+ E326 87                   add a,a                         ; y = HUD_SPR_Y - human * 16
 127+ E327 87                   add a,a
 128+ E328 87                   add a,a
 129+ E329 87                   add a,a
 130+ E32A 5F                   ld e,a
 131+ E32B 3E E8                ld a,HUD_SPR_Y
 132+ E32D 93                   sub e
 133+ E32E 5F                   ld e,a
 134+ E32F 16 00                ld d,0
 135+ E331 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 136+ E334 3D                   dec a
 137+ E335 67                   ld h,a                          ; the state's pattern
 138+ E336 2E 00                ld l,0                          ; no mirror, rotation
 139+ E338 79                   ld a,c
 140+ E339 01 08 00             ld bc,HUD_SPR_X
 141+ E33C CD 62 E0             call spr_set                    ; (sprites.asm)
 142+ E33F 18 04                jr .nx
 143+ E341 79           .hide:  ld a,c
 144+ E342 CD D9 E0             call spr_hide
 145+ E345 11 0D 00     .nx:    ld de,HREC
 146+ E348 DD 19                add ix,de
 147+ E34A C1                   pop bc
 148+ E34B 0C                   inc c
 149+ E34C 79                   ld a,c
 150+ E34D FE 14                cp HUMAN_MAX
 151+ E34F 20 C9                jr nz,.lp
 152+ E351 C9                   ret
 153+ E352
 154+ E352
 155+ E352 00           cs_spr:     db 0                        ; compass_spr: the next mark's sprite
 156+ E353
# file closed: hud_sprites.asm

1412  E353                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_INIT, CODE_ADDR
1413  E000                      ORG CODE_ADDR
1414  E000                      INCLUDE "initialise.asm"
# file opened: initialise.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - start-up initialisation (8K page PAGE_INIT, runs at $E000 in
   3+ E000              ; slot 7)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Code needed only once, at start-up, kept out of the resident page: start
   6+ E000              ; (zpolice.asm) pages this in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INIT and calls
   7+ E000              ; initgame, then slot 7 is free for the other core pages.
   8+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   9+ E000
  10+ E000              ; Hardware set-up: 28MHz, Layer 2 mode, transparency, layer priority, Layer
  11+ E000              ; 2 scroll and clip window, the memory map of the always-mapped slots, the
  12+ E000              ; peripheral settings (AY sound, turbo sound, internal speaker, no
  13+ E000              ; contention), and a black border.
  14+ E000              initgame:
  15+ E000 ED 91 07 03          nextreg $07,3                   ; 28MHz
  16+ E004 ED 91 70 00          nextreg $70,0                   ; Layer 2 256x192 mode
  17+ E008 ED 91 14 E3          nextreg $14,$E3                 ; global transparency colour
  18+ E00C ED 91 15 08          nextreg $15,%00001000           ; priority S U L (ULA over L2); sprites
  19+ E010                                                      ; off until sprites_init
  20+ E010 ED 91 16 00          nextreg $16,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll X
  21+ E014 ED 91 17 00          nextreg $17,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll Y
  22+ E018 ED 91 1C 01          nextreg $1C,%00000001           ; reset L2 clip index
  23+ E01C ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; Layer 2 clip: x 0..255,
  24+ E020 ED 91 18 FF          nextreg $18,255
  25+ E024 ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; y 0..191: the viewport, and the
  26+ E028 ED 91 18 BF          nextreg $18,191                 ; city map through the ULA panel
  27+ E02C ED 91 54 0A          nextreg ULA_MMU,PAGE_ULA0       ; ULA screen (bank 5) at $8000
  28+ E030 ED 91 51 05          nextreg ZOMBIE_MMU,PAGE_ZOMBIE  ; zombie code/data stays at $2000
  29+ E034 ED 91 52 06          nextreg OCC_MMU,PAGE_OCC0       ; occlusion data at $4000-$5FFF,
  30+ E038 ED 91 53 07          nextreg TABLES_MMU,PAGE_OCC1    ; city_col at $6000-$6FFF
  31+ E03C                      ; Peripheral settings.  $06 and $08 are read, changed and written
  32+ E03C                      ; back, so the bits not named here keep their values (in $06, bit 4
  33+ E03C                      ; is the divMMC automap, which the file handler's RST $08 needs).
  34+ E03C 3E 06                ld a,$06                        ; Peripheral 2: audio chip mode
  35+ E03E CD 9F A4             call read_nextreg               ; (bits 1-0) = %01, AY
  36+ E041 E6 FC                and %11111100
  37+ E043 F6 01                or %00000001
  38+ E045 ED 92 06             nextreg $06,a
  39+ E048 3E 08                ld a,$08                        ; Peripheral 3:
  40+ E04A CD 9F A4             call read_nextreg
  41+ E04D F6 52                or %01010010                    ; bit 6: no RAM / I/O contention,
  42+ E04F ED 92 08             nextreg $08,a                   ; bit 4: internal speaker on,
  43+ E052                                                      ; bit 1: turbo sound on
  44+ E052 ED 91 09 00          nextreg $09,0                   ; Peripheral 4: AY stereo (not
  45+ E056                                                      ; mono), no sprite id lockstep, HDMI
  46+ E056                                                      ; audio on, no scanlines
  47+ E056 AF                   xor a
  48+ E057 D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; black border
  49+ E059 C9                   ret
  50+ E05A
# file closed: initialise.asm

1417  E05A                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_FILE, CODE_ADDR

1419  E000                      INCLUDE "file_handler.asm"
# file opened: file_handler.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - file handling: loading and saving on the SD card (8K page
   3+ E000              ; PAGE_FILE, runs at $E000 in slot 7: page it in with nextreg
   4+ E000              ; CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE before calling)
   5+ E000              ;
   6+ E000              ; General-purpose routines over the esxDOS / NextZXOS file API (RST $08 with
   7+ E000              ; a function code after it).  Game-specific loading and saving (save games,
   8+ E000              ; high scores, settings) go on top of these.
   9+ E000              ;
  10+ E000              ; The ROM: the divMMC only traps RST $08 while the ROM is paged in at
  11+ E000              ; $0000-$3FFF, but the game keeps other pages there (slot 0: a Layer 2
  12+ E000              ; section or the sine table; slot 1: the zombie page).  So every call pages
  13+ E000              ; the ROM into slots 0 and 1 (fh_rom_in), makes the RST $08, then puts the
  14+ E000              ; game's pages back (fh_rom_out).  Interrupts stay off throughout: the game
  15+ E000              ; never enables them (IY is in use and slot 2 holds game data, so the ROM's
  16+ E000              ; interrupt routine must not run).
  17+ E000              ;
  18+ E000              ; Rules for callers:
  19+ E000              ;   * page this in at $E000 (slot 7) first, and call from outside slot 7:
  20+ E000              ;     resident code or a game logic page in slot 6;
  21+ E000              ;   * file names and data buffers must be in $4000-$DFFF: not in slots 0-1
  22+ E000              ;     (the ROM and esxDOS are there during a call) nor slot 7 (this page);
  23+ E000              ;   * file names end with a 0 byte.
  24+ E000              ;
  25+ E000              ; Every routine returns carry clear on success; carry set on failure with
  26+ E000              ; A = the esxDOS error code.  A file that was opened is closed again on
  27+ E000              ; any error in fh_load / fh_save.
  28+ E000              ;
  29+ E000              ; Routines:
  30+ E000              ;   fh_init      get the default drive (call once, before the others)
  31+ E000              ;   fh_open      HL = name, B = mode (FA_*)      -> A = handle
  32+ E000              ;   fh_close     A = handle
  33+ E000              ;   fh_read      A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes read
  34+ E000              ;   fh_write     A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes written
  35+ E000              ;   fh_seek      A = handle, BCDE = offset, L = SEEK_* -> BCDE = position
  36+ E000              ;   fh_size      A = handle                      -> BCDE = file size
  37+ E000              ;   fh_load      HL = name, DE = address, BC = most bytes -> BC = bytes read
  38+ E000              ;   fh_save      HL = name, DE = address, BC = length (created / replaced)
  39+ E000              ;   fh_exists    HL = name -> carry clear if the file exists
  40+ E000              ;   fh_delete    HL = name
  41+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  42+ E000
  43+ E000              ; esxDOS function codes (the byte after RST $08)
  44+ E000              M_GETSETDRV     equ $89             ; A = 0: get the default drive
  45+ E000              F_OPEN          equ $9A
  46+ E000              F_CLOSE         equ $9B
  47+ E000              F_READ          equ $9D
  48+ E000              F_WRITE         equ $9E
  49+ E000              F_SEEK          equ $9F
  50+ E000              F_FSTAT         equ $A1
  51+ E000              F_UNLINK        equ $AD
  52+ E000
  53+ E000              ; F_OPEN modes (B)
  54+ E000              FA_READ         equ $01             ; read
  55+ E000              FA_WRITE        equ $02             ; write
  56+ E000              FA_OPEN_EX      equ $00             ; open an existing file only
  57+ E000              FA_OPEN_AL      equ $08             ; open, or create if it doesn't exist
  58+ E000              FA_CREATE_NEW   equ $04             ; create, fail if it exists
  59+ E000              FA_CREATE_AL    equ $0C             ; create, replacing any existing file
  60+ E000              FA_OVERWRITE    equ FA_WRITE|FA_CREATE_AL   ; for saving a whole file
  61+ E000
  62+ E000              ; F_SEEK modes (L)
  63+ E000              SEEK_START      equ 0               ; from the start of the file
  64+ E000              SEEK_FWD        equ 1               ; forwards from the current position
  65+ E000              SEEK_BACK       equ 2               ; backwards from the current position
  66+ E000
  67+ E000              FSTAT_SIZE      equ 7               ; F_FSTAT buffer: file size, 4 bytes
  68+ E000              FSTAT_LEN       equ 11              ; F_FSTAT buffer length
  69+ E000
  70+ E000              ESX_DEFAULT     equ '*'             ; drive: the default drive
  71+ E000
  72+ E000              ; An esxDOS call: the ROM in at $0000-$3FFF for the RST $08, the game's
  73+ E000              ; pages back after.  Registers in and out are the function's own; flags
  74+ E000              ; and A come back from esxDOS (carry set = error, A = code).
  75+ E000                      MACRO ESXDOS fn
  76+ E000 ~                    call fh_rom_in
  77+ E000 ~                    rst $08
  78+ E000 ~                    db fn
  79+ E000 ~                    call fh_rom_out
  80+ E000                      ENDM
  81+ E000
  82+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83+ E000              ; Get the default drive into fh_drive (else the '*' default is used)
  84+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  85+ E000              fh_init:
  86+ E000 AF                   xor a                           ; A = 0: get
  87+ E001                      ESXDOS M_GETSETDRV
  87+ E001 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
  87+ E004 CF          >        rst $08
  87+ E005 89          >        db M_GETSETDRV
  87+ E006 CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
  88+ E009 D8                   ret c
  89+ E00A 32 01 E1             ld (fh_drive),a
  90+ E00D C9                   ret
  91+ E00E
  92+ E00E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  93+ E00E              ; HL -> file name, B = mode (FA_*) -> A = handle (also in fh_handle)
  94+ E00E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  95+ E00E              fh_open:
  96+ E00E DD E5                push ix
  97+ E010 E5                   push hl
  98+ E011 DD E1                pop ix                          ; IX = HL = the name (both
  99+ E013 3A 01 E1             ld a,(fh_drive)                 ; conventions: programs, dot
 100+ E016                      ESXDOS F_OPEN                   ; commands)
 100+ E016 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 100+ E019 CF          >        rst $08
 100+ E01A 9A          >        db F_OPEN
 100+ E01B CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 101+ E01E DD E1                pop ix
 102+ E020 D8                   ret c
 103+ E021 32 02 E1             ld (fh_handle),a
 104+ E024 C9                   ret
 105+ E025
 106+ E025              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 107+ E025              ; A = handle: close it
 108+ E025              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 109+ E025              fh_close:
 110+ E025                      ESXDOS F_CLOSE
 110+ E025 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 110+ E028 CF          >        rst $08
 110+ E029 9B          >        db F_CLOSE
 110+ E02A CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 111+ E02D C9                   ret
 112+ E02E
 113+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 114+ E02E              ; A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes read (fewer at the end
 115+ E02E              ; of the file)
 116+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 117+ E02E              fh_read:
 118+ E02E DD E5                push ix
 119+ E030 E5                   push hl
 120+ E031 DD E1                pop ix
 121+ E033                      ESXDOS F_READ
 121+ E033 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 121+ E036 CF          >        rst $08
 121+ E037 9D          >        db F_READ
 121+ E038 CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 122+ E03B DD E1                pop ix
 123+ E03D C9                   ret
 124+ E03E
 125+ E03E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 126+ E03E              ; A = handle, HL = address, BC = length -> BC = bytes written
 127+ E03E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 128+ E03E              fh_write:
 129+ E03E DD E5                push ix
 130+ E040 E5                   push hl
 131+ E041 DD E1                pop ix
 132+ E043                      ESXDOS F_WRITE
 132+ E043 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 132+ E046 CF          >        rst $08
 132+ E047 9E          >        db F_WRITE
 132+ E048 CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 133+ E04B DD E1                pop ix
 134+ E04D C9                   ret
 135+ E04E
 136+ E04E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 137+ E04E              ; A = handle, BCDE = offset (32-bit), L = mode (SEEK_*) -> BCDE = position
 138+ E04E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 139+ E04E              fh_seek:
 140+ E04E DD E5                push ix
 141+ E050 E5                   push hl
 142+ E051 E5                   push hl
 143+ E052 DD E1                pop ix                          ; mode in IXL (programs) and L
 144+ E054                      ESXDOS F_SEEK
 144+ E054 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 144+ E057 CF          >        rst $08
 144+ E058 9F          >        db F_SEEK
 144+ E059 CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 145+ E05C E1                   pop hl
 146+ E05D DD E1                pop ix
 147+ E05F C9                   ret
 148+ E060
 149+ E060              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 150+ E060              ; A = handle -> BCDE = file size (32-bit)
 151+ E060              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 152+ E060              fh_size:
 153+ E060 DD E5                push ix
 154+ E062 E5                   push hl
 155+ E063 21 05 E1             ld hl,fh_stat
 156+ E066 E5                   push hl
 157+ E067 DD E1                pop ix
 158+ E069                      ESXDOS F_FSTAT
 158+ E069 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 158+ E06C CF          >        rst $08
 158+ E06D A1          >        db F_FSTAT
 158+ E06E CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 159+ E071 38 0A                jr c,.x
 160+ E073 2A 0C E1             ld hl,(fh_stat+FSTAT_SIZE)
 161+ E076 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = low word
 162+ E077 2A 0E E1             ld hl,(fh_stat+FSTAT_SIZE+2)
 163+ E07A 44                   ld b,h                          ; BC = high word
 164+ E07B 4D                   ld c,l
 165+ E07C B7                   or a
 166+ E07D E1           .x:     pop hl
 167+ E07E DD E1                pop ix
 168+ E080 C9                   ret
 169+ E081
 170+ E081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 171+ E081              ; Load a file: HL -> name, DE = address, BC = most bytes to read
 172+ E081              ; -> BC = bytes read.  The file is opened, read and closed.
 173+ E081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 174+ E081              fh_load:
 175+ E081 D5                   push de
 176+ E082 C5                   push bc
 177+ E083 06 01                ld b,FA_READ|FA_OPEN_EX
 178+ E085 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 179+ E088 C1                   pop bc
 180+ E089 E1                   pop hl                          ; HL = address
 181+ E08A D8                   ret c                           ; couldn't open (A = error)
 182+ E08B CD 2E E0             call fh_read
 183+ E08E 18 0D                jr fh_done                      ; close it, keeping any error
 184+ E090
 185+ E090              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 186+ E090              ; Save a file: HL -> name, DE = address, BC = length.  The file is created,
 187+ E090              ; replacing any file of that name.
 188+ E090              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 189+ E090              fh_save:
 190+ E090 D5                   push de
 191+ E091 C5                   push bc
 192+ E092 06 0E                ld b,FA_OVERWRITE
 193+ E094 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 194+ E097 C1                   pop bc
 195+ E098 E1                   pop hl
 196+ E099 D8                   ret c
 197+ E09A CD 3E E0             call fh_write
 198+ E09D                      ; fall through
 199+ E09D
 200+ E09D              ; After a read / write on fh_handle: close the file.  Carry and A as from
 201+ E09D              ; that read / write if it failed, else as from the close.
 202+ E09D              fh_done:
 203+ E09D F5                   push af
 204+ E09E 3A 02 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)
 205+ E0A1 CD 25 E0             call fh_close
 206+ E0A4 38 02                jr c,.cerr
 207+ E0A6 F1                   pop af                          ; the read / write's result
 208+ E0A7 C9                   ret
 209+ E0A8 E1           .cerr:  pop hl                          ; the close failed: if the read /
 210+ E0A9 CB 45                bit 0,l                         ; write had already failed, report
 211+ E0AB 28 01                jr z,.ce                        ; that, else the close's error
 212+ E0AD 7C                   ld a,h
 213+ E0AE 37           .ce:    scf
 214+ E0AF C9                   ret
 215+ E0B0
 216+ E0B0              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 217+ E0B0              ; HL -> name: carry clear if the file exists (it can be opened to read)
 218+ E0B0              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 219+ E0B0              fh_exists:
 220+ E0B0 06 01                ld b,FA_READ|FA_OPEN_EX
 221+ E0B2 CD 0E E0             call fh_open
 222+ E0B5 D8                   ret c
 223+ E0B6 C3 25 E0             jp fh_close
 224+ E0B9
 225+ E0B9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 226+ E0B9              ; HL -> name: delete the file
 227+ E0B9              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 228+ E0B9              fh_delete:
 229+ E0B9 DD E5                push ix
 230+ E0BB E5                   push hl
 231+ E0BC DD E1                pop ix
 232+ E0BE 3A 01 E1             ld a,(fh_drive)
 233+ E0C1                      ESXDOS F_UNLINK
 233+ E0C1 CD CC E0    >        call fh_rom_in
 233+ E0C4 CF          >        rst $08
 233+ E0C5 AD          >        db F_UNLINK
 233+ E0C6 CD F1 E0    >        call fh_rom_out
 234+ E0C9 DD E1                pop ix
 235+ E0CB C9                   ret
 236+ E0CC
 237+ E0CC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 238+ E0CC              ; Page the ROM into slots 0 and 1 for RST $08, remembering the game's pages
 239+ E0CC              ; (fh_rom_in), and put them back (fh_rom_out).  Both keep every register
 240+ E0CC              ; and flag.
 241+ E0CC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 242+ E0CC              fh_rom_in:
 243+ E0CC F5                   push af
 244+ E0CD C5                   push bc
 245+ E0CE 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 246+ E0D1 3E 50                ld a,MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER        ; slot 0's page now
 247+ E0D3 ED 79                out (c),a
 248+ E0D5 04                   inc b
 249+ E0D6 ED 78                in a,(c)
 250+ E0D8 32 03 E1             ld (fh_mmu0),a
 251+ E0DB 05                   dec b
 252+ E0DC 3E 51                ld a,MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER        ; slot 1's
 253+ E0DE ED 79                out (c),a
 254+ E0E0 04                   inc b
 255+ E0E1 ED 78                in a,(c)
 256+ E0E3 32 04 E1             ld (fh_mmu1),a
 257+ E0E6 ED 91 50 FF          nextreg MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER,PAGE_ROM
 258+ E0EA ED 91 51 FF          nextreg MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER,PAGE_ROM
 259+ E0EE C1                   pop bc
 260+ E0EF F1                   pop af
 261+ E0F0 C9                   ret
 262+ E0F1
 263+ E0F1              fh_rom_out:
 264+ E0F1 F3                   di                              ; (in case esxDOS enabled them)
 265+ E0F2 F5                   push af
 266+ E0F3 3A 03 E1             ld a,(fh_mmu0)
 267+ E0F6 ED 92 50             nextreg MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER,a
 268+ E0F9 3A 04 E1             ld a,(fh_mmu1)
 269+ E0FC ED 92 51             nextreg MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER,a
 270+ E0FF F1                   pop af
 271+ E100 C9                   ret
 272+ E101
 273+ E101              ;==============================================================================
 274+ E101              ; File handler data
 275+ E101              ;==============================================================================
 276+ E101 2A           fh_drive:   db ESX_DEFAULT              ; drive for F_OPEN (fh_init)
 277+ E102 00           fh_handle:  db 0                        ; the last file opened
 278+ E103 00           fh_mmu0:    db 0                        ; the game's slot 0 / 1 pages during
 279+ E104 00           fh_mmu1:    db 0                        ; an esxDOS call
 280+ E105 00 00 00...  fh_stat:    ds FSTAT_LEN                ; F_FSTAT buffer (fh_size)

# file opened: audio.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - audio (8K page PAGE_AUDIO, runs at $E000 in slot 7: page it in
   3+ E000              ; with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_AUDIO before calling)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Sound devices: the Next's TurboSound, three AY-3-8912 chips.  initgame
   6+ E000              ; (initialise.asm) has already set the chip mode to AY (NextReg $06),
   7+ E000              ; turned TurboSound and the internal speaker on (NextReg $08) and the AY
   8+ E000              ; outputs to stereo (NextReg $09).  init_audio then sets every chip to a
   9+ E000              ; known, silent state.  Sound effects and music will go on top of this.
  10+ E000              ;
  11+ E000              ; TurboSound ports:
  12+ E000              ;   $FFFD write %1LR111cc: select AY chip cc (AY_CHIP_1..3) with its left
  13+ E000              ;         (L) and right (R) outputs on;
  14+ E000              ;         any value 0..15: select that register of the selected chip
  15+ E000              ;   $BFFD write: the value of the selected register
  16+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  17+ E000
  18+ E000              AY_CTRL_PORT    equ $FFFD           ; chip / register select
  19+ E000              AY_DATA_PORT    equ $BFFD           ; register value
  20+ E000
  21+ E000              ; Chip select ($FFFD): AY_SELECT | AY_LEFT | AY_RIGHT | AY_CHIP_n
  22+ E000              AY_SELECT       equ %10011100       ; bit 7 and bits 4-2 must be set
  23+ E000              AY_LEFT         equ %01000000       ; left output on
  24+ E000              AY_RIGHT        equ %00100000       ; right output on
  25+ E000              AY_CHIP_1       equ %11
  26+ E000              AY_CHIP_2       equ %10
  27+ E000              AY_CHIP_3       equ %01
  28+ E000
  29+ E000              ; AY registers
  30+ E000              AY_TONE_A       equ 0               ; channel A tone period: fine, coarse (4)
  31+ E000              AY_TONE_B       equ 2
  32+ E000              AY_TONE_C       equ 4
  33+ E000              AY_NOISE        equ 6               ; noise period (5 bits)
  34+ E000              AY_MIXER        equ 7               ; tone / noise enable, 0 = on:
  35+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_A    equ %00000001       ;   bits 0-2 tone A-C off,
  36+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_B    equ %00000010
  37+ E000              AY_NO_TONE_C    equ %00000100
  38+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_A   equ %00001000       ;   bits 3-5 noise A-C off
  39+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_B   equ %00010000
  40+ E000              AY_NO_NOISE_C   equ %00100000
  41+ E000              AY_VOL_A        equ 8               ; amplitude 0-15 (bit 4: use envelope)
  42+ E000              AY_VOL_B        equ 9
  43+ E000              AY_VOL_C        equ 10
  44+ E000              AY_ENV_FINE     equ 11              ; envelope period
  45+ E000              AY_ENV_COARSE   equ 12
  46+ E000              AY_ENV_SHAPE    equ 13              ; envelope shape
  47+ E000              AY_REGS         equ 14
  48+ E000
  49+ E000              AY_ENV_ATTACK_HOLD equ %1101        ; envelope: rise once, then hold
  50+ E000              AY_MIXER_INIT   equ AY_NO_NOISE_A|AY_NO_NOISE_B|AY_NO_NOISE_C
  51+ E000                                                  ; tones on, noise off
  52+ E000
  53+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  54+ E000              ; Set up the sound devices: each AY chip selected with both outputs, every
  55+ E000              ; register 0 (silent: all volumes 0), then the mixer to tones only and the
  56+ E000              ; envelope to attack-and-hold.  AY 1 is left selected.
  57+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  58+ E000              init_audio:
  59+ E000 3E 01                ld a,AY_CHIP_3
  60+ E002 CD 0C E0             call ay_init_chip
  61+ E005 3E 02                ld a,AY_CHIP_2
  62+ E007 CD 0C E0             call ay_init_chip
  63+ E00A 3E 03                ld a,AY_CHIP_1
  64+ E00C                      ; fall through
  65+ E00C
  66+ E00C              ; A = chip (AY_CHIP_n): select it in stereo, clear it, mixer and envelope
  67+ E00C              ay_init_chip:
  68+ E00C CD 28 E0             call ay_select
  69+ E00F 16 0D                ld d,AY_REGS-1                  ; registers 13..0 = 0
  70+ E011 7A           .clr:   ld a,d
  71+ E012 1E 00                ld e,0
  72+ E014 CD 30 E0             call ay_write
  73+ E017 15                   dec d
  74+ E018 F2 11 E0             jp p,.clr
  75+ E01B 3E 07                ld a,AY_MIXER
  76+ E01D 1E 38                ld e,AY_MIXER_INIT
  77+ E01F CD 30 E0             call ay_write
  78+ E022 3E 0D                ld a,AY_ENV_SHAPE
  79+ E024 1E 0D                ld e,AY_ENV_ATTACK_HOLD
  80+ E026 18 08                jr ay_write
  81+ E028
  82+ E028              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83+ E028              ; A = chip (AY_CHIP_n): select it, with both outputs on
  84+ E028              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  85+ E028              ay_select:
  86+ E028 F6 FC                or AY_SELECT|AY_LEFT|AY_RIGHT
  87+ E02A 01 FD FF             ld bc,AY_CTRL_PORT
  88+ E02D ED 79                out (c),a
  89+ E02F C9                   ret
  90+ E030
  91+ E030              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  92+ E030              ; A = register, E = value: write it to the selected chip.  Keeps D, E, HL.
  93+ E030              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  94+ E030              ay_write:
  95+ E030 01 FD FF             ld bc,AY_CTRL_PORT
  96+ E033 ED 79                out (c),a
  97+ E035 06 BF                ld b,high AY_DATA_PORT
  98+ E037 ED 59                out (c),e
  99+ E039 C9                   ret
 100+ E03A
# file closed: audio.asm

1429  E03A              ; the core page in slot 7 (main_loop has the keys there while they run).

   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - player page (8K page PAGE_PLAYER, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Keyboard input, movement along the heading, world-edge clamping and
   5+ C000              ; building collision.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,
   6+ C000              ; PAGE_PLAYER, with the keys page (key_held) in slot 7.
   7+ C000              ; Uses the resident helpers get_sin, fmul and bld_height; camera state (cam_x/z/y, yaw, pitch, speed) is shared and
   8+ C000              ; stays in zpolice.asm.
   9+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  10+ C000
  11+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  12+ C000              ; Player input and movement
  13+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  14+ C000              update_player:
  15+ C000 AF                   xor a
  16+ C001 D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; border back to black
  17+ C003                      ; remember position in case we fly into something
  18+ C003 21 B8 A7             ld hl,cam_x                     ; (and the heading: turning swings
  19+ C006 11 89 C2             ld de,old_pos                   ; the VTOL's nose and tail)
  20+ C009 01 0A 00             ld bc,10                        ; cam_x(3) cam_z(3) cam_y(2) yaw(2)
  21+ C00C ED B0                ldir
  22+ C00E 3A D6 A7             ld a,(vtol_dead)
  23+ C011 B7                   or a
  24+ C012 C2 0E C2             jp nz,vtol_wreck                ; destroyed: out of control
  25+ C015
  26+ C015                      ; (keys: kbd_scan has read them; actions from keys.asm)
  27+ C015                      ; --- speed ---
  28+ C015 3E 00                ld a,K_SPEED_UP                 ; (by SPEED_STEP, 1)
  29+ C017 CD 52 E0             call key_held
  30+ C01A 28 0B                jr z,.nq
  31+ C01C 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
  32+ C01F FE 1C                cp SPEED_MAX
  33+ C021 28 04                jr z,.nq
  34+ C023 3C                   inc a
  35+ C024 32 C4 A7             ld (speed),a
  36+ C027 3E 01        .nq:    ld a,K_SPEED_DN
  37+ C029 CD 52 E0             call key_held
  38+ C02C 28 0B                jr z,.na
  39+ C02E 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
  40+ C031 FE FA                cp (SPEED_MIN) & $FF
  41+ C033 28 04                jr z,.na
  42+ C035 3D                   dec a
  43+ C036 32 C4 A7             ld (speed),a
  44+ C039              .na:    ; --- air brake: while held, the speed falls AIRBRAKE_STEP a frame
  45+ C039                      ; towards 0, going forwards or backwards.  Each braking frame tips the
  46+ C039                      ; nose one pitch step, as a nose key press does: up when braking
  47+ C039                      ; forward motion, down when braking reverse.  The pitch damper waits
  48+ C039                      ; while it brakes (ab_on), as it does while a nose key is held.
  49+ C039 AF                   xor a
  50+ C03A 32 94 C2             ld (ab_on),a
  51+ C03D 3E 02                ld a,K_AIRBRAKE
  52+ C03F CD 52 E0             call key_held
  53+ C042 28 3C                jr z,.nw
  54+ C044 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
  55+ C047 B7                   or a
  56+ C048 28 36                jr z,.nw                        ; stopped: nothing to brake
  57+ C04A 32 94 C2             ld (ab_on),a                    ; (non-zero: braking this frame)
  58+ C04D FA 69 C0             jp m,.rev
  59+ C050 D6 02                sub AIRBRAKE_STEP               ; forwards: slower, not past 0
  60+ C052 30 01                jr nc,.fwd
  61+ C054 AF                   xor a
  62+ C055 32 C4 A7     .fwd:   ld (speed),a
  63+ C058 AF                   xor a
  64+ C059 32 C2 A7             ld (pitch_frac),a               ; whole steps, as the keys
  65+ C05C 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
  66+ C05F FE 18                cp PMAX
  67+ C061 28 1D                jr z,.nw
  68+ C063 3C                   inc a                           ; nose up
  69+ C064 32 C3 A7             ld (pitch),a
  70+ C067 18 17                jr .nw
  71+ C069 C6 02        .rev:   add a,AIRBRAKE_STEP             ; backwards: towards 0, not past it
  72+ C06B 30 01                jr nc,.bk
  73+ C06D AF                   xor a
  74+ C06E 32 C4 A7     .bk:    ld (speed),a
  75+ C071 AF                   xor a
  76+ C072 32 C2 A7             ld (pitch_frac),a
  77+ C075 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
  78+ C078 FE E8                cp (-PMAX) & $FF
  79+ C07A 28 04                jr z,.nw
  80+ C07C 3D                   dec a                           ; nose down
  81+ C07D 32 C3 A7             ld (pitch),a
  82+ C080              .nw:
  83+ C080                      ; --- yaw: YAW_RATE a frame, but YAW_FINE on the frame a turn key is
  84+ C080                      ; first pressed (a tap turns 1 degree: fine control)
  85+ C080 3E 03                ld a,K_LEFT
  86+ C082 CD 52 E0             call key_held
  87+ C085 28 16                jr z,.ns
  88+ C087 11 80 01             ld de,YAW_RATE
  89+ C08A 3E 03                ld a,K_LEFT
  90+ C08C CD 59 E0             call key_pressed
  91+ C08F 28 03                jr z,.lr
  92+ C091 11 B6 00             ld de,YAW_FINE
  93+ C094 2A C0 A7     .lr:    ld hl,(yaw_frac)                ; 8.8 heading
  94+ C097 B7                   or a
  95+ C098 ED 52                sbc hl,de
  96+ C09A 22 C0 A7             ld (yaw_frac),hl
  97+ C09D 3E 04        .ns:    ld a,K_RIGHT
  98+ C09F CD 52 E0             call key_held
  99+ C0A2 28 14                jr z,.nf
 100+ C0A4 11 80 01             ld de,YAW_RATE
 101+ C0A7 3E 04                ld a,K_RIGHT
 102+ C0A9 CD 59 E0             call key_pressed
 103+ C0AC 28 03                jr z,.rr
 104+ C0AE 11 B6 00             ld de,YAW_FINE
 105+ C0B1 2A C0 A7     .rr:    ld hl,(yaw_frac)
 106+ C0B4 19                   add hl,de
 107+ C0B5 22 C0 A7             ld (yaw_frac),hl
 108+ C0B8              .nf:
 109+ C0B8                      ; --- pitch ---
 110+ C0B8 3E 08                ld a,K_NOSE_DN
 111+ C0BA CD 52 E0             call key_held
 112+ C0BD 28 0F                jr z,.ng
 113+ C0BF AF                   xor a
 114+ C0C0 32 C2 A7             ld (pitch_frac),a               ; keys move in whole steps
 115+ C0C3 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
 116+ C0C6 FE E8                cp (-PMAX) & $FF
 117+ C0C8 28 04                jr z,.ng
 118+ C0CA 3D                   dec a
 119+ C0CB 32 C3 A7             ld (pitch),a
 120+ C0CE 3E 07        .ng:    ld a,K_NOSE_UP
 121+ C0D0 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 122+ C0D3 28 0F                jr z,.nt
 123+ C0D5 AF                   xor a
 124+ C0D6 32 C2 A7             ld (pitch_frac),a
 125+ C0D9 3A C3 A7             ld a,(pitch)
 126+ C0DC FE 18                cp PMAX
 127+ C0DE 28 04                jr z,.nt
 128+ C0E0 3C                   inc a
 129+ C0E1 32 C3 A7             ld (pitch),a
 130+ C0E4              .nt:    ; --- pitch damper: ease back to level when the pitch keys are released
 131+ C0E4                      ; (and the air brake isn't tipping the nose)
 132+ C0E4 3A 94 C2             ld a,(ab_on)
 133+ C0E7 B7                   or a
 134+ C0E8 20 1B                jr nz,.held
 135+ C0EA 3E 07                ld a,K_NOSE_UP
 136+ C0EC CD 52 E0             call key_held
 137+ C0EF 20 14                jr nz,.held
 138+ C0F1 3E 08                ld a,K_NOSE_DN
 139+ C0F3 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 140+ C0F6 20 0D                jr nz,.held
 141+ C0F8 21 93 C2             ld hl,pd_count
 142+ C0FB 35                   dec (hl)
 143+ C0FC 20 0C                jr nz,.pdx
 144+ C0FE 36 01                ld (hl),PDAMP_FRAMES
 145+ C100 CD 5C C2             call pitch_damp
 146+ C103 18 05                jr .pdx
 147+ C105 3E 01        .held:  ld a,PDAMP_FRAMES               ; a full interval after letting go
 148+ C107 32 93 C2             ld (pd_count),a
 149+ C10A              .pdx:
 150+ C10A                      ; --- height ---
 151+ C10A 3E 05                ld a,K_CLIMB
 152+ C10C CD 52 E0             call key_held
 153+ C10F 28 10                jr z,.ne
 154+ C111 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
 155+ C114 11 04 00             ld de,CLIMB_RATE
 156+ C117 19                   add hl,de
 157+ C118 11 C0 00             ld de,MAX_Y
 158+ C11B CD E6 C1             call min_hl_de
 159+ C11E 22 BE A7             ld (cam_y),hl
 160+ C121 3E 06        .ne:    ld a,K_DESCEND
 161+ C123 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 162+ C126 28 19                jr z,.nd
 163+ C128 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
 164+ C12B 11 FC FF             ld de,-CLIMB_RATE
 165+ C12E 19                   add hl,de
 166+ C12F 11 08 00             ld de,MIN_Y
 167+ C132 CD EE C1             call max_hl_de
 168+ C135 E5                   push hl                         ; and not below the floor under the
 169+ C136 CD 09 A5             call floor_y                    ; VTOL: it lands on the road or the
 170+ C139 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; highest roof under it (no collision)
 171+ C13A E1                   pop hl
 172+ C13B CD EE C1             call max_hl_de
 173+ C13E 22 BE A7             ld (cam_y),hl
 174+ C141              .nd:
 175+ C141                      ; --- move along heading: pos += speed * (sin, cos) ---
 176+ C141 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 177+ C144 B7                   or a
 178+ C145 28 30                jr z,.moved
 179+ C147 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)
 180+ C14A CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 181+ C14D EB                   ex de,hl
 182+ C14E 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 183+ C151 67                   ld h,a
 184+ C152 2E 00                ld l,0
 185+ C154 CD 7F A7             call fmul                       ; HL = speed*sin in 1/256 units
 186+ C157 DD 21 B8 A7          ld ix,cam_x
 187+ C15B CD 9F C1             call add_pos
 188+ C15E 3A C1 A7             ld a,(yaw)
 189+ C161 C6 40                add a,64
 190+ C163 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 191+ C166 EB                   ex de,hl
 192+ C167 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 193+ C16A 67                   ld h,a
 194+ C16B 2E 00                ld l,0
 195+ C16D CD 7F A7             call fmul
 196+ C170 DD 21 BB A7          ld ix,cam_z
 197+ C174 CD 9F C1             call add_pos
 198+ C177              .moved:
 199+ C177 CD F6 C1             call check_collision
 200+ C17A C8                   ret z
 201+ C17B                      ; crashed - restore and stop, taking |speed| / 4 (a 2-bit shift
 202+ C17B                      ; right, rounded down) as damage
 203+ C17B 21 89 C2             ld hl,old_pos
 204+ C17E 11 B8 A7             ld de,cam_x
 205+ C181 01 0A 00             ld bc,10
 206+ C184 ED B0                ldir
 207+ C186 3A C4 A7             ld a,(speed)
 208+ C189 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 209+ C18B 28 02                jr z,.dmg
 210+ C18D ED 44                neg                             ; (reversing into it)
 211+ C18F CB 3F        .dmg:   srl a
 212+ C191 CB 3F                srl a
 213+ C193 CD F5 A4             call vtol_damage                ; (zpolice.asm)
 214+ C196 AF                   xor a
 215+ C197 32 C4 A7             ld (speed),a
 216+ C19A 3E 03                ld a,C_WARN
 217+ C19C D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; red border flash
 218+ C19E C9                   ret
 219+ C19F
 220+ C19F              ; IX -> 24-bit position (frac, lo, hi). HL = signed delta in 1/256 units.
 221+ C19F              ; Clamps the integer part to WORLD_MIN..WORLD_MAX.
 222+ C19F              add_pos:
 223+ C19F 7C                   ld a,h
 224+ C1A0 17                   rla
 225+ C1A1 9F                   sbc a,a
 226+ C1A2 4F                   ld c,a                          ; sign extension
 227+ C1A3 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 228+ C1A6 85                   add a,l
 229+ C1A7 DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 230+ C1AA DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 231+ C1AD 8C                   adc a,h
 232+ C1AE DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 233+ C1B1 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 234+ C1B4 89                   adc a,c
 235+ C1B5 DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 236+ C1B8 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 237+ C1BB DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 238+ C1BE CB 7C                bit 7,h
 239+ C1C0 20 1B                jr nz,.lo
 240+ C1C2 11 40 00             ld de,WORLD_MIN
 241+ C1C5 B7                   or a
 242+ C1C6 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 243+ C1C8 38 13                jr c,.lo
 244+ C1CA DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 245+ C1CD DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 246+ C1D0 11 C0 3F             ld de,WORLD_MAX
 247+ C1D3 CD E6 C1             call min_hl_de
 248+ C1D6 DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 249+ C1D9 DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 250+ C1DC C9                   ret
 251+ C1DD DD 36 01 40  .lo:    ld (ix+1),WORLD_MIN & $FF
 252+ C1E1 DD 36 02 00          ld (ix+2),WORLD_MIN >> 8
 253+ C1E5 C9                   ret
 254+ C1E6
 255+ C1E6              ; Unsigned 16-bit clamps
 256+ C1E6              min_hl_de:
 257+ C1E6 E5                   push hl
 258+ C1E7 B7                   or a
 259+ C1E8 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 260+ C1EA E1                   pop hl
 261+ C1EB D8                   ret c
 262+ C1EC EB                   ex de,hl
 263+ C1ED C9                   ret
 264+ C1EE
 265+ C1EE              max_hl_de:
 266+ C1EE E5                   push hl
 267+ C1EF B7                   or a
 268+ C1F0 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 269+ C1F2 E1                   pop hl
 270+ C1F3 D0                   ret nc
 271+ C1F4 EB                   ex de,hl
 272+ C1F5 C9                   ret
 273+ C1F6
 274+ C1F6              ; Returns NZ if the VTOL hits a building: its footprint (10 m x 3 m, turned
 275+ C1F6              ; to the heading; vtol_ground) is over a roof less than COLL_H below it.
 276+ C1F6              check_collision:
 277+ C1F6 CD 74 A5             call vtol_ground                ; HL = highest ground under it
 278+ C1F9 C8                   ret z                           ; all road
 279+ C1FA 11 0C 00             ld de,COLL_H
 280+ C1FD 19                   add hl,de
 281+ C1FE ED 5B BE A7          ld de,(cam_y)
 282+ C202 B7                   or a
 283+ C203 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; height+M - camY
 284+ C205 38 05                jr c,.no
 285+ C207 28 03                jr z,.no
 286+ C209 F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ: collision
 287+ C20B C9                   ret
 288+ C20C AF           .no:    xor a
 289+ C20D C9                   ret
 290+ C20E
 291+ C20E              ; The VTOL has been destroyed (vtol_dead): the controls do nothing.  It
 292+ C20E              ; spins DEAD_SPIN a frame and falls, a unit a frame faster each frame (up
 293+ C20E              ; to DEAD_FALL_MAX), until it hits the ground under it (floor_y: the road,
 294+ C20E              ; or the highest roof under its footprint).  Once down it lies still for
 295+ C20E              ; DEAD_PAUSE frames, then sets game_over (main_loop: the GAME OVER screen,
 296+ C20E              ; then the main menu).
 297+ C20E              vtol_wreck:
 298+ C20E AF                   xor a
 299+ C20F 32 C4 A7             ld (speed),a
 300+ C212 3A D7 A7             ld a,(dead_down)
 301+ C215 B7                   or a
 302+ C216 20 39                jr nz,.down
 303+ C218 2A C0 A7             ld hl,(yaw_frac)                ; spin
 304+ C21B 11 00 08             ld de,DEAD_SPIN
 305+ C21E 19                   add hl,de
 306+ C21F 22 C0 A7             ld (yaw_frac),hl
 307+ C222 3A D8 A7             ld a,(dead_vy)                  ; fall, faster each frame
 308+ C225 FE 08                cp DEAD_FALL_MAX
 309+ C227 30 04                jr nc,.vy
 310+ C229 3C                   inc a
 311+ C22A 32 D8 A7             ld (dead_vy),a
 312+ C22D 5F           .vy:    ld e,a
 313+ C22E 16 00                ld d,0
 314+ C230 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
 315+ C233 B7                   or a
 316+ C234 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 317+ C236 30 03                jr nc,.y
 318+ C238 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 319+ C23B E5           .y:     push hl
 320+ C23C CD 09 A5             call floor_y                    ; HL = the ground under it
 321+ C23F EB                   ex de,hl
 322+ C240 E1                   pop hl
 323+ C241 B7                   or a
 324+ C242 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 325+ C244 19                   add hl,de
 326+ C245 30 06                jr nc,.set                      ; still above it
 327+ C247 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; hit the ground: on it
 328+ C248 3E 01                ld a,1
 329+ C24A 32 D7 A7             ld (dead_down),a
 330+ C24D 22 BE A7     .set:   ld (cam_y),hl
 331+ C250 C9                   ret
 332+ C251 21 D9 A7     .down:  ld hl,dead_wait                 ; on the ground: the pause, then
 333+ C254 35                   dec (hl)                        ; GAME OVER
 334+ C255 C0                   ret nz
 335+ C256 3E 01                ld a,1
 336+ C258 32 DC A7             ld (game_over),a
 337+ C25B C9                   ret
 338+ C25C
 339+ C25C              ; One damping step: move the signed 8.8 pitch (pitch_frac/pitch) towards 0
 340+ C25C              ; by max(PDAMP_MIN, |whole pitch| * PDAMP_K), stopping exactly at 0.
 341+ C25C              ; Calculated rather than looked up: the Z80N MUL makes it as quick as a
 342+ C25C              ; table, and the curve stays editable through the two equates.
 343+ C25C              pitch_damp:
 344+ C25C 2A C2 A7             ld hl,(pitch_frac)
 345+ C25F 7C                   ld a,h
 346+ C260 B5                   or l
 347+ C261 C8                   ret z                           ; already level
 348+ C262 7C                   ld a,h
 349+ C263 F5                   push af                         ; sign
 350+ C264 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 351+ C267 54                   ld d,h                          ; whole part of |pitch|
 352+ C268 1E 2B                ld e,PDAMP_K
 353+ C26A ED 30                mul d,e                         ; DE = step
 354+ C26C 7A                   ld a,d
 355+ C26D B7                   or a
 356+ C26E 20 08                jr nz,.sub
 357+ C270 7B                   ld a,e
 358+ C271 FE 20                cp PDAMP_MIN
 359+ C273 30 03                jr nc,.sub
 360+ C275 11 20 00             ld de,PDAMP_MIN
 361+ C278 B7           .sub:   or a
 362+ C279 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 363+ C27B 30 03                jr nc,.sgn
 364+ C27D 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; would pass level: stop at 0
 365+ C280 F1           .sgn:   pop af
 366+ C281 17                   rla                             ; carry = was nose down
 367+ C282 DC 73 A7             call c,neg_hl
 368+ C285 22 C2 A7             ld (pitch_frac),hl
 369+ C288 C9                   ret
 370+ C289
 371+ C289              ;==============================================================================
 372+ C289              ; Player page data
 373+ C289              ;==============================================================================
 374+ C289 00 00 00...  old_pos:    ds 10                       ; cam_x, cam_z, cam_y, yaw before the move
 375+ C293 01           pd_count:   db PDAMP_FRAMES             ; frames to the next damping step
 376+ C294 00           ab_on:      db 0                        ; non-zero: the air brake acted this frame
 377+ C295
# file closed: player.asm
