1436  C295                      MMU LOGIC_SLOT e, PAGE_WEAPONS, LOGIC_ADDR
1437  C000                      ORG LOGIC_ADDR
1438  C000                      INCLUDE "weapons.asm"
# file opened: weapons.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - weapons page (8K page PAGE_WEAPONS, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Player weapons.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
   5+ C000              ; (the keys page in slot 7) after
   6+ C000              ; the frame is set up (it uses sin_y/cos_y/sin_p/cos_p) and the zombies have
   7+ C000              ; moved.  Bullet state lives in the resident `bullets` table so that the city
   8+ C000              ; page can draw the tracers (draw_bullets).
   9+ C000              ;
  10+ C000              ; Keys 1-5 select the weapon slot that SPACE fires (wpn_sel: 1 = the centre
  11+ C000              ; slot, 2-5 = the wing pylons).  An empty slot isn't selected: the HUD
  12+ C000              ; flashes it instead (wflash_slot / wflash_t).  What is fitted where is set
  13+ C000              ; on the load-out screen between levels (loadout.asm).
  14+ C000              ;
  15+ C000              ; Machine gun (WPN_GUN, the centre slot): hold SPACE to fire one round every
  16+ C000              ; GUN_INTERVAL frames.  Rounds leave the ship's centre line 0.5 m below the
  17+ C000              ; camera and fly straight along the view direction at 500 m/s until they hit
  18+ C000              ; something.
  19+ C000              ;
  20+ C000              ; Collision, every frame for every round in flight: the path from its last
  21+ C000              ; position to its new one (BULLET_STEP long) is walked cell by cell through
  22+ C000              ; the city grid, in order (a 2D DDA on the horizontal distance s along the
  23+ C000              ; path).  The first thing met ends the path:
  24+ C000              ;   * a building cell: hit where the path enters the cell if it is below the
  25+ C000              ;     roof there (a wall), otherwise where it drops below the roof (the roof);
  26+ C000              ;   * a street cell marked as holding zombies (CELL_ZOMBIE): those in it, each a box
  27+ C000              ;     0.5 m wide and 2 m tall, nearest along the path first;
  28+ C000              ;   * the ground, where the path reaches height 0;
  29+ C000              ;   * leaving the GRID x GRID map: the round is gone.
  30+ C000              ; A round that hits is drawn once at the impact point, then freed.  A zombie
  31+ C000              ; that is hit loses B_DMG hit points; at 0 it is killed (its map blip
  32+ C000              ; removed, its record made inactive).  A rocket also bursts (rocket_blast).
  33+ C000              ;
  34+ C000              ; Rocket pod (WPN_ROCKETS, a wing pylon, ROCKET_SHOTS rockets): rockets are
  35+ C000              ; rounds too (B_KIND = BK_ROCKET), at 150 m/s and drawn as a short white
  36+ C000              ; streak with a yellow trail.  Wherever one stops (a building, the ground,
  37+ C000              ; a zombie) rocket_blast does BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human and base tile
  38+ C000              ; within BLAST_R on each axis, and sprays BLAST_PARTS orange particles.
  39+ C000              ;
  40+ C000              ; Bofors cannon (WPN_BOFORS, a wing pylon, BOFORS_SHOTS shells): the machine
  41+ C000              ; gun's rounds (B_KIND = BK_BOFORS, drawn as tracers) one every
  42+ C000              ; BOFORS_INTERVAL frames; each bursts where it stops: BOFORS_DMG within
  43+ C000              ; BOFORS_R (3 m) on each axis, BOFORS_PARTS particles.
  44+ C000              ;
  45+ C000              ; Proximity cannon (WPN_PROX, a wing pylon, PROX_SHOTS shells): the Bofors
  46+ C000              ; cannon (B_KIND = BK_PROX), its shells also bursting when they pass within
  47+ C000              ; PROX_R (2 m) of a monster (b_pr widens the monster hit boxes,
  48+ C000              ; cell_zombies); the burst does PROX_DMG within PROX_R on each axis.
  49+ C000              ;
  50+ C000              ; Missile pod (WPN_MISSILE, a wing pylon, MISSILE_SHOTS missiles): as the
  51+ C000              ; rockets (B_KIND = BK_MISSILE, drawn the same) at half the speed; the burst
  52+ C000              ; does MISSILE_DMG within MISSILE_R (1.0 tile) on each axis, and sprays
  53+ C000              ; MISSILE_PARTS particles.
  54+ C000              ;
  55+ C000              ; Damage: each round carries its damage (B_DMG): machine gun GUN_DMG, chain
  56+ C000              ; gun CHAIN_DMG, the laser LASER_DMG a beam frame, a rocket none directly
  57+ C000              ; (only its blast).  A zombie dies when its hit points (+18) run out.
  58+ C000              ;
  59+ C000              ; Positions are world units (1 tile = 20 m = 256 units, 1 m = 12.8).  Along the path,
  60+ C000              ; x = x0 + s*sinY, z = z0 + s*cosY and y = y0 + s*slope, with sinY/cosY the
  61+ C000              ; S7.8 heading at firing and slope = tan(pitch) in S7.8.
  62+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  63+ C000
  64+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  65+ C000              ; Per frame: fire, then move every round in flight
  66+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  67+ C000              update_weapons:
  68+ C000 CD 56 C5             call part_update                ; age and move the burst particles
  69+ C003 3A D6 A7             ld a,(vtol_dead)
  70+ C006 B7                   or a
  71+ C007 28 05                jr z,.alive
  72+ C009 CD B3 C3             call las_reset                  ; destroyed: no firing, no beam
  73+ C00C 18 20                jr .move
  74+ C00E              .alive:
  75+ C00E                  IF DEBUG_KILLALL
  76+ C00E CD 2E C3             call debug_killall              ; (debug: key 0)
  77+ C011                  ENDIF
  78+ C011 CD FC C2             call wpn_select                 ; (slots can be chosen when landed)
  79+ C014 CD 59 C2             call gun_aim                    ; and the centre gun tilted
  80+ C017 CD 5F A5             call vtol_landed
  81+ C01A 20 08                jr nz,.fire
  82+ C01C CD B3 C3             call las_reset                  ; landed: no firing, no beam, until
  83+ C01F CD C8 C3             call pad_service                ; it takes off; on the pad: repairs
  84+ C022 18 0A                jr .move                        ; and reloads
  85+ C024 AF           .fire:  xor a
  86+ C025 32 2A CC             ld (svc_on),a                   ; (service starts afresh next landing)
  87+ C028 CD 51 C0             call gun_fire
  88+ C02B CD 3B C4             call laser_update
  89+ C02E DD 21 76 A8  .move:  ld ix,bullets
  90+ C032 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
  91+ C034 C5           .lp:    push bc
  92+ C035 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
  93+ C038 B7                   or a
  94+ C039 28 0D                jr z,.nx
  95+ C03B FE 02                cp 2
  96+ C03D 20 06                jr nz,.fly
  97+ C03F DD 36 00 00          ld (ix+B_STATE),0               ; its impact was shown last frame
  98+ C043 18 03                jr .nx
  99+ C045 CD C5 C5     .fly:   call bullet_step
 100+ C048 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC
 101+ C04B DD 19                add ix,de
 102+ C04D C1                   pop bc
 103+ C04E 10 E4                djnz .lp
 104+ C050 C9                   ret
 105+ C051
 106+ C051              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 107+ C051              ; Machine gun / chain gun: SPACE held with one of them in the centre slot,
 108+ C051              ; ammo, and a free round
 109+ C051              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 110+ C051              gun_fire:
 111+ C051 21 29 CC             ld hl,gun_wait
 112+ C054 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 113+ C055 B7                   or a
 114+ C056 28 02                jr z,.ready
 115+ C058 35                   dec (hl)                        ; still cycling
 116+ C059 C9                   ret
 117+ C05A 3E 09        .ready: ld a,K_FIRE
 118+ C05C CD 52 E0             call key_held
 119+ C05F C8                   ret z                           ; fire not pressed
 120+ C060 CD BE C3             call sel_slot
 121+ C063 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; the selected slot's weapon
 122+ C064 FE 03                cp WPN_CHAIN
 123+ C066 28 7E                jr z,.chain
 124+ C068 FE 04                cp WPN_ROCKETS
 125+ C06A 28 27                jr z,.rocket
 126+ C06C FE 05                cp WPN_MISSILE
 127+ C06E 28 3B                jr z,.missile
 128+ C070 FE 07                cp WPN_BOFORS
 129+ C072 28 53                jr z,.bofors
 130+ C074 FE 08                cp WPN_PROX
 131+ C076 28 4B                jr z,.prox
 132+ C078 FE 01                cp WPN_GUN
 133+ C07A C0                   ret nz
 134+ C07B CD 86 C1             call free_round                 ; machine gun: one round
 135+ C07E D8                   ret c
 136+ C07F CD 78 C1             call take_ammo
 137+ C082 D8                   ret c
 138+ C083 3E 03                ld a,GUN_INTERVAL-1
 139+ C085 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 140+ C088 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 141+ C08B CD 9A C1             call spawn_round
 142+ C08E DD 36 19 04          ld (ix+B_DMG),GUN_DMG
 143+ C092 C9                   ret
 144+ C093              .rocket:
 145+ C093 CD 86 C1             call free_round                 ; rocket pod: one rocket
 146+ C096 D8                   ret c
 147+ C097 CD 78 C1             call take_ammo
 148+ C09A D8                   ret c
 149+ C09B 3E 06                ld a,ROCKET_INTERVAL-1
 150+ C09D 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 151+ C0A0 21 89 00             ld hl,ROCKET_STEP
 152+ C0A3 CD 9A C1             call spawn_round
 153+ C0A6 DD 36 1A 01          ld (ix+B_KIND),BK_ROCKET        ; no direct damage: its blast
 154+ C0AA C9                   ret
 155+ C0AB              .missile:
 156+ C0AB CD 86 C1             call free_round                 ; missile pod: one missile
 157+ C0AE D8                   ret c
 158+ C0AF CD 78 C1             call take_ammo
 159+ C0B2 D8                   ret c
 160+ C0B3 3E 06                ld a,MISSILE_INTERVAL-1
 161+ C0B5 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 162+ C0B8 21 44 00             ld hl,MISSILE_STEP
 163+ C0BB CD 9A C1             call spawn_round
 164+ C0BE DD 36 1A 02          ld (ix+B_KIND),BK_MISSILE       ; no direct damage: its blast
 165+ C0C2 C9                   ret
 166+ C0C3 3E 04        .prox:  ld a,BK_PROX                    ; proximity cannon: one shell
 167+ C0C5 18 02                jr .shell
 168+ C0C7              .bofors:
 169+ C0C7 3E 03                ld a,BK_BOFORS                  ; Bofors: one shell
 170+ C0C9 32 72 CC     .shell: ld (sh_kind),a
 171+ C0CC CD 86 C1             call free_round
 172+ C0CF D8                   ret c
 173+ C0D0 CD 78 C1             call take_ammo
 174+ C0D3 D8                   ret c
 175+ C0D4 3E 0F                ld a,BOFORS_INTERVAL-1
 176+ C0D6 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 177+ C0D9 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 178+ C0DC CD 9A C1             call spawn_round
 179+ C0DF 3A 72 CC             ld a,(sh_kind)
 180+ C0E2 DD 77 1A             ld (ix+B_KIND),a                ; no direct damage: its burst
 181+ C0E5 C9                   ret
 182+ C0E6                      ASSERT PROX_INTERVAL == BOFORS_INTERVAL
 183+ C0E6              .chain: ; chain gun: one round every CHAIN_INTERVAL frames
 184+ C0E6 CD 86 C1             call free_round
 185+ C0E9 D8                   ret c
 186+ C0EA CD 78 C1             call take_ammo
 187+ C0ED D8                   ret c
 188+ C0EE 3E 00                ld a,CHAIN_INTERVAL-1
 189+ C0F0 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 190+ C0F3                      ; fall through
 191+ C0F3
 192+ C0F3              ; A chain gun round from IX: the view direction turned by a random angle
 193+ C0F3              ; of -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV steps (1/256 rad) sideways and up/down
 194+ C0F3              chain_round:
 195+ C0F3 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 196+ C0F6 CD 9A C1             call spawn_round
 197+ C0F9 DD 36 19 02          ld (ix+B_DMG),CHAIN_DMG
 198+ C0FD CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 199+ C100 E5                   push hl
 200+ C101 7D                   ld a,l
 201+ C102 CD 69 C1             call dev_n                      ; sideways: rotate (sin, cos) by d/256
 202+ C105 B7                   or a
 203+ C106 28 3F                jr z,.v
 204+ C108 67                   ld h,a
 205+ C109 2E 00                ld l,0
 206+ C10B 22 39 CC             ld (cg_d),hl                    ; d * 256
 207+ C10E DD 5E 09             ld e,(ix+B_COS)                 ; sin += round(d * cos / 256)
 208+ C111 DD 56 0A             ld d,(ix+B_COS+1)
 209+ C114 CD 5C C1             call dmul
 210+ C117 22 3B CC             ld (cg_s),hl
 211+ C11A 2A 39 CC             ld hl,(cg_d)                    ; cos -= round(d * sin / 256)
 212+ C11D DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)
 213+ C120 DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 214+ C123 CD 5C C1             call dmul
 215+ C126 EB                   ex de,hl
 216+ C127 DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 217+ C12A DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 218+ C12D B7                   or a
 219+ C12E ED 52                sbc hl,de
 220+ C130 DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 221+ C133 DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 222+ C136 DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 223+ C139 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 224+ C13C ED 5B 3B CC          ld de,(cg_s)
 225+ C140 19                   add hl,de
 226+ C141 DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 227+ C144 DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 228+ C147 E1           .v:     pop hl                          ; up/down: slope (tan) +/- d/256
 229+ C148 7C                   ld a,h
 230+ C149 CD 69 C1             call dev_n
 231+ C14C DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 232+ C14F DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
 233+ C152 CD 72 C1             call add_sa
 234+ C155 DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 235+ C158 DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 236+ C15B C9                   ret
 237+ C15C
 238+ C15C              ; HL = d * 256, DE = S7.8 value -> HL = d * value / 256, rounded
 239+ C15C              dmul:
 240+ C15C CD 7F A7             call fmul                       ; HL = d * value (exact)
 241+ C15F 11 80 00             ld de,128
 242+ C162 19                   add hl,de
 243+ C163 6C                   ld l,h                          ; >> 8, keeping the sign
 244+ C164 7C                   ld a,h
 245+ C165 17                   rla
 246+ C166 9F                   sbc a,a
 247+ C167 67                   ld h,a
 248+ C168 C9                   ret
 249+ C169
 250+ C169              ; A = random byte -> A = -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV, evenly spread
 251+ C169              dev_n:
 252+ C169 57                   ld d,a
 253+ C16A 1E 13                ld e,2*CHAIN_DEV+1
 254+ C16C ED 30                mul d,e
 255+ C16E 7A                   ld a,d
 256+ C16F D6 09                sub CHAIN_DEV
 257+ C171 C9                   ret
 258+ C172
 259+ C172              ; HL = HL + A (signed)
 260+ C172              add_sa:
 261+ C172 5F                   ld e,a
 262+ C173 17                   rla
 263+ C174 9F                   sbc a,a
 264+ C175 57                   ld d,a
 265+ C176 19                   add hl,de
 266+ C177 C9                   ret
 267+ C178
 268+ C178              ; One round from the selected slot's ammo: carry set if it is empty.
 269+ C178              ; AMMO_INF (255) is never counted down.
 270+ C178              take_ammo:
 271+ C178 CD BE C3             call sel_slot
 272+ C17B 23                   inc hl                          ; its ammo counter
 273+ C17C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 274+ C17D FE FF                cp AMMO_INF
 275+ C17F C8                   ret z                           ; infinite (carry clear)
 276+ C180 B7                   or a
 277+ C181 37                   scf
 278+ C182 C8                   ret z                           ; empty
 279+ C183 35                   dec (hl)
 280+ C184 B7                   or a
 281+ C185 C9                   ret
 282+ C186
 283+ C186              ; IX = a free round, carry clear; carry set if all are in flight
 284+ C186              free_round:
 285+ C186 DD 21 76 A8          ld ix,bullets
 286+ C18A 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
 287+ C18C 11 1B 00             ld de,BREC
 288+ C18F DD 7E 00     .f:     ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 289+ C192 B7                   or a
 290+ C193 C8                   ret z                           ; (carry clear)
 291+ C194 DD 19                add ix,de
 292+ C196 10 F7                djnz .f
 293+ C198 37                   scf
 294+ C199 C9                   ret
 295+ C19A              ; Start round IX at the muzzle along the view direction, travelling HL
 296+ C19A              ; units (along its path) per frame
 297+ C19A              spawn_round:
 298+ C19A 22 33 CC             ld (sr_len),hl
 299+ C19D DD 36 00 81          ld (ix+B_STATE),$81             ; in flight, fresh: its first path
 300+ C1A1                                                      ; starts at the muzzle (B_PX..)
 301+ C1A1 AF                   xor a                           ; cell intervals: on its first step
 302+ C1A2 DD 77 15             ld (ix+B_TDX),a
 303+ C1A5 DD 77 16             ld (ix+B_TDX+1),a
 304+ C1A8 DD 77 1A             ld (ix+B_KIND),a                ; a round, no damage (the caller
 305+ C1AB DD 77 19             ld (ix+B_DMG),a                 ; sets them)
 306+ C1AE                      ; muzzle: the ship's centre line, GUN_DY below the camera
 307+ C1AE 2A B9 A7             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 308+ C1B1 DD 75 01             ld (ix+B_X),l
 309+ C1B4 DD 74 02             ld (ix+B_X+1),h
 310+ C1B7 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)
 311+ C1BA 11 FA FF             ld de,GUN_DY
 312+ C1BD 19                   add hl,de
 313+ C1BE DD 75 03             ld (ix+B_Y),l
 314+ C1C1 DD 74 04             ld (ix+B_Y+1),h
 315+ C1C4 2A BC A7             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 316+ C1C7 DD 75 05             ld (ix+B_Z),l
 317+ C1CA DD 74 06             ld (ix+B_Z+1),h
 318+ C1CD DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 319+ C1D0 DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 320+ C1D3 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 321+ C1D6 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 322+ C1D9 DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 323+ C1DC DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 324+ C1DF DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 325+ C1E2 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 326+ C1E5 DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 327+ C1E8 DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 328+ C1EB                      ; direction: the view direction this frame
 329+ C1EB 2A EA AD             ld hl,(sin_y)
 330+ C1EE DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 331+ C1F1 DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 332+ C1F4 2A EC AD             ld hl,(cos_y)
 333+ C1F7 DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 334+ C1FA DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 335+ C1FD 2A EE AD             ld hl,(sin_p)                   ; its climb: the view's pitch, or from
 336+ C200 22 35 CC             ld (sr_sin),hl                  ; the centre slot pitch + gun_tilt
 337+ C203 2A F0 AD             ld hl,(cos_p)
 338+ C206 22 37 CC             ld (sr_cos),hl
 339+ C209 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 340+ C20C FE 00                cp WPN_CENTRE
 341+ C20E 20 1A                jr nz,.pt
 342+ C210 3A DF A7             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 343+ C213 B7                   or a
 344+ C214 28 14                jr z,.pt
 345+ C216 21 C3 A7             ld hl,pitch
 346+ C219 86                   add a,(hl)
 347+ C21A F5                   push af
 348+ C21B CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 349+ C21E 22 35 CC             ld (sr_sin),hl
 350+ C221 F1                   pop af
 351+ C222 C6 40                add a,64
 352+ C224 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 353+ C227 22 37 CC             ld (sr_cos),hl
 354+ C22A 2A 33 CC     .pt:    ld hl,(sr_len)                  ; horizontal distance per frame
 355+ C22D ED 5B 37 CC          ld de,(sr_cos)
 356+ C231 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 357+ C234 DD 75 0D             ld (ix+B_S),l
 358+ C237 DD 74 0E             ld (ix+B_S+1),h
 359+ C23A 2A 35 CC             ld hl,(sr_sin)                  ; slope = sin / cos  (S7.8)
 360+ C23D 7C                   ld a,h
 361+ C23E F5                   push af
 362+ C23F CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 363+ C242 7C                   ld a,h                          ; A:HL = |sin| << 8
 364+ C243 65                   ld h,l
 365+ C244 2E 00                ld l,0
 366+ C246 ED 5B 37 CC          ld de,(sr_cos)
 367+ C24A CD F2 CB             call udiv24
 368+ C24D F1                   pop af
 369+ C24E 17                   rla
 370+ C24F DC 73 A7             call c,neg_hl
 371+ C252 DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 372+ C255 DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 373+ C258 C9                   ret
 374+ C259
 375+ C259              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 376+ C259              ; R / F: tilt the centre gun up / down (GUN_TILT_STEP a frame, to
 377+ C259              ; GUN_TILT_UP up, GUN_TILT_DN down); V: back towards level at GUN_CTR_STEP.
 378+ C259              ; Then sight_y: the gunsight's row for the selected weapon, VIEW_CY minus
 379+ C259              ; 128 * tan(tilt) for the centre slot (the projection's scale), kept
 380+ C259              ; SIGHT_MARGIN rows inside the view.
 381+ C259              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 382+ C259              gun_aim:
 383+ C259 3E 0A                ld a,K_GUN_UP
 384+ C25B CD 52 E0             call key_held
 385+ C25E 28 0E                jr z,.dn
 386+ C260 3A DF A7             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 387+ C263 C6 80                add a,$80                       ; (signed compare)
 388+ C265 FE 8B                cp GUN_TILT_UP+$80
 389+ C267 30 05                jr nc,.dn                       ; at the top
 390+ C269 D6 7F                sub $80-GUN_TILT_STEP
 391+ C26B 32 DF A7             ld (gun_tilt),a
 392+ C26E 3E 10        .dn:    ld a,K_GUN_DN
 393+ C270 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 394+ C273 28 0E                jr z,.ct
 395+ C275 3A DF A7             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 396+ C278 C6 80                add a,$80
 397+ C27A FE 61                cp $80-GUN_TILT_DN+1
 398+ C27C 38 05                jr c,.ct                        ; at the bottom
 399+ C27E D6 81                sub $80+GUN_TILT_STEP
 400+ C280 32 DF A7             ld (gun_tilt),a
 401+ C283 3E 11        .ct:    ld a,K_GUN_CTR
 402+ C285 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 403+ C288 28 1A                jr z,.sight
 404+ C28A 3A DF A7             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 405+ C28D B7                   or a
 406+ C28E 28 14                jr z,.sight
 407+ C290 FA 9B C2             jp m,.neg
 408+ C293 D6 02                sub GUN_CTR_STEP                ; tilted up: down towards 0
 409+ C295 F2 A1 C2             jp p,.st
 410+ C298 AF                   xor a
 411+ C299 18 06                jr .st
 412+ C29B C6 02        .neg:   add a,GUN_CTR_STEP              ; tilted down: up towards 0
 413+ C29D FA A1 C2             jp m,.st
 414+ C2A0 AF                   xor a
 415+ C2A1 32 DF A7     .st:    ld (gun_tilt),a
 416+ C2A4 0E 40        .sight: ld c,VIEW_CY
 417+ C2A6 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 418+ C2A9 FE 00                cp WPN_CENTRE
 419+ C2AB 20 29                jr nz,.sy                       ; a pylon: straight ahead
 420+ C2AD 3A DF A7             ld a,(gun_tilt)
 421+ C2B0 B7                   or a
 422+ C2B1 28 23                jr z,.sy
 423+ C2B3 FA BF C2             jp m,.sd
 424+ C2B6 21 DB C2             ld hl,tilt_rows                 ; up: higher on the screen
 425+ C2B9 ED 31                add hl,a
 426+ C2BB 79                   ld a,c
 427+ C2BC 96                   sub (hl)
 428+ C2BD 18 09                jr .sc
 429+ C2BF ED 44        .sd:    neg                             ; down: lower
 430+ C2C1 21 DB C2             ld hl,tilt_rows
 431+ C2C4 ED 31                add hl,a
 432+ C2C6 79                   ld a,c
 433+ C2C7 86                   add a,(hl)
 434+ C2C8 4F           .sc:    ld c,a
 435+ C2C9 FE 06                cp SIGHT_MARGIN
 436+ C2CB 30 02                jr nc,.lo
 437+ C2CD 0E 06                ld c,SIGHT_MARGIN
 438+ C2CF 79           .lo:    ld a,c
 439+ C2D0 FE 7A                cp VIEW_H-SIGHT_MARGIN
 440+ C2D2 38 02                jr c,.sy
 441+ C2D4 0E 79                ld c,VIEW_H-SIGHT_MARGIN-1
 442+ C2D6 79           .sy:    ld a,c
 443+ C2D7 32 3B A8             ld (sight_y),a
 444+ C2DA C9                   ret
 445+ C2DB
 446+ C2DB              ; round(128 * tan(k * 360/256 deg)), k = 0..GUN_TILT_DN: the gunsight's
 447+ C2DB              ; rows from the centre for a tilt of k
 448+ C2DB 00 03 06 09  tilt_rows:  db 0, 3, 6, 9, 13, 16, 19, 22, 25, 29, 32, 35, 39, 42, 46, 49
 448+ C2DF 0D 10 13 16
 448+ C2E3 19 1D 20 23
 448+ C2E7 27 2A 2E 31
 449+ C2EB 35 39 3D 40              db 53, 57, 61, 64, 68, 73, 77, 81, 86, 90, 95, 100, 105, 110
 449+ C2EF 44 49 4D 51
 449+ C2F3 56 5A 5F 64
 449+ C2F7 69 6E
 450+ C2F9 74 7A 80                 db 116, 122, 128
 451+ C2FC                      ASSERT $ - tilt_rows == GUN_TILT_DN + 1
 452+ C2FC                      ASSERT GUN_TILT_UP <= GUN_TILT_DN
 453+ C2FC
 454+ C2FC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 455+ C2FC              ; Keys 1-5: select the weapon slot SPACE fires (an empty one isn't selected:
 456+ C2FC              ; the HUD flashes it instead)
 457+ C2FC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 458+ C2FC              wpn_select:
 459+ C2FC 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; slot keys, newly pressed
 460+ C2FE 79           .k:     ld a,c
 461+ C2FF C6 0B                add a,K_SLOT1
 462+ C301 CD 59 E0             call key_pressed
 463+ C304 20 07                jr nz,.sel
 464+ C306 0C                   inc c
 465+ C307 79                   ld a,c
 466+ C308 FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 467+ C30A 20 F2                jr nz,.k
 468+ C30C C9                   ret
 469+ C30D 79           .sel:   ld a,c
 470+ C30E 87                   add a,a
 471+ C30F 21 C5 A7             ld hl,wpn_slots
 472+ C312 ED 31                add hl,a
 473+ C314 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 474+ C315 B7                   or a
 475+ C316 20 0A                jr nz,.fitted
 476+ C318 79                   ld a,c                          ; empty: keep the current slot and
 477+ C319 32 D0 A7             ld (wflash_slot),a              ; have the HUD flash the asked-for one
 478+ C31C 3E 1E                ld a,6*WFLASH
 479+ C31E 32 D1 A7             ld (wflash_t),a
 480+ C321 C9                   ret
 481+ C322              .fitted:
 482+ C322 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 483+ C325 B9                   cp c
 484+ C326 C8                   ret z                           ; already selected
 485+ C327 79                   ld a,c
 486+ C328 32 CF A7             ld (wpn_sel),a
 487+ C32B C3 B3 C3             jp las_reset                    ; no beam carried to the new slot
 488+ C32E
 489+ C32E                  IF DEBUG_KILLALL
 490+ C32E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 491+ C32E              ; Debug: key 0 kills every monster at once (zombies, ghouls, ghosts and
 492+ C32E              ; banshees, none left fading) and counts every human still in the city or
 493+ C32E              ; aboard as rescued.  zombie_update then finds the wave wiped out: the next
 494+ C32E              ; wave, or after the last the level complete.
 495+ C32E              ; Key 9 does the same and ends the level, on to the next city's first level
 496+ C32E              ; (level 2 -> 6): level becomes this city's last (level_complete_screen
 497+ C32E              ; moves it on one, or after the last city the game is complete) and the
 498+ C32E              ; wave the level's last.
 499+ C32E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 500+ C32E              debug_killall:
 501+ C32E 21 02 04             ld hl,KB_9
 502+ C331 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
 503+ C334 28 18                jr z,.k0
 504+ C336 3A D2 A7             ld a,(level)                    ; (level-1) mod CITY_LEVELS - CITY_LEVELS
 505+ C339 3D                   dec a
 506+ C33A D6 05        .m:     sub CITY_LEVELS
 507+ C33C 30 FC                jr nc,.m
 508+ C33E ED 44                neg                             ; CITY_LEVELS-1 - (level-1) mod
 509+ C340 3D                   dec a                           ; CITY_LEVELS: levels left in the city
 510+ C341 21 D2 A7             ld hl,level
 511+ C344 86                   add a,(hl)
 512+ C345 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; the city's last level
 513+ C346 3A 65 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_WAVES)
 514+ C349 32 DB A7             ld (wave_number),a              ; its last wave
 515+ C34C 18 07                jr .all
 516+ C34E 21 01 04     .k0:    ld hl,KB_0
 517+ C351 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
 518+ C354 C8                   ret z
 519+ C355 DD 21 CB 2F  .all:   ld ix,zombies
 520+ C359 06 26                ld b,NMON
 521+ C35B C5           .z:     push bc
 522+ C35C CD 11 3B             call zblip_off                  ; (map.asm)
 523+ C35F DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; gone (zflags unmarks its cell)
 524+ C363 11 1F 00             ld de,ZREC
 525+ C366 DD 19                add ix,de
 526+ C368 C1                   pop bc
 527+ C369 10 F0                djnz .z
 528+ C36B 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags                ; nobody attacking
 529+ C36E 06 26                ld b,NMON
 530+ C370 36 00        .a:     ld (hl),FALSE
 531+ C372 23                   inc hl
 532+ C373 10 FB                djnz .a
 533+ C375 DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 534+ C379 06 14                ld b,HUMAN_MAX
 535+ C37B C5           .h:     push bc
 536+ C37C DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 537+ C37F FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 538+ C381 28 04                jr z,.s
 539+ C383 FE 02                cp HS_ABOARD
 540+ C385 20 0D                jr nz,.hn
 541+ C387 0E 00        .s:     ld c,0                          ; dot off,
 542+ C389 CD 74 3B             call hum_dot
 543+ C38C DD 36 06 03          ld (ix+6),HS_SAVED              ; rescued
 544+ C390 21 1F 39             ld hl,saved
 545+ C393 34                   inc (hl)
 546+ C394 11 0D 00     .hn:    ld de,HREC
 547+ C397 DD 19                add ix,de
 548+ C399 C1                   pop bc
 549+ C39A 10 DF                djnz .h
 550+ C39C AF                   xor a
 551+ C39D 32 1E 39             ld (passengers),a
 552+ C3A0 C3 B8 36             jp hum_flags                    ; (no waiting humans' cells marked)
 553+ C3A3                  ENDIF
 554+ C3A3
 555+ C3A3              ; New game: no gun cool-down, no beam
 556+ C3A3              weapons_reset:
 557+ C3A3 AF                   xor a
 558+ C3A4 32 29 CC             ld (gun_wait),a
 559+ C3A7 32 2A CC             ld (svc_on),a
 560+ C3AA 32 DF A7             ld (gun_tilt),a                 ; the centre gun level
 561+ C3AD 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 562+ C3AF 32 3B A8             ld (sight_y),a
 563+ C3B2 AF                   xor a
 564+ C3B3                      ; fall through
 565+ C3B3
 566+ C3B3              ; Stop any laser beam or cool-down
 567+ C3B3              las_reset:
 568+ C3B3 AF                   xor a
 569+ C3B4 32 31 CC             ld (las_on),a
 570+ C3B7 32 32 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 571+ C3BA 32 FE AA             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a
 572+ C3BD C9                   ret
 573+ C3BE
 574+ C3BE              ; HL -> the selected weapon slot (type, then ammo)
 575+ C3BE              sel_slot:
 576+ C3BE 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 577+ C3C1 87                   add a,a
 578+ C3C2 21 C5 A7             ld hl,wpn_slots
 579+ C3C5 ED 31                add hl,a
 580+ C3C7 C9                   ret
 581+ C3C8
 582+ C3C8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 583+ C3C8              ; Landed (update_weapons): on the base pad, repair and reload.  The VTOL
 584+ C3C8              ; gains 1 hit point every REPAIR_FRAMES frames up to VTOL_HP; each slot
 585+ C3C8              ; whose weapon reloads (wpn_cfg, zpolice.asm: its full load and frames a
 586+ C3C8              ; shot) gains a
 587+ C3C8              ; shot every that many frames up to its full load.  The counts start afresh
 588+ C3C8              ; each landing (svc_on), so a shot or hit point takes the whole interval.
 589+ C3C8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 590+ C3C8              pad_service:
 591+ C3C8 CD DF A4             call cam_cell_ptr
 592+ C3CB 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 593+ C3CC E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 594+ C3CE FE 10                cp PAD_CODE
 595+ C3D0 28 05                jr z,.pad
 596+ C3D2 AF                   xor a                           ; landed elsewhere: nothing
 597+ C3D3 32 2A CC             ld (svc_on),a
 598+ C3D6 C9                   ret
 599+ C3D7 3A 2A CC     .pad:   ld a,(svc_on)
 600+ C3DA B7                   or a
 601+ C3DB 20 13                jr nz,.go
 602+ C3DD 3C                   inc a                           ; just landed: start the counts
 603+ C3DE 32 2A CC             ld (svc_on),a
 604+ C3E1 3E 05                ld a,REPAIR_FRAMES
 605+ C3E3 32 2B CC             ld (svc_rep),a
 606+ C3E6 21 2C CC             ld hl,svc_rel
 607+ C3E9 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 608+ C3EB 36 00        .cl:    ld (hl),0                       ; (0: its interval starts next)
 609+ C3ED 23                   inc hl
 610+ C3EE 10 FB                djnz .cl
 611+ C3F0 21 2B CC     .go:    ld hl,svc_rep                   ; repair
 612+ C3F3 35                   dec (hl)
 613+ C3F4 20 0D                jr nz,.rl
 614+ C3F6 36 05                ld (hl),REPAIR_FRAMES
 615+ C3F8 3A D5 A7             ld a,(vtol_hp)
 616+ C3FB FE 32                cp VTOL_HP
 617+ C3FD 30 04                jr nc,.rl                       ; (undamaged)
 618+ C3FF 3C                   inc a
 619+ C400 32 D5 A7             ld (vtol_hp),a
 620+ C403 DD 21 C5 A7  .rl:    ld ix,wpn_slots                 ; reload, slot by slot
 621+ C407 21 2C CC             ld hl,svc_rel
 622+ C40A 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 623+ C40C C5           .sl:    push bc
 624+ C40D DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+WPN_TYPE)
 625+ C410 E5                   push hl
 626+ C411 CD 33 A5             call wpn_cfg_ptr                ; (resident)
 627+ C414 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 628+ C415 4F                   ld c,a                          ; C = its full load (WCFG_LOAD)
 629+ C416 23                   inc hl
 630+ C417 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; frames a shot (WCFG_RELOAD)
 631+ C418 E1                   pop hl
 632+ C419 B7                   or a
 633+ C41A 28 16                jr z,.nx                        ; doesn't reload (or an empty slot)
 634+ C41C 47                   ld b,a                          ; B = frames a shot
 635+ C41D DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+WPN_AMMO)
 636+ C420 B9                   cp c
 637+ C421 30 0D                jr nc,.full
 638+ C423 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 639+ C424 B7                   or a
 640+ C425 20 01                jr nz,.cnt
 641+ C427 70                   ld (hl),b                       ; a new interval
 642+ C428 35           .cnt:   dec (hl)
 643+ C429 20 07                jr nz,.nx
 644+ C42B DD 34 01             inc (ix+WPN_AMMO)               ; one more shot
 645+ C42E 18 02                jr .nx
 646+ C430 36 00        .full:  ld (hl),0
 647+ C432 23           .nx:    inc hl
 648+ C433 DD 23                inc ix
 649+ C435 DD 23                inc ix
 650+ C437 C1                   pop bc
 651+ C438 10 D2                djnz .sl
 652+ C43A C9                   ret
 653+ C43B
 654+ C43B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 655+ C43B              ; Pulse laser: SPACE held with the laser in the centre slot fires a beam for
 656+ C43B              ; LASER_ON frames, then it cools for LASER_COOL frames.  Each beam frame the
 657+ C43B              ; beam is traced from the muzzle along the current view direction (the round
 658+ C43B              ; walk, LASER_RANGE long) to the first building, ground or zombie, killing a
 659+ C43B              ; zombie it reaches.  It is kept in laser_rec as a round (state 3) whose
 660+ C43B              ; path runs from the muzzle to where the beam stops; draw_bullets clips it
 661+ C43B              ; to the view, so it rises from the bottom edge of the screen.
 662+ C43B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 663+ C43B              laser_update:
 664+ C43B 3A 31 CC             ld a,(las_on)
 665+ C43E B7                   or a
 666+ C43F 20 20                jr nz,.beam
 667+ C441 32 FE AA             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a        ; no beam this frame
 668+ C444 3A 32 CC             ld a,(las_cool)
 669+ C447 B7                   or a
 670+ C448 28 05                jr z,.ready
 671+ C44A 3D                   dec a                           ; cooling down
 672+ C44B 32 32 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 673+ C44E C9                   ret
 674+ C44F CD BE C3     .ready: call sel_slot
 675+ C452 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 676+ C453 FE 02                cp WPN_LASER
 677+ C455 C0                   ret nz
 678+ C456 3E 09                ld a,K_FIRE
 679+ C458 CD 52 E0             call key_held
 680+ C45B C8                   ret z
 681+ C45C 3E 03                ld a,LASER_ON
 682+ C45E 32 31 CC             ld (las_on),a
 683+ C461 21 31 CC     .beam:  ld hl,las_on
 684+ C464 35                   dec (hl)
 685+ C465 20 05                jr nz,.on
 686+ C467 3E 08                ld a,LASER_COOL                 ; last beam frame: cool down next
 687+ C469 32 32 CC             ld (las_cool),a
 688+ C46C DD 21 FE AA  .on:    ld ix,laser_rec
 689+ C470 21 00 06             ld hl,LASER_RANGE
 690+ C473 CD 9A C1             call spawn_round                ; (start of path = the muzzle)
 691+ C476 DD 36 19 01          ld (ix+B_DMG),LASER_DMG         ; per beam frame on a target
 692+ C47A CD C5 C5             call bullet_step                ; trace it to the first thing it meets
 693+ C47D DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 694+ C480 B7                   or a
 695+ C481 C8                   ret z                           ; off the map (can't, inside the wall)
 696+ C482 DD 36 00 03          ld (ix+B_STATE),3               ; beam
 697+ C486 C9                   ret
 698+ C487
 699+ C487              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 700+ C487              ; Particles (pool `particles`, drawn by draw_particles): zombie bursts and
 701+ C487              ; rocket / missile bursts
 702+ C487              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 703+ C487              ; IX = a zombie just killed: spray NPART_BURST green particles from its body
 704+ C487              ; (PART_Y up), each with a random velocity: sideways -4..3 units a frame in
 705+ C487              ; x and z, upwards 2..5
 706+ C487              part_burst:
 707+ C487 DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
 708+ C48A DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 709+ C48D 22 77 CC             ld (ps_x),hl
 710+ C490 21 0D 00             ld hl,PART_Y
 711+ C493 22 79 CC             ld (ps_y),hl
 712+ C496 DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 713+ C499 DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 714+ C49C 22 7B CC             ld (ps_z),hl
 715+ C49F 21 3B 08             ld hl,PART_COL0 | PART_LIFE<<8
 716+ C4A2 22 7D CC             ld (ps_col),hl                  ; (and ps_life)
 717+ C4A5 AF                   xor a
 718+ C4A6 32 7F CC             ld (ps_fire),a
 719+ C4A9 0E 0A                ld c,NPART_BURST
 720+ C4AB 18 21                jr part_spray
 721+ C4AD
 722+ C4AD              ; A rocket or missile burst at rb_x/y/z: rb_parts orange particles for
 723+ C4AD              ; FIRE_LIFE frames, flying out faster than a zombie's (x and z -8..7 units
 724+ C4AD              ; a frame, y -2..5)
 725+ C4AD              fire_burst:
 726+ C4AD 2A 80 CC             ld hl,(rb_x)
 727+ C4B0 22 77 CC             ld (ps_x),hl
 728+ C4B3 2A 82 CC             ld hl,(rb_y)
 729+ C4B6 22 79 CC             ld (ps_y),hl
 730+ C4B9 2A 84 CC             ld hl,(rb_z)
 731+ C4BC 22 7B CC             ld (ps_z),hl
 732+ C4BF 21 83 0A             ld hl,FIRE_COL0 | FIRE_LIFE<<8
 733+ C4C2 22 7D CC             ld (ps_col),hl
 734+ C4C5 3E 01                ld a,1
 735+ C4C7 32 7F CC             ld (ps_fire),a
 736+ C4CA 3A 76 CC             ld a,(rb_parts)
 737+ C4CD 4F                   ld c,a
 738+ C4CE                      ; fall through
 739+ C4CE
 740+ C4CE              ; Spray C particles from ps_x/y/z, colour ps_col (age 1), lasting ps_life
 741+ C4CE              ; frames, velocities by ps_fire.  Uses free particles only; if the pool
 742+ C4CE              ; runs out, fewer.  Preserves IX.
 743+ C4CE              part_spray:
 744+ C4CE FD E5                push iy
 745+ C4D0 FD 21 19 AB          ld iy,particles
 746+ C4D4 06 3C                ld b,NPART
 747+ C4D6 FD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(iy+0)
 748+ C4D9 B7                   or a
 749+ C4DA 20 70                jr nz,.nx                       ; in use
 750+ C4DC FD 36 00 01          ld (iy+0),1                     ; age 1: brightest
 751+ C4E0 2A 77 CC             ld hl,(ps_x)
 752+ C4E3 FD 75 01             ld (iy+1),l
 753+ C4E6 FD 74 02             ld (iy+2),h
 754+ C4E9 2A 79 CC             ld hl,(ps_y)
 755+ C4EC FD 75 03             ld (iy+3),l
 756+ C4EF FD 74 04             ld (iy+4),h
 757+ C4F2 2A 7B CC             ld hl,(ps_z)
 758+ C4F5 FD 75 05             ld (iy+5),l
 759+ C4F8 FD 74 06             ld (iy+6),h
 760+ C4FB 2A 7D CC             ld hl,(ps_col)
 761+ C4FE FD 75 0A             ld (iy+10),l                    ; its colour and life
 762+ C501 FD 74 0B             ld (iy+11),h
 763+ C504 C5                   push bc
 764+ C505 CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 765+ C508 3A 7F CC             ld a,(ps_fire)
 766+ C50B B7                   or a
 767+ C50C 20 1E                jr nz,.fire
 768+ C50E 7D                   ld a,l
 769+ C50F E6 07                and 7
 770+ C511 D6 04                sub 4
 771+ C513 FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a                     ; vx -4..3
 772+ C516 7C                   ld a,h
 773+ C517 E6 07                and 7
 774+ C519 D6 04                sub 4
 775+ C51B FD 77 09             ld (iy+9),a                     ; vz -4..3
 776+ C51E 7D                   ld a,l
 777+ C51F 0F                   rrca
 778+ C520 0F                   rrca
 779+ C521 0F                   rrca
 780+ C522 0F                   rrca
 781+ C523 E6 03                and 3
 782+ C525 C6 02                add a,2
 783+ C527 FD 77 08             ld (iy+8),a                     ; vy 2..5 (up)
 784+ C52A 18 1C                jr .v
 785+ C52C 7D           .fire:  ld a,l
 786+ C52D E6 0F                and 15
 787+ C52F D6 08                sub 8
 788+ C531 FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a                     ; vx -8..7
 789+ C534 7C                   ld a,h
 790+ C535 E6 0F                and 15
 791+ C537 D6 08                sub 8
 792+ C539 FD 77 09             ld (iy+9),a                     ; vz -8..7
 793+ C53C 7D                   ld a,l
 794+ C53D 0F                   rrca
 795+ C53E 0F                   rrca
 796+ C53F 0F                   rrca
 797+ C540 0F                   rrca
 798+ C541 E6 07                and 7
 799+ C543 D6 02                sub 2
 800+ C545 FD 77 08             ld (iy+8),a                     ; vy -2..5
 801+ C548 C1           .v:     pop bc
 802+ C549 0D                   dec c
 803+ C54A 28 07                jr z,.done
 804+ C54C 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 805+ C54F FD 19                add iy,de
 806+ C551 10 83                djnz .lp
 807+ C553 FD E1        .done:  pop iy
 808+ C555 C9                   ret
 809+ C556
 810+ C556              ; Age every particle; past its life (+11) it is freed.  Move the rest by
 811+ C556              ; their velocity, with gravity, stopping on the ground.
 812+ C556              part_update:
 813+ C556 DD 21 19 AB          ld ix,particles
 814+ C55A 06 3C                ld b,NPART
 815+ C55C DD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(ix+0)
 816+ C55F B7                   or a
 817+ C560 28 5B                jr z,.nx
 818+ C562 3C                   inc a
 819+ C563 4F                   ld c,a
 820+ C564 DD 7E 0B             ld a,(ix+11)
 821+ C567 B9                   cp c
 822+ C568 79                   ld a,c
 823+ C569 30 01                jr nc,.live
 824+ C56B AF                   xor a                           ; gone
 825+ C56C DD 77 00     .live:  ld (ix+0),a
 826+ C56F B7                   or a
 827+ C570 28 4B                jr z,.nx
 828+ C572 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; x += vx
 829+ C575 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 830+ C578 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 831+ C57B CD 72 C1             call add_sa
 832+ C57E DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 833+ C581 DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 834+ C584 DD 7E 09             ld a,(ix+9)                     ; z += vz
 835+ C587 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+5)
 836+ C58A DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+6)
 837+ C58D CD 72 C1             call add_sa
 838+ C590 DD 75 05             ld (ix+5),l
 839+ C593 DD 74 06             ld (ix+6),h
 840+ C596 DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)                     ; y += vy, vy -= gravity
 841+ C599 DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+3)
 842+ C59C DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+4)
 843+ C59F CD 72 C1             call add_sa
 844+ C5A2 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 845+ C5A4 28 09                jr z,.up
 846+ C5A6 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; on the ground: stays there
 847+ C5A9 DD 36 08 00          ld (ix+8),0
 848+ C5AD 18 08                jr .y
 849+ C5AF DD 7E 08     .up:    ld a,(ix+8)
 850+ C5B2 D6 01                sub PART_GRAV
 851+ C5B4 DD 77 08             ld (ix+8),a
 852+ C5B7 DD 75 03     .y:     ld (ix+3),l
 853+ C5BA DD 74 04             ld (ix+4),h
 854+ C5BD 11 0C 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 855+ C5C0 DD 19                add ix,de
 856+ C5C2 10 98                djnz .lp
 857+ C5C4 C9                   ret
 858+ C5C5
 859+ C5C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 860+ C5C5              ; Move round IX one frame along its path, stopping at the first thing it hits
 861+ C5C5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 862+ C5C5              bullet_step:
 863+ C5C5                      ; start of this frame's path; kept for drawing (B_PX..), except on a
 864+ C5C5                      ; fresh round's first step, which is drawn from the muzzle
 865+ C5C5 DD CB 00 7E          bit 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 866+ C5C9 DD CB 00 BE          res 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 867+ C5CD 20 24                jr nz,.fresh
 868+ C5CF DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 869+ C5D2 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 870+ C5D5 DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 871+ C5D8 DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 872+ C5DB DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 873+ C5DE DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 874+ C5E1 DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 875+ C5E4 DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 876+ C5E7 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 877+ C5EA DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 878+ C5ED DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 879+ C5F0 DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 880+ C5F3 DD 6E 01     .fresh: ld l,(ix+B_X)
 881+ C5F6 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 882+ C5F9 22 3D CC             ld (b_x0),hl
 883+ C5FC DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 884+ C5FF DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 885+ C602 22 3F CC             ld (b_y0),hl
 886+ C605 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 887+ C608 DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 888+ C60B 22 41 CC             ld (b_z0),hl
 889+ C60E DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 890+ C611 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 891+ C614 22 43 CC             ld (b_sin),hl
 892+ C617 DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 893+ C61A DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 894+ C61D 22 45 CC             ld (b_cos),hl
 895+ C620 DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 896+ C623 DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
 897+ C626 22 47 CC             ld (b_slope),hl
 898+ C629 DD 6E 0D             ld l,(ix+B_S)
 899+ C62C DD 66 0E             ld h,(ix+B_S+1)
 900+ C62F 22 49 CC             ld (b_send),hl
 901+ C632 DD 7E 19             ld a,(ix+B_DMG)
 902+ C635 32 6B CC             ld (b_dmg),a
 903+ C638 DD 7E 1A             ld a,(ix+B_KIND)                ; a proximity shell: monsters' hit
 904+ C63B FE 04                cp BK_PROX                      ; boxes PROX_R bigger every way
 905+ C63D 3E 00                ld a,0
 906+ C63F 20 02                jr nz,.pr
 907+ C641 3E 19                ld a,PROX_R
 908+ C643 32 71 CC     .pr:    ld (b_pr),a
 909+ C646 AF                   xor a
 910+ C647 32 4B CC             ld (b_gnd),a
 911+ C64A                      ; does it reach the ground this frame?  s = y0 / -slope
 912+ C64A 2A 47 CC             ld hl,(b_slope)
 913+ C64D CB 7C                bit 7,h
 914+ C64F 28 23                jr z,.nognd
 915+ C651 2A 49 CC             ld hl,(b_send)
 916+ C654 CD 9F CB             call b_y_at                     ; HL = height at the end
 917+ C657 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 918+ C659 28 19                jr z,.nognd
 919+ C65B 2A 47 CC             ld hl,(b_slope)
 920+ C65E CD 73 A7             call neg_hl
 921+ C661 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = -slope
 922+ C662 2A 3F CC             ld hl,(b_y0)
 923+ C665 7C                   ld a,h                          ; A:HL = y0 << 8
 924+ C666 65                   ld h,l
 925+ C667 2E 00                ld l,0

1037+ C78B              ; HL = S7.8 direction component -> HL = path distance s between cell
1038+ C78B              ; boundaries along that axis, 256 * 256 / |c|  ($FFFF = never)
1039+ C78B              cell_span:
1040+ C78B 7C                   ld a,h
1041+ C78C B5                   or l
1042+ C78D 28 0C                jr z,.never
1043+ C78F CD 7A A7             call abs_hl

1045+ C793 3E 01                ld a,1
1046+ C795 21 00 00             ld hl,0

1048+ C79B 2B           .never: dec hl                          ; $FFFF
1049+ C79C C9                   ret
1050+ C79D
1051+ C79D              ; HL = S7.8 direction component, A = low byte of the start coordinate,
1052+ C79D              ; DE = cell_span for it.  -> HL = s to the first cell boundary ($FFFF =
1053+ C79D              ; never), A = cell step (+1, -1, 0).  s = distance * span / 256, by MUL.
1054+ C79D              dda_axis:

1057+ C79F B5                   or l

1061+ C7A6 C9                   ret

1063+ C7A8 32 6A CC             ld (dd_sg),a
1064+ C7AB 17                   rla
1065+ C7AC 38 07                jr c,.neg
1066+ C7AE AF                   xor a                           ; moving up: 256 - (x & 255)
1067+ C7AF 90                   sub b
1068+ C7B0 20 04                jr nz,.part
1069+ C7B2 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; (x & 255) = 0: a whole cell
1070+ C7B3 18 12                jr .sgn
1071+ C7B5 78           .neg:   ld a,b                          ; moving down: x & 255
1072+ C7B6 4F           .part:  ld c,a                          ; HL = (dist * span) >> 8
1073+ C7B7 D5                   push de
1074+ C7B8 57                   ld d,a
1075+ C7B9 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span low

1077+ C7BC D1                   pop de
1078+ C7BD 5A                   ld e,d
1079+ C7BE 51                   ld d,c
1080+ C7BF ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span high

1082+ C7C2 6F                   ld l,a

1084+ C7C4 CE 00                adc a,0
1085+ C7C6 67                   ld h,a

1089+ C7CD D0                   ret nc
1090+ C7CE 3E FF                ld a,-1
1091+ C7D0 C9                   ret

1345+ C9E2              ; and base tile within its reach (rb_r: BLAST_R, MISSILE_R, BOFORS_R) on

# file opened: menu.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - main menu (8K page PAGE_MENU, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Shown after start-up.  Layer 2 is hidden and the menu is drawn on the ULA
   5+ C000              ; screen with opaque paper.  Every frame it advances the random number
   6+ C000              ; generator (zrand), so the game doesn't start from the same seed each time
   7+ C000              ; (the wait for a key press varies), then reads the keys:
   8+ C000              ;   1  New game   -> newGame (zpolice.asm): level 1
   9+ C000              ;   2  Save game  (not yet)
  10+ C000              ;   3  Load game  (not yet)
  11+ C000              ;   9  Redefine keys -> keydef_screen (keydef.asm)
  12+ C000              ; game_over_screen (the landing pad destroyed) shows GAME OVER for 10
  13+ C000              ; seconds, then returns here.
  14+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  15+ C000
  16+ C000
  17+ C000              main_menu:
  18+ C000 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; the keys at $E000 (this page: $C000)
  19+ C004 CD 6A A0             call menu_clear
  20+ C007 21 05 C4             ld hl,menu_text                 ; title and options
  21+ C00A 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  22+ C00B FE FF                cp $FF
  23+ C00D 28 0B                jr z,.loop
  24+ C00F 57                   ld d,a                          ; row
  25+ C010 23                   inc hl
  26+ C011 5E                   ld e,(hl)                       ; column
  27+ C012 23                   inc hl
  28+ C013 4E                   ld c,(hl)                       ; attribute
  29+ C014 23                   inc hl
  30+ C015 CD 99 A0             call menu_print                 ; HL -> the text, past it after
  31+ C018 18 F0                jr .txt
  32+ C01A CD E3 A0     .loop:  call menu_frame                 ; one video frame
  33+ C01D CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; stir the random number generator
  34+ C020 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  35+ C023 21 01 03             ld hl,KB_1
  36+ C026 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  37+ C029 C2 59 A3             jp nz,newGame
  38+ C02C 21 02 04             ld hl,KB_9                      ; redefine keys
  39+ C02F CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  40+ C032 C2 51 A5             jp nz,keydef_go                 ; (zpolice.asm)
  41+ C035 21 02 03             ld hl,KB_2                      ; save, load: to come
  42+ C038 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  43+ C03B 21 04 03             ld hl,KB_3
  44+ C03E CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  45+ C041 18 D7                jr .loop
  46+ C043
  47+ C043              ; The landing pad has been destroyed: GAME OVER for GAME_OVER_FR frames
  48+ C043              ; (10 seconds), then the main menu
  49+ C043              game_over_screen:
  50+ C043 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top
  51+ C046 CD 6A A0             call menu_clear
  52+ C049 16 0B                ld d,11
  53+ C04B 1E 0B                ld e,11
  54+ C04D 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  55+ C04F 21 68 C0             ld hl,txt_game_over
  56+ C052 CD 99 A0             call menu_print
  57+ C055 01 F4 01             ld bc,GAME_OVER_FR
  58+ C058 C5           .w:     push bc
  59+ C059 CD E3 A0             call menu_frame
  60+ C05C CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  61+ C05F C1                   pop bc
  62+ C060 0B                   dec bc
  63+ C061 78                   ld a,b
  64+ C062 B1                   or c
  65+ C063 20 F3                jr nz,.w
  66+ C065 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu
  67+ C068
  68+ C068              txt_game_over:
  69+ C068 47 41 4D 45          db "GAME OVER", 0
  69+ C06C 20 4F 56 45
  69+ C070 52 00
  70+ C072
  71+ C072              ; The level's last wave is wiped out: LEVEL COMPLETE for LEVEL_DONE_FR
  72+ C072              ; frames (10 seconds), then the next level (next_level, zpolice.asm)
  73+ C072              LEVEL_DONE_FR   equ 500             ; 10 s of 50 Hz frames
  74+ C072              level_complete_screen:
  75+ C072 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top
  76+ C075 3A D2 A7             ld a,(level)                    ; the last level: the whole game
  77+ C078 FE 32                cp LEVEL_LAST
  78+ C07A D2 36 C3             jp nc,game_complete_screen
  79+ C07D CD 6A A0             call menu_clear
  80+ C080 16 08                ld d,8
  81+ C082 1E 09                ld e,9
  82+ C084 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  83+ C086 21 AC C0             ld hl,txt_level_done
  84+ C089 CD 99 A0             call menu_print
  85+ C08C CD BB C0             call rearm_all                  ; every weapon's ammo full again
  86+ C08F CD 85 37             call hum_unload_all             ; (human.asm) those aboard: rescued
  87+ C092 CD DD C0             call salvage_award              ; the humans saved: salvage, unlocks
  88+ C095 01 F4 01             ld bc,LEVEL_DONE_FR
  89+ C098 C5           .w:     push bc
  90+ C099 CD E3 A0             call menu_frame
  91+ C09C CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  92+ C09F C1                   pop bc
  93+ C0A0 0B                   dec bc
  94+ C0A1 78                   ld a,b
  95+ C0A2 B1                   or c
  96+ C0A3 20 F3                jr nz,.w
  97+ C0A5 21 D2 A7             ld hl,level
  98+ C0A8 34                   inc (hl)
  99+ C0A9 C3 4A A5             jp loadout_go                   ; (zpolice.asm) the load-out screen,
 100+ C0AC                                                      ; then the next level
 101+ C0AC
 102+ C0AC              txt_level_done:
 103+ C0AC 4C 45 56 45          db "LEVEL COMPLETE", 0
 103+ C0B0 4C 20 43 4F
 103+ C0B4 4D 50 4C 45
 103+ C0B8 54 45 00
 104+ C0BB
 105+ C0BB              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 106+ C0BB              ; End of a level: every slot's weapon gets its full load again (wpn_cfg,
 107+ C0BB              ; WCFG_LOAD; AMMO_INF for the guns, 0 for an empty slot), and the screen
 108+ C0BB              ; says so
 109+ C0BB              rearm_all:
 110+ C0BB DD 21 C5 A7          ld ix,wpn_slots
 111+ C0BF 06 05                ld b,WPN_SLOTS
 112+ C0C1 DD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(ix+WPN_TYPE)
 113+ C0C4 CD 33 A5             call wpn_cfg_ptr                ; (resident; preserves BC)
 114+ C0C7 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; WCFG_LOAD
 115+ C0C8 DD 77 01             ld (ix+WPN_AMMO),a
 116+ C0CB DD 23                inc ix
 117+ C0CD DD 23                inc ix
 118+ C0CF 10 F0                djnz .lp
 119+ C0D1 16 0E                ld d,14
 120+ C0D3 1E 07                ld e,7
 121+ C0D5 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 122+ C0D7 21 1F C3             ld hl,txt_sa_rearm
 123+ C0DA C3 99 A0             jp menu_print
 124+ C0DD                      ASSERT WCFG_LOAD == 0
 125+ C0DD
 126+ C0DD              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 127+ C0DD              ; Salvage, at the end of a level: each human saved this level (saved) adds
 128+ C0DD              ; a salvage point (salvage_points).  Every point total in salvage_tab that
 129+ C0DD              ; the new total reaches (passed over from the old one) gives its rewards: a
 130+ C0DD              ; weapon unlocked (one more of that type in wpn_stock, for the load-out page)
 131+ C0DD              ; and / or a full repair (vtol_hp = VTOL_HP).  After the table's last total,
 132+ C0DD              ; SALVAGE_TAIL, every SALVAGE_STEP more points is a full repair.  Shown on
 133+ C0DD              ; the LEVEL COMPLETE screen: the humans saved, the salvage, and a line for
 134+ C0DD              ; each reward (rows SA_ROW0..SA_ROW_MAX; any more aren't shown).
 135+ C0DD              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 136+ C0DD              SALVAGE_TAIL    equ 145
 137+ C0DD              SALVAGE_STEP    equ 5
 138+ C0DD              SA_ROW0         equ 16
 139+ C0DD              SA_ROW_MAX      equ 22
 140+ C0DD              salvage_award:
 141+ C0DD 16 0B                ld d,11                         ; HUMANS SAVED n
 142+ C0DF 1E 07                ld e,7
 143+ C0E1 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 144+ C0E3 21 FA C2             ld hl,txt_sa_saved
 145+ C0E6 CD 99 A0             call menu_print
 146+ C0E9 3A 1F 39             ld a,(saved)
 147+ C0EC 6F                   ld l,a
 148+ C0ED 26 00                ld h,0
 149+ C0EF 11 15 0B             ld de,11<<8 | 21
 150+ C0F2 CD BB C1             call menu_num
 151+ C0F5 2A DD A7             ld hl,(salvage_points)          ; the new total
 152+ C0F8 22 14 C2             ld (sa_old),hl
 153+ C0FB 3A 1F 39             ld a,(saved)
 154+ C0FE ED 31                add hl,a
 155+ C100 22 DD A7             ld (salvage_points),hl
 156+ C103 16 0C                ld d,12                         ; SALVAGE n
 157+ C105 1E 07                ld e,7
 158+ C107 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 159+ C109 21 07 C3             ld hl,txt_sa_points
 160+ C10C CD 99 A0             call menu_print
 161+ C10F 2A DD A7             ld hl,(salvage_points)
 162+ C112 11 15 0C             ld de,12<<8 | 21
 163+ C115 CD BB C1             call menu_num
 164+ C118 3E 10                ld a,SA_ROW0
 165+ C11A 32 16 C2             ld (sa_row),a
 166+ C11D 21 17 C2             ld hl,salvage_tab
 167+ C120 5E           .lp:    ld e,(hl)                       ; DE = its points total
 168+ C121 23                   inc hl
 169+ C122 56                   ld d,(hl)
 170+ C123 23                   inc hl
 171+ C124 7A                   ld a,d
 172+ C125 A3                   and e
 173+ C126 3C                   inc a
 174+ C127 28 0F                jr z,.tail                      ; ($FFFF: the end)
 175+ C129 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; B = weapon, C = repair
 176+ C12A 47                   ld b,a
 177+ C12B 23                   inc hl
 178+ C12C 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 179+ C12D 23                   inc hl
 180+ C12E E5                   push hl
 181+ C12F CD 55 C1             call sa_reached
 182+ C132 DC 67 C1             call c,sa_reward
 183+ C135 E1                   pop hl
 184+ C136 18 E8                jr .lp
 185+ C138 11 91 00     .tail:  ld de,SALVAGE_TAIL              ; then a repair every SALVAGE_STEP
 186+ C13B 21 05 00     .tl:    ld hl,SALVAGE_STEP
 187+ C13E 19                   add hl,de
 188+ C13F EB                   ex de,hl
 189+ C140 2A DD A7             ld hl,(salvage_points)
 190+ C143 B7                   or a
 191+ C144 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 192+ C146 D8                   ret c                           ; beyond the new total
 193+ C147 06 00                ld b,WPN_NONE
 194+ C149 0E 01                ld c,1
 195+ C14B D5                   push de
 196+ C14C CD 55 C1             call sa_reached
 197+ C14F DC 67 C1             call c,sa_reward
 198+ C152 D1                   pop de
 199+ C153 18 E6                jr .tl
 200+ C155
 201+ C155              ; Carry set if total DE is reached this time: sa_old < DE <= salvage_points.
 202+ C155              ; Preserves BC, DE.
 203+ C155              sa_reached:
 204+ C155 2A 14 C2             ld hl,(sa_old)
 205+ C158 B7                   or a
 206+ C159 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 207+ C15B 38 02                jr c,.lo
 208+ C15D B7                   or a                            ; old >= DE: reached before
 209+ C15E C9                   ret
 210+ C15F 2A DD A7     .lo:    ld hl,(salvage_points)
 211+ C162 B7                   or a
 212+ C163 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; new - DE: no carry if reached
 213+ C165 3F                   ccf
 214+ C166 C9                   ret
 215+ C167
 216+ C167              ; Reward: B = weapon type (WPN_NONE: none) unlocked, C = 1: a full repair.
 217+ C167              ; A line on the screen for each.
 218+ C167              sa_reward:
 219+ C167 78                   ld a,b
 220+ C168 B7                   or a
 221+ C169 28 2F                jr z,.rep
 222+ C16B 21 45 A8             ld hl,wpn_unlocked              ; unlocked (load-out screen lists it)
 223+ C16E ED 31                add hl,a
 224+ C170 36 01                ld (hl),1
 225+ C172 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock
 226+ C175 ED 31                add hl,a
 227+ C177 34                   inc (hl)
 228+ C178 20 01                jr nz,.nm
 229+ C17A 35                   dec (hl)                        ; (at most 255)
 230+ C17B C5           .nm:    push bc
 231+ C17C 78                   ld a,b                          ; "NEW " and its name
 232+ C17D 87                   add a,a
 233+ C17E 21 89 C2             ld hl,wpn_names
 234+ C181 ED 31                add hl,a
 235+ C183 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 236+ C184 23                   inc hl
 237+ C185 66                   ld h,(hl)
 238+ C186 6F                   ld l,a
 239+ C187 E5                   push hl
 240+ C188 21 0F C3             ld hl,txt_sa_new
 241+ C18B 1E 07                ld e,7
 242+ C18D CD AF C1             call sa_line
 243+ C190 E1                   pop hl
 244+ C191 1E 0B                ld e,11
 245+ C193 CD AF C1             call sa_line.at
 246+ C196 CD AA C1             call sa_next
 247+ C199 C1                   pop bc
 248+ C19A 79           .rep:   ld a,c
 249+ C19B B7                   or a
 250+ C19C C8                   ret z
 251+ C19D 3E 32                ld a,VTOL_HP                    ; full repair
 252+ C19F 32 D5 A7             ld (vtol_hp),a
 253+ C1A2 21 13 C3             ld hl,txt_sa_repair
 254+ C1A5 1E 07                ld e,7
 255+ C1A7 CD AF C1             call sa_line
 256+ C1AA                      ; fall through
 257+ C1AA
 258+ C1AA              ; The next reward line
 259+ C1AA              sa_next:
 260+ C1AA 21 16 C2             ld hl,sa_row
 261+ C1AD 34                   inc (hl)
 262+ C1AE C9                   ret
 263+ C1AF
 264+ C1AF              ; HL -> text, E = column: print it on the reward row (if there is room)
 265+ C1AF              sa_line:
 266+ C1AF 3A 16 C2     .at:    ld a,(sa_row)
 267+ C1B2 FE 17                cp SA_ROW_MAX+1
 268+ C1B4 D0                   ret nc
 269+ C1B5 57                   ld d,a
 270+ C1B6 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
 271+ C1B8 C3 99 A0             jp menu_print
 272+ C1BB
 273+ C1BB              ; HL = number, D = row, E = column: printed in decimal, no leading zeros
 274+ C1BB              menu_num:
 275+ C1BB ED 53 12 C2          ld (mn_pos),de
 276+ C1BF DD 21 08 C2          ld ix,mn_pow
 277+ C1C3 06 00                ld b,0                          ; 1 once a digit has been printed
 278+ C1C5 DD 5E 00     .dg:    ld e,(ix+0)
 279+ C1C8 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
 280+ C1CB 3E 2F                ld a,'0'-1
 281+ C1CD 3C           .sb:    inc a
 282+ C1CE B7                   or a
 283+ C1CF ED 52                sbc hl,de
 284+ C1D1 30 FA                jr nc,.sb
 285+ C1D3 19                   add hl,de
 286+ C1D4 4F                   ld c,a
 287+ C1D5 7B                   ld a,e
 288+ C1D6 3D                   dec a
 289+ C1D7 B2                   or d
 290+ C1D8 28 09                jr z,.pr                        ; the units: always
 291+ C1DA 79                   ld a,c
 292+ C1DB FE 30                cp '0'
 293+ C1DD 20 04                jr nz,.pr
 294+ C1DF CB 40                bit 0,b
 295+ C1E1 28 17                jr z,.nx                        ; a leading zero
 296+ C1E3 06 01        .pr:    ld b,1
 297+ C1E5 E5                   push hl
 298+ C1E6 C5                   push bc
 299+ C1E7 79                   ld a,c
 300+ C1E8 ED 5B 12 C2          ld de,(mn_pos)
 301+ C1EC 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 302+ C1EE CD A9 A0             call menu_char
 303+ C1F1 C1                   pop bc
 304+ C1F2 E1                   pop hl
 305+ C1F3 3A 12 C2             ld a,(mn_pos)
 306+ C1F6 3C                   inc a
 307+ C1F7 32 12 C2             ld (mn_pos),a
 308+ C1FA DD 23        .nx:    inc ix
 309+ C1FC DD 23                inc ix
 310+ C1FE DD 7E FE             ld a,(ix-2)
 311+ C201 3D                   dec a
 312+ C202 DD B6 FF             or (ix-1)
 313+ C205 20 BE                jr nz,.dg
 314+ C207 C9                   ret
 315+ C208
 316+ C208 10 27 E8 03  mn_pow:     dw 10000, 1000, 100, 10, 1
 316+ C20C 64 00 0A 00
 316+ C210 01 00
 317+ C212 00 00        mn_pos:     dw 0                        ; column (low), row (high)
 318+ C214 00 00        sa_old:     dw 0                        ; the salvage before this level
 319+ C216 00           sa_row:     db 0                        ; the next reward line
 320+ C217
 321+ C217              ; Salvage totals and their rewards: points (word), weapon unlocked
 322+ C217              ; (WPN_NONE: none), full repair (1); $FFFF ends it
 323+ C217              salvage_tab:
 324+ C217 0A 00                dw 10
 325+ C219 02 00                db WPN_LASER, 0
 326+ C21B 0F 00                dw 15
 327+ C21D 00 01                db WPN_NONE, 1
 328+ C21F 14 00                dw 20
 329+ C221 03 00                db WPN_CHAIN, 0

 346+ C243 41 00                dw 65

 348+ C247 46 00                dw 70

 352+ C24F 50 00                dw 80
 353+ C251 04 00                db WPN_ROCKETS, 0
 354+ C253 55 00                dw 85
 355+ C255 00 01                db WPN_NONE, 1
 356+ C257 5A 00                dw 90
 357+ C259 02 00                db WPN_LASER, 0
 358+ C25B 5F 00                dw 95
 359+ C25D 03 00                db WPN_CHAIN, 0
 360+ C25F 64 00                dw 100
 361+ C261 04 01                db WPN_ROCKETS, 1
 362+ C263 69 00                dw 105
 363+ C265 07 01                db WPN_BOFORS, 1
 364+ C267 6E 00                dw 110
 365+ C269 07 01                db WPN_BOFORS, 1
 366+ C26B 73 00                dw 115
 367+ C26D 05 01                db WPN_MISSILE, 1
 368+ C26F 78 00                dw 120
 369+ C271 07 01                db WPN_BOFORS, 1
 370+ C273 7D 00                dw 125
 371+ C275 07 01                db WPN_BOFORS, 1
 372+ C277 82 00                dw 130
 373+ C279 05 01                db WPN_MISSILE, 1
 374+ C27B 87 00                dw 135
 375+ C27D 05 01                db WPN_MISSILE, 1
 376+ C27F 8C 00                dw 140
 377+ C281 05 01                db WPN_MISSILE, 1
 378+ C283 91 00                dw SALVAGE_TAIL
 379+ C285 05 01                db WPN_MISSILE, 1
 380+ C287 FF FF                dw $FFFF

 383+ C289 9B C2 A3 C2  wpn_names:  dw txt_wn_none, txt_wn_gun, txt_wn_laser, txt_wn_chain
 383+ C28D AF C2 B5 C2

 384+ C295 D6 C2 DB C2

 386+ C29B                      ASSERT ($ - wpn_names)/2 == WPN_LAST+1
 387+ C29B 4E 4F 54 48  txt_wn_none:    db "NOTHING", 0
 387+ C29F 49 4E 47 00

 402+ C336              ; The last level (LEVEL_LAST) complete: CONGRATULATIONS for GAME_DONE_FR

 405+ C336              game_complete_screen:
