# file opened: level_load.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - level loading (8K page PAGE_LEVEL, game logic: runs at $C000 in
   3+ C000              ; slot 6; it pages the file handler, PAGE_FILE, into slot 7)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; Each level is a file, ZPDATA/LEVELnn.ZPL, naming its city, ZPDATA/CITYnn.ZPC
   6+ C000              ; (both made by gentables.py; the formats are described there).  A city is
   7+ C000              ; played for several levels.  load_level reads both:
   8+ C000              ;   * the level record into level_cur (levels.asm): city name, zombie and
   9+ C000              ;     wave counts, the draw distance, ...;
  10+ C000              ;   * the city into the city bank (page 7, slot 3): city_col at $6000 and
  11+ C000              ;     city_map at $7000, one 8K read;
  12+ C000              ;   * the level's own map changes (x, z, cell code, city_col), for hand-made
  13+ C000              ;     layouts;
  14+ C000              ;   * the city's palette and sky colour into the Layer 2 palette;
  15+ C000              ;   * view_r / dot_r from the draw distance (2..VIEW_R tiles).
  16+ C000              ; Loading the map also clears every cell's zombie and human marks.  The
  17+ C000              ; files must be in the ZPDATA folder beside zpolice.nex on the SD card.
  18+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  19+ C000
  20+ C000              ; Level file
  21+ C000              LVF_CITY        equ 5               ; city number
  22+ C000              LVF_REC         equ 6               ; the level record (LV_SIZE bytes)
  23+ C000              LVF_NCHG        equ LVF_REC+LV_SIZE ; number of map changes,
  24+ C000              LVF_CHG         equ LVF_NCHG+1      ; then x, z, cell code, city_col each
  25+ C000              LVF_CHG_MAX     equ 64
  26+ C000              LVF_MAX         equ LVF_CHG+4*LVF_CHG_MAX
  27+ C000              ; City file
  28+ C000              CTF_SKY         equ 14              ; sky colour: RRRGGGBB, low blue bit
  29+ C000              CTF_PAL         equ 32              ; the palette, 256 pairs
  30+ C000              CTF_DATA        equ CTF_PAL+512     ; then city_col, city_map
  31+ C000              FILE_VERSION    equ 1               ; both files' format version (+4)
  32+ C000              LDERR_BADFILE   equ 255             ; error code: not a valid file
  33+ C000
  34+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  35+ C000              ; A = level (1..99): load it.  Carry set on failure, A = the esxDOS error
  36+ C000              ; code or LDERR_BADFILE.  Leaves PAGE_FILE in slot 7.
  37+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  38+ C000              load_level:
  39+ C000 0E 2F                ld c,'0'-1                      ; the level's two digits
  40+ C002 0C           .tens:  inc c
  41+ C003 D6 0A                sub 10
  42+ C005 30 FB                jr nc,.tens
  43+ C007 C6 3A                add a,'0'+10
  44+ C009 32 2C C1             ld (ll_level+13),a
  45+ C00C 79                   ld a,c
  46+ C00D 32 2B C1             ld (ll_level+12),a
  47+ C010 ED 91 57 10          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_FILE
  48+ C014 21 1F C1             ld hl,ll_level                  ; the level file
  49+ C017 11 4C C1             ld de,ll_lbuf
  50+ C01A 01 16 01             ld bc,LVF_MAX
  51+ C01D CD 81 E0             call fh_load
  52+ C020 D8                   ret c
  53+ C021 21 4C C1             ld hl,ll_lbuf
  54+ C024 11 44 C1             ld de,ll_lmagic
  55+ C027 CD 0D C1             call ll_check
  56+ C02A D8                   ret c
  57+ C02B 21 52 C1             ld hl,ll_lbuf+LVF_REC           ; its record
  58+ C02E 11 5B A0             ld de,level_cur
  59+ C031 01 0F 00             ld bc,LV_SIZE
  60+ C034 ED B0                ldir
  61+ C036 3A 51 C1             ld a,(ll_lbuf+LVF_CITY)         ; its city file: two digits
  62+ C039 0E 2F                ld c,'0'-1
  63+ C03B 0C           .ctens: inc c
  64+ C03C D6 0A                sub 10
  65+ C03E 30 FB                jr nc,.ctens
  66+ C040 C6 3A                add a,'0'+10
  67+ C042 32 3E C1             ld (ll_city+12),a
  68+ C045 79                   ld a,c
  69+ C046 32 3D C1             ld (ll_city+11),a
  70+ C049 21 32 C1             ld hl,ll_city
  71+ C04C 06 01                ld b,FA_READ|FA_OPEN_EX
  72+ C04E CD 0E E0             call fh_open
  73+ C051 D8                   ret c
  74+ C052 21 62 C2             ld hl,ll_cbuf                   ; header and palette
  75+ C055 01 20 02             ld bc,CTF_DATA
  76+ C058 CD 2E E0             call fh_read
  77+ C05B DA 03 C1             jp c,.cerr
  78+ C05E 3A 02 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)                ; city_col and city_map: the whole
  79+ C061 21 00 60             ld hl,city_col                  ; city bank, in one read
  80+ C064 01 00 20             ld bc,2*GRID*GRID
  81+ C067 CD 2E E0             call fh_read
  82+ C06A DA 03 C1             jp c,.cerr
  83+ C06D 78                   ld a,b                          ; all of it?
  84+ C06E FE 20                cp high (2*GRID*GRID)
  85+ C070 3E FF                ld a,LDERR_BADFILE
  86+ C072 37                   scf
  87+ C073 C2 03 C1             jp nz,.cerr
  88+ C076 3A 02 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)
  89+ C079 CD 25 E0             call fh_close
  90+ C07C D8                   ret c
  91+ C07D 21 62 C2             ld hl,ll_cbuf
  92+ C080 11 48 C1             ld de,ll_cmagic
  93+ C083 CD 0D C1             call ll_check
  94+ C086 D8                   ret c
  95+ C087                      ; the level's map changes
  96+ C087 3A 61 C1             ld a,(ll_lbuf+LVF_NCHG)
  97+ C08A FE 41                cp LVF_CHG_MAX+1
  98+ C08C 3E FF                ld a,LDERR_BADFILE
  99+ C08E 3F                   ccf
 100+ C08F D8                   ret c
 101+ C090 3A 61 C1             ld a,(ll_lbuf+LVF_NCHG)
 102+ C093 B7                   or a
 103+ C094 28 2C                jr z,.pal
 104+ C096 47                   ld b,a
 105+ C097 DD 21 62 C1          ld ix,ll_lbuf+LVF_CHG
 106+ C09B DD 6E 01     .chg:   ld l,(ix+1)                     ; row z
 107+ C09E 26 00                ld h,0
 108+ C0A0                      ROWOFS
 108+ C0A0 29          >        add hl,hl
 108+ C0A1 29          >        add hl,hl
 108+ C0A2 29          >        add hl,hl
 108+ C0A3 29          >        add hl,hl
 108+ C0A4 29          >        add hl,hl
 108+ C0A5 29          >        add hl,hl
 109+ C0A6 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; column x
 110+ C0A9 ED 31                add hl,a
 111+ C0AB 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 112+ C0AE 19                   add hl,de
 113+ C0AF DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 114+ C0B2 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; the cell code
 115+ C0B3 11 00 F0             ld de,city_col-city_map
 116+ C0B6 19                   add hl,de
 117+ C0B7 DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 118+ C0BA 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; and its colour
 119+ C0BB 11 04 00             ld de,4
 120+ C0BE DD 19                add ix,de
 121+ C0C0 10 D9                djnz .chg
 122+ C0C2              .pal:   ; the city's palette (the first Layer 2 palette), then its sky
 123+ C0C2 ED 91 43 10          nextreg $43,%00010000           ; edit Layer 2 palette 0, auto-inc
 124+ C0C6 ED 91 40 00          nextreg $40,0
 125+ C0CA 21 82 C2             ld hl,ll_cbuf+CTF_PAL
 126+ C0CD 06 00                ld b,0                          ; 256 entries
 127+ C0CF 7E           .pl:    ld a,(hl)
 128+ C0D0 23                   inc hl
 129+ C0D1 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; RRRGGGBB
 130+ C0D4 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 131+ C0D5 23                   inc hl
 132+ C0D6 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; low blue bit
 133+ C0D9 10 F4                djnz .pl
 134+ C0DB ED 91 40 03          nextreg $40,SKY_COL
 135+ C0DF 3A 70 C2             ld a,(ll_cbuf+CTF_SKY)
 136+ C0E2 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a
 137+ C0E5 3A 71 C2             ld a,(ll_cbuf+CTF_SKY+1)
 138+ C0E8 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a
 139+ C0EB                      ; the draw distance, 2..VIEW_R tiles; the ground dots one less
 140+ C0EB 3A 69 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_VIEW_R)
 141+ C0EE FE 02                cp 2
 142+ C0F0 30 02                jr nc,.v1
 143+ C0F2 3E 02                ld a,2
 144+ C0F4 FE 07        .v1:    cp VIEW_R+1
 145+ C0F6 38 02                jr c,.v2
 146+ C0F8 3E 06                ld a,VIEW_R
 147+ C0FA 32 D3 A7     .v2:    ld (view_r),a
 148+ C0FD 3D                   dec a
 149+ C0FE 32 D4 A7             ld (dot_r),a
 150+ C101 B7                   or a                            ; carry clear: loaded
 151+ C102 C9                   ret
 152+ C103 F5           .cerr:  push af                         ; an error with the city file open:
 153+ C104 3A 02 E1             ld a,(fh_handle)                ; close it, keep the error
 154+ C107 CD 25 E0             call fh_close
 155+ C10A F1                   pop af
 156+ C10B 37                   scf
 157+ C10C C9                   ret
 158+ C10D
 159+ C10D              ; HL -> a file's first bytes, DE -> its 4-byte magic: carry clear if they
 160+ C10D              ; match and the version byte after them is FILE_VERSION, else carry set
 161+ C10D              ; and A = LDERR_BADFILE
 162+ C10D              ll_check:
 163+ C10D 06 04                ld b,4
 164+ C10F 1A           .lp:    ld a,(de)
 165+ C110 BE                   cp (hl)
 166+ C111 20 08                jr nz,.bad
 167+ C113 13                   inc de
 168+ C114 23                   inc hl
 169+ C115 10 F8                djnz .lp
 170+ C117 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 171+ C118 FE 01                cp FILE_VERSION
 172+ C11A C8                   ret z                           ; (carry clear)
 173+ C11B 3E FF        .bad:   ld a,LDERR_BADFILE
 174+ C11D 37                   scf
 175+ C11E C9                   ret
 176+ C11F
 177+ C11F              ;==============================================================================
 178+ C11F              ; Level loading data
 179+ C11F              ;==============================================================================
 180+ C11F 5A 50 44 41  ll_level:   db "ZPDATA/LEVEL00.ZPL", 0  ; digits at +12, +13

# file opened: loadout.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - load-out screen (8K page PAGE_LOADOUT, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Between levels, after LEVEL COMPLETE (loadout_go pages it in).  On the
   5+ C000              ; left, the store: every weapon type unlocked (wpn_unlocked) with the number
   6+ C000              ; in store (wpn_stock), even 0 when the only one is fitted.  On the right,
   7+ C000              ; the VTOL's 5 weapon slots and what is fitted in each.
   8+ C000              ;   1-5    select a slot (yellow)
   9+ C000              ;   Q / A  move the highlight up / down the store list.  The highlighted
  10+ C000              ;          weapon is green if it can be fitted in the selected slot (the
  11+ C000              ;          slot allows it, wpn_fits, and one is in store), red if not; the
  12+ C000              ;          rest of the list is white.
  13+ C000              ;   SPACE  fit the highlighted weapon in the selected slot, if it can be:
  14+ C000              ;          one comes out of the store, with a full load (WCFG_LOAD), and
  15+ C000              ;          whatever was fitted there goes back into the store
  16+ C000              ;   0      done: the next level (next_level)
  17+ C000              ; Uses the resident text routines (text.asm) and the keys page in slot 7.
  18+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  19+ C000
  20+ C000              LO_ROW0         equ 5               ; first row of the lists
  21+ C000              LO_NAME_COL     equ 1               ; store: names,
  22+ C000              LO_CNT_COL      equ 13              ;   numbers in store
  23+ C000              LO_SLOT_COL     equ 18              ; slots: number, then the weapon
  24+ C000              LO_NAME_LEN     equ 11              ; names are at most this long
  25+ C000              LO_SLOT_STEP    equ 2               ; rows between the slots
  26+ C000
  27+ C000              loadout_screen:
  28+ C000 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top
  29+ C003 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; the keys at $E000
  30+ C007 CD 6A A0             call menu_clear
  31+ C00A 21 02 C2             ld hl,lo_text                   ; titles and help
  32+ C00D 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  33+ C00E FE FF                cp $FF
  34+ C010 28 0B                jr z,.list
  35+ C012 57                   ld d,a
  36+ C013 23                   inc hl
  37+ C014 5E                   ld e,(hl)
  38+ C015 23                   inc hl
  39+ C016 4E                   ld c,(hl)
  40+ C017 23                   inc hl
  41+ C018 CD 99 A0             call menu_print
  42+ C01B 18 F0                jr .txt
  43+ C01D 21 D7 C2     .list:  ld hl,lo_list               ; the store list: types unlocked, in
  44+ C020 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; type order
  45+ C022 06 01                ld b,1                          ; (from the first weapon type)
  46+ C024 78           .ul:    ld a,b
  47+ C025 11 45 A8             ld de,wpn_unlocked
  48+ C028 ED 32                add de,a
  49+ C02A 1A                   ld a,(de)
  50+ C02B B7                   or a
  51+ C02C 28 03                jr z,.un
  52+ C02E 70                   ld (hl),b
  53+ C02F 23                   inc hl
  54+ C030 0C                   inc c
  55+ C031 04           .un:    inc b
  56+ C032 78                   ld a,b
  57+ C033 FE 09                cp WPN_LAST+1
  58+ C035 20 ED                jr nz,.ul
  59+ C037 79                   ld a,c
  60+ C038 32 CC C2             ld (lo_n),a
  61+ C03B AF                   xor a
  62+ C03C 32 CD C2             ld (lo_cur),a
  63+ C03F 3A CF A7             ld a,(wpn_sel)                  ; start on the slot in use
  64+ C042 32 CE C2             ld (lo_slot),a
  65+ C045 CD 13 C1     .draw:  call lo_draw
  66+ C048 CD E3 A0     .loop:  call menu_frame
  67+ C04B CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  68+ C04E CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  69+ C051 21 01 04             ld hl,KB_0                      ; done
  70+ C054 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  71+ C057 C2 B3 A3             jp nz,next_level
  72+ C05A 21 01 02             ld hl,KB_Q                      ; up the list
  73+ C05D CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  74+ C060 28 0C                jr z,.dn
  75+ C062 3A CD C2             ld a,(lo_cur)
  76+ C065 B7                   or a
  77+ C066 28 06                jr z,.dn
  78+ C068 3D                   dec a
  79+ C069 32 CD C2             ld (lo_cur),a
  80+ C06C 18 D7                jr .draw
  81+ C06E 21 01 01     .dn:    ld hl,KB_A                      ; down the list
  82+ C071 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  83+ C074 28 10                jr z,.sl
  84+ C076 3A CC C2             ld a,(lo_n)
  85+ C079 4F                   ld c,a
  86+ C07A 3A CD C2             ld a,(lo_cur)
  87+ C07D 3C                   inc a
  88+ C07E B9                   cp c
  89+ C07F 30 05                jr nc,.sl
  90+ C081 32 CD C2             ld (lo_cur),a
  91+ C084 18 BF                jr .draw
  92+ C086 21 B0 C0     .sl:    ld hl,lo_slot_keys              ; 1-5: a slot
  93+ C089 06 00                ld b,0
  94+ C08B 5E           .sk:    ld e,(hl)
  95+ C08C 23                   inc hl
  96+ C08D 56                   ld d,(hl)
  97+ C08E 23                   inc hl
  98+ C08F E5                   push hl
  99+ C090 EB                   ex de,hl
 100+ C091 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
 101+ C094 E1                   pop hl
 102+ C095 28 06                jr z,.sn
 103+ C097 78                   ld a,b
 104+ C098 32 CE C2             ld (lo_slot),a
 105+ C09B 18 A8                jr .draw
 106+ C09D 04           .sn:    inc b
 107+ C09E 78                   ld a,b
 108+ C09F FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 109+ C0A1 20 E8                jr nz,.sk
 110+ C0A3 21 01 07             ld hl,KB_SPACE                  ; fit it
 111+ C0A6 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
 112+ C0A9 28 9D                jr z,.loop
 113+ C0AB CD CB C0             call lo_fit
 114+ C0AE 18 95                jr .draw
 115+ C0B0
 116+ C0B0 01 03 02 03  lo_slot_keys:   dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5
 116+ C0B4 04 03 08 03
 116+ C0B8 10 03
 117+ C0BA
 118+ C0BA              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 119+ C0BA              ; Carry set if store weapon A can go in the selected slot (lo_slot): the
 120+ C0BA              ; slot allows it and one is in store
 121+ C0BA              lo_ok:
 122+ C0BA 4F                   ld c,a
 123+ C0BB 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock
 124+ C0BE ED 31                add hl,a
 125+ C0C0 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 126+ C0C1 B7                   or a
 127+ C0C2 C8                   ret z                           ; none in store (carry clear)
 128+ C0C3 79                   ld a,c
 129+ C0C4 21 CE C2             ld hl,lo_slot
 130+ C0C7 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 131+ C0C8 C3 3C A5             jp wpn_fits                     ; (resident)
 132+ C0CB
 133+ C0CB              ; SPACE: fit the highlighted weapon in the selected slot, if it can go
 134+ C0CB              ; there; whatever was there goes back into the store
 135+ C0CB              lo_fit:
 136+ C0CB CD 03 C1             call lo_cur_type
 137+ C0CE C8                   ret z                           ; (an empty list)
 138+ C0CF 32 D0 C2             ld (lo_t),a
 139+ C0D2 CD BA C0             call lo_ok
 140+ C0D5 D0                   ret nc
 141+ C0D6 3A CE C2             ld a,(lo_slot)                  ; HL -> the slot
 142+ C0D9 87                   add a,a
 143+ C0DA 21 C5 A7             ld hl,wpn_slots
 144+ C0DD ED 31                add hl,a
 145+ C0DF 46                   ld b,(hl)                       ; B = what was there
 146+ C0E0 3A D0 C2             ld a,(lo_t)
 147+ C0E3 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; fitted,
 148+ C0E4 23                   inc hl
 149+ C0E5 E5                   push hl
 150+ C0E6 C5                   push bc
 151+ C0E7 CD 33 A5             call wpn_cfg_ptr
 152+ C0EA 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; with a full load (WCFG_LOAD)
 153+ C0EB C1                   pop bc
 154+ C0EC E1                   pop hl
 155+ C0ED 77                   ld (hl),a
 156+ C0EE 3A D0 C2             ld a,(lo_t)                     ; one fewer in store
 157+ C0F1 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock
 158+ C0F4 ED 31                add hl,a
 159+ C0F6 35                   dec (hl)
 160+ C0F7 78                   ld a,b                          ; the old one back in store
 161+ C0F8 B7                   or a
 162+ C0F9 C8                   ret z                           ; (the slot was empty)
 163+ C0FA 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock
 164+ C0FD ED 31                add hl,a
 165+ C0FF 34                   inc (hl)
 166+ C100 C0                   ret nz
 167+ C101 35                   dec (hl)                        ; (at most 255)
 168+ C102 C9                   ret
 169+ C103
 170+ C103              ; A = the highlighted store weapon's type; Z if the list is empty
 171+ C103              lo_cur_type:
 172+ C103 3A CC C2             ld a,(lo_n)
 173+ C106 B7                   or a
 174+ C107 C8                   ret z
 175+ C108 3A CD C2             ld a,(lo_cur)
 176+ C10B 21 D7 C2             ld hl,lo_list
 177+ C10E ED 31                add hl,a
 178+ C110 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 179+ C111 B7                   or a                            ; (a type is never 0: NZ)
 180+ C112 C9                   ret
 181+ C113
 182+ C113              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 183+ C113              ; Draw both lists: the store (the highlighted weapon green / red, the rest
 184+ C113              ; white) and the slots (the selected one yellow)
 185+ C113              lo_draw:
 186+ C113 AF                   xor a
 187+ C114 32 CF C2     .st:    ld (lo_i),a
 188+ C117 21 CC C2             ld hl,lo_n
 189+ C11A BE                   cp (hl)
 190+ C11B 30 4C                jr nc,.slots
 191+ C11D 21 D7 C2             ld hl,lo_list
 192+ C120 ED 31                add hl,a
 193+ C122 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 194+ C123 32 D0 C2             ld (lo_t),a
 195+ C126 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR                  ; white
 196+ C128 3A CD C2             ld a,(lo_cur)
 197+ C12B 21 CF C2             ld hl,lo_i
 198+ C12E BE                   cp (hl)
 199+ C12F 20 0C                jr nz,.col
 200+ C131 3A D0 C2             ld a,(lo_t)                     ; highlighted: can it go there?
 201+ C134 CD BA C0             call lo_ok
 202+ C137 0E 0C                ld c,MENU_OK
 203+ C139 38 02                jr c,.col
 204+ C13B 0E 0B                ld c,MENU_BAD
 205+ C13D 79           .col:   ld a,c
 206+ C13E 32 D1 C2             ld (lo_attr),a
 207+ C141 3A CF C2             ld a,(lo_i)
 208+ C144 C6 05                add a,LO_ROW0
 209+ C146 57                   ld d,a
 210+ C147 1E 01                ld e,LO_NAME_COL
 211+ C149 3A D0 C2             ld a,(lo_t)
 212+ C14C CD AA C1             call lo_name
 213+ C14F 3A CF C2             ld a,(lo_i)
 214+ C152 C6 05                add a,LO_ROW0
 215+ C154 57                   ld d,a
 216+ C155 1E 0D                ld e,LO_CNT_COL
 217+ C157 3A D0 C2             ld a,(lo_t)
 218+ C15A 21 3C A8             ld hl,wpn_stock
 219+ C15D ED 31                add hl,a
 220+ C15F 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 221+ C160 CD CB C1             call lo_num
 222+ C163 3A CF C2             ld a,(lo_i)
 223+ C166 3C                   inc a
 224+ C167 18 AB                jr .st
 225+ C169 AF           .slots: xor a                           ; the slots
 226+ C16A 32 CF C2     .sl:    ld (lo_i),a
 227+ C16D 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 228+ C16F 21 CE C2             ld hl,lo_slot
 229+ C172 BE                   cp (hl)
 230+ C173 20 02                jr nz,.sc

# file opened: keydef.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - redefine keys screen (8K page PAGE_KEYDEF, runs at $C000 in
   3+ C000              ; slot 6)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; From the main menu (option 9, keydef_go pages it in).  Lists every control
   6+ C000              ; (K_ACTIONS of them) with its key (key_defs, keys.asm).
   7+ C000              ;   Q / A  move the highlight up / down the list (white; highlighted green)
   8+ C000              ;   SPACE  "SELECT KEY", a second's pause, then the next key pressed becomes
   9+ C000              ;          the highlighted control's key, unless another control already
  10+ C000              ;          uses it: then "KEY ALREADY IN USE" (for a second) and no change
  11+ C000              ;   0      every key back to its default (key_defaults, keys.asm)
  12+ C000              ;   1      back to the main menu (menu_go)
  13+ C000              ; These keys are fixed (key_code_pressed), whatever the controls are set to.
  14+ C000              ; Uses the resident text routines (text.asm) and the keys page in slot 7.
  15+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  16+ C000
  17+ C000              KD_ROW0         equ 2               ; first row of the list
  18+ C000              KD_NAME_COL     equ 1               ; control names,
  19+ C000              KD_KEY_COL      equ 18              ; their keys
  20+ C000              KD_KEY_LEN      equ 7               ; key names are at most this long
  21+ C000              KD_MSG_ROW      equ 21              ; messages
  22+ C000              KD_PAUSE        equ 50              ; a second of 50 Hz frames
  23+ C000                      ASSERT KD_ROW0 + K_ACTIONS <= KD_MSG_ROW
  24+ C000
  25+ C000              keydef_screen:
  26+ C000 31 16 AF             ld sp,stack_top
  27+ C003 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; the keys at $E000
  28+ C007 CD 6A A0             call menu_clear
  29+ C00A 21 AE C1             ld hl,kd_text                   ; title and help
  30+ C00D 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  31+ C00E FE FF                cp $FF
  32+ C010 28 0B                jr z,.go
  33+ C012 57                   ld d,a
  34+ C013 23                   inc hl
  35+ C014 5E                   ld e,(hl)
  36+ C015 23                   inc hl
  37+ C016 4E                   ld c,(hl)
  38+ C017 23                   inc hl
  39+ C018 CD 99 A0             call menu_print
  40+ C01B 18 F0                jr .txt
  41+ C01D AF           .go:    xor a
  42+ C01E 32 7C C4             ld (kd_cur),a
  43+ C021 CD 2C C1     .draw:  call kd_draw
  44+ C024 CD E3 A0     .loop:  call menu_frame
  45+ C027 CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  46+ C02A CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  47+ C02D 21 01 03             ld hl,KB_1                      ; back to the main menu
  48+ C030 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  49+ C033 C2 58 A5             jp nz,menu_go                   ; (zpolice.asm)
  50+ C036 21 01 04             ld hl,KB_0                      ; the defaults
  51+ C039 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  52+ C03C 28 0D                jr z,.up
  53+ C03E 21 FC E0             ld hl,key_defaults
  54+ C041 11 D8 E0             ld de,key_defs
  55+ C044 01 24 00             ld bc,K_ACTIONS*2
  56+ C047 ED B0                ldir
  57+ C049 18 D6                jr .draw
  58+ C04B 21 01 02     .up:    ld hl,KB_Q
  59+ C04E CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  60+ C051 28 0C                jr z,.dn
  61+ C053 3A 7C C4             ld a,(kd_cur)
  62+ C056 B7                   or a
  63+ C057 28 06                jr z,.dn
  64+ C059 3D                   dec a
  65+ C05A 32 7C C4             ld (kd_cur),a
  66+ C05D 18 C2                jr .draw
  67+ C05F 21 01 01     .dn:    ld hl,KB_A
  68+ C062 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  69+ C065 28 0D                jr z,.sp
  70+ C067 3A 7C C4             ld a,(kd_cur)
  71+ C06A 3C                   inc a
  72+ C06B FE 12                cp K_ACTIONS
  73+ C06D 30 05                jr nc,.sp
  74+ C06F 32 7C C4             ld (kd_cur),a
  75+ C072 18 AD                jr .draw
  76+ C074 21 01 07     .sp:    ld hl,KB_SPACE                  ; change the highlighted one
  77+ C077 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  78+ C07A 28 A8                jr z,.loop
  79+ C07C CD 81 C0             call kd_change
  80+ C07F 18 A0                jr .draw
  81+ C081
  82+ C081              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83+ C081              ; SPACE: "SELECT KEY", a second's pause, then the next key pressed becomes
  84+ C081              ; the highlighted control's key, if no other control uses it
  85+ C081              kd_change:
  86+ C081 21 FD C1             ld hl,txt_kd_select
  87+ C084 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  88+ C086 CD 17 C1             call kd_msg
  89+ C089 06 32                ld b,KD_PAUSE                   ; a second (keys still read, so one
  90+ C08B C5           .p:     push bc                         ; held now isn't new after it)
  91+ C08C CD E3 A0             call menu_frame
  92+ C08F CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  93+ C092 C1                   pop bc
  94+ C093 10 F6                djnz .p
  95+ C095 CD E3 A0     .w:     call menu_frame                 ; wait for a key newly pressed
  96+ C098 CD B5 2C             call zrand
  97+ C09B CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  98+ C09E CD F8 C0             call kd_new_key
  99+ C0A1 28 F2                jr z,.w
 100+ C0A3 ED 53 7F C4          ld (kd_code),de
 101+ C0A7 06 00                ld b,0                          ; in use by another control?
 102+ C0A9 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 103+ C0AC 3A 7C C4     .u:     ld a,(kd_cur)
 104+ C0AF B8                   cp b
 105+ C0B0 28 0A                jr z,.nu                        ; (its own key: fine)
 106+ C0B2 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 107+ C0B3 BB                   cp e
 108+ C0B4 20 06                jr nz,.nu
 109+ C0B6 23                   inc hl
 110+ C0B7 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 111+ C0B8 2B                   dec hl
 112+ C0B9 BA                   cp d
 113+ C0BA 28 20                jr z,.used
 114+ C0BC 23           .nu:    inc hl
 115+ C0BD 23                   inc hl
 116+ C0BE 04                   inc b
 117+ C0BF 78                   ld a,b
 118+ C0C0 FE 12                cp K_ACTIONS
 119+ C0C2 20 E8                jr nz,.u
 120+ C0C4 3A 7C C4             ld a,(kd_cur)                   ; free: it's the control's key now
 121+ C0C7 87                   add a,a
 122+ C0C8 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 123+ C0CB ED 31                add hl,a
 124+ C0CD ED 5B 7F C4          ld de,(kd_code)
 125+ C0D1 73                   ld (hl),e

 128+ C0D4 21 1B C2             ld hl,txt_kd_blank              ; (the message gone)
 129+ C0D7 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 130+ C0D9 C3 17 C1             jp kd_msg
 131+ C0DC 21 08 C2     .used:  ld hl,txt_kd_used               ; already a control's key: no change
 132+ C0DF 0E 0B                ld c,MENU_BAD
 133+ C0E1 CD 17 C1             call kd_msg
 134+ C0E4 06 32                ld b,KD_PAUSE
 135+ C0E6 C5           .q:     push bc
 136+ C0E7 CD E3 A0             call menu_frame
 137+ C0EA CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
 138+ C0ED C1                   pop bc
 139+ C0EE 10 F6                djnz .q
 140+ C0F0 21 1B C2             ld hl,txt_kd_blank
 141+ C0F3 0E 0F                ld c,MENU_ATTR
 142+ C0F5 C3 17 C1             jp kd_msg
 143+ C0F8
 144+ C0F8              ; NZ if a key went down since the last scan: DE = its key code (bank in D,
 145+ C0F8              ; its bit in E; the lowest bank and bit if several)
 146+ C0F8              kd_new_key:
 147+ C0F8 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 148+ C0FB 16 00                ld d,0
 149+ C0FD E5           .b:     push hl
 150+ C0FE 3E 0A                ld a,KEY_BANKS                  ; key_prev, the same bank
 151+ C100 ED 31                add hl,a
 152+ C102 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 153+ C103 2F                   cpl                             ; not down before
 154+ C104 E1                   pop hl
 155+ C105 A6                   and (hl)                        ; and down now
 156+ C106 20 09                jr nz,.got
 157+ C108 23                   inc hl
 158+ C109 14                   inc d
 159+ C10A 7A                   ld a,d
 160+ C10B FE 0A                cp KEY_BANKS
 161+ C10D 20 EE                jr nz,.b
 162+ C10F AF                   xor a                           ; none (Z)
 163+ C110 C9                   ret
 164+ C111 4F           .got:   ld c,a                          ; the lowest bit: A AND -A
 165+ C112 ED 44                neg
 166+ C114 A1                   and c
 167+ C115 5F                   ld e,a                          ; (NZ)
 168+ C116 C9                   ret
 169+ C117
 170+ C117              ; HL -> message, C = attribute: shown on the message row (cleared first)

1461  C481                      MMU LOGIC_SLOT e, PAGE_SPECIAL, LOGIC_ADDR

# file opened: specials.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - special monsters (8K page PAGE_SPECIAL, game logic: runs at
   3+ C000              ; $C000 in slot 6)
   4+ C000              ;
   5+ C000              ; Special monsters use the zombie records after the zombies' own (NZOMB ..
   6+ C000              ; NMON-1, zombie.asm), so they share the zombies' drawing (zombie_cell,
   7+ C000              ; fig_draw), map blips, cell marks (zflags), weapon hits and zdamage.  The
   8+ C000              ; record's type (+15) says which monster it is.  spec_init places the
   9+ C000              ; level's specials (level_cur: LV_SPECIAL of them, ids up to
  10+ C000              ; LV_SPECIAL_MAX); spec_update moves them each frame, after zombie_update.
  11+ C000              ;
  12+ C000              ; The ghost (ZT_GHOST, special monster id 0): a stick man under a white
  13+ C000              ; sheet.  It can't attack the base.  It flies at twice the zombies' speed,
  14+ C000              ; at any angle, and up and down between the ground and the VTOL's ceiling
  15+ C000              ; (MAX_Y), over buildings lower than it.
  16+ C000              ;   * Roaming: it flies in a random direction (a random climb or descent
  17+ C000              ;     too) until something makes it turn: a building in the way or the map
  18+ C000              ;     edge (a new random direction at once), or, at its decision check
  19+ C000              ;     every GHOST_DECIDE (30) frames, a human or the VTOL within GHOST_SEE
  20+ C000              ;     (3 tiles) that it can see.
  21+ C000              ;   * Chasing: it flies straight at the nearest one it can see, aiming
  22+ C000              ;     again every GHOST_TRACK (5) frames.  If it loses sight of its target
  23+ C000              ;     (behind a taller building) or the target is gone, it roams again in a
  24+ C000              ;     random direction, back on the 30-frame cycle.
  25+ C000              ;   * Attacking: within ATTACK_R (0.1 tile) of its target on each axis it
  26+ C000              ;     stops and takes GHOST_DMG hit point every GHOST_HIT_FRAMES (10)
  27+ C000              ;     frames: from a human (HUMAN_HP) until it dies, or from the VTOL
  28+ C000              ;     (vtol_hp, VTOL_HP; at 0 it is destroyed and the game ends).  Its map blip is red
  29+ C000              ;     meanwhile (zatk_flags, zblip_red), as an attacking zombie's.  If the
  30+ C000              ;     target moves out of reach it chases it again; once the human is dead
  31+ C000              ;     it roams again.
  32+ C000              ;   * It has GHOST_HP (10) hit points; any weapon can shoot it.  Killed, it
  33+ C000              ;     fades out where it is, white to near black in 4 steps of 3 frames
  34+ C000              ;     (GM_DYING; zdamage starts it, zombie_draw colours it, g_dying ends
  35+ C000              ;     it), and can't be hit meanwhile.
  36+ C000              ; Ghost record fields, besides the zombies' (zombie.asm):
  37+ C000              ;   +11 frames to its next decision check, +16 mode (GM_*), +17 frames to
  38+ C000              ;   its next blow, +18 hit points, +21,+22 height of its feet, +23,+24 and
  39+ C000              ;   +25,+26 velocity x, z (signed 8.8 units a frame), +27,+28 the x and z
  40+ C000              ;   position fractions, +29 climb (signed units a frame), +30 target
  41+ C000              ;   (TG_NONE, TG_VTOL, or a human's index).
  42+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  43+ C000
  44+ C000              GHOST_HP        equ 10              ; hit points
  45+ C000              GHOST_DECIDE    equ 30              ; frames between decisions while roaming
  46+ C000              GHOST_TRACK     equ 5               ; and while chasing
  47+ C000              GHOST_SEE       equ 3*TILE_UNITS    ; it spots humans / the VTOL this near
  48+ C000              GHOST_HIT_FRAMES equ 10             ; frames between blows
  49+ C000              GHOST_DMG       equ 1               ; hit points a blow
  50+ C000              GHOST_CLIMB     equ 1               ; units a frame up or down
  51+ C000              GHOST_AIM_MIN   equ 80              ; (aiming: nearer counts as this far)
  52+ C000              GM_ROAM         equ 0               ; modes (+16)
  53+ C000              GM_CHASE        equ 1
  54+ C000              GM_ATTACK       equ 2
  55+ C000              GM_DYING        equ 3               ; killed: fading out (+17 frames left)
  56+ C000              GHOST_FADE_FRAMES equ 12            ; the fade: 4 steps of 3 frames
  57+ C000              TG_NONE         equ $FF             ; targets (+30)
  58+ C000              TG_VTOL         equ $FE
  59+ C000              TG_BASE         equ $C0             ; + a base tile's index (base_hp, 0..24)
  60+ C000              ; The banshee (ZT_BANSHEE, special monster id 1): a ghost that flies twice
  61+ C000              ; as fast, reaches BANSHEE_R (0.5 tile), does BANSHEE_DMG a blow and also
  62+ C000              ; attacks the base (the terrace and pad tiles).  One spawns at the start of
  63+ C000              ; every even wave up to level 10, two up to level 30, three after that.
  64+ C000              ; The ghoul (ZT_GHOUL, zombie.asm): a half-height zombie walking at five
  65+ C000              ; times the zombies' speed.  It is placed here (spec_wave) but moved, drawn
  66+ C000              ; and hit as a zombie.  One spawns at the start of every odd wave on levels
  67+ C000              ; 21-29, two on levels 30-39, three from level 40.
  68+ C000              BANSHEE_HP      equ 8
  69+ C000              BANSHEE_DMG     equ 5
  70+ C000              BANSHEE_R       equ TILE_UNITS/2
  71+ C000              BASE_TILES      equ (2*TERRACE_R+1)*(2*TERRACE_R+1)
  72+ C000
  73+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  74+ C000              ; New game: place the level's special monsters (all ghosts for now: the only
  75+ C000              ; special, id 0), leave the other special records inactive
  76+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  77+ C000              spec_init:
  78+ C000 AF                   xor a
  79+ C001 32 6A 34             ld (zwave_new),a
  80+ C004 3E 03                ld a,ZT_GHOST                   ; the level's ghosts
  81+ C006 32 4B C7             ld (sp_type),a
  82+ C009 3A 67 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_SPECIAL)
  83+ C00C FE 09                cp NSPEC+1
  84+ C00E 38 02                jr c,.n
  85+ C010 3E 08                ld a,NSPEC
  86+ C012 32 4D C7     .n:     ld (sp_left),a
  87+ C015 DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
  88+ C019 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
  89+ C01B 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB                      ; record index
  90+ C01D C5           .lp:    push bc
  91+ C01E 79                   ld a,c
  92+ C01F 32 4C C7             ld (sp_idx),a
  93+ C022 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags                ; not attacking
  94+ C025 ED 31                add hl,a
  95+ C027 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
  96+ C029 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; inactive,
  97+ C02D DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0                    ; no blip,
  98+ C031 DD 36 13 FF          ld (ix+19),$FF                  ; no cell marked
  99+ C035 3A 4D C7             ld a,(sp_left)
 100+ C038 B7                   or a
 101+ C039 28 07                jr z,.nx
 102+ C03B 3D                   dec a
 103+ C03C 32 4D C7             ld (sp_left),a
 104+ C03F CD 51 C0             call ghost_init
 105+ C042 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 106+ C045 DD 19                add ix,de
 107+ C047 C1                   pop bc
 108+ C048 0C                   inc c
 109+ C049 10 D2                djnz .lp
 110+ C04B CD 35 C1             call spec_wave                  ; wave 1's ghouls
 111+ C04E C3 95 26             jp zflags                       ; (zombie.asm) mark their cells
 112+ C051
 113+ C051              ; Special monster IX (record sp_idx) of type sp_type: at a random street
 114+ C051              ; position and height, roaming in a random direction
 115+ C051              ghost_init:
 116+ C051 3A 4B C7             ld a,(sp_type)
 117+ C054 DD 77 0F             ld (ix+15),a
 118+ C057 CD C7 C1             call g_params                   ; (its speed for its first direction)
 119+ C05A CD B5 2C     .try:   call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 120+ C05D 7D                   ld a,l
 121+ C05E E6 3F                and GRID-1
 122+ C060 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a                     ; x cell
 123+ C063 7C                   ld a,h
 124+ C064 E6 3F                and GRID-1
 125+ C066 DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a                     ; z cell
 126+ C069 CD FA 2A             call zmap_ptr
 127+ C06C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 128+ C06D E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 129+ C06F 20 E9                jr nz,.try                      ; a building: pick again
 130+ C071 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 131+ C074 7D                   ld a,l
 132+ C075 E6 7F                and 127
 133+ C077 C6 40                add a,64                        ; 64..191 inside the cell
 134+ C079 DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 135+ C07C 7C                   ld a,h
 136+ C07D E6 7F                and 127
 137+ C07F C6 40                add a,64
 138+ C081 DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 139+ C084 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 140+ C087 7D                   ld a,l
 141+ C088 E6 7F                and 127                         ; height 0..127 (MAX_Y 192)
 142+ C08A DD 77 15             ld (ix+21),a
 143+ C08D DD 36 16 00          ld (ix+22),0
 144+ C091 AF                   xor a
 145+ C092 DD 77 1B             ld (ix+27),a                    ; position fractions
 146+ C095 DD 77 1C             ld (ix+28),a
 147+ C098 DD 77 05             ld (ix+5),a
 148+ C09B DD 77 07             ld (ix+7),a
 149+ C09E DD 36 10 00          ld (ix+16),GM_ROAM
 150+ C0A2 DD 36 12 0A          ld (ix+18),GHOST_HP
 151+ C0A6 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 152+ C0A9 FE 04                cp ZT_BANSHEE
 153+ C0AB 20 04                jr nz,.hp
 154+ C0AD DD 36 12 08          ld (ix+18),BANSHEE_HP
 155+ C0B1              .hp:
 156+ C0B1 DD 36 1E FF          ld (ix+30),TG_NONE
 157+ C0B5 DD 36 0A 01          ld (ix+10),1                    ; active
 158+ C0B9 3A 4C C7             ld a,(sp_idx)                   ; first decisions spread over the
 159+ C0BC 5F                   ld e,a                          ; 30 frames
 160+ C0BD 16 07                ld d,7
 161+ C0BF ED 30                mul d,e
 162+ C0C1 7B                   ld a,e
 163+ C0C2 D6 1E        .mod:   sub GHOST_DECIDE
 164+ C0C4 30 FC                jr nc,.mod
 165+ C0C6 C6 1F                add a,GHOST_DECIDE+1
 166+ C0C8 DD 77 0B             ld (ix+11),a
 167+ C0CB CD 2F C2             call g_random
 168+ C0CE C3 DB 3A             jp zblip                        ; (map.asm)
 169+ C0D1
 170+ C0D1              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 171+ C0D1              ; Per frame (after zombie_update): move every special monster, then mark
 172+ C0D1              ; the cells again
 173+ C0D1              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 174+ C0D1              spec_update:
 175+ C0D1 3A 6A 34             ld a,(zwave_new)                ; a new wave: its banshees or ghouls
 176+ C0D4 B7                   or a
 177+ C0D5 C4 35 C1             call nz,spec_wave
 178+ C0D8 DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
 179+ C0DC 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
 180+ C0DE 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB
 181+ C0E0 C5           .lp:    push bc
 182+ C0E1 79                   ld a,c
 183+ C0E2 32 4C C7             ld (sp_idx),a
 184+ C0E5 DD CB 0A 46          bit 0,(ix+10)
 185+ C0E9 28 0D                jr z,.nx                        ; gone
 186+ C0EB DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 187+ C0EE FE 03                cp ZT_GHOST
 188+ C0F0 38 06                jr c,.nx                        ; (a ghoul walks: zombie_update)
 189+ C0F2 CD C7 C1             call g_params                   ; a ghost or banshee: its settings
 190+ C0F5 CD 04 C1             call ghost_move
 191+ C0F8 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 192+ C0FB DD 19                add ix,de
 193+ C0FD C1                   pop bc
 194+ C0FE 0C                   inc c
 195+ C0FF 10 DF                djnz .lp
 196+ C101 C3 95 26             jp zflags                       ; their cells, as they are now
 197+ C104
 198+ C104              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 199+ C104              ; Ghost IX, one frame
 200+ C104              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 201+ C104              ghost_move:
 202+ C104 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 203+ C107 FE 03                cp GM_DYING
 204+ C109 CA 01 C2             jp z,g_dying
 205+ C10C FE 02                cp GM_ATTACK
 206+ C10E CA 73 C3             jp z,g_attack
 207+ C111 DD 35 0B             dec (ix+11)                     ; decision check due?
 208+ C114 CC 0A C2             call z,g_decide
 209+ C117 CD 66 C2             call g_step                     ; fly
 210+ C11A DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 211+ C11D FE 01                cp GM_CHASE

 213+ C122 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 214+ C125 FE 02                cp GM_ATTACK

 218+ C12E 86                   add a,(hl)
 219+ C12F E6 07                and 7
 220+ C131 C0                   ret nz
 221+ C132 C3 DB 3A             jp zblip
 222+ C135
 223+ C135              ; A new wave began (zwave_new, zombie.asm; spec_init for wave 1): into
 224+ C135              ; free special records, on an even wave banshees: 1 up to level 10, 2 up to
 225+ C135              ; level 30, 3 after; on an odd wave ghouls: 1 on levels 21-29, 2 on 30-39,
 226+ C135              ; 3 from 40
 227+ C135              spec_wave:
 228+ C135 AF                   xor a
 229+ C136 32 6A 34             ld (zwave_new),a
 230+ C139 3A DB A7             ld a,(wave_number)
 231+ C13C E6 01                and 1
 232+ C13E 20 13                jr nz,.odd
 233+ C140 3A D2 A7             ld a,(level)

 235+ C145 FE 0B                cp 11
 236+ C147 38 06                jr c,.n
 237+ C149 0C                   inc c
 238+ C14A FE 1F                cp 31

 241+ C14F 3E 04        .n:     ld a,ZT_BANSHEE

 244+ C156 0E 00                ld c,0
 245+ C158 FE 15                cp 21

 250+ C160 0C                   inc c
 251+ C161 FE 28                cp 40
 252+ C163 38 01                jr c,.g
 253+ C165 0C                   inc c
 254+ C166 3E 02        .g:     ld a,ZT_GHOUL
 255+ C168 32 4B C7     .go:    ld (sp_type),a

 257+ C16C 32 4D C7             ld (sp_left),a
 258+ C16F DD 21 6D 33          ld ix,zombies+NZOMB*ZREC
 259+ C173 06 08                ld b,NSPEC
 260+ C175 0E 1E                ld c,NZOMB
 261+ C177 3A 4D C7     .lp:    ld a,(sp_left)

 263+ C17B C8                   ret z                           ; all placed
 264+ C17C DD CB 0A 46          bit 0,(ix+10)
 265+ C180 20 25                jr nz,.nx                       ; in use
 266+ C182 C5                   push bc
 267+ C183 79                   ld a,c
 268+ C184 32 4C C7             ld (sp_idx),a
 269+ C187 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags
 270+ C18A ED 31                add hl,a
 271+ C18C 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
 272+ C18E DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0                    ; no blip yet
 273+ C192 21 4D C7             ld hl,sp_left
 274+ C195 35                   dec (hl)
 275+ C196 3A 4B C7             ld a,(sp_type)
 276+ C199 FE 02                cp ZT_GHOUL
 277+ C19B 28 06                jr z,.gh
 278+ C19D CD 51 C0             call ghost_init
 279+ C1A0 C1                   pop bc
 280+ C1A1 18 04                jr .nx
 281+ C1A3 CD B0 C1     .gh:    call ghoul_init
 282+ C1A6 C1                   pop bc
 283+ C1A7 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 284+ C1AA DD 19                add ix,de
 285+ C1AC 0C                   inc c
 286+ C1AD 10 C8                djnz .lp
 287+ C1AF C9                   ret                             ; (no room for the rest)
 288+ C1B0
 289+ C1B0              ; A ghoul in record IX (sp_idx): placed as a zombie (zplace, zombie.asm),
 290+ C1B0              ; then made a ghoul; its first decision check spread by its index
 291+ C1B0              ghoul_init:
 292+ C1B0 CD 9F 25             call zplace
 293+ C1B3 DD 36 0F 02          ld (ix+15),ZT_GHOUL
 294+ C1B7 DD 36 12 05          ld (ix+18),GHOUL_HP
 295+ C1BB 3A 4C C7             ld a,(sp_idx)
 296+ C1BE E6 07                and 7
 297+ C1C0 3C                   inc a
 298+ C1C1 DD 77 0B             ld (ix+11),a
 299+ C1C4 C3 DB 3A             jp zblip                        ; (map.asm)
 300+ C1C7
 301+ C1C7              ; Special monster IX's settings, from its type (+15): g_speed (8.8 units a
 302+ C1C7              ; frame: a ghost twice the zombies' zspeed, a banshee four times), gp_range
 303+ C1C7              ; (its reach on each axis), gp_dmg (a blow), gp_base (1: attacks the base)
 304+ C1C7              g_params:
 305+ C1C7 3A 6B 2F             ld a,(zspeed)                   ; zspeed (4.4) * 32 = twice it, 8.8
 306+ C1CA 6F                   ld l,a
 307+ C1CB 26 00                ld h,0
 308+ C1CD 29                   add hl,hl
 309+ C1CE 29                   add hl,hl
 310+ C1CF 29                   add hl,hl
 311+ C1D0 29                   add hl,hl
 312+ C1D1 29                   add hl,hl
 313+ C1D2 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 314+ C1D5 FE 04                cp ZT_BANSHEE
 315+ C1D7 28 13                jr z,.b
 316+ C1D9 22 45 C7             ld (g_speed),hl                 ; a ghost
 317+ C1DC 21 1A 00             ld hl,ATTACK_R
 318+ C1DF 22 47 C7             ld (gp_range),hl
 319+ C1E2 3E 01                ld a,GHOST_DMG
 320+ C1E4 32 49 C7             ld (gp_dmg),a
 321+ C1E7 AF                   xor a
 322+ C1E8 32 4A C7             ld (gp_base),a
 323+ C1EB C9                   ret
 324+ C1EC 29           .b:     add hl,hl                       ; a banshee: twice as fast
 325+ C1ED 22 45 C7             ld (g_speed),hl
 326+ C1F0 21 80 00             ld hl,BANSHEE_R
 327+ C1F3 22 47 C7             ld (gp_range),hl
 328+ C1F6 3E 05                ld a,BANSHEE_DMG
 329+ C1F8 32 49 C7             ld (gp_dmg),a
 330+ C1FB 3E 01                ld a,1
 331+ C1FD 32 4A C7             ld (gp_base),a
 332+ C200 C9                   ret
 333+ C201
 334+ C201              ; Killed (zdamage): it hangs where it is, fading out (zombie_draw colours
 335+ C201              ; it by the frames left), then is gone
 336+ C201              g_dying:
 337+ C201 DD 35 11             dec (ix+17)
 338+ C204 C0                   ret nz
 339+ C205 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; gone (zflags unmarks its cell)
 340+ C209 C9                   ret
 341+ C20A
 342+ C20A              ; Decision check: look for a human or the VTOL to chase
 343+ C20A              g_decide:
 344+ C20A CD D7 C4             call g_find                     ; carry: a target, at gt_x/z/y
 345+ C20D 30 0B                jr nc,.none
 346+ C20F DD 36 10 01          ld (ix+16),GM_CHASE
 347+ C213 DD 36 0B 05          ld (ix+11),GHOST_TRACK

 349+ C21A DD 36 0B 1E  .none:  ld (ix+11),GHOST_DECIDE
 350+ C21E DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 351+ C221 B7                   or a
 352+ C222 C8                   ret z                           ; roaming: fly on
 353+ C223                      ; lost it (gone, or out of sight): roam again, a new direction
 354+ C223 DD 36 10 00  g_lose: ld (ix+16),GM_ROAM
 355+ C227 DD 36 0B 1E          ld (ix+11),GHOST_DECIDE
 356+ C22B DD 36 1E FF          ld (ix+30),TG_NONE
 357+ C22F                      ; fall through
 358+ C22F
 359+ C22F              ; A random direction: velocity g_speed at a random angle, and a random climb
 360+ C22F              ; (-1, 0, +1, 0 units a frame)
 361+ C22F              g_random:
 362+ C22F CD B5 2C             call zrand
 363+ C232 E5                   push hl
 364+ C233 7D                   ld a,l
 365+ C234 F5                   push af
 366+ C235 CD C8 A6             call get_sin                    ; (resident; pages the sine table)
 367+ C238 EB                   ex de,hl
 368+ C239 2A 45 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)
 369+ C23C CD 7F A7             call fmul                       ; vx = speed * sin (8.8)
 370+ C23F DD 75 17             ld (ix+23),l
 371+ C242 DD 74 18             ld (ix+24),h
 372+ C245 F1                   pop af
 373+ C246 C6 40                add a,64
 374+ C248 CD C8 A6             call get_sin
 375+ C24B EB                   ex de,hl
 376+ C24C 2A 45 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)
 377+ C24F CD 7F A7             call fmul                       ; vz = speed * cos
 378+ C252 DD 75 19             ld (ix+25),l
 379+ C255 DD 74 1A             ld (ix+26),h
 380+ C258 E1                   pop hl
 381+ C259 7C                   ld a,h
 382+ C25A E6 03                and 3
 383+ C25C 21 41 C7             ld hl,g_climbs
 384+ C25F ED 31                add hl,a
 385+ C261 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 386+ C262 DD 77 1D             ld (ix+29),a
 387+ C265 C9                   ret
 388+ C266
 389+ C266              ; Fly one frame: x, z by the velocity, unless that runs into a building
 390+ C266              ; taller than its feet or the map edge (then a new random direction, and a
 391+ C266              ; chase is given up); then the climb, kept between the roof or ground under
 392+ C266              ; it and MAX_Y
 393+ C266              g_step:
 394+ C266 DD 6E 1B             ld l,(ix+27)                    ; keep the old position
 395+ C269 DD 66 00             ld h,(ix+0)

 403+ C281 32 53 C7             ld (gs_z+2),a

 405+ C287 DD 56 18             ld d,(ix+24)

 409+ C28D 4F                   ld c,a                          ; sign
 410+ C28E DD 7E 1B             ld a,(ix+27)
 411+ C291 83                   add a,e
 412+ C292 DD 77 1B             ld (ix+27),a

 414+ C298 8A                   adc a,d
 415+ C299 DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a

 417+ C29F 89                   adc a,c
 418+ C2A0 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 419+ C2A3 DD 5E 19             ld e,(ix+25)                    ; z likewise
 420+ C2A6 DD 56 1A             ld d,(ix+26)
 421+ C2A9 7A                   ld a,d
 422+ C2AA 17                   rla
 423+ C2AB 9F                   sbc a,a
 424+ C2AC 4F                   ld c,a
 425+ C2AD DD 7E 1C             ld a,(ix+28)
 426+ C2B0 83                   add a,e

 433+ C2BF DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a

 437+ C2CA DD 56 16             ld d,(ix+22)

 442+ C2D3 2A 4E C7     .blk:   ld hl,(gs_x)                    ; blocked: back, and turn
 443+ C2D6 DD 75 1B             ld (ix+27),l
 444+ C2D9 DD 74 00             ld (ix+0),h
 445+ C2DC 3A 50 C7             ld a,(gs_x+2)
 446+ C2DF DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 447+ C2E2 2A 51 C7             ld hl,(gs_z)

 455+ C2F8 C3 23 C2             jp g_lose                       ; chasing: give it up

 457+ C2FE DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)
 458+ C301 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 459+ C304 5F                   ld e,a
 460+ C305 17                   rla
 461+ C306 9F                   sbc a,a
 462+ C307 57                   ld d,a
 463+ C308 19                   add hl,de
 464+ C309 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 465+ C30B 28 03                jr z,.pos
 466+ C30D 21 00 00             ld hl,0

 468+ C313 CD 42 C3             call g_min
 469+ C316 EB                   ex de,hl
 470+ C317 D5                   push de
 471+ C318 CD 26 C3             call g_ground                   ; nor below the roof under it
 472+ C31B D1                   pop de
 473+ C31C CD 49 C3             call g_max_de
 474+ C31F DD 75 15             ld (ix+21),l
 475+ C322 DD 74 16             ld (ix+22),h
 476+ C325 C9                   ret
 477+ C326
 478+ C326              ; HL = the roof height (0 on a road) of ghost IX's cell; carry set if the
 479+ C326              ; cell is off the map (then HL = $FFFF)
 480+ C326              g_ground:
 481+ C326 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)

 491+ C33C C9                   ret

 494+ C341 C9                   ret

 496+ C342              ; HL = min(HL, DE), unsigned

 501+ C347 EB                   ex de,hl
 502+ C348 C9                   ret
 503+ C349              ; HL = max(HL, DE), unsigned

 510+ C34F C9                   ret
 511+ C350
 512+ C350              ; Chasing: aim at the target's place again (g_find left it in gt_*) is done
 513+ C350              ; at the decision checks; here, check whether it is within reach and if so

 515+ C350              g_reach:

 526+ C36A              ; HL -> ghost IX's zatk_flags entry

 531+ C373
 532+ C373              ; Attacking: one blow every GHOST_HIT_FRAMES frames while the target is in
 533+ C373              ; reach

 580+ C3E6
 581+ C3E6              ; IX -> the record of ghost IX's target human (+30).  (Push IX first.)

 662+ C48D DD 6E 15             ld l,(ix+21)
 663+ C490 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
 664+ C493 ED 5B 64 C7          ld de,(gt_y)
 665+ C497                      ; fall through
 666+ C497              ; Carry set if |HL - DE| <= gp_range (the monster's reach)
 667+ C497              g_close:
 668+ C497 B7                   or a
 669+ C498 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 670+ C49A CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 671+ C49D ED 5B 47 C7          ld de,(gp_range)
 672+ C4A1 13                   inc de
 673+ C4A2 B7                   or a
 674+ C4A3 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 675+ C4A5 C9                   ret
 676+ C4A6
 677+ C4A6              ; A = a base tile's index (row * 5 + column of the 5x5 square round the
 678+ C4A6              ; pad) -> E, D = its cell
 679+ C4A6              g_basecell:
 680+ C4A6 16 1E                ld d,PAD_Z-TERRACE_R
 681+ C4A8 FE 05        .r:     cp 2*TERRACE_R+1
 682+ C4AA 38 05                jr c,.c
 683+ C4AC D6 05                sub 2*TERRACE_R+1
 684+ C4AE 14                   inc d
 685+ C4AF 18 F7                jr .r
 686+ C4B1 C6 1E        .c:     add a,PAD_X-TERRACE_R
 687+ C4B3 5F                   ld e,a
 688+ C4B4 C9                   ret
 689+ C4B5
 690+ C4B5              ; gf_x/z/y = the centre of base tile (E, D) and its top (its height now)
 691+ C4B5              g_tilepos:
 692+ C4B5 63                   ld h,e
 693+ C4B6 2E 80                ld l,128
 694+ C4B8 22 6A C7             ld (gf_x),hl

 696+ C4BC 22 6C C7             ld (gf_z),hl
 697+ C4BF 6A                   ld l,d
 698+ C4C0 26 00                ld h,0
 699+ C4C2                      ROWOFS
 699+ C4C2 29          >        add hl,hl
 699+ C4C3 29          >        add hl,hl
 699+ C4C4 29          >        add hl,hl
 699+ C4C5 29          >        add hl,hl
 699+ C4C6 29          >        add hl,hl
 699+ C4C7 29          >        add hl,hl
 700+ C4C8 7B                   ld a,e
 701+ C4C9 ED 31                add hl,a
 702+ C4CB 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 703+ C4CE 19                   add hl,de
 704+ C4CF 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 705+ C4D0 CD 3F A6             call bld_height
 706+ C4D3 22 6E C7             ld (gf_y),hl
 707+ C4D6 C9                   ret
 708+ C4D7
 709+ C4D7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 710+ C4D7              ; Look for the nearest human (waiting) or the VTOL within GHOST_SEE of ghost
 711+ C4D7              ; IX on each axis that it can see (g_sight).  Carry set if found: +30 = the
 712+ C4D7              ; target, gt_x/z/y = where it is.
 713+ C4D7              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 714+ C4D7              g_find:

 719+ C4E2 2A B9 A7             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 720+ C4E5 22 6A C7             ld (gf_x),hl
 721+ C4E8 2A BC A7             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 722+ C4EB 22 6C C7             ld (gf_z),hl
 723+ C4EE 2A BE A7             ld hl,(cam_y)

 727+ C4F9 FD 21 31 39          ld iy,humans
 728+ C4FD 06 14                ld b,HUMAN_MAX
 729+ C4FF 0E 00                ld c,0
 730+ C501 C5           .hl:    push bc
 731+ C502 FD 7E 06             ld a,(iy+6)
 732+ C505 FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 733+ C507 20 28                jr nz,.hn
 734+ C509 79                   ld a,c
 735+ C50A 32 69 C7             ld (gf_who),a
 736+ C50D FD 6E 00             ld l,(iy+0)
 737+ C510 FD 66 01             ld h,(iy+1)
 738+ C513 22 6A C7             ld (gf_x),hl
 739+ C516 FD 6E 02             ld l,(iy+2)
 740+ C519 FD 66 03             ld h,(iy+3)
 741+ C51C 22 6C C7             ld (gf_z),hl

 743+ C522 FD 66 05             ld h,(iy+5)
 744+ C525 22 6E C7             ld (gf_y),hl

 746+ C529 C5                   push bc

 754+ C538 10 C7                djnz .hl

 757+ C53F B7                   or a

 761+ C547 A5                   and l
 762+ C548 3C                   inc a

 764+ C54A 3F                   ccf
 765+ C54B C8                   ret z                           ; nobody (carry clear)

 768+ C552 37                   scf
 769+ C553 C9                   ret
 770+ C554
 771+ C554              ; The base tiles still standing (base_hp) as candidates for ghost IX

 777+ C55B ED 31                add hl,a
 778+ C55D 7E                   ld a,(hl)

 780+ C55F 28 10                jr z,.nx                        ; destroyed (road now)
 781+ C561 79                   ld a,c
 782+ C562 C6 C0                add a,TG_BASE

 784+ C567 79                   ld a,c
 785+ C568 CD A6 C4             call g_basecell
 786+ C56B CD B5 C4             call g_tilepos                  ; gf_x/z/y = the candidate
 787+ C56E CD 79 C5             call g_cand
 788+ C571 C1           .nx:    pop bc
 789+ C572 0C                   inc c
 790+ C573 79                   ld a,c
 791+ C574 FE 19                cp BASE_TILES
 792+ C576 20 DE                jr nz,.lp
 793+ C578 C9                   ret
 794+ C579
 795+ C579              ; Candidate gf_who at gf_x/z/y for ghost IX: if within GHOST_SEE on each
 796+ C579              ; axis, nearer than the best so far and in sight, it becomes the best
 797+ C579              ; (gf_best = its distance, gf_tgt, gt_x/z/y).  Preserves IX.
 798+ C579              g_cand:

 827+ C5B7 CD 7A A7             call abs_hl

 841+ C5D1 CD F4 C5             call g_sight

 844+ C5D8 22 66 C7             ld (gf_best),hl

 846+ C5DE 32 68 C7             ld (gf_tgt),a

 853+ C5F3 C9                   ret
 854+ C5F4
 855+ C5F4              ; Can ghost IX see gf_x/z/y?  Carry set if a building is in the way: the
 856+ C5F4              ; line between them is sampled at 7 points (1/8 .. 7/8 of the way), each
 857+ C5F4              ; checked against the roof of the cell it is over (not the target's own
 858+ C5F4              ; cell).  Preserves IX.

 909+ C671 26 00                ld h,0
 910+ C673                      ROWOFS
 910+ C673 29          >        add hl,hl
 910+ C674 29          >        add hl,hl

 929+ C698              ; HL = HL / 8, signed

 936+ C6A4 C9                   ret
 937+ C6A5
 938+ C6A5              ; Aim ghost IX at gt_x/z/y: velocity g_speed straight at it, climbing or
 939+ C6A5              ; descending towards its height
 940+ C6A5              g_aim:
 941+ C6A5 DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)                     ; dx, dz = target - ghost

 953+ C6C0 ED 52                sbc hl,de

 956+ C6C8 EB                   ex de,hl
 957+ C6C9 2A 70 C7             ld hl,(gf_dx)
 958+ C6CC CD 7A A7             call abs_hl
 959+ C6CF B7                   or a
 960+ C6D0 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 961+ C6D2 19                   add hl,de
 962+ C6D3 30 01                jr nc,.mx
 963+ C6D5 EB                   ex de,hl
 964+ C6D6 E5           .mx:    push hl
 965+ C6D7 EB                   ex de,hl
 966+ C6D8 54                   ld d,h
 967+ C6D9 5D                   ld e,l
 968+ C6DA 29                   add hl,hl
 969+ C6DB 19                   add hl,de
 970+ C6DC CB 3C                srl h
 971+ C6DE CB 1D                rr l
 972+ C6E0 CB 3C                srl h
 973+ C6E2 CB 1D                rr l
 974+ C6E4 CB 3C                srl h
 975+ C6E6 CB 1D                rr l                            ; 3/8 min
 976+ C6E8 D1                   pop de
 977+ C6E9 19                   add hl,de
 978+ C6EA 11 50 00             ld de,GHOST_AIM_MIN             ; (so the factor stays <= 1.0)
 979+ C6ED CD 49 C3             call g_max_de
 980+ C6F0 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = distance
 981+ C6F1 2A 45 C7             ld hl,(g_speed)                 ; factor = speed * 32 / distance
 982+ C6F4 29                   add hl,hl                       ; (at most 1.0, even for a banshee)
 983+ C6F5 29                   add hl,hl
 984+ C6F6 29                   add hl,hl

 986+ C6F8 29                   add hl,hl

 990+ C702 ED 5B 76 C7          ld de,(ga_t)
 991+ C706 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 992+ C709 29                   add hl,hl
 993+ C70A 29                   add hl,hl
 994+ C70B 29                   add hl,hl
 995+ C70C DD 75 17             ld (ix+23),l
 996+ C70F DD 74 18             ld (ix+24),h

 998+ C715 ED 5B 76 C7          ld de,(ga_t)

1000+ C71C 29                   add hl,hl
1001+ C71D 29                   add hl,hl
1002+ C71E 29                   add hl,hl
1003+ C71F DD 75 19             ld (ix+25),l
1004+ C722 DD 74 1A             ld (ix+26),h

1006+ C728 DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+22)
1007+ C72B EB                   ex de,hl
1008+ C72C 2A 64 C7             ld hl,(gt_y)
1009+ C72F B7                   or a
1010+ C730 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1011+ C732 3E 00                ld a,0

# file opened: zombie.asm
   1+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 2000              ; Z-POLICE - zombie page (8K page PAGE_ZOMBIE, mapped at $2000 in MMU slot 1)
   3+ 2000              ;
   4+ 2000              ; Placeholder zombies: Layer 2 stick-man silhouettes walking the streets.
   5+ 2000              ; The page stays mapped at $2000 the whole time (slot 0 is used for paging
   6+ 2000              ; Layer 2 sections, slot 7 for the other code pages), so the city page can
   7+ 2000              ; call in here while it draws.
   8+ 2000              ;
   9+ 2000              ; Each zombie is a ZREC-byte record:
  10+ 2000              ;   +0,+1  x  8.8 fixed point in cell units (high byte = cell, low = fraction;
  11+ 2000              ;             the same as world units, one cell = 256)
  12+ 2000              ;   +2,+3  z  likewise
  13+ 2000              ;   +4     heading 0 = +z, 1 = +x, 2 = -z, 3 = -x  (yaw angle = heading*64;
  14+ 2000              ;             heading+1 turns right, heading+3 turns left)
  15+ 2000              ;   +5     walk animation phase 0..3
  16+ 2000              ;   +6     step accumulator: fraction of a world unit (4 bits)
  17+ 2000              ;   +7     wait: frames left standing still (0 = walking)
  18+ 2000              ;   +8,+9  cell (x, z) of the last turn decision ($FF = none)
  19+ 2000              ;   +10    active: 1 = in play, 0 = gone (not moved, linked or drawn)
  20+ 2000              ;   +11    frames until this zombie's next decision check
  21+ 2000              ;   +12,+13  cell (x, z) of its blip on the street map
  22+ 2000              ;   +14    1 = blip plotted
  23+ 2000              ;   +15    type: 0 = zombie, 1 = fast zombie (walks at twice the speed),
  24+ 2000              ;          ZT_GHOUL (a special: 5x, half height), ZT_GHOST, ZT_BANSHEE
  25+ 2000              ;   +16    mode: 0 = walking, 1 = going for a base tile, 2 = attacking it
  26+ 2000              ;   +17    frames to its next hit on the tile
  27+ 2000              ;   +18    hit points left (ZHP, ZHP_FAST; weapons.asm zdamage)
  28+ 2000              ;   +19,+20  cell (x, z) marked with CELL_ZOMBIE in city_map ($FF = none)
  29+ 2000              ;   +21,+22  height of its feet (0 for zombies; special monsters fly)
  30+ 2000              ;   +23..+30 special monsters' own fields (specials.asm)
  31+ 2000              ; Records 0..NZOMB-1 are the zombies, NZOMB..NMON-1 the special monsters
  32+ 2000              ; (specials.asm), which share the drawing, cell marks, map blips and weapon
  33+ 2000              ; hits; their type (+15) says which.
  34+ 2000              ; zatk_flags (one byte per zombie, by index): TRUE while it attacks a base
  35+ 2000              ; tile, when its map dot is red; FALSE again once the tile is destroyed.
  36+ 2000              ;
  37+ 2000              ; The base: at junctions a zombie leans towards the road that points at the
  38+ 2000              ; landing pad (zbias_t, from the level).  A base tile (terrace or pad) in its
  39+ 2000              ; way isn't avoided: it walks up to ATTACK_R from it and hits it every
  40+ 2000              ; ZHIT_FRAMES frames.  A destroyed terrace tile becomes road and the zombie
  41+ 2000              ; walks on; the pad destroyed sets game_over.
  42+ 2000              ;
  43+ 2000              ; Street map blips: zblip / zblip_off (map.asm) are called on the zombie's
  44+ 2000              ; decision-check frames.  Call zblip_off for a zombie before making it gone.
  45+ 2000              ;
  46+ 2000              ; Decisions are staggered: each active zombie only checks for junctions and
  47+ 2000              ; obstacles once every zsteps frames, with the zombies' check frames spread
  48+ 2000              ; out so only about NZOMB/zsteps of them check on any one frame.  In between
  49+ 2000              ; they just walk, so the look-ahead (zlook) covers zsteps frames of walking
  50+ 2000              ; plus a margin: ZMARGIN + zsteps*zspeed/16.
  51+ 2000              ;
  52+ 2000              ; Movement (zmove), every frame:
  53+ 2000              ;   * Standing still: count the wait down and do nothing else.
  54+ 2000              ;   * Not this zombie's check frame: step forwards (see the last point).
  55+ 2000              ;   * Junctions: once per cell, when the zombie is 0.25-0.75 of the way
  56+ 2000              ;     through the cell along its heading, look for a road (street cell) to
  57+ 2000              ;     the left and to the right.  Left is tested first: if there is a road,
  58+ 2000              ;     turn left at random (1 in 2); if not taken, the same for the right.
  59+ 2000              ;     The cell is then marked as decided, so it isn't tested again until the
  60+ 2000              ;     zombie has been somewhere else.
  61+ 2000              ;   * Blocked (building or map edge within zlook ahead): turn left if there
  62+ 2000              ;     is a road there, else right; otherwise turn round and stand still for
  63+ 2000              ;     ZWAIT frames before testing anything again.
  64+ 2000              ;   * Otherwise step forwards zspeed/16 world units (the remainder is kept,
  65+ 2000              ;     so slow speeds still move smoothly on average).
  66+ 2000              ;
  67+ 2000              ; Zombies are only ever on street (empty) cells.  They are drawn by the city
  68+ 2000              ; traversal when it reaches their cell (draw_cell jumps to zombie_cell for
  69+ 2000              ; empty cells), so the painter's algorithm hides them behind nearer buildings.
  70+ 2000              ; A cell holding zombies has bit 7 (CELL_ZOMBIE) of its city_map byte set,
  71+ 2000              ; by zflags every frame; zombie_cell then searches the records for them.
  72+ 2000              ;
  73+ 2000              ; Sprites (tables_zombie.inc, generated): 8 scales from 1x4 to 8x16 pixels,
  74+ 2000              ; forward and left facings, 4 walk frames.  Rear and right are the mirrored
  75+ 2000              ; forward and left.  One byte per row, bit 7 = leftmost pixel.
  76+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
  77+ 2000
  78+ 2000              NZOMB           equ 30          ; zombie records: the most in a wave
  79+ 2000              NSPEC           equ 8           ; most special monsters (5 ghosts, banshees)
  80+ 2000              NMON            equ NZOMB+NSPEC ; records: zombies, then special monsters
  81+ 2000              ZREC            equ 31          ; bytes per record
  82+ 2000              ZT_FAST         equ 1           ; +15: fast zombie
  83+ 2000              ZT_GHOUL        equ 2           ; +15: a ghoul: a special monster that walks
  84+ 2000                                              ; as a zombie (zmove), half height, 5x speed
  85+ 2000              ZT_GHOST        equ 3           ; +15: a ghost (specials.asm); types from
  86+ 2000              ZT_BANSHEE      equ 4           ; here on fly (specials.asm moves them)
  87+ 2000              ZHP             equ 5           ; hit points: a zombie (level 1)
  88+ 2000              ZHP_FAST        equ 4           ; and a fast zombie
  89+ 2000              GHOUL_HP        equ ZHP         ; and a ghoul
  90+ 2000              ZSPEED_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zspeed: 0.63 units (4.9 cm) per
  91+ 2000                                              ; frame, ~0.7 m/s at 14 fps
  92+ 2000              ZSTEPS_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zsteps: decide every 10 frames
  93+ 2000              ZMARGIN         equ 16          ; keep at least this far from walls: 1.25 m
  94+ 2000              ZWAIT           equ 100         ; frames to stand still when stuck
  95+ 2000              ZOMBIE_H        equ 26          ; height: 2 m (the same as the hit box, ZHIT_H)
  96+ 2000              GHOUL_H         equ ZOMBIE_H/2  ; a ghoul's height: 1 m
  97+ 2000                      ASSERT ZSPEED_INIT*5+15 < 256   ; a ghoul's step (zcalc_step) fits
  98+ 2000
  99+ 2000                      INCLUDE "tables_zombie.inc"     ; sprites, sizes, bitrev
