 101+ 2500              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 102+ 2500              ; Seed NZOMB zombies at random street positions, away from building walls
 103+ 2500              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 104+ 2500              zombie_init:
 105+ 2500 0E 7F                ld c,~CELL_ZOMBIE & $FF         ; no zombie marks in city_map (a new
 106+ 2502 CD E5 26             call map_mask                   ; game reuses it)
 107+ 2505                      ; (zseed: seeded at power-on, stirred by the menu every frame)
 108+ 2505 3A D2 A7             ld a,(level)                    ; junction bias towards the pad:
 109+ 2508 87                   add a,a                         ; min(ZBIAS_MAX, ZBIAS_BASE +
 110+ 2509 C6 30                add a,ZBIAS_BASE                ; ZBIAS_STEP * level) percent
 111+ 250B FE 5F                cp ZBIAS_MAX
 112+ 250D 38 02                jr c,.bp
 113+ 250F 3E 5F                ld a,ZBIAS_MAX
 114+ 2511 57           .bp:    ld d,a                          ; as a 0..255 threshold: pct*41/16
 115+ 2512 1E 29                ld e,41
 116+ 2514 ED 30                mul d,e
 117+ 2516 06 04                ld b,4
 118+ 2518 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 119+ 251A 7B                   ld a,e
 120+ 251B 32 70 2F             ld (zbias_t),a
 121+ 251E DD 21 CB 2F          ld ix,zombies                   ; no zombies, no cells marked
 122+ 2522 06 1E                ld b,NZOMB
 123+ 2524 11 1F 00             ld de,ZREC
 124+ 2527 DD 36 0A 00  .off:   ld (ix+10),0
 125+ 252B DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0
 126+ 252F DD 36 13 FF          ld (ix+19),$FF
 127+ 2533 DD 19                add ix,de
 128+ 2535 10 F0                djnz .off
 129+ 2537                      ; fall through: the first wave
 130+ 2537
 131+ 2537              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 132+ 2537              ; A wave: the level's LV_ZOMBIES zombies (level_cur; at most NZOMB), LV_FAST
 133+ 2537              ; of them fast, at random street positions; the other zombie records stay
 134+ 2537              ; inactive.  zombie_update calls it again when a wave is wiped out.  (The
 135+ 2537              ; records' cell marks, +19 / +20, are left to zflags: a zombie killed this
 136+ 2537              ; frame may still have one.)
 137+ 2537              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 138+ 2537              zombie_wave:
 139+ 2537 3A 66 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_FAST)
 140+ 253A 32 C9 2F             ld (zi_fast),a
 141+ 253D 3A 64 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_ZOMBIES)
 142+ 2540 FE 1F                cp NZOMB+1
 143+ 2542 38 02                jr c,.n
 144+ 2544 3E 1E                ld a,NZOMB
 145+ 2546 32 CA 2F     .n:     ld (zi_left),a
 146+ 2549 DD 21 CB 2F          ld ix,zombies
 147+ 254D 06 1E                ld b,NZOMB
 148+ 254F 0E 01                ld c,1                          ; first check frame, spread 1..zsteps
 149+ 2551 C5           .lp:    push bc
 150+ 2552 3A CA 2F             ld a,(zi_left)                  ; this wave's zombies placed?
 151+ 2555 B7                   or a
 152+ 2556 20 08                jr nz,.one
 153+ 2558 DD 36 0A 00          ld (ix+10),0                    ; the rest: inactive
 154+ 255C C1                   pop bc
 155+ 255D C3 93 25             jp .nx
 156+ 2560 3D           .one:   dec a
 157+ 2561 32 CA 2F             ld (zi_left),a
 158+ 2564 CD 9F 25             call zplace
 159+ 2567 3E 1E                ld a,NZOMB                      ; not attacking
 160+ 2569 90                   sub b
 161+ 256A 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags
 162+ 256D ED 31                add hl,a
 163+ 256F 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE
 164+ 2571 3A C9 2F             ld a,(zi_fast)                  ; zi_fast are fast zombies
 165+ 2574 B7                   or a
 166+ 2575 28 0C                jr z,.typ
 167+ 2577 3D                   dec a
 168+ 2578 32 C9 2F             ld (zi_fast),a
 169+ 257B DD 36 0F 01          ld (ix+15),ZT_FAST
 170+ 257F DD 36 12 04          ld (ix+18),ZHP_FAST
 171+ 2583              .typ:
 172+ 2583 CD DB 3A             call zblip
 173+ 2586 C1                   pop bc
 174+ 2587 DD 71 0B             ld (ix+11),c                    ; first decision check
 175+ 258A 3A 6C 2F             ld a,(zsteps)
 176+ 258D B9                   cp c
 177+ 258E 20 02                jr nz,.nc
 178+ 2590 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; wrap after zsteps
 179+ 2592 0C           .nc:    inc c
 180+ 2593 11 1F 00     .nx:    ld de,ZREC
 181+ 2596 DD 19                add ix,de
 182+ 2598 05                   dec b
 183+ 2599 C2 51 25             jp nz,.lp
 184+ 259C C3 95 26             jp zflags
 185+ 259F
 186+ 259F              ; Record IX: a zombie (type 0, ZHP) at a random street position, walking a
 187+ 259F              ; random heading, active; no blip yet (the caller sets the type, then
 188+ 259F              ; zblip), check frame and attack flag left to the caller.  Preserves BC.
 189+ 259F              zplace:
 190+ 259F CD B5 2C     .try:   call zrand
 191+ 25A2 7D                   ld a,l
 192+ 25A3 E6 3F                and GRID-1
 193+ 25A5 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a                     ; x cell
 194+ 25A8 7C                   ld a,h
 195+ 25A9 E6 3F                and GRID-1
 196+ 25AB DD 77 03             ld (ix+3),a                     ; z cell
 197+ 25AE CD FA 2A             call zmap_ptr
 198+ 25B1 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 199+ 25B2 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 200+ 25B4 20 E9                jr nz,.try                      ; building: pick again
 201+ 25B6 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 202+ 25B9 7D                   ld a,l
 203+ 25BA E6 7F                and 127
 204+ 25BC C6 40                add a,64                        ; 64..191 inside the cell
 205+ 25BE DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 206+ 25C1 7C                   ld a,h
 207+ 25C2 E6 7F                and 127
 208+ 25C4 C6 40                add a,64
 209+ 25C6 DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 210+ 25C9 CD B5 2C             call zrand
 211+ 25CC 7D                   ld a,l
 212+ 25CD E6 03                and 3
 213+ 25CF DD 77 04             ld (ix+4),a
 214+ 25D2 7C                   ld a,h
 215+ 25D3 E6 03                and 3
 216+ 25D5 DD 77 05             ld (ix+5),a
 217+ 25D8 AF                   xor a
 218+ 25D9 DD 77 06             ld (ix+6),a                     ; step accumulator
 219+ 25DC DD 77 07             ld (ix+7),a                     ; walking
 220+ 25DF 3D                   dec a
 221+ 25E0 DD 77 08             ld (ix+8),a                     ; no decision cell yet
 222+ 25E3 DD 77 09             ld (ix+9),a
 223+ 25E6 DD 36 0A 01          ld (ix+10),1                    ; active
 224+ 25EA DD 36 0E 00          ld (ix+14),0                    ; no blip yet
 225+ 25EE DD 36 10 00          ld (ix+16),0                    ; walking
 226+ 25F2 DD 36 11 00          ld (ix+17),0
 227+ 25F6 DD 36 15 00          ld (ix+21),0                    ; on the ground
 228+ 25FA DD 36 16 00          ld (ix+22),0
 229+ 25FE DD 36 0F 00          ld (ix+15),0                    ; a zombie
 230+ 2602 DD 36 12 05          ld (ix+18),ZHP
 231+ 2606 C9                   ret
 232+ 2607
 233+ 2607              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 234+ 2607              ; Per frame: walk every zombie and mark the cells they stand in.  Call after
 235+ 2607              ; setup_frame (uses cos_p/sin_p for the zombie height vector).
 236+ 2607              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 237+ 2607              zombie_update:
 238+ 2607                      ; a wave wiped out (no monster record active: zombies, ghosts and
 239+ 2607                      ; banshees, a dying special still counting until it has faded)?
 240+ 2607                      ; The next wave, or with none left the level is complete
 241+ 2607                      ; (level_done; main_loop)
 242+ 2607 DD 21 CB 2F          ld ix,zombies
 243+ 260B 06 26                ld b,NMON

 245+ 2610 DD CB 0A 46  .cnt:   bit 0,(ix+10)
 246+ 2614 20 1F                jr nz,.alive

 248+ 2618 10 F6                djnz .cnt
 249+ 261A 3A 65 A0             ld a,(level_cur+LV_WAVES)
 250+ 261D 21 DB A7             ld hl,wave_number
 251+ 2620 BE                   cp (hl)
 252+ 2621 28 0D                jr z,.done
 253+ 2623 38 0B                jr c,.done
 254+ 2625 34                   inc (hl)                        ; the next wave
 255+ 2626 3E 01                ld a,1
 256+ 2628 32 6A 34             ld (zwave_new),a                ; (spec_update: banshees on even ones)
 257+ 262B CD 37 25             call zombie_wave
 258+ 262E 18 05                jr .alive
 259+ 2630 3E 01        .done:  ld a,1
 260+ 2632 32 DA A7             ld (level_done),a
 261+ 2635              .alive:
 262+ 2635 21 67 34             ld hl,zanim
 263+ 2638 34                   inc (hl)
 264+ 2639 21 1A 00             ld hl,ZOMBIE_H                  ; height vector (0, H cosP, H sinP)
 265+ 263C ED 5B F0 AD          ld de,(cos_p)
 266+ 2640 CD 7F A7             call fmul
 267+ 2643 22 6D 34             ld (zh_y),hl
 268+ 2646 21 1A 00             ld hl,ZOMBIE_H
 269+ 2649 ED 5B EE AD          ld de,(sin_p)
 270+ 264D CD 7F A7             call fmul
 271+ 2650 22 6F 34             ld (zh_z),hl
 272+ 2653 3A 6C 2F             ld a,(zsteps)                   ; look-ahead: walking between checks
 273+ 2656 5F                   ld e,a                          ; plus the wall margin
 274+ 2657 3A 6B 2F             ld a,(zspeed)
 275+ 265A 57                   ld d,a
 276+ 265B ED 30                mul d,e
 277+ 265D 06 04                ld b,4
 278+ 265F ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 279+ 2661 21 10 00             ld hl,ZMARGIN
 280+ 2664 19                   add hl,de
 281+ 2665 22 6E 2F             ld (zlook),hl
 282+ 2668 19                   add hl,de                       ; fast zombies walk twice as far
 283+ 2669 22 C5 2F             ld (zlook_fast),hl              ; between checks
 284+ 266C 19                   add hl,de                       ; ghouls five times
 285+ 266D 19                   add hl,de
 286+ 266E 19                   add hl,de

 289+ 2676 06 26                ld b,NMON                       ; special records
 290+ 2678 C5           .mv:    push bc

 292+ 267B 90                   sub b
 293+ 267C 32 71 2F             ld (zcur_i),a

 299+ 268D C1           .mn:    pop bc
 300+ 268E 11 1F 00             ld de,ZREC
 301+ 2691 DD 19                add ix,de
 302+ 2693 10 E3                djnz .mv
 303+ 2695                      ; fall through
 304+ 2695
 305+ 2695              ; Mark the zombies' cells in city_map (CELL_ZOMBIE, bit 7): first take the
 306+ 2695              ; mark off every record's marked cell (+19, +20), then mark the cell of each
 307+ 2695              ; active zombie and remember it.  Two passes, so two zombies sharing a cell
 308+ 2695              ; keep it marked, and one killed since the last pass loses its mark.
 309+ 2695              zflags:
 310+ 2695 DD 21 CB 2F          ld ix,zombies
 311+ 2699 06 26                ld b,NMON

 313+ 269E FE 40                cp GRID

 315+ 26A2 DD 6E 14             ld l,(ix+20)
 316+ 26A5 26 00                ld h,0
 317+ 26A7                      ROWOFS
 317+ 26A7 29          >        add hl,hl
 317+ 26A8 29          >        add hl,hl
 317+ 26A9 29          >        add hl,hl
 317+ 26AA 29          >        add hl,hl
 317+ 26AB 29          >        add hl,hl
 317+ 26AC 29          >        add hl,hl

 336+ 26DD 11 1F 00     .sn:    ld de,ZREC
 337+ 26E0 DD 19                add ix,de
 338+ 26E2 10 E2                djnz .set
 339+ 26E4 C9                   ret
 340+ 26E5
 341+ 26E5              ; City_map bytes AND= C, all of them (clears marks at a new game)
 342+ 26E5              map_mask:

 344+ 26E8 16 10                ld d,GRID*GRID/256

 355+ 26F6              ; Move zombie IX for one frame (see the header for the rules)
 356+ 26F6              zmove:
 357+ 26F6 DD 7E 10             ld a,(ix+16)
 358+ 26F9 B7                   or a
 359+ 26FA C2 ED 27             jp nz,zattack                   ; going for / attacking a base tile
 360+ 26FD DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 361+ 2700 B7                   or a

 374+ 2721 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)

 383+ 2738 C3 C2 27             jp zforward
 384+ 273B              .blocked:

 386+ 273E CD AB 2A             call zside
 387+ 2741 CD 48 28             call zbase

 392+ 274F 0E 00        .side:  ld c,0                          ; roads to the left and right
 393+ 2751 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)

 398+ 275D CD 97 29             call zroad
 399+ 2760 79                   ld a,c
 400+ 2761 B7                   or a
 401+ 2762 28 2A                jr z,.dead
 402+ 2764 CD B5 2C             call zrand                      ; biased: the one towards the pad
 403+ 2767 3A 70 2F             ld a,(zbias_t)
 404+ 276A 47                   ld b,a
 405+ 276B 7D                   ld a,l
 406+ 276C B8                   cp b
 407+ 276D 30 07                jr nc,.old
 408+ 276F 79                   ld a,c
 409+ 2770 CD BC 29             call zbest
 410+ 2773 C3 99 2A             jp zturn

 412+ 2779 C6 03                add a,3                         ; else right

 426+ 279B
 427+ 279B              ; This frame's distance for zombie IX (zspeed, twice for a fast zombie,
 428+ 279B              ; five times for a ghoul), 4.4 fixed with the fraction kept -> zstep.
 429+ 279B              ; Keeps B.
 430+ 279B              zcalc_step:
 431+ 279B DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)
 432+ 279E FE 01                cp ZT_FAST
 433+ 27A0 3A 6B 2F             ld a,(zspeed)                   ; distance this frame, 4.4 fixed
 434+ 27A3 38 09                jr c,.reg
 435+ 27A5 4F                   ld c,a
 436+ 27A6 28 05                jr z,.fast
 437+ 27A8 87                   add a,a                         ; a ghoul: five times the speed
 438+ 27A9 87                   add a,a
 439+ 27AA 81                   add a,c
 440+ 27AB 18 01                jr .reg
 441+ 27AD 87           .fast:  add a,a                         ; fast zombie: twice the speed
 442+ 27AE DD 86 06     .reg:   add a,(ix+6)
 443+ 27B1 4F                   ld c,a
 444+ 27B2 E6 0F                and 15
 445+ 27B4 DD 77 06             ld (ix+6),a                     ; keep the fraction
 446+ 27B7 79                   ld a,c
 447+ 27B8 0F                   rrca
 448+ 27B9 0F                   rrca
 449+ 27BA 0F                   rrca
 450+ 27BB 0F                   rrca
 451+ 27BC E6 0F                and 15
 452+ 27BE 32 6D 2F             ld (zstep),a
 453+ 27C1 C9                   ret
 454+ 27C2
 455+ 27C2              ; Move zombie IX A units along its heading
 456+ 27C2              zforward:

 458+ 27C3 26 00                ld h,0
 459+ 27C5 DD 5E 04             ld e,(ix+4)
 460+ 27C8 CB 4B                bit 1,e

 462+ 27CD CB 43                bit 0,e
 463+ 27CF 20 0E                jr nz,.sx
 464+ 27D1 DD 4E 02             ld c,(ix+2)
 465+ 27D4 DD 46 03             ld b,(ix+3)
 466+ 27D7 09                   add hl,bc
 467+ 27D8 DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
 468+ 27DB DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h
 469+ 27DE C9                   ret
 470+ 27DF DD 4E 00     .sx:    ld c,(ix+0)
 471+ 27E2 DD 46 01             ld b,(ix+1)

 474+ 27E9 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h

 476+ 27ED
 477+ 27ED              ; Zombie IX going for (mode 1) or attacking (mode 2) the base tile ahead
 478+ 27ED              zattack:
 479+ 27ED DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)
 480+ 27F0 CD AB 2A             call zside                      ; still a base tile ahead?
 481+ 27F3 CD 48 28             call zbase
 482+ 27F6 20 35                jr nz,.gone
 483+ 27F8 CD 55 28             call zedge                      ; distance to its edge
 484+ 27FB D6 1A                sub ATTACK_R

 486+ 27FF 38 0E                jr c,.at
 487+ 2801 47                   ld b,a                          ; still to go: walk on, no further
 488+ 2802 CD 9B 27             call zcalc_step

 491+ 2809 38 01                jr c,.mv
 492+ 280B 78                   ld a,b

 495+ 2812 FE 02                cp 2
 496+ 2814 28 0C                jr z,.hit

 499+ 281D 36 01                ld (hl),TRUE
 500+ 281F CD 44 3B             call zblip_red                  ; (map.asm)
 501+ 2822 DD 35 11     .hit:   dec (ix+17)
 502+ 2825 C0                   ret nz

 504+ 282A C3 6E 28             jp base_hit

 506+ 2830 DD 36 10 00          ld (ix+16),0                    ; destroyed: walk on (towards the pad)
 507+ 2834 FE 02                cp 2
 508+ 2836 C0                   ret nz                          ; (was only on its way)
 509+ 2837 CD 3F 28             call zflag                      ; attack flag off, map dot back to
 510+ 283A 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE                   ; its usual shade
 511+ 283C C3 DB 3A             jp zblip
 512+ 283F
 513+ 283F              ; HL -> zatk_flags entry of the zombie being moved (zcur_i)
 514+ 283F              zflag:

 516+ 2842 21 72 2F             ld hl,zatk_flags

 519+ 2848
 520+ 2848              ; A = map code: Z if it is a base tile (the pad or the terrace)
 521+ 2848              zbase:

 523+ 284A 38 06                jr c,.no
 524+ 284C FE 12                cp TERRACE_CODE+1

 528+ 2852 F6 01        .no:    or 1                            ; NZ
 529+ 2854 C9                   ret
 530+ 2855
 531+ 2855              ; A = distance from zombie IX to the edge of the next cell along its
 532+ 2855              ; heading (0..255)
 533+ 2855              zedge:

 535+ 2858 1F                   rra                             ; carry: along x
 536+ 2859 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 537+ 285C 38 03                jr c,.ax
 538+ 285E DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 539+ 2861 4F           .ax:    ld c,a
 540+ 2862 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)

 667+ 2924              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 668+ 2924              ; Barricades: terrace tiles away from the base, improvised barricades.  Each
 669+ 2924              ; has its own hit points, in barr_tab (resident): NBARR entries of BARR_REC
 670+ 2924              ; bytes, x, z, hit points (0: none, or destroyed).  barricade_init fills it
 671+ 2924              ; at the level's start from the city map (after the level's map changes),
 672+ 2924              ; the first NBARR found; any more stay but can't be destroyed.  Zombies and
 673+ 2924              ; blasts damage them through base_damage (zbase: any terrace tile is a base

# file opened: human.asm
   1+ 3489              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 3489              ; Z-POLICE - humans to rescue (in the zombie page, after zombie.asm: kept
   3+ 3489              ; mapped in slot 1)
   4+ 3489              ;
   5+ 3489              ; Up to HUMAN_MAX humans, nhumans of them placed at random at start-up (on a
   6+ 3489              ; road, or on a roof low enough to land on).  They stand still, always face
   7+ 3489              ; the player and wave their right arm.  On the city map each is a blue dot,
   8+ 3489              ; flashing 3 frames on, 3 off.
   9+ 3489              ;
  10+ 3489              ; Base: landed on the landing pad (map code PAD_CODE), the passengers get off
  11+ 3489              ; one every DISEMBARK frames (a second) and are counted as saved.
  12+ 3489              ;
  13+ 3489              ; Rescue: when the craft has landed (speed 0, resting on the road or on the
  14+ 3489              ; roof under it), carries fewer than PASSENGER_MAX and a human stands within
  15+ 3489              ; RESCUE_R at the same height (a roof of the same height, or the road), the
  16+ 3489              ; human runs to it at HUMAN_RUN units a frame and climbs in.  A human on a
  17+ 3489              ; taller or lower roof cannot be reached.
  18+ 3489              ;
  19+ 3489              ; Each human is an HREC-byte record:
  20+ 3489              ;   +0,+1  x  (world units: high byte = cell, as for zombies)
  21+ 3489              ;   +2,+3  z
  22+ 3489              ;   +4,+5  y  height of its feet (roof height, or 0 on the road)
  23+ 3489              ;   +6     state (HS_*): 0 unused, 1 waiting in the city, 2 aboard the
  24+ 3489              ;          VTOL, 3 rescued (off at the landing pad), 4 dead
  25+ 3489              ;   +7,+8  cell (x, z) of its map dot
  26+ 3489              ;   +9     1 = map dot shown
  27+ 3489              ;   +10    hit points left (HUMAN_HP; a rocket blast, weapons.asm)
  28+ 3489              ;   +11,+12  cell (x, z) marked with CELL_HUMAN in city_map ($FF = none)
  29+ 3489              ;
  30+ 3489              ; Drawing: draw_cell (city page) tests the cell's CELL_HUMAN mark in
  31+ 3489              ; city_map (bit 6, set by hum_flags) and draws them with human_cell: after
  32+ 3489              ; the building on whose roof they stand, so the roof doesn't cover them.  With
  33+ 3489              ; OCCLUSION_CULL the cell goes on the draw list instead (as its city_col
  34+ 3489              ; address, $6000-$6FFF) and is drawn from there.
  35+ 3489              ;==============================================================================
  36+ 3489
  37+ 3489              HREC            equ 13
  38+ 3489              HS_NONE         equ 0               ; human states (+6)
  39+ 3489              HS_WAIT         equ 1               ; waiting in the city to be rescued
  40+ 3489              HS_ABOARD       equ 2               ; aboard the VTOL
  41+ 3489              HS_SAVED        equ 3               ; rescued: off at the landing pad
  42+ 3489              HS_DEAD         equ 4               ; killed (a rocket blast, a ghost)
  43+ 3489
  44+ 3489                      INCLUDE "tables_human.inc"      ; hum_ptr, waving stick men

# file opened: map.asm
   1+ 3A35              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 3A35              ; Z-POLICE - city map (in the zombie page, after human.asm: kept mapped in
   3+ 3A35              ; slot 1)
   4+ 3A35              ;
   5+ 3A35              ; Everything that draws the GRID x GRID city map at the right of the
   6+ 3A35              ; instrument panel (pixel columns MAP_X.., rows 128-191, one pixel per cell,
   7+ 3A35              ; north up):
   8+ 3A35              ;   * map_init / map_l2: the map itself, drawn once at start-up into both
   9+ 3A35              ;     Layer 2 buffers, and the ULA set-up over it (called from ula_init);
  10+ 3A35              ;   * map_player: the player's flashing white dot, on the ULA (draw_hud);
  11+ 3A35              ;   * zblip / zblip_off: zombie blips, bright green (zombie.asm, weapons.asm);
  12+ 3A35              ;   * hum_dots / hum_dot: humans' flashing blue dots (human.asm);
  13+ 3A35              ;   * map_pix / map_under: set one map cell's pixel in both Layer 2 buffers,
  14+ 3A35              ;     and the colour the map itself has there.
  15+ 3A35              ; Page 7 is always mapped, so all the pages above can call in here.
  16+ 3A35              ;==============================================================================
  17+ 3A35
  18+ 3A35              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  19+ 3A35              ; City map: GRID x GRID cells at one pixel each (64x64), north up, at pixel
  20+ 3A35              ; columns MAP_X.. and rows 128-191.  It is drawn on Layer 2, into both
  21+ 3A35              ; buffers once (the viewport clear only touches rows 0-127), so each
  22+ 3A35              ; building can be its own colour; zombie blips and human dots are written
  23+ 3A35              ; into it too.  The ULA above it is transparent paper with white ink for the
  24+ 3A35              ; player's flashing dot.
  25+ 3A35              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  26+ 3A35              map_init:
  27+ 3A35 AF                   xor a                           ; no player dot drawn yet
  28+ 3A36 32 DF 3B             ld (mp_mask),a
  29+ 3A39 21 18 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR+16*32+MAP_COL    ; map area: transparent paper,
  30+ 3A3C 0E 08                ld c,8                          ; white ink
  31+ 3A3E 06 08        .ar:    ld b,32-MAP_COL
  32+ 3A40 E5                   push hl
  33+ 3A41 36 07        .ac:    ld (hl),7
  34+ 3A43 23                   inc hl
  35+ 3A44 10 FB                djnz .ac
  36+ 3A46 E1                   pop hl
  37+ 3A47 11 20 00             ld de,32
  38+ 3A4A 19                   add hl,de
  39+ 3A4B 0D                   dec c
  40+ 3A4C 20 F0                jr nz,.ar
  41+ 3A4E 21 18 90             ld hl,ULA_BMP+$1000+MAP_COL     ; no panel divider over the map
  42+ 3A51 06 08                ld b,32-MAP_COL
  43+ 3A53 36 00        .dv:    ld (hl),0
  44+ 3A55 23                   inc hl
  45+ 3A56 10 FB                djnz .dv
  46+ 3A58 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A                  ; both Layer 2 buffers
  47+ 3A5A CD 5F 3A             call map_l2
  48+ 3A5D 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
  49+ 3A5F                      ; fall through
  50+ 3A5F
  51+ 3A5F              ; A = first 8K page of a Layer 2 buffer: draw the map into it.  Streets are
  52+ 3A5F              ; MAP_BG, buildings their colour family's base shade.
  53+ 3A5F              map_l2:
  54+ 3A5F 32 E1 3B             ld (ml_base),a
  55+ 3A62 21 00 70             ld hl,city_map
  56+ 3A65 11 00 60             ld de,city_col
  57+ 3A68 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; C = j (row), +Z
  58+ 3A6A 3E BF        .row:   ld a,191                        ; y = 191 - j  (north up)
  59+ 3A6C 91                   sub c
  60+ 3A6D F5                   push af
  61+ 3A6E 07                   rlca
  62+ 3A6F 07                   rlca
  63+ 3A70 07                   rlca
  64+ 3A71 E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
  65+ 3A73 47                   ld b,a
  66+ 3A74 3A E1 3B             ld a,(ml_base)
  67+ 3A77 80                   add a,b
  68+ 3A78 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a                   ; paged in at $0000
  69+ 3A7B F1                   pop af
  70+ 3A7C E6 1F                and 31
  71+ 3A7E DD 67                ld ixh,a
  72+ 3A80 DD 2E C0             ld ixl,MAP_X
  73+ 3A83 06 40                ld b,GRID
  74+ 3A85 7E           .px:    ld a,(hl)
  75+ 3A86 E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
  76+ 3A88 28 09                jr z,.bg                        ; a street,
  77+ 3A8A FE 12                cp VOID_CODE
  78+ 3A8C 28 05                jr z,.bg                        ; or out of bounds: black
  79+ 3A8E 1A                   ld a,(de)
  80+ 3A8F F6 01                or SHADE_FACE
  81+ 3A91 18 02                jr .st
  82+ 3A93 3E 27        .bg:    ld a,MAP_BG
  83+ 3A95 DD 77 00     .st:    ld (ix+0),a
  84+ 3A98 DD 23                inc ix
  85+ 3A9A 23                   inc hl
  86+ 3A9B 13                   inc de
  87+ 3A9C 10 E7                djnz .px
  88+ 3A9E 0C                   inc c
  89+ 3A9F 79                   ld a,c
  90+ 3AA0 FE 40                cp GRID
  91+ 3AA2 20 C6                jr nz,.row
  92+ 3AA4 C9                   ret
  93+ 3AA5
  94+ 3AA5              ; D = y, E = x -> HL = ULA byte (screen paged at ULA_BMP), A = pixel mask
  95+ 3AA5              ula_pixel:
  96+ 3AA5 ED 94                pixelad                         ; HL = $4000-based address
  97+ 3AA7 7C                   ld a,h
  98+ 3AA8 C6 40                add a,high (ULA_BMP-$4000)
  99+ 3AAA 67                   ld h,a
 100+ 3AAB ED 95                setae                           ; A = $80 >> (x & 7)
 101+ 3AAD C9                   ret
 102+ 3AAE
 103+ 3AAE              ; Flashing player position: undo last frame's XOR, then on alternate frames
 104+ 3AAE              ; XOR the pixel under the player (1 map pixel per cell).
 105+ 3AAE              map_player:
 106+ 3AAE 2A DD 3B             ld hl,(mp_addr)
 107+ 3AB1 3A DF 3B             ld a,(mp_mask)
 108+ 3AB4 AE                   xor (hl)
 109+ 3AB5 77                   ld (hl),a
 110+ 3AB6 AF                   xor a
 111+ 3AB7 32 DF 3B             ld (mp_mask),a
 112+ 3ABA 21 E0 3B             ld hl,mp_frame
 113+ 3ABD 34                   inc (hl)
 114+ 3ABE CB 46                bit 0,(hl)
 115+ 3AC0 C8                   ret z                           ; off this frame
 116+ 3AC1 3A BA A7             ld a,(cam_x+2)                  ; cell x: one map pixel per cell
 117+ 3AC4 C6 C0                add a,MAP_X
 118+ 3AC6 5F                   ld e,a
 119+ 3AC7 3A BD A7             ld a,(cam_z+2)                  ; cell z
 120+ 3ACA ED 44                neg
 121+ 3ACC C6 BF                add a,191                       ; north up
 122+ 3ACE 57                   ld d,a
 123+ 3ACF CD A5 3A             call ula_pixel
 124+ 3AD2 22 DD 3B             ld (mp_addr),hl
 125+ 3AD5 32 DF 3B             ld (mp_mask),a
 126+ 3AD8 AE                   xor (hl)
 127+ 3AD9 77                   ld (hl),a
 128+ 3ADA C9                   ret
 129+ 3ADB
 130+ 3ADB              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 131+ 3ADB              ; Zombie blips: each zombie is one bright green (MAP_ZOMBIE) pixel at its cell,
 132+ 3ADB              ; using record bytes +12,+13 (blip cell) and +14 (bit 0: plotted; bits 1-2:
 133+ 3ADB              ; its place in the shade cycle).  Each time zblip draws it, the blip takes
 134+ 3ADB              ; the next shade of zshades: bright, mid, dark, mid, bright...  Zombies only
 135+ 3ADB              ; stand in street cells, so taking a blip off puts the street colour (MAP_BG)
 136+ 3ADB              ; back.  (When two zombies share a cell and one leaves, the other's blip is
 137+ 3ADB              ; gone until its own next check.)
 138+ 3ADB              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 139+ 3ADB              ; Move zombie IX's street map blip to its current cell, in its next shade
 140+ 3ADB              zblip:
 141+ 3ADB CD 11 3B             call zblip_off
 142+ 3ADE DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)                     ; cell x
 143+ 3AE1 DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)                     ; cell z
 144+ 3AE4 DD 73 0C             ld (ix+12),e
 145+ 3AE7 DD 72 0D             ld (ix+13),d
 146+ 3AEA DD 7E 0E             ld a,(ix+14)                    ; shade step (bits 1-2), plotted off
 147+ 3AED 4F                   ld c,a
 148+ 3AEE C6 02                add a,2                         ; next step, and plotted
 149+ 3AF0 E6 06                and 6
 150+ 3AF2 3C                   inc a
 151+ 3AF3 DD 77 0E             ld (ix+14),a
 152+ 3AF6 DD 7E 0F             ld a,(ix+15)                    ; a ghost or banshee: its own shades
 153+ 3AF9 21 D1 3B             ld hl,zshades
 154+ 3AFC FE 03                cp ZT_GHOST
 155+ 3AFE 38 08                jr c,.z
 156+ 3B00 21 D5 3B             ld hl,gshades
 157+ 3B03 28 03                jr z,.z
 158+ 3B05 21 D9 3B             ld hl,bshades
 159+ 3B08 79           .z:     ld a,c
 160+ 3B09 0F                   rrca
 161+ 3B0A E6 03                and 3
 162+ 3B0C ED 31                add hl,a
 163+ 3B0E 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; this step's shade
 164+ 3B0F 18 11                jr map_pix
 165+ 3B11
 166+ 3B11              ; Take zombie IX's blip off the map
 167+ 3B11              zblip_off:
 168+ 3B11 DD CB 0E 46          bit 0,(ix+14)
 169+ 3B15 C8                   ret z
 170+ 3B16 DD CB 0E 86          res 0,(ix+14)                   ; (keeps its shade step)
 171+ 3B1A DD 5E 0C             ld e,(ix+12)
 172+ 3B1D DD 56 0D             ld d,(ix+13)
 173+ 3B20 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG                     ; a street cell again
 174+ 3B22                      ; fall through
 175+ 3B22
 176+ 3B22              ; Set map pixel for cell (E, D) to colour A in both Layer 2 buffers.  Pages
 177+ 3B22              ; their section in at $0000 (slot 0, which drawing re-pages as it goes).
 178+ 3B22              map_pix:
 179+ 3B22 4F                   ld c,a
 180+ 3B23 3E BF                ld a,191                        ; y = 191 - z  (north up)
 181+ 3B25 92                   sub d
 182+ 3B26 47                   ld b,a
 183+ 3B27 07                   rlca
 184+ 3B28 07                   rlca
 185+ 3B29 07                   rlca
 186+ 3B2A E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
 187+ 3B2C 57                   ld d,a
 188+ 3B2D 78                   ld a,b
 189+ 3B2E E6 1F                and 31
 190+ 3B30 67                   ld h,a
 191+ 3B31 7B                   ld a,e
 192+ 3B32 C6 C0                add a,MAP_X
 193+ 3B34 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = pixel in the section
 194+ 3B35 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 195+ 3B37 82                   add a,d
 196+ 3B38 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 197+ 3B3B 71                   ld (hl),c
 198+ 3B3C 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 199+ 3B3E 82                   add a,d
 200+ 3B3F ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 201+ 3B42 71                   ld (hl),c
 202+ 3B43 C9                   ret
 203+ 3B44
 204+ 3B44              ; Zombie IX attacking: its blip red (MAP_ZOMBIE_ATK) at its current cell,
 205+ 3B44              ; until zblip draws it normally again
 206+ 3B44              zblip_red:
 207+ 3B44 CD 11 3B             call zblip_off
 208+ 3B47 DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)
 209+ 3B4A DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 210+ 3B4D DD 73 0C             ld (ix+12),e
 211+ 3B50 DD 72 0D             ld (ix+13),d
 212+ 3B53 DD CB 0E C6          set 0,(ix+14)                   ; plotted (keeps its shade step)
 213+ 3B57 3E 13                ld a,MAP_ZOMBIE_ATK
 214+ 3B59 C3 22 3B             jp map_pix
 215+ 3B5C
 216+ 3B5C              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 217+ 3B5C              ; Human dots: flashing blue (HUMAN_BLIP), using record bytes +7,+8 (dot cell)
 218+ 3B5C              ; and +9 (shown).
 219+ 3B5C              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 220+ 3B5C              ; C = 1: show, 0: hide the map dots of all waiting humans
 221+ 3B5C              hum_dots:
 222+ 3B5C DD 21 31 39          ld ix,humans
 223+ 3B60 06 14                ld b,HUMAN_MAX
 224+ 3B62 C5           .lp:    push bc
 225+ 3B63 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 226+ 3B66 FE 01                cp HS_WAIT
 227+ 3B68 CC 74 3B             call z,hum_dot
 228+ 3B6B 11 0D 00             ld de,HREC
 229+ 3B6E DD 19                add ix,de
 230+ 3B70 C1                   pop bc
 231+ 3B71 10 EF                djnz .lp
 232+ 3B73 C9                   ret
 233+ 3B74
 234+ 3B74              ; Human IX's map dot: C = 1 show (at its current cell), 0 hide.  Keeps C.
 235+ 3B74              hum_dot:
 236+ 3B74 C5                   push bc
 237+ 3B75 DD CB 09 46          bit 0,(ix+9)                    ; take the old dot off first
 238+ 3B79 28 10                jr z,.on
 239+ 3B7B DD 36 09 00          ld (ix+9),0
 240+ 3B7F DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+7)
 241+ 3B82 DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+8)
 242+ 3B85 CD A8 3B             call map_under                  ; A = what the map shows there
 243+ 3B88 CD 22 3B             call map_pix
 244+ 3B8B C1           .on:    pop bc
 245+ 3B8C C5                   push bc
 246+ 3B8D CB 41                bit 0,c
 247+ 3B8F 28 15                jr z,.x
 248+ 3B91 DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)
 249+ 3B94 DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 250+ 3B97 DD 73 07             ld (ix+7),e
 251+ 3B9A DD 72 08             ld (ix+8),d
 252+ 3B9D DD 36 09 01          ld (ix+9),1
 253+ 3BA1 3E 2F                ld a,HUMAN_BLIP
 254+ 3BA3 CD 22 3B             call map_pix
 255+ 3BA6 C1           .x:     pop bc
 256+ 3BA7 C9                   ret
 257+ 3BA8
 258+ 3BA8              ; E = cell x, D = cell z -> A = the map colour of that cell.  Keeps DE.
 259+ 3BA8              map_under:
 260+ 3BA8 D5                   push de
 261+ 3BA9 6A                   ld l,d
 262+ 3BAA 26 00                ld h,0
 263+ 3BAC                      ROWOFS
 263+ 3BAC 29          >        add hl,hl
 263+ 3BAD 29          >        add hl,hl
 263+ 3BAE 29          >        add hl,hl
 263+ 3BAF 29          >        add hl,hl
 263+ 3BB0 29          >        add hl,hl
 263+ 3BB1 29          >        add hl,hl
 264+ 3BB2 7B                   ld a,e
 265+ 3BB3 ED 31                add hl,a
 266+ 3BB5 E5                   push hl
 267+ 3BB6 11 00 70             ld de,city_map
 268+ 3BB9 19                   add hl,de
 269+ 3BBA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 270+ 3BBB E1                   pop hl
 271+ 3BBC E6 1F                and CELL_CODE
 272+ 3BBE 28 0D                jr z,.bg                        ; street,
 273+ 3BC0 FE 12                cp VOID_CODE
 274+ 3BC2 28 09                jr z,.bg                        ; or out of bounds: black
 275+ 3BC4 11 00 60             ld de,city_col
 276+ 3BC7 19                   add hl,de
 277+ 3BC8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 278+ 3BC9 F6 01                or SHADE_FACE
 279+ 3BCB 18 02                jr .x
 280+ 3BCD 3E 27        .bg:    ld a,MAP_BG
 281+ 3BCF D1           .x:     pop de
 282+ 3BD0 C9                   ret
 283+ 3BD1
 284+ 3BD1              ;==============================================================================
 285+ 3BD1              ; Map data
 286+ 3BD1              ;==============================================================================
 287+ 3BD1 0F 33 37 33  zshades:    db MAP_ZOMBIE, MAP_ZOMBIE_MID, MAP_ZOMBIE_DARK, MAP_ZOMBIE_MID
 288+ 3BD5 63 67 6B 67  gshades:    db GHOST_COL, MAP_GHOST_MID, MAP_GHOST_DARK, MAP_GHOST_MID
 289+ 3BD9 73 77 7B 77  bshades:    db BANSHEE_COL, MAP_BANSHEE_MID, MAP_BANSHEE_DARK, MAP_BANSHEE_MID
 290+ 3BDD 00 80        mp_addr:    dw ULA_BMP                  ; player dot: ULA byte, mask, flash
 291+ 3BDF 00           mp_mask:    db 0
 292+ 3BE0 00           mp_frame:   db 0
 293+ 3BE1 00           ml_base:    db 0                        ; map_l2: first page of the buffer
 294+ 3BE2
# file closed: map.asm
1476  3BE2                      DISPLAY "Map end:   ",/A,$
1477  3BE2                      INCLUDE "interrupts.asm"        ; IM 2 vector table and routine
# file opened: interrupts.asm
   1+ 3BE2              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 3BE2              ; Z-POLICE - interrupt mode 2 (in the zombie page, after map.asm: kept
   3+ 3BE2              ; mapped in slot 1, so the table and routine are there whatever page is
   4+ 3BE2              ; swapped in; the ROM replaces slot 1 only during esxDOS calls, when
   5+ 3BE2              ; interrupts are off)
   6+ 3BE2              ;
   7+ 3BE2              ; In IM 2 an interrupt calls the address held in the word at I*256 + the
   8+ 3BE2              ; byte on the data bus, which can be anything.  So:
   9+ 3BE2              ;   * im2_table is 256 words, every one the address of im2_routine, at a
  10+ 3BE2              ;     256-byte boundary (I holds the high byte of its address): any bus
  11+ 3BE2              ;     byte, even $FF (bytes $FF and $100), reads a word inside it;
  12+ 3BE2              ;   * an odd bus byte reads a word that straddles two entries, low byte
  13+ 3BE2              ;     from one and high byte from the next, so im2_routine sits at an
  14+ 3BE2              ;     address whose high and low bytes are the same (IM2_ROUTINE, $xyxy):
  15+ 3BE2              ;     read either way round, it's the same address.
  16+ 3BE2              ; im2_init sets I and IM 2 (start calls it after init_audio).  It doesn't
  17+ 3BE2              ; enable interrupts: the game still runs with them off (the span filler
  18+ 3BE2              ; borrows SP, the file handler swaps the ROM in), so EI is for later, once
  19+ 3BE2              ; the main loop is ready for them.
  20+ 3BE2              ;==============================================================================
  21+ 3BE2
  22+ 3BE2              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  23+ 3BE2              ; Point I at the vector table and select interrupt mode 2.  Interrupts stay
  24+ 3BE2              ; disabled.
  25+ 3BE2              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  26+ 3BE2              im2_init:
  27+ 3BE2 3E 3C                ld a,high im2_table
  28+ 3BE4 ED 47                ld i,a
  29+ 3BE6 ED 5E                im 2
  30+ 3BE8 C9                   ret
  31+ 3BE9
  32+ 3BE9 00 00 00...          ALIGN 256
  33+ 3C00              im2_table:
  34+ 3C00                      REPT 256
  35+ 3C00 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine
  35+ 3C02 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine
  35+ 3C04 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine
  35+ 3C06 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine
  35+ 3C08 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine
  35+ 3C0A 3E 3E       >        dw im2_routine

1497  0200              ; Sprite pattern banks, swapped into slot 7 ($E000) while they load
1498  0200                      MMU SPRBANK_SLOT e, PAGE_SPR0, SPRBANK_ADDR
1499  E000                      ORG SPRBANK_ADDR
1500  E000                      INCLUDE "sprite_data.asm"
# file opened: sprite_data.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - sprite pattern banks (8K pages, assembled at $E000)
   3+ E000              ;
   4+ E000              ; Hardware sprite patterns are only needed when they are loaded into the
   5+ E000              ; sprite pattern memory, so they live in their own pages, out of the way,
   6+ E000              ; each with its own loader so that no resident memory is used:
   7+ E000              ;
   8+ E000              ;       nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPR0      ; the bank into slot 7
   9+ E000              ;       call spr_load_bank              ; copy its patterns
  10+ E000              ;
  11+ E000              ; Call it from outside slot 7 (resident code or a game logic page in slot
  12+ E000              ; 6), and page back whatever slot 7 had afterwards if it is still needed.
  13+ E000              ; spr_load_bank is the same address ($E000) in every bank.
  14+ E000              ;
  15+ E000              ; Each bank (SPRBANK_ADDR = $E000):
  16+ E000              ;   +0          spr_load_bank: the loader (SPRB_LOADER), the same in each
  17+ E000              ;   SPRB_COUNT  number of patterns (1..31: 256 bytes each, 8-bit 16x16)
  18+ E000              ;   SPRB_FIRST  the pattern number the first one loads to (0..63)
  19+ E000              ;   SPRB_DATA   the patterns, one after another
  20+ E000              ; More sprites: add a bank (the next free page, its own PAGE_SPRn in
  21+ E000              ; memory_bank_defs.asm, the MMU / ORG lines in zpolice.asm and a block here
  22+ E000              ; starting with SPRB_LOADER).
  23+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  24+ E000
  25+ E000              ; Copy this bank's patterns into the sprite pattern memory.  Runs from the
  26+ E000              ; bank itself: reads only, so it needs no variables.
  27+ E000                      MACRO SPRB_LOADER
  28+ E000 ~                    ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_FIRST)  ; from this pattern
  29+ E000 ~                    ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
  30+ E000 ~                    out (c),a
  31+ E000 ~                    ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_COUNT)
  32+ E000 ~                    ld d,a
  33+ E000 ~                    ld hl,SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_DATA
  34+ E000 ~                    ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
  35+ E000 ~            .lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes a pattern
  36+ E000 ~                    otir
  37+ E000 ~                    dec d
  38+ E000 ~                    jr nz,.lp
  39+ E000 ~                    ret
  40+ E000                      ENDM
  41+ E000
  42+ E000              spr_load_bank   equ SPRBANK_ADDR        ; the loader, in every bank
  43+ E000              SPRB_COUNT      equ spr_bank0_hdr-spr_bank0
  44+ E000              SPRB_FIRST      equ SPRB_COUNT+1
  45+ E000              SPRB_DATA       equ SPRB_COUNT+2
  46+ E000
  47+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  48+ E000              ; Bank 0 (PAGE_SPR0): the human status sprites (patterns 0-3), then the
  49+ E000              ; compass bar's letters, dots and pointer (CSPR_*, hud_sprites.asm)
  50+ E000              ; (zpolice.asm maps PAGE_SPR0 to SPRBANK_SLOT, ORG SPRBANK_ADDR, first)
  51+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  52+ E000              spr_bank0:
  53+ E000                      SPRB_LOADER
  53+ E000 3A 1A E0    >        ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_FIRST)  ; from this pattern
  53+ E003 01 3B 30    >        ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
  53+ E006 ED 79       >        out (c),a
  53+ E008 3A 19 E0    >        ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_COUNT)
  53+ E00B 57          >        ld d,a
  53+ E00C 21 1B E0    >        ld hl,SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_DATA
  53+ E00F 0E 5B       >        ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
  53+ E011 06 00       >.lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes a pattern
  53+ E013 ED B3       >        otir
  53+ E015 15          >        dec d
  53+ E016 20 F9       >        jr nz,.lp
  53+ E018 C9          >        ret
  54+ E019              spr_bank0_hdr:
  55+ E019 0B                   db SPRB0_PATTERNS               ; SPRB_COUNT
  56+ E01A 00                   db 0                            ; SPRB_FIRST
  57+ E01B                      INCLUDE "tables_hspr.inc"       ; hspr_patterns (gentables.py):
# file opened: tables_hspr.inc
